mēṉāḷ varalāṟṟut tiṇikkaḷat talivar
maturik kāmarācar palkalik kaḻakam





amiḻtam patippakam
ceṉṉi - 600 017



nūṟ kuṟippu
nūṟpeyar	:	tamiḻaka varalāṟṟu varici - 9

aḷavu	:	1/8 temmi
eḻuttu	:	12 puḷḷi
pakkam	:	14+ 298 = 312
nūl kaṭṭamippu	:	iyalpu (cātāraṇam)
vili	:	urupā. 290 /-

		ti.nakar, ceṉṉi - 17.

aṟimukavuri
ulaka mutaṉmoḻi tamiḻ! ulaka mutal māntaṉ tamiḻaṉ. ulakam tōṉṟiya pōtē uṭaṉ tōṉṟi iṉṟaḷavum kaṉṉiyākavē iruppatu tamiḻnilam. attamiḻ nilattit tāyakamākak koṇṭavaṉē tamiḻaṉ. anta tamiḻaṉē tolmāntaṉ. avaṉuṭiya tolpaḻaṅ kālantoṭṭu iṟṟaḷavumuḷḷa varalāṟṟuc cuvaṭukaḷic cuṭṭik kāṭṭuvatē it "tāynilavaralāṟu"ākum.
tamiḻaka varalāṟṟil taliciṟantu niṟpatu kaḻaka kālamākum. varalāṟṟu ilakkaṇappaṭi taṉṉaka, ayalaka, tolporuḷ, nāṇayac cāṉṟukaḷik kuṟiviṉṟik koṇṭamiyāl kaḻakak kālattip "poṟkālam” eṉkiṟōm. kaṭikkaḻakak kālam ki.mu. 300 mutal ki.pi. 300 variyilāṉatu aṭuttu varum kaḷappirar kālam ki. pi. 300 mutal ki.pi. 600 variyilāṉatu. itaṉi "iruṇṭa kālam" eṉṟu kūṟuvatu kuruṭṭut taṉamāṉa varalāṟṟu marapākiviṭṭatu. eppoḻutellām āriyap paṇpukaḷum, camaṟkiruta moḻiyum makkaḷiṉ vāḻkkiyil tākkatti uṇṭākkukiṟatō ak kālattip “poṟkālam" eṉṟum āriya moḻikkum paṇpāṭṭiṟkum tāḻvu ēṟpaṭukiṟa akkālatti “'iruṇṭakālam" eṉṟum kūṟuvatittāṉ kuruṭṭuttaṉamāṉa varalāṟṟu marapu eṉkiṟōm. ivvāṟu tāṉ āriyap paṇpāṭṭiyum, moḻiyiyum maṅkac ceytu, kaḻakak kālap paṇpāṭṭiyum, moḻiyiyum malarac ceyta kālamē kaḷappirar kālattiltāṉ. ikkālam tāṉ camaṇattukkum, cākkiyattukkum āriyattiṟkum cāvumaṇi aṭitta kālamākum. tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷukku ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷiyārtta kālamē kaḷappirar kālam tāṉ. eṉavē, itaṉi iruṇṭa kālam eṉkiṉṟaṉar.
kaḷappirari yaṭuttu tāynilamākiya tamiḻakattit tōrāyamāka ki, pi, 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭiliruntu ki.pi. 9ām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari āṇṭa pallavar kālattil āriyam tali tūkkiyatu. pallavarkaḷ, ayalakattiliruntu āriya pūcārikaḷit tamiḻakattiṟku iṟakkumati ceytu, tāynilatti' "tēvatāṉam", ''pirammatēyam", makātēva maṅkalam", mutaliyaṉavāka kūṟupōṭṭu, tamiḻ vaḻipāṭukaḷi oḻittaṉar. pūvip paṭittu pūci ceyta tamiḻaṉ arccaṉikku āṭpaṭṭāṉ!
ki.pi. 9ām nūṟṟāṇṭiliruntu ki.pi. 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭu variyāṇṭa muṟkālap pāṇṭiyarum, cōḻarum tamiḻar uṭalōṭum āriyap paṇpōṭumtāṉ āṇṭaṉar. kaliyum, paṇpāṭum, vaḻipāṭum māṟupaṭṭaṉa. āṉāl, kōyiṟ kalikaḷum, iṟineṟi (pakti) ilakkiyaṅkaḷum tamiḻiṉ karuvūlak kaṇakkil cērntuviṭṭaṉa.
piṟkālap pāṇṭiyarukkuppiṉ, teluṅkariṉ vicaya nakara āṭciyum, nāyakkar, pāḷiyakkārar āṭcikaḷum tamiḻakatti alikkaḻittu āṅkila āṭcikku aṭikōliṉa. āṅkila āṭci ki.pi. 1750liruntu ki.pi. 1947vari tamiḻakattil nilaviyatu. atu, putukkāla āṭciyāka virintatu.
āṅkila āṭciyi viṭutali vēṭkik koṇṭu akaṟṟiya piṉ, tamiḻakattil araciyal kaṭci cārnta pārāḷumaṉṟa muṟiyilāṉa makkaḷāṭci ēṟpaṭṭatu. mīṇṭum tamiḻppaṇpāṭu kaḷi malarac ceyya kaḻaka āṭcikaḷ muyalukiṉṟaṉa.
potuvāka, intiya varalāṟṟil ovvoru āṟu nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṭivilum paṇpāṭṭu maṟumalarcci ēṟpaṭuvati uṇarukiṟōm attakiya maṟumalarcciyit tamiḻaka varalāṟṟilum kāṇkiṟōm. āṉāl, kaḻakakkālatti aṭuttu varum attiyāyaṅkaḷ ovvoru mūṉṟu nūṟṟāṇṭukkoru muṟi putiya maṟumalarcciyi ēṟpaṭutti yuḷḷaṉa. ettakiya māṟupaṭṭac cūḻalilum, tamiḻppaṇpāṭu, aṭi yōṭu māṟiyatum, maṟikkap paṭṭatumilli. itaṟku orē karaṇiyam cemmoḻiyāṉa tamiḻēyākum. ammoḻiyic cemmippaṭutta mutal, iṭi, kaṭikkaḻakaṅkaḷ tōrāyamāka pattu āyiram āṇṭukaḷ uḻittaṉa. iṉṟaḷavum tōṇṭattōṇṭac curakkum maṇaṟkēṇi pōl ciṟantu viḷaṅkum nāṟpatu kaḻakak kālanūlkaḷum, eṇṇilaṭaṅkā iṟineṟi ilakkiyaṅkaḷukku tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ karuvūlamāka uḷḷaṉa.
pērūḻikkālaṅkaḷilum tamiḻmaṇ cāyntatilli! āriyam taṉmaya mākkiyum tamiḻppaṇpu māṟiyattilli! nāṉku varuṇa pākupāṭu iṅku illi. vēṟṟumoḻikaḷālum, mēlināṭṭup paṇpāṭukaḷālum tamiḻ moḻiyiyum, paṇpāṭukaḷiyumāṟṟi amikka muṭiyavilli. kāṟṟacivāl aciyum nāṇalpōl vaḷiyum, āṉāl nimirntu viṭum! cila poḻutu māṟupaṭṭatip pōl tōṟṟameṭukkum. āṉāl oṭintu urumāṟiyatilli eṉpatutāṉ varalāṟṟu uṇmi . ,
"ōṭum, uṭkārum, tāvum, tāṇṭikkutikkum āṉāl orē aṭiyāka aṭiccuvaṭu aṟṟup pōkātu. ituvē varalāṟṟu ilakkaṇam" eṉpār ulaka nākarika varalāṟṟu nūli yārtta ē.kē, tāyiṉpi. itaṟku muṟṟilum eṭuttukkāṭṭākat tikaḻvatē tamiḻaka varalāṟu ākum. viṭutalikkuppiṉṉum itē taṉit taṉmiyōṭu tāṉ tamiḻaka varalāṟu toṭarukiṟatu. talicāyntu pōṉatip pōla uṇaruvōm. āṉāl, koṭi cāyntu viṭavilli eṉpatutāṉ uṇmi!
“mūlattiṉ kūṟupāṭu palavāyiram āṇṭukaḷukkup piṉṉum mīṇṭum mūlamākavē tōṉṟum eṉpatu māntaviyal aṟiviyalār kaṇṭamuṭivu ākum. āṉāl, tamiḻkkurutiyōṭu kālantōṟum aṟupaṭātu tikaḻnta tamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷāl tamiḻppaṇpum tāynila varalāṟum kaṭṭik kākkap peṟṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ vīriya maṟumalarccikku untu caktikaḷākat tōṉṟiyavarkaḷē tanti periyārum, pēraṟiñar aṇṇāvumāvar. eṉavē, tol paḻaṅkālan toṭṭu, iṉṟaḷavum aṟupaṭāmal toṭarvatu tāynila varalāṟē.
intiya varalāṟṟil iṭiyiṭiyē ēṟpaṭṭa anniyar āṭcikaḷukku maṟutali illāmal tamiḻakattilum vēṟṟu nilattār, vēṟṟumoḻiyār āṭcikaḷēṟpaṭṭālum avi acivukaḷākavē niṉṟaṉavē oḻiya muṟṟup puḷḷiyākavilli. ituvē, tāynila varalāṟṟiṉ cīriya taṉit taṉmiyākum. ittakiya māṇpuṟu varalāṟṟuc cuvaṭukaḷic cuṭṭik kāṭṭic celvatē icciṟunūl.
innūl uruvāka utaviya aṉivarukkum, kuṟippāka itaṉi accēṟṟi aḻakuṟa amittu, 'tamiḻmaṇ' patippaka urimiyāḷar, tamiḻkkāvalar kō. iḷavaḻakaṉār avarkaḷiṉ tuṇiviyār amiḻtam patippakam vaḻi veḷiyiṭukiṟār. avarkaḷukku naṉṟik kaṭappāṭuṭiyēṉ.
- kō. taṅkavēlu


patappuri
'varalāṟu eṉpatu uṇarccikaḷiṉ tokuppu alla. uṇmi nikaḻvukaḷiṉ tokuppu:' 'varalāṟu eṉpatu ōr iṉattiṉ vāḻvukkum tāḻvukkum cāṉṟu kūṟum niṉivuc ciṉṉam', 'varalāṟu eṉpatu mānta iṉattiṉ aṟivukkaruvūlam', 'tamiḻiṉattiṉ varalāṟu neṭiyatu; nīṇṭatu; paḻamperumi mikkatu; āyvulakiṟku ariya pāṭaṅkaḷit taruvatu varalāṟu eṉpatu kaṭanta kālattiṉ nikaḻvukaḷiyum, vāḻum kālattiṉ nikaḻvukaḷiyum, etirkālattiṉ nikaḻvukaḷiyum uyirppākak kāṭṭuvatu.
“varalāṟu eṉpatu eṉṉa? muṟkālattil eṉṉa naṭantirukkalām eṉpatippaṟṟi pintiya tali muṟiyic cārnta māntaṉoruvaṉ taṉ maṉattil uruvākkik koḷvatē varalāṟākum eṉpar kāri pekmaṉ(GayBatmatJAOS-2005). ivvaralāṟṟi eḻutupavar aṟivunili, maṉanili, camutāyap pārvi paṭṭaṟivu, kaṇṇōṭṭam, piṟacāṉṟukaḷi muṉvittu eḻutuvar. aḵtu aṟiviyal, kali, camayam ākiyavaṟṟiṉ ūṟṟākat tikaḻvatu.
ōr iṉattiṉ vāḻvum tāḻvum anta iṉam naṭantu vanta kālaṭiccuvaṭukaḷip poṟuttē amiyum. oru nāṭṭiṉ varalāṟṟiṟku an nāṭṭil eḻunta ilakkiyaṅkaḷ,caṇṭaṟiyappaṭṭuḷḷa kalveṭṭukkaḷ, putiporuḷkaḷ, ceppēṭukaḷ, nāṇayaṅkaḷ, paḻaṅkālak kaṭṭaṭac citivukaḷ, ciṟpacciṉṉaṅkaḷ, camayaṅkaḷ tāṉ cāṉṟu pakarvaṉa. attaṉiyum perumaḷavu uḷḷa maṇ tamiḻmaṇṇē ākum. moḻi, iṉa, nāṭṭu varalāṟu eṉpatu oru kuṭumpattiṟku eḻutappaṭum āvaṇam pōṉṟatu. ivvaralāṟu uṇmi varalāṟāka amiya vēṇṭum eṉpār moḻinūl mūtaṟiñar pāvāṇar.
intiyap perunilattiṉ varalāṟu vaṭakkiliruntu eḻutappaṭak kūṭātu. anta varalāṟu intiyāviṉ teṉmuṉiyiliruntu eḻutappaṭavēṇṭum. atuvē uṇmi varalāṟāka amiyum eṉpatu maṉōṇ maṇīyam cuntaraṉār kūṟiyatu; viṉceṉṭ cumit ēṟṟatu.
tamiḻiṉattiṉ paḻamiyip pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷ piṉṉōkkip pārttu, tolkāppiyam uṭpaṭṭa caṅkanūlkaḷ aṉittiyum muḻumiyākac cevvaṉē payaṉpaṭutti āṅkilattil mutaṉmutalil 1929il tamiḻar varalāṟu eḻutiyavar piṭi.cīṉivācayyaṅkār āvār. annūlil tamiḻarē teṉṉāṭṭil, ēṉ intiyāvil, toṉṟu toṭṭu vāḻntu varum maṇṇiṉ mintarkaḷ eṉpatit tam āḻnta āypula aṟivāl niṟuviṉār. tamiḻnāṭṭil iṉṟu varalāṟu, moḻiyiyal pōṉṟa tuṟikaḷil vallunarākat tikaḻum aṟiñar pi. irāmanātaṉ avarkaḷ annūliṉi tamiḻākkam ceytuḷḷār. ataṉit tamiḻar varalāṟu (kipi600 vari) eṉum nūlākat tamiḻmaṇ patippakam 2008il veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu. ivvaralāṟṟu varicikkum tiru. irāmanātaṉ avarkaḷ aṟimukavurikaḷ vaḻaṅki aṇicērttuḷḷār. '
pāṇṭiyar varalāṟu, piṟkālac cōḻar carittiram, pallavar varalāṟu, kaḷappirar kālat tamiḻakam (varalāṟṟāciriyar nālvar nūlkaḷiṉ tokuppu), koṅku nāṭṭu varalāṟu, tuḷu nāṭṭu varalāṟu, tamiḻaka varalāṟu, tāynila varalāṟu, tamiḻakac camūkap paṇpāṭṭu varalāṟu, eṉum nūlkaḷit tamiḻaka varalāṟṟukkup perumi cērkkum vakiyil tamiḻaka varalāṟṟu varici eṉum talippil veḷiyiṭukiṟōm. ivaṟṟuḷ perumpālāṉavi iṉṟum varalāṟṟu māṇavarkaḷum, varalāṟṟu āyvāḷarkaḷum paṭikkavēṇṭiya karuvi nūlkaḷākavum, eṉṟum payaṉtarum (classic) nūlkaḷākavum karutappaṭa vēṇṭiyavi. paḷḷikaḷ, kallūrikaḷ, palkalik kaḻaka varalāṟṟut tuṟikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ māṇavarkaḷum, kallūri! palkalikkaḻaka āyvuttuṟikaḷum innūlkaḷi vāṅkip payaṉkoḷvārāka.
tamiḻaka varalāṟṟukku aṇikalaṉāka viḷaṅkum ivvaralāṟṟuk karuvūlaṅkaḷip paḻamikkum putumikkum perumi cērkkum vakiyil ti.vi.catācivap paṇṭārattār, oḷavi turicāmip piḷḷi, mā.irācamāṇikkaṉār, ira.paṉṉīrcelvam, mayili cīṉi.vēṅkaṭacāmi, mu.aruṇācalam, pulavar kuḻanti, naṭaṉa.kāci nātaṉ, pērā. muṉivar. kō, taṅkavēlu pōṉṟa perumakkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷaṉar. ivvaruntamiḻ perumakkaḷi naṉṟi uṇarvōṭu niṉivukūrkiṟōm.
talimuṟi talimuṟiyākac collappaṭṭum kēṭkappaṭṭum vanta ariya ceytikaḷit tam aṟiviṉ āḻattāl viruppu veṟuppu kaḷukku appāṟpaṭṭu naṭunili niṉṟu uṇmi varalāṟāka eḻuti yuḷḷa in nūlkaḷ āyvulakiṟkum, tamiḻ ulakiṟkum peritum payaṉ paṭat takka ariya varalāṟṟup peṭṭakamākum. paṇṭit tamiḻkkulam naṭantu vanta pātiyi aṟintu koḷḷavum, iṉi naṭakkavēṇṭiya pāti itu eṉa aṟutiyiṭṭukkoḷḷavum, taḷḷa vēṇṭiyavi ivi, koḷḷavēṇṭiyavi ivi eṉat tiṭṭa miṭṭut tam etirkāla vāḻvukku vaḷamāṉavaṟṟi ākkik koḷḷavum, tamiḻ iṉattit tāṅki niṟkum maṇṇiṉ perumiyi uṇaravum, tolinōkkuppārviyuṭaṉ ivvaralāṟṟu variciyit tamiḻar muṉ tantuḷḷōm. vāṅkip payaṉkoḷḷuṅkaḷ.

eṉum pāvēntar varikaḷi neñcil niṟuttuṅkaḷ.


moḻiyālum, iṉattālum aṭimippaṭṭa varalāṟu tamiḻariṉ varalāṟu. ivvaralāṟṟi mīṭṭeṭukka uḻitta perumakkaḷi vaṇaṅkuvōm. avvakiyil uḻittu nam kaṇmuṉṉē vāḻntu maṟinta peru makkaḷuḷ taliciṟantōr tanti periyārum, moḻi ñāyiṟu pāvāṇarum āvar. ipperumakkaḷ aṉivariyum naṉṟiyuṇarvuṭaṉ inta nērattil vaṇaṅkuvōm. avarkaḷ vaḻi payaṇam toṭaruvōm. tamiḻ iṉamē! eṉ tamiḻ iḷiyōrē! nam muṉṉōriṉ vāḻviyumtāḻviyum āḻaniṉiyuṅkaḷ. iṉattiṉ mīḷāttuyari mīṭṭeṭukka muṉiyuṅkaḷ.
- patippāḷar.

tāynila varalāṟu 
(mutal pākam)



(1) tamiḻakattiṉ iyaṟkiyamippu
iṟṟit tamiḻnāṭu intiyat tuṇikkaṇṭattiṉ teṉ kiḻakku mūliyil uḷḷatu. ñālap paṭattil itaṉip pārkkumpōtu teṟkē 8, 5° neṭuṅkōṭṭiliruntu vaṭakkē 13, 6° neṭuṅkōṭu variyilum, mēṟkē 76, 15° kuṟuṅkōṭṭiliruntu kiḻakkē 80, 20 kiḻakkuk kuṟuṅ kōṭu variyilum paraviyiruppatik kāṇalām.
tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku vaṭakkil āntiram, vaṭamēṟkil karunāṭakam, mēṟkil kēraḷam ākiya mānilaṅkaḷ nila ellikaḷākavum uḷḷaṉa. kiḻakkil vaṅkakkaṭalum, teṟkil intiyap peruṅkaṭalum nīrelli cuḷākavum amintu uḷḷaṉa. tamiḻakam nilattāl intiyat tuṇik kaṇṭattuṭaṉ iṇintu kāṇappaṭiṉum taṉ paṇpāṭṭilum moḻiyilum taṉittu niṉṟu taṉakkeṉa mutirntavoru paṇpaṭṭa varalāṟṟip peṟṟuḷḷatu. inta uṇmiyit tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ nilaviyal toṉmi uṇarttukiṟatu.
tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ ellikaḷ iṉṟu ivvāṟu kāṇappaṭiṉum paṇṭik kālattil itaṉ vaṭavelli tiruvēṅkaṭa maliyākum; teṟkelli kumarimuṉiyākum. itaṉi 'vaṭavēṅkaṭam teṉ kumariyā yiṭit tamiḻkūṟu nallulaku' eṉṉum kaḻaka(caṅkappāṭṭāl aṟiyalām. eṉavē, ivvellikaḷ kaḻaka(caṅkak kālattilirunta tamiḻakattiṉ ellikaḷ ākumeṉṟum, ataṟku muṟpaṭṭa kālaṅkaḷil vevvēṟu ellikaḷ iruntirukka vēṇṭu meṉṟum aṟikiṟōm. aḵtāvatu mutaṟ kaḻakam allatu talikkaḻakak kālattilum, iraṇṭām kaḻakam allatu iṭikkaḻakak kālattilum tamiḻakattiṉ teṟkelli ippōtuḷḷa kumarimuṉikkut teṟkē nilapparappāyiruntu, piṉṉar kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiya kumari vari iruntatu eṉpataṉit tolkāppiyam pōlum paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷiliruntu aṟikiṟōm. āṉāl, talikkaḻakak kālattil tamiḻnāṭṭu ellikaḷit teṟṟeṉa aṟivataṟkāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ namakku ituvari kiṭikkap peṟavilli. āṉāl, kaṭik kaḻakak kālattil mēṟkē arapikkaṭalum, kiḻakkē vaṅkakkaṭalum, teṟkē kumarimuṉiyum, vaṭakkē vēṅkaṭamaliyum ellikaḷāka iruntaṉa. veṉpatit teḷivāka aṟikiṟōm. aḵtāvatu, ippōtuḷḷa kēraḷam, āntiram, karunāṭakam eṉṉum nāṭukaḷiyum uḷḷaṭakkiya vāṟē kaḻakak kālattut tamiḻakam viḷaṅkiyatu. ak kālattiṟku muṉ piruntē tamiḻarkaḷ cintu, kaṅkic camaveḷip pakutikaḷilum paravi vāḻn tārkaḷ eṉṟu kaḻakakkāla ilakkiyaṅkaḷum, piṟavaralāṟṟuc cāṉṟu kaḷum kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. paṉimali(imayamali) vaṭavelliyāka iruntatu.
tamiḻnāṭṭiluḷḷa malikaḷum, āṟukaḷum mikavum paḻami yāṉavi yeṉṟu āyvāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. iṅkuḷḷa malikaḷ paṉimalikaḷ alla; payiriṭuvataṟkēṟṟa maṇvaḷamkoṇṭavi. eṉavē, tamiḻnāṭṭu malikaḷ palavaṟṟil ceḻippāṉa kāṭukaḷum, maram, ceṭi, koṭicuḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. paṇṭikkālat tamiḻakam nilattiṉ vaḷattiṟkēṟpa nāṟperum pirivukaḷākap pirikkap paṭṭiruntatu eṉpataṉi aṟivōm. avaṟṟuḷ kuṟiñcittiṇi nākarikam malikaḷil tāṉ tōṉṟi vaḷarntatu. 
mēṟkuttoṭarcci malikaḷ 
tamiḻakattiṉ mēṟkil mikap periyatoru cuvarpōl nīṇṭu kāṇappaṭum malittoṭarukku mēṟkut toṭarccimali' eṉpatu peyar. iḵtoru cuvar pōl toṭarcciyākak kāṇappaṭiṉum iṭi yiṭiyē pala kaṇavāykaḷip peṟṟuḷḷatu. kōvikku arukiluḷḷa pālikkāṭṭuk kaṇavāyum, kaṉṉiyākumari māvaṭṭatti luḷḷa āralvāymoḻik kaṇavāyum, nelli māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa ceṅ kōṭṭik kaṇavāyum mukāmi vāyntaviyākum. ivaṟṟit tavira kollūr, nīlampūr, pōṭināyakkaṉūr, accaṉ kōyil mutaliya kaṇavāykaḷum im malittoṭaril uḷḷaṉa. mēṟku malittoṭaril kutiri mali, kuṭakumali, āṉimali, potikimali mutaliya malikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. aḵtāvatu, immalittoṭarccikku āṅkāṅkē vaḻaṅkum peyarkaḷē ivi. im malittoṭarccimēlik kaṭaṟkari yōrattil paṭikkaṭṭu kaḷip pōl amintiruppatāl itaṉi āṅkilēyar 'mēlip paṭikaḷ' (Western ghats) eṉṟu aḻittaṉar. mēṟku malittoṭariluḷḷa āṉimalikaḷil kāṭukaḷ aṭarntu kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. āḻamāṉa paḷḷattākkukaḷ palavum, uyaramāṉa malimukaṭukaḷum uḷḷaṉa. ik kāṭukaḷil tēkku, mūṅkil, kaṭukkāy, vēṅki, nūkkamaram ākiyavi ceḻittu vaḷarntuḷḷaṉa. ikkāṭukaḷil vāḻum vēṅki, puli, ciṟutti, karaṭi, kāṭṭerumi, māṉ, kaṭampi, kāṭṭuppaṉṟi. muḷḷam paṉṟi mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷik kāṭṭilum yāṉikaḷē mika atikamākak kāṇappaṭuvatāl im malikaḷukku yāṉi malikaḷ' eṉṟu peyar - ēṟpaṭṭatu pōlum! mēṟku malittoṭarcciyil tēkkaṭi, kōṭi kāṉal, paḻanimali mutaliya kōṭi vāḻiṭaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. mēṟkut toṭarcci maliyum, kiḻakkut toṭarcci maliyum tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ mēṟkuppakutiyil kūṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivi kūṭum iṭattiṟkuk koṅku nāṭu' eṉṟu peyar. iṅkuḷḷa malikaḷi 'nīlamalikaḷ" eṉṟu aḻippar. iṅku utakamaṇṭalam, kūṉūr mutaliya kōṭi vāḻiṭaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. iṉṟiya tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ mēṟkelli nīlamalikaḷ eṉil atu poruttamākak kāṇappaṭum. nīlakiri maliyil mikavuyarnta toṭṭa peṭṭā ' eṉum malimukaṭu cikaram uḷḷatu. itaṉ uyaram 277 mīṭṭar ākum. nīlamalikkut teṟkē ēṟattāḻa 24 kilō mīṭṭar tolivuvari iṭiveḷiyuḷḷatu. itaṟkup 'pālik kāṭṭuk kaṇavāy' eṉpatu peyar.potuvāka mēṟkuttoṭarcci maliyiluḷḷa āṉimalikaḷil kāṇappaṭum 'āṉimuṭi ' eṉṉum mali mukaṭutāṉ teṉṉintiyāvilēyē mika uyarnta maliyucci cikaramyākum, itu toṭṭapeṭṭā cikarattiviṭa 22 mīṭṭar atikamāṉa uyaram uṭiyatu. 
āṉimalikkut teṟkē paḻanimalikaḷum avaṟṟiṉ kiḻakkup pakutiyil kōṭikkāṉal malikaḷum uḷḷaṉa. paḻanimali kaḷukkut teṟkil ēlamaliyum, acuttiya maliyum uḷḷaṉa. akattiya malikaḷil tāṉ āriyaṉ kāvukkaṇavāy, āralvāy moḻik kaṇavāy ākiyavi uḷḷaṉa. tamiḻ nāṭṭiliruntu kēraḷattiṟkuc celvataṟku āriyaṉkāvuk cukkavāy mūlam toṭar vaḻi (Railway5]p pāti amikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. āralvāymoḻik kaṇavāyiṉ vaḻiyākavum kēraḷattiṟkuc cellalām. mēṟkut toṭarcci malikaḷil vāḻum makkaḷ maraṅkaḷi veṭṭiyum, tēyili, ēlam, kamuku pākku mutaliyavaṟṟip payiriṭṭum piḻik kiṉṟaṉar. tolpaḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷāṉa kāṭar, paḷiyar, iruḷar tōtavar, mutuvar, puliyar mutaliyōrum im malit toṭarkaḷil vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. 
kiḻakkuttoṭarcci malikaḷ 
tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ mēlikkariyiluḷḷa nīlamaliyiliruntu tiruppati vari toṭarntu kāṇappaṭum malittoṭarik kiḻakkut toṭarcci mali eṉkiṟōm. ik kiḻakkuttoṭarcci malikaḷuk kiṭiyē mēṟkut toṭarcci malikaḷiviṭa atika iṭiveḷikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ uyaramum mikakkuṟivē. 300 mutal 1000 mīṭṭar vari uyaramuḷḷa malikaḷē ittoṭaril kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. im malittoṭaril cavvātumali, cērvarāyaṉmali, kallirāyaṉ mali, kollimali, paccimali, kañcamali mutaliya malikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. cērvarāyaṉmaliyiṉ ucciyiltāṉ 'ērkkāṭu' eṉum mali vāḻiṭam irukkiṟatu. mēṟkuttoṭarcci malikaḷiviṭak kiḻakkut toṭarcci malikaḷē paḻamiyāṉavi yeṉṟu maṇṇiyalār (Geologist) āyntu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl, kiḻakkiluḷḷa vaṅkakkaṭaliviṭamēṟki luḷḷa arapikkaṭalē paḻamiyāṉateṉṟu aṟiviyalār kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. 
ivviru malittoṭarkaḷum nīlamaliyil oṉṟukūṭit teṟkil īḻam variyil toṭarntu celkiṉṟaṉa. ataṟkappāl intumāk kaṭalilum im malittoṭar toṭarntiruntatu eṉpatik kaṭa vārāycciyāḷar (ceanographers) kaṇṭupiṭittuḷḷaṉar. ivvāṟu kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiya maliyiyē cilappatikāram kumarikkōṭu' eṉṟu kuṟippiṭukiṉṟatu pōlum! paṉiimayamali tōṉṟuvataṟku muṉ ik kumarimalitāṉ ulakiluḷḷa malikaḷukkellām mucuṭāka viḷaṅkiya teṉṟum, appōtu ataṉ pakkak kuṉṟukaḷ pōl mēṟku, kiḻakku malittoṭarccikaḷ viḷaṅkiṉaveṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. 'āṟṟup paṭukikaḷiltāṉ nākarikaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉ' veṉṟa kōṭpāṭṭiṟku māṟāka, malittoṭarkaḷil (kuṟiñci nilattil tamiḻar nākarikam tōṉṟi vaḷarntatu eṉum karuttu nilavu kiṉṟamikku mēṟkaṇṭa cāṉṟukaḷ tuṇipurikiṉṟaṉa. 
āṟukaḷ 
tamiḻnāṭṭu nilapparappup potuvākat teṉkiḻakku nōkkic caṟṟuc carivākavē uḷḷatu. eṉavētāṉ perumpālāṉa āṟukaḷ kīḻtticiyi nōkki ōṭukiṉṟaṉa. māperum āṟukaḷ mēṟkut toṭarcci maliyil tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. kiḻakkut toṭarcci mali kaḷilum āṟukaḷ tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ pala mēṟkuttoṭarcci maliyil tōṉṟum āṟukaḷuṭaṉum kalakkiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻnāṭṭil pāyum āṟukaḷip potuvākak 'kāṭṭāṟukaḷ' eṉṟē aḻippar. avi cintu, kaṅkiyāṟucuḷippōl vaṟṟāta pērāṟukaḷ alla, maḻik kālaṅkaḷil maṭṭum veḷḷam kari puraṇṭōṭum āṟu kaḷum, kōṭik kālaṅkaḷil vaṟaṇṭu kiṭakkum āṟukaḷum tamiḻakattil ērāḷamāyuḷḷaṉa. āṉāl, ilakkiyac cāṉṟukaḷiṉ paṭiyum, aṟiviyal āyvukaḷiṉpaṭiyum nōkkumpōtu tamiḻakatti lirunta pala āṟukaḷil eppōtum taṇṇīr ōṭikkoṇṭēyiruntatu eṉpati aṟikiṟōm. pālāṟu, teṉpeṇṇi, kāviri, veḷḷāṟu, teṉveḷḷāṟu. viyi, kuṇṭāṟu, vippāṟu, poruti tāmparaparaṇi, paḻiyāṟu mutaliyavi tamiḻnāṭṭil pāyntu vaṅkak kaṭalil kalakkum āṟukaḷākum. cantirakiriyāṟu, nēttirakiriyāṟu, corṇa kiriyāṟu, cītiyāṟu, poṉṉāṉiyāṟu, pēyp pūrāṟu, vāvāḷarppaṭṭiṉattāṟu, periyāṟu, pampiyāṟu mutaliya āṟukaḷ mēṟkuttoṭarcci maliyil tōṉṟi, arapikkaṭalil kalakkiṉṟaṉa. inta āṟukaḷiṉ karikaḷiltām paṇṭik kālattil periya nakaraṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. an nakaraṅkaḷ nākarikattoṭṭil kaḷākavum viḷaṅkiṉa. kaḻakak kālattu ilakkiyaṅkaḷiliruntu aṟiyappaṭum paḵṟuḷiyāṟu, kumariyāṟu mutaliyavi tamiḻakattiṉ teṉpākattik kaṭal koṇṭapōtu aḻintu pōyiṉa, 
pālāṟu 
inta āṟu karunāṭaka mānilattiluḷḷa nantiturkkam eṉum maliyiṉ arukil tōṉṟi, curunāṭaka mānilattik kaṭantu, āntira mānilattiluḷḷa cittūr māvaṭṭattiṉ vaḻiyākat tamiḻnāṭṭi aṭikiṟatu. tamiḻnāṭṭil vēlūr, tiruvaḷḷūr, māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil pāyntu caturaṅkappaṭṭiṉattukku arukil vaṅkakkaṭalil kalakkiṟatu. itaṉ nīḷam tōrāyamāka 890 kilō mīṭṭar ākum. ceyyāṟu, vēkavati yāṟu ākiyavi kāñcipurattukku arukiluḷḷa tirumuk kūṭal eṉumiṭat til pālāṟṟuṭaṉ kalakkiṉṟaṉa. oru kālattil vaṟṟāta nīrōṭṭattip peṟṟirunta pālāṟu iṉṟu kāṭṭāṟṟip pōl maḻikkālaṅkaḷil maṭṭum nīrippeṟum āṟākavuḷḷatu. 
teṉpeṇṇiyāṟu 
itu karunāṭaka mānilattiluḷḷa ceṉṉakēcava maliyil tōṉṟik karunāṭaka mānilattiṉ vaḻiyākap pāyntu, cēlam māvaṭ ṭattit tāṇṭi, vēlūr, tiruvaṇṇāmali, viḻuppuram, kaṭalūr māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil pāyntu, kaṭalūrukku arukil vaṅkakkaṭalil kalakkiṟatu. tiruvaṇṇāmalikku arukiluḷḷa cāttaṉūr eṉṉu miṭattil teṉ peṇṇiyāṟṟiṉ kuṟukkē cāttaṉār aṇi kaṭṭappeṟṟuḷḷatu.  inta āṟṟiṉ nīḷam tōrāyamāka 420 kilō mīṭṭar ākum. 
kāviriyāṟu 
varalāṟṟuc ciṟappumikka ik kāviriyāṟu teṉṉāṭṭuk kaṅki" eṉap pōṟṟappaṭuvatākum. itu karunāṭaka mānilattil kuṭaku nāṭṭil ciya maliyil tōṉṟukiṟatu. inta mali mēṟkut toṭarcci maliyiṉ oru pakutiyākum. karunāṭaka mānilattiṉ teṉpakutiyil pāyntu ataṉi vaḷamākkum kāviri aṅkuk kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa kaṇṇampāṭi eṉum aṇiyit tāṇṭi civa camuttiram aruvi yākak kāṭciyaḷittup piṉṉar, tamiḻnāṭṭit toṭukiṟatu. kōvi, cēlam ākiya māvaṭ ṭaṅkaḷukku elliyāka ōṭit takaṭūr (tarumapuri māvaṭṭattiyaṭintu, takaṭūrukku 48 kimī tolivil tōrā.! māka 22 mīṭṭar uyarattiliruntu ōr aruviyāka viḻukiṟatu. inta aruvikku 'ōkēṉakkal nīrvīḻcci" eṉpatu peyar. piṉṉar, kāviriyāṟu cēlam māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa mēṭṭūriyaṭiyumpōtu ataṉ kuṟukkē mēṭṭūr aṇi kaṭṭappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. ivvaṇik kaṭṭiliruntutāṉ tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku vēṇṭiya miṉvici uruvākkap paṭukiṟatu. cēlam māvaṭṭattiliruntu kāviri tirucci māvaṭṭatti yaṭiyumuṉ itaṉuṭaṉ pavāṉi, noyyal, tirumaṇimuttāṟu ākiya tuṇiyāṟukaḷ kalakkiṉṟaṉa. tirucci māvaṭ ṭattil pukuntavuṭaṉ āṉporuni (amarāvati) eṉum tuṇiyāṟu kalakkiṟatu. tiruccikku mēṟkē oṉpatāvatu kallil kāviriyāṟu iru kiḷikaḷākak pirikiṟatu. teṉ kiḻakkil kāviri eṉṟum, vaṭakiḻakkil koḷḷiṭam eṉṟum iḵtu aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu. ivviru kiḷikaḷum ciṟitu tolivu ōṭi mīṇṭum oṉṟu cērukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ iṭiyiṭṭa tīviltāṉ "tiruvaraṅkam (srīraṅkam eṉum peyarpeṟṟa māviyat viṇava talam uḷḷatu. itaṉiyaṭuttut tiruvāṉikkā' eṉum civat talamum uḷḷatu. tiruvaraṅkattippōlavē karunāṭaka mānilattil civa camuttiram aruvikku arukil amintuḷḷa taliyaraṅkamum, micūr nakarattiṟku 16 kal toliviluḷḷa tiruvaraṅkamum (srīraṅkam) māliyat talaṅkaḷ ākum. im mūṉṟiyum mūvaraṅkam eṉa aḻippar. 
koḷḷiṭattiṉ kuṟukkē kaṭṭappaṭṭa aṇikku mēlaṇi eṉṟu peyar. kāviriyiṉ kuṟukkē kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa aṇikkuk kallaṇi eṉṟu peyar. itaṉik karikāṟcōḻaṉ kaṭṭiṉāṉ eṉpatu varalāṟu. ik kallaṇiyit tāṇṭiyatum kāviri pala ciṟṟāṟu kaḷāyp pirikiṟatu. avaṟṟuḷ mukāmiyāṉavi veṇṇāṟum, atiliruntu piriyum kuṭamuruṭṭiyāṟu, veṭṭāṟu, aricilāṟu, vīracōḻaṉ āṟu, tirumalirācaṉ āṟu ākiyaviyum ākum, ivi yāvum tañci māvaṭṭattil pāykiṉṟaṉa. taraṅkampāṭi eṉum ūrukkarukil kāviri kaṭaluṭaṉ kalakkiṟatu. tamiḻnāṭṭil ōṭum āṟukaḷil kāviriyātu mikavum nīḷamāṉatu. itaṉ nīḷam tōrāyamāka 720 ki.mī. ākum. itu kaṭaluṭaṉ kalakkumiṭattil cōḻarkaḷiṉ paṇṭiya kōnakarāṉa pūmpukār amintiruntatu. iṉṟu akaḻvāyviṉ payaṉāka ippaṭṭiṉat tiṉ amippum, atu kaṭal vaḻiyāka urōmāpuri mutaliya mēli nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ koṇṭirunta toṭarpum viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pūmpukārē yaṉṟi vaḷamikka nakaraṅkaḷ palavum ivvāṟṟiṉ kariyil iruntaṉa eṉpati aṟikiṟōm. 
inta āṟu mēṟkumalittoṭariṉ pakutiyākiya ēlakkāy maliyiṉ kiḻakkup pakutiyil tōṉṟi maturi, tēṉi, civakaṅki, irāmaṉātapuram ākiya māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil pāyntu maṉṉār vaḷikuṭā vil kalakkiṟatu. ēlamaliyiṉ mēṟkiltaṭukiṉṟa pēriyāṟṟiṉ kuṟukkākap periyāṟu aṇi eṉum aṇiyik kaṭṭi nīrit tiruppic curuḷi yāṟṟil kalappatāl vikiyāṟu nīri mikutiyākap peṟṟu ōṭukiṟatu. vikiyāṟṟiṉ kuṟukkē vikiyaṇi eṉum aṇi kaṭṭappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. 


poruniyāṟu (tāmparaparaṇi) 
itu poruniyāṟu eṉṟum tāmparaparaṇiyāṟu eṉṟum aḻikkappaṭukiṟatu. itu mēṟku malittoṭarcciyiluḷḷa akattiya maliyil tōṉṟit tirunelvēli māvaṭṭattil pāyntu vaṅkak kaṭaliluḷḷa maṉṉār vaḷikuṭāvil kalakkiṟatu. vaṭamēṟkup paruvak kāṟṟiṉāl mēṟku malittoṭariṉ teṉkōṭiyil peyyum maḻinīr veḷḷam puraṇṭōṭit tāmparaparaṇiyāṟṟi vaṟṟāta āṟākac ceḻikkac ceykiṟatu. itaṉit teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ 'nīlāṟu' (NilE River) eṉpar. itaṉ nīḷam tōrāyamāka 120ki.mī. ākum. itil pāpanācam aruviyum uḷḷatu. maṇimuttāṟu, citrā āṟu eṉpaṉa itaṉ mutaṉmi yāṉa tuṇiyāṟukaḷākum. citrā vāṟṟāltāṉ kuṟṟālam aruvi ēṟpaṭukiṟatu. tāmparaparaṇiyāṟṟiṉ kuṟukkē kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa pala aṇikkaṭṭukaḷāl tirunelvēli māvaṭṭattiṉ perumpakuti nīrvaḷampeṟṟuḷḷatu. 
kōtiyāṟu, paḻiyāṟu 
kumari māvaṭṭattil pāykiṉṟa mukāmiyāṉa āṟukaḷ kōtiyāṟu, paḻiyāṟu eṉpaṉavākum, kōtiyāṟu, makēntirakiri maliyil tōṉṟi, viḷavaṅkōṭu, kalkuḷam ākiya vaṭṭaṅkaḷil pāyntu arapik kaṭalil kalakkiṟatu. paḻiyāṟu iraṇṭāvari maliyil tōṉṟit tōvāḷi, akattīcuvaram vaṭṭaṅkaḷil pāyntu maṇakkuṭikkāmal eṉumiṭattil kaṭalil kalakkiṟatu. paḻi yāṟṟāl nākarkōvil, cucīntiram, pūtappāṇṭi mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷ payaṉpeṟukiṉṟaṉa. paḻiyāṟṟip 'paṟaḷiyāṟu' eṉṟum aḻippar. paḻam paḵṟuḷiyāṟē paṟaḷiyāṟāki iṉṟu paḻiyāṟu eṉṟu āyiṟṟeṉpar cilar. 
camaveḷikaḷ 
tamiḻakattiṉ nilapparappu, kiḻakku nōkkic caṟṟut tāḻntiruppa tāl ataṉ kiḻakkup pakkattil paranta camaveḷi ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṉik 'karunāṭakac camaveḷi' eṉpar. itaṉ kaṭaṟkarip pakutiyic cōḻa maṇṭilakkari' eṉavaḻippar. ip periya karunāṭakac camaveḷiyi mūṉṟu pirivukaḷākap pirittu, kāviri pāyum camaveḷi: vikiyum, tāmparaparaṇiyum pāyum camaveḷi: vaṭakkēyuḷḷa tāḻ nilaṅkaḷ eṉavum aṟiyalām. potuvākak karunāṭakac camaveḷiyil āṅkāṅkē kiḻakku malit toṭaric cērnta ciṟumalikaḷum, kuṉṟukaḷum uḷḷaṉa, kōvi māvaṭṭam, takaṭūr(tarumapuri māvaṭṭam, cēlam māvaṭṭam, tirucci māvaṭṭam, tañci māvaṭṭam ākiya aintu māvaṭṭa nilap parappik kāviri pāyum camaveḷi eṉkiṟōm. kāviriyum, ataṉ tuṇiyāṟukaḷāṉa pavāṉi, noyyal, āṉ poruṉi amarāvati) ākiya āṟukaḷum ic camaveḷiyic ceḻikkac ceykiṉṟaṉa. nel, cōḷam, kampu, kēḻvaraku, parutti, nilakkaṭali, pukiyili mutaliyavi ic camaveḷiyiṉ viḷi poruḷkaḷākum, 
maturi, mukavi, nelli ākiya māvaṭṭaṅkaḷ vikiyum, tāmparaparaṇiyayum pāyum camaveḷip pakutikaḷākum. iyaṟkiyākavē ivi kuṟivāṉa maḻiyip peṟukiṉṟaṉa. inta āṟukaḷāltām ic camaveḷi ōraḷavu ceḻippuṟṟuk kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. nel, kēḻvaraku, parutti mutaliyavi ic camaveḷiyil payirākiṉṟaṉa. 
kāviri pāyum camaveḷikkum, kaṭaṟkarikkumiṭiyēyuḷḷa pakutiyiyē vaṭakkiluḷḷa tāḻnilaṅkaḷ' eṉkiṟōm. ip pakutiyil ceṉṉi, ceṅkaḻunīrppaṭṭu, teṉṉārkkāṭu, vaṭārkkāṭu ākiya nāṉku māvaṭṭaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ip pakutiyil āṇṭoṉṟukkuc carācaricemī. maḻi peykiṟatu. pālāṟu, teṉpeṇṇi , ceyyāṟu, āraṇiyāṟu mutaliya āṟukaḷ it tāḻnilap pakutiyic ceḻikkac ceykiṉṟaṉa. nel, kēḻvaraku, kampu, nilakkaṭali, karumpu mutaliyavi ip pakutiyil payirākiṉṟaṉa. 
taṭpaveppa nili 
tamiḻnāṭu 8.5 vaṭaneṭuṅkōṭṭiliruntu 13.3 vaṭaneṭuṅkōṭu vari paraviyuḷḷatiyaṟivōm. nila naṭukkōṭu tamiḻakattiṉ teṟkē celkiṟatu. nila naṭukkōṭṭiliruntu vaṭakkē 23.5 pākiyil cuṭacuvāri (kaṭakarēki celkiṟatu. atāvatu vaṭa intiyāviṟkum, teṉṉintiyā viṟkum caṟṟēṟakkuṟiya naṭuvil kaṭakavari celkiṟatu. nilanaṭukkōṭṭi viruntu kaṭa kavari vari veppa maṇṭalamāka irukkum eṉpati aṟivōm. eṉavē, tamiḻakam vaṭaveppa maṇṭalattil uḷḷatu. mārc 7 ām nāḷ mutal, cepṭempar 2 ām nāḷ variyil katiravaṉ nilanaṭukkōṭṭiṟku vaṭakkē iyaṅkuvatāl tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ mītu katiravaṉuṭiya katirkaḷ ceṅkuttāka viḻukiṉṟaṉa. kuṟippāka mē, cūṉ, cūli, ākacṭu mātaṅkaḷil katiravaṉuṭiya katirkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ mītu ceṅkuttāka viḻuvatāl veppam atika māka irukkiṟatu. kaṭalkaḷ veku arukil cūḻntiruppatāl kaṭaṟ karip pakutikkum uḷnāṭṭup pakutikkum veppa aḷavil vēṟupāṭu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ākavētāṉ cūṉ, cūli, ākacṭu ākiya kōṭi mātaṅkaḷil uḷnāṭṭup pakutikkum, kaṭaṟkarip pakutikkumuḷḷa veppattākkam vekuvāka vēṟupaṭṭuk kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. 
cepṭempar 2 mutal, mārc 21 vari katiravaṉ nila naṭukkōṭ ṭiṟkut teṟkē iyaṅkuvatāl navampar mutal pippiravari vari tamiḻnāṭ ṭil kuḷir atikamāka irukkiṟatu. akṭōpar, navampar, ticampar mātaṅkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭil maḻi peyyum kālamākak koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu. ik kālattil kaṭaṟkarip pakutikaḷiviṭa uḷnāṭṭil kuḷir atikamāka irukkum. tamiḻnāṭu teṉmēṟkup paruvakkāṟṟu, vaṭakiḻakkup paruvak kāṟṟukaḷāl maḻipeṟukiṟatu. 
maṇ vakikaḷ 
tamiḻnāṭṭil pala āṟukaḷ ōṭuvatāl vaṇṭal maṇ paṭinta nilapparappukaḷ palavum ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ittakiya vaṇṭalmaṇ paṭinta nilapparappiltāṉ nel, vāḻi, karumpu, veṟṟili mutaliya payirkaḷ ceḻittu vaḷarum. itaṟkuc ciṟanta eṭuttukkāṭṭu. tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ neṟkaḷañciyam eṉap pukaḻappaṭum tañci māvaṭṭam ākum. itu kāviriyiṉ kaḻimukattil amintuḷḷatu. neruppup pāṟikaḷāl uṇṭākum karuniṟamuḷḷa maṇṇukkuk karical maṇ eṉṟu peyar. ittakiya karicalmaṇ tamiḻnāṭṭil kōvi, maturi, mukaviirām nātapuram tirunelvēli ākiya māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil atikamākak kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟatu. im maṇ parutti viḷivataṟku ēṟṟatākum. paṭivup pāṟikaḷ noṟuṅkuvatāl uṇṭākum maṇṇukkuc caraḷimaṇ eṉṟu peyar. itil nīrma amilac cattu atikam irukkum. maliccarivukaḷil ittakiya maṇ atikamākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. caraḷi maṇṇuṭaṉ tāḻnilap pakutikaḷil kuṟumaṇṇum, kaḷimaṇṇum kalanta puṉceyt tavacaṅkaḷ payiriṭap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. ittakiya kalappumaṇ tamiḻnāṭṭil vēluār, tiruvaṇṇāmali, kaṭalūr, viḻuppuram tirucci, perampalūr, irāmanātapuram, virutunakar, cuṉṉiyākumari, ceṉṉiyiṉ vaṭakku, vaṭamēṟku āciya pakutikaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. taṉic caraḷimaṇ utaki pōṉṟa maliccarivukaḷil tēyili payiriṭuvataṟku ēṟṟatāyuḷḷatu. 
kaṉipporuḷkaḷ (Minerals) 
tamiḻakattil cēlam māvaṭṭattil kañcamali, kōtumali, tīrttamali, kollimali, paccimali, cuṇṇāmpukkaraṭu, cīrāp paḷḷi, cuñcalūr, perumāḷmali, cittērimali, irācippūr, āṟṟūr (āttūr) mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil irumpukkaṉi(tātu) kiṭikkiṟatu, vēlūr māvaṭṭattilum irumputtātuvi akaḻnteṭuppataṟkāṉa ārāycci naṭipeṟukiṟatu. irumpukku aṭuttapaṭiyāka alumiṉiyam ceyyap payaṉpaṭum "pākciṭṭu' eṉṉum tātupporuḷum cēlam māvaṭṭattil cērvarāyaṉ malippakutiyil kiṭikkiṟatu. kaḷi maṇṇuṭaṉ kalantu piñcuti cimeṇṭu ceyya utavum cuṇṇām pukkal cēlam māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa caṅkakiri turukkam, tirucceṅkōṭu mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum, matukkariyilum, tirucci māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa kallakkuṭi yilum, tirunelvēli māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa tāḻiyūṟṟu. kaḷakkāṭu, vaḷḷiyūr, irātāpuram, cāttāṉkuḷam ākiya iṭaṅkaḷilum cuṇṇām pukkal kiṭikkiṟatu. uyir vāḻa iṉṟiyamiyāta poruḷ kaḷuḷ oṉṟākak kāṇappaṭum kaṭal uppu tamiḻakattil ērāḷamākak kiṭikciṟatu. tamiḻakak kaṭalōrap pakutikaḷiluḷḷa ṉarkaḷiluppi uruvākkum periya uppaḷaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. 
nilit tiṇikaḷ (tāvaraṅkaḷ) 
māntaṉuṭiya muyaṟci illāmal tāmākavē vaḷarum maram, ceṭi, koṭi, pul, pūṇṭu ākiyavaṟṟi nilittiṇikaḷ (tāvaraṅkaḷ) eṉkiṟōm. potuvāka, atikamāka maḻi peyyumiṭaṅ kaḷil kāṭukaḷum, aḷavāka maḻi peyyumiṭaṅkaḷil pulveḷikaḷum, maḻi kuṟivāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil vaṟaṇṭa pakutikaḷil muṭputarkaḷum kāṇappaṭum. ittakiya mūvakiyāṉa pakutikaḷum tamiḻ nāṭṭiluḷḷaṉa. tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ teṉ mēṟkup pakutiyil 10 ce.mī.kku mēl maḻi peykiṟatu, ataṉōṭu ap pakutiyil veppamum atikamākavuḷḷatu. kuṟippākat tamiḻnāṭṭil teṉmēṟkup pakutiyiluḷḷa ēlakkāy malic carivukaḷil perumaḻi yum, atika veppamum uḷḷatāl toyvam(rubber), karuṅkāli, koṅku mutaliya maraṅkaḷ vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa. ivi eppoḻutum pacumiyākavē kāṇappaṭu vatāl ik kāṭukaḷip 'pacumimāṟāk kāṭukaḷ' eṉa aḻippar. aṭuttut tēkku, nākku, cantaṉam pūtaliya akaṉṟa ilikaḷi yuṭiya maraṅkaḷ vaḷarum kāṭukaḷukku 'akalilik kāṭukaḷ' eṉṟu peyar. ittakiya kāṭukaḷ nīlamali, paḻanimali, āṉimali, cērvarāyaṉ mali ākiya malippakutikaḷil atikam kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. nīlamali kaḷil uyaramāṉa pakutikaḷil 'yūkkāḷipṭaka', 'ciṉkōṉā' ākiya maraṅkaḷ vaḷarkkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ ilikaḷi nōkki, ivi vaḷarum nāṭukaḷi 'paciyilik kāṭukaḷ' eṉpar. ivaṟṟit tavira tamiḻnāṭu muḻuvatum maḻi kuṟivāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil kuṭṭiyāṉa maraṅkaḷum, muṭputarkaḷum kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. ik kāṭukaḷukkuk 'kuṟuṅkāṭukaḷ' eṉṟu peyar. potuvāka nilittiṇikaḷēyaṉṟi avaṟṟil vāḻntu varum vilaṅkiṉaṅ kaḷum, paṟavikaḷumkūṭat tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ celvaṅkaḷākak koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. 
uṇavup poruṭkaḷ 
tamiḻnāṭṭiluḷḷa makkaḷ yāvarum peruvāriyāka arici yuṇaviyē atikamāka uṭkoḷkiṉṟaṉar. nel, tamiḻnāṭṭiluḷḷa āṟṟuppaṭukikaḷilum, camaveḷip pakutikaḷilum payiriṭappaṭukiṟatu: nīrppācaṉa ēntu illāta viṭaṅkaḷilum kiṇaṟṟuppācaṉam vaḻiyākap payiriṭappaṭukiṟatu. potuvākat tamiḻnāṭu muḻuvatilum nel payiriṭap paṭukiṟatu. ariciyuṇaviṟku aṭuttapaṭiyākat tamiḻaka makkaḷ uṭ koḷvatu kēḻvaraku, kampu, cōḷam, varaku, tiṉi, cāmi mutaliya tavaca (tāṉiya vakikaḷākum. ivaṟṟuḷḷum kēḻvaraku, kampu, cōḷam ākiyaviyē atikamāka uṭkoḷḷap peṟum tavacaṅkaḷākum. ivi tirunelvēli, mukavi, kōvi ākiya māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil atikamākap payiriṭappeṟukiṉṟaṉa. kōtumi iṟakkumati ceyyappaṭukiṟatu. nīlamali māvaṭṭattil mikacciṟiya aḷavil itu payiriṭappeṟukiṟatu. ivaṟṟit tavira tamiḻaka makkaḷ payaṟu vakikaḷiyum, mīṉ, iṟicci ākiyavaṟṟiyum uṇpar. karumpu, parutti, pukiyili, eṇṇey vittukaḷ mutaliyaṉavum tamiḻakattil payirākiṉṟaṉa. malic carivukaḷil payirākum kuḷampi (Coffee), tēyili (Tea), naṟumaṇapporuḷkaḷ ākiyaviyum mēṟkūṟiya curumpu, parutti, pukiyili, eṇṇey vittukaḷ ākiyaviyum pākka (vaṇikap payirkaḷākak koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku vēṇṭiya parutti tamiḻakattilēyē viḷivatōṭu piṟaviṭaṅkaḷukkum ēṟṟumati ceyyum aḷaviṟkup parutti āṭikaḷiṉ uruvākkam (uṟpatti kāṇap paṭukiṟatu. 
pācaṉa ēntukaḷ 
tamiḻnāṭu, payirttoḻili uyirnāṭiyākap peṟṟuḷḷa nāṭākum. āṉāl, vaṟṟātōṭum āṟukaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭil atikam illi. eṉavē, avvappōtu peyyum maḻiyāl veḷḷapperuk keṭuttu ōṭivarum āṟukaḷukkuk kuṟukkē aṇikaḷik kaṭṭiyum, iyaṟkiyāka ēri, kuḷaṅkaḷil nīrit tēkkiyum pācaṉa ēntukaḷic camaṉ ceytu payirttoḻil ceykiṉṟaṉar. ittakiya ēntukaḷum illātaviṭaṅkaḷil kiṇaṟukaḷi veṭṭip pācaṉa ēntukaḷip perukkiyuḷḷaṉar. ākavē tamiḻakattil āṟṟuppācaṉam. ērippācaṉam, kiṇaṟṟuppācaṉam eṉa mūvakip pācaṉaṅkaḷ naṭimuṟiyil uḷḷaṉa. āṟṟu nīrik kālvāykaḷiṉ mūlam vayalkaḷukkup pāyccuvatuṇṭu. potuvākat tamiḻnāṭṭiluḷḷa kālvāykaḷil veḷḷak kālattil maṭṭum nīrip peṟum kālvāykaḷum, āṟu kaḷiṉ mūlam nīrip peṟṟu, vaṟṟātōṭum kālvāykaḷum, nīrt tēkkaṅkaḷiliruntu nīrpeṟṟu ōṭum nīrttēkkak kālvāykaḷumākiya mūvakik kālvāykaḷ uḷḷaṉa. 
tamiḻakattil mēṭṭūrk kālvāyt tiṭṭam, maṇimuttāṟṟut tiṭṭam, āraṇiyāṟṟut tiṭṭam, āṉporuni amarāvati aṇit tiṭṭam, viki aṇittiṭṭam, cāttaṉūr aṇittiṭṭam, kiruṭṭiṇakiri aṇittiṭṭam, putiya kaṭṭaḷik kālvāy aṇit tiṭṭam, putiya puḷḷampāṭikkālvāy aṇittiṭṭam, vīṭūr aṇit tiṭṭam, parampikkuḷat tiṭṭam mutaliya āṟṟup pācaṉa nīrttēkkat tiṭṭaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa, tamiḻakattil ciṟiyavippum, piṭāriyavippumākac cumār ēṟattāḻa 300 ērikaḷum kuḷaṅkaḷum uḷḷaṉa. kāñcipuram, tiruvaḷḷūr, viḻuppuram, tiruvaṇṇāmali, vēlūr ākiya māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil ērikaḷ atikam uḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷḷum kāñcipuram māvaṭṭattiltāṉ mika atikamāṉa ērikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. cemparampākkam ēri, puḻal ēri, maturāntakam ēri, ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu ēri ākiyavi im māvaṭṭattil kuṟippiṭattakka mikapperiya ērikaḷākum. vēlūr māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa kāvērippākkam ēriyum, viḻuppuram māvaṭṭatti luḷḷa vīrāṇam ēriyum mikapperiya ērikaḷākum, pallavarkaḷum, cōḻarkaḷum peyarpeṟṟa pala taṭākaṅ caḷi (ērikaḷ veṭṭi makkaḷiṉ nalvāḻviṟkup pāṭupaṭṭaṉar eṉpati varalāṟṟuc cāṉṟukaḷ pulappaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. 
āṟṟup pācaṉamumērippācaṉamum illātaviṭaṅkaḷil kiṇaṟu kaḷi veṭṭip pācaṉa ēntukaḷic ceytuḷḷaṉar. iṉṟu kuḻāyk kiṇaṟukaḷi amikkum putiya muyaṟci āṅkāṅkē mēṟkoḷḷappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. 
piṟa toḻilkaḷ 
 vēḷāṇmiyōṭu toṭarpuṭiya toḻilkaḷit tavirtta maṟṟa vaṟṟip piṟa toḻilkaḷ' eṉak kuṟippiṭukiṟōm. avaṟṟik kuṟippāka ālit toḻilkaḷ, kuṭicit toḻilkaḷ eṉa iruvakikaḷākap pirittuk kūṟalām. tamiḻakattil kōvi māṭṭattil peyarpeṟṟa paruttiyālikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. eṉavētāṉ im māvaṭṭattit teṉṉintiyāviṉ māṉcecuṭar' (MoncEster) eṉṟu aḻikkiṉṟaṉar. kōyamputtūr māvaṭṭam, paruttiyālikaḷil melliya nūli nūṟpataṟku ēṟṟavāṟu īrappatamuḷḷa kāṟṟip peṟukiṟatu. parutti atikamāka viḷikiṟatu. miṉṉāṟṟal kiṭikkiṟatu. periya toḻiṟ cālikaḷi amikkac celvarkaḷ uḷḷaṉar. kōvi māvaṭṭam tavira maturi tarattukkuṭi, pāpanācam, kōvilpaṭṭi, nācarēttu, nāṅkunēri, āralvāy moḻi, nākar kōyil, ceṉṉi , cēlam mutaliyaviṭaṅ kaḷilum parutti yālikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. 
tamiḻakattil paruttiyālittoḻilukku aṭuttapaṭiyāka irumpu eḵku ālittoḻil ciṟappāka naṭipeṟukiṟatu. irumpu mūlakkaṉi, nilakkari, māṅkaṉīcu, cuṇṇāmpukkal ākiya mūlapporuḷkaḷ irumpu eḵku toḻilukkut tēvippaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cēlam māvaṭṭattil irumpu mūlakkaṉiyum, cuṇṇāmpukkallum kiṭik kiṉṟaṉa. neyvēliyil nilakkari kiṭikkiṟatu. itaṉāl cēlam māvaṭṭattil irumpu eḵku ālikaḷi ēṟpaṭuttiyuḷḷaṉar, tamiḻ nāṭṭil karumpu atikamāka viḷivatāl viḻuppuram māvaṭṭatti luḷḷa nellikkuppam, maturi māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa pāṇṭiyarācapuram, tañci māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa tirumaṇṭalkuṭi, kuruṅkuḷam, tukili, karūr māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa pukaḻur, tirucci, kāṭṭūr, pēṭṭivāyttali, kōvi māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa āppakkūṭal, cēlam māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa mōkaṉūr, takaṭūr māvaṭṭatti luḷḷa pālakkōṭu, vēlūr māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa āmpūr, kāñcipuram māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa maturāntakam ākiyaviṭaṅkaḷil carkkariyālikaḷ kaḷḷaṉa. 
tamiḻakattil cuṇṇāmpukkal, kaḷimaṇ, 'jipcam' ākiya mūlap poruḷkaḷik koṇṭu matukkari, kallakkuṭi, ālaṅkuḷam, tulukkap paṭṭi, caṅkakiriturukkam, tāḻiyūṟṟu, karuvūr, ariyaluār ākiyaviṭaṅ kaḷil piñcuti cimeṇṭu uruvākkam ceyyum ālikaḷ ēṟpaṭuttap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. 
vēḷāṇmit toḻilukku uṟutuṇiyāṉatu urattoḻil ākum, vētiyiyal iracāyaṉa urat toḻiṟcālikaḷ tamiḻakattil neyvēli, kaṭalūr, aṟṟūr. tūttukkuṭi irāṇippēṭṭieṇṇūr ākiyaviṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷaṉa. 
mēṟkaṇṭa ālit toḻilkaḷit tavira tamiḻnāṭṭiluḷḷa perumpālāṉa makkaḷukku ūṉṟukōlāyiruppatu cittaṟi necavut toḻilum, piṟa poruḷkaḷ ceyyum kuṭicit toḻilkaḷum ākum. parutti, paṭṭu, tāvari(kampaḷi necavut toḻilkaḷi iṉṟum tamiḻakat til kuṭicit toḻilkaḷāka mēṟkoṇṭuḷḷa nakaraṅkaḷum ūrkaḷum atikamāka uḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟit tavira marap poruḷkaḷ, pirampup poruḷkaḷ, ñekiḻip(Plastic) poruḷkaḷ, tōl pataṉiṭutal, tōṟ poruḷ ceytal, iliccuruṭṭut(Berdi) toḻil, ceṅkal, ōṭu ceytal, kōrippāy piṉṉutal, maṇmāḻi ēṉaṅkaḷ (pāttiraṅkaḷ! ceytal, pommikaḷ ceytal mutaliya kuṭicit toḻilkaḷ peruvāri yāka naṭipeṟukiṉṟaṉa. kinūṟ katar) kit toḻil niliyaṅkaḷum, tamiḻnāṭu ciṟutoḻil niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷum ittakiya kuṭicittoḻil kaḷukku uyirūṭṭip pēṇi vaḷark kiṉṟaṉa. 
vāṇika vaḷarcci 
paṇṭiya tamiḻakattiṉ vāṇika vaḷarcciyi ārāyntuḷḷa aṟiñarkaḷ tamiḻakam naṇṇilak kaṭal(MediterEnean Sea) vaḻiyāka mēli nāṭukaḷuṭaṉum, intumāvāri(Indian GCEMan)yiṉ vaḻiyākat tolik kiḻakku nāṭukaḷuṭaṉum vāṇikattoṭarpu koṇṭiruntatu eṉkiṉṟaṉar. iṉṟu namatu akaḻvārāycciyil kiṭittuḷḷa poruḷkaḷum, ilakkiya, varalāṟṟuc cāṉṟukaḷum ik kūṟṟi meyppikkiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟiya tamiḻakamum ittakiyak kaṭal vāṇikattilum, nila vāṇikatti lum ciṟantu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. tamiḻaka aracu vāṅkiyuḷḷa periyār', 'aṇṇā ' eṉṉum peyar pūṇṭa iraṇṭu nāvāykaḷ pūmpukār nāvāy vāriyam' eṉṉum pēramippiṉ kīḻ veḷḷōṭṭam ceṉṟu paḻan tamiḻar pukaḻi mīṇṭum ulakeṅkum parappukiṉṟaṉa. 
tamiḻakattiliruntu tōl, veṅkāyam, paruttit tuṇivakikaḷ mutaliyavi peruvāriyāka veḷināṭukaḷukku ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. iyantiraṅkaḷ, maruntuvakikaḷ, cila mūlapporuḷkaḷ, iyaṅkikaḷ mōṭṭarkaḷ, tāḷ(paper) mutalāṉa poruḷkaḷit tamiḻakam ayal nāṭukaḷiliruntu iṟakkumati ceykiṟatu. uḷnāṭṭu vāṇikattilum tamiḻakam ciṟanta iṭattip peṟṟuḷḷatu. tamiḻnāṭṭi liruntu arici, nilakkaṭali, parutti, tīppeṭṭi, mattāppu,ēlam mutaliya poruḷkaḷ intiyāviluḷḷa piṟa mānilaṅkaḷukku ēṟṟumati ceyyap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. carkkari, kōtumi, payaṟu vakikaḷ, maram, caṇal, kampaḷi, tāḷ mutaliya poruḷkaḷ piṟa mānilaṅkaḷiliruntu tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟkut taruvikkappeṟukiṉṟaṉa. 
vāṇika vaḷarccikku uyirnāṭiyākaviruppatu cālip pōkku varattu ākum. tēciyac cālikaḷ, mānilac cālikaḷ, māvaṭṭac cālikaḷ, ūrc cālikaḷ ākiya nālvakic cālikaḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭil uḷnāṭṭup pōkkuvarattukku utaviyāka vāṇika vaḷarccikkup payaṉpāṭu ! kiṉṟaṉa, tēciyac cālikaḷ perumpālum talinakaraṅkaḷiyum, tuṟimukaṅkaḷiyum, mānilac cālikaḷiyum iṇikkiṉṟaṉa. it tēciyac cālikaḷi intiya aracum, piṟa cālikaḷit tamiḻaka aracum  kaṇkāṇikkiṉṟaṉa. ceṉṉiyi liruntu kolkattāvukkuc cellum neṭuñcāliyum, ceṉṉiyi liruntu mumpikkuc cellum neṭuñ cāliyum tamiḻakattiliruntu cellum mukāmiyāṉa neṭuñcālik ḷākum. ivaṟṟit tavira tamiḻakam muḻuvatilumuḷḷa ciṟṟurarkaḷiyum pērūrkaḷiyum iṇikkum cālip pōkkuvarattukaḷiyum, miṉ iṇippu ēntukaḷiyum tamiḻaka aracu mēṟkoṇṭu varukiṟatu. ippāti kaḷit tavira tamiḻakattiluḷḷa mukāmi nakaraṅkaḷiyum, tuṟi mukaṅkaḷiyum iṇikkum iruppuppātikaḷum uḷḷaṉa. tirucci, maturi, kōyamputtūr ākiyaviṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa ciṟiyavāṉūrarti niliyaṅ kaḷum, ceṉṉiyiluḷḷa periya vāṉūrti niliyamum, vāṉvaḻip pōkkuvarattukkup peritum payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itip pōlavē tamiḻnāṭṭil nīrvaḻi, uḷnāṭṭu, ayalnāṭṭup pōkku varattukkup payaṉpaṭukiṉṟatu. tamiḻnāṭṭiluḷḷa āṟukaḷ etuvum uḷnāṭṭu nīrvaḻiyākap payaṉpaṭavilli. āṉāl, nākappaṭṭiṉam, maṟikkāṭu vētāraṇyam kālvāyum, āntira nāṭṭil kiruṭṭiṇi māvaṭṭat tiluḷḷa pettakāñcam eṉṉumiṭattiliruntu, tamiḻ nāṭṭil kaṭalūr māvaṭṭattil marakkāṇam variyil cellum pakkiṅkām kālvāyum orukālattil uḷnāṭṭu nīrvaḻikaḷāyp payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. tamiḻnāṭṭi luḷḷa tuṟimukaṅkaḷil mikavum periyatum, mukāmiyāṉatum ceṉṉit tuṟimukam ākum. tamiḻ nāṭṭuk kaṭaṟkarikaḷ iyaṟkit tuṟimukaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭuvataṟku ēṟṟaviyāyilli. eṉavētāṉ atikamāṉa tuṟimukaṅkaḷ amiyavilli. āyiṉum tūttukkuṭit tuṟimukam ceyaṟkit tuṟimukamāṉa ceṉṉit tuṟimukatti yaṭuttup peritum payaṉpaṭum vakiyil tiruttiyamikkappaṭṭuc ceyalpaṭṭu varukiṟatu. 
makkaḷ toki 
2001 ām āṇṭu makkaḷtokik kaṇakkiṉpaṭi tamiḻnāṭṭil 8,31I3839 pēr vāḻvatāka aṟikiṟōm. ivarkaḷil perumpāṉmi yōr tamiḻar. ciṟupāṉmiyiṉarāṉa teluṅkar, cuṉṉaṭar, mali yāḷiyar ākiyōrum, urutu, āṅkilam ākiya moḻikaḷ pēcuvōrum uḷḷaṉar. intukkaḷ ēṟattāḻa 80 viḻukkāṭu uḷḷaṉar. kiṟittavar ēṟattāḻa 5 viḻukkāṭum, iculāmiyar ēṟattāḻa 5 viḻukkāṭum uḷḷaṉar. ivar kaḷit tavirac camaṇarum, cīkkiyarum, piṟarum uḷḷaṉar. curuṅkak kūṟiṉ moḻivaḻiyākap pārkkumpōtu tamiḻarkaḷēṟattāḻa 84.5 viḻukkāṭum, teluṅkarkaḷ 8.75 viḻukkāṭum, kaṉṉaṭar 25 viḻukkāṭum, urutu pēcuvōr 2 viḻukkāṭum, maliyāḷiyar 1, 4 viḻukkāṭum uḷḷaṉar. tamiḻakattiluḷḷa mānilat talinakarattilum, māvaṭṭat talinakaraṅkaḷi lum, puṉitat talaṅkaḷuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷilum, toṭarvaḻic cantippukaḷilum makkaḷ nerukkam atikamākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. carācariyāka oru caturak kilōmīṭṭarukku 317 pēr vāḻvatākak kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ mottap parappu 1971 ām āṇṭiṉ kaṇakkuppaṭi 13089 caturak kilōmīṭṭarākum. 
tamiḻaka nilapparappiṉ toṉmi 
* poyyakava nāḷum pukaḻviḷittal eṉviyayappām 
    	  viyakam pōrtta vayaṅkoḷinīr - cēykalak 
         kaṟṟōṉṟi maṇṭōṉṟāk kālattē vāḷoṭu 
         muṟṟōṉṟi mūtta kuṭi "  		  puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāli 
ulakattil kallum maṇṇum tōṉṟuvataṟku muṉpākavē tamiḻaka nilapparapput tōṉṟiyatu eṉṟum, avvāṟu tōṉṟiya tiruntum iṉṟu vari annilapparappu taṉatu kaṉṉittaṉmiyōṭu viḷaṅkukiṟatu eṉṟum kūṟum moḻi pukaḻ moḻiyaṉṟu eṉpati iṉṟiya ārāycciyāḷarkaḷ meyppittuḷḷaṉar. aḵtāvatu, ulakam tōṉṟiyapōtu ulakil iṉṟu nām kāṇumpaṭiyuḷḷa nilappirivu kaḷum nāṭukaḷum illi. pala tāḻikaḷiṉ māṟṟaṅkaḷāl avvap pōtu nilapparappukaḷ māṟiyum, maṟintum, eḻuntum, kuṟintum, kūṭiyum vantaṉa. attakiya vaḻikaḷi : lum tamiḻakam taṉ nilap parappiṉ teṟkup pakutiyik kaṭaluḷ iḻantu, tāṉmaṭṭum ettakiya māṟutalukkum āṭpaṭāmal taṉittu niṉṟatu! eṭuttukkāṭṭāka iṅkilāntu, ayarlāntu, ḵpirāṉcu ākiya nāṭukaḷ oru kālattil oruṅkiṇintiruntaṉa eṉṟum, itip pōlavē alāskā, cipīriyā, cappāṉ ākiyaviyum, malināṭu malēciyā), cumaturi cumatrā), cālittīvu(Java), poruniyōpōrṉiyō) ākiyaviyum ācuttirēliyā, niyūkiṉi, tācu mēṉiyā ākiyaviyum, intiyā, ilaṅki ākiyaviyum oṉṟiṇintiruntaṉa eṉṟum ārāycci yāḷarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar, ivvamippiltāṉ teṟkē kumarik kaṇṭam eṉṟa nilapparappu oṉṟu iruntateṉṟum, atuvē piṟaku kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiviṭṭatu eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. 
ivvāṟu ulaka mēṟparappil ēṟpaṭṭa palvēṟu māṟutal kaḷukkup piṉṉum tamiḻakattiṉ nilapparappu maṭṭum ettakiya māṟutal kaḷukkum āṭpaṭāmaliruppati ataṉuṭiya maṇ, pāṟi, uyiriṉaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi ārāyntu ataṉ toṉmiyi aṟiñarkaḷ meyppit tiruppatik kaṇṭu viyappaṭikiṟōm. aḵtāvatu nām vāḻum inta ulakam iṉṟikku 500 kōṭiyāṇṭu kaḷukkumuṉ ñāyiṟṟiṉiṭamiruntu pirintu vantateṉṟum, mutalil taṇaṟ piḻampāka irunta ivvulakattiṉ mēṟparappil taṇaṟ pāṟi kaḷē tōṉṟiṉa veṉṟum, piṉṉar aṭiyil liruntu taṇaṟkuḻampu mēṟparappukkuk koṇṭu varappaṭṭu āṟiyapiṉ paṭivuppāṟikaḷāka māṟiṉaveṉṟum kūṟuvar. iviyē piṉṉar iyipiyal māṟṟattiṉpōtu tōṉṟiya iṭiviṭāta maḻiyāl ūṟiya nīrppakutikaṭalkaḷ āṉapōtu malikaḷākavum, kuṉṟukaḷākavum māṟiṉa. 
im malikaḷum, kaṭalkaḷum ēṟattāḻa 450 kōṭiyāṇṭu kaḷukku muṉ tōṉṟiṉa. piṉṉar kaṭal nīr poṅki mēṭṭup pakutiyik koḷvatum vaṟṟuvatumāka iruntatu. ittakiya kaṭal kōḷi uṇaḻikkālam' eṉpar. ik kaṭalkōḷkaḷ, pēraḷavil tākkiya kālattip pērūḻi" eṉpar. ittakiya ūḻi", pērūḻi māṟṟaṅkaḷ kālantōṟum nikaḻntu koṇ ṭēyiruntaṉa. vāyppāṉa cūḻnilikaḷ ēṟpaṭṭapōtu iṉṟikku ēṟattāḻa 8 kōṭiyāṇṭu kaḷukku muṉ ulakil uyiriṉaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. ēṟattāḻa4 kōṭi yāṇṭukaḷukku muṉtāṉ māntaṉ, kuraṅku ākiyavaṟṟiṉ mūtāti kaḷāṉa 'kippaṉ' eṉṉum talimikaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. ittakiya oḻi kaḷum, pērūḻikaḷum nikaḻnta kālaṅkaḷilum tamiḻakam muḻuvatu mācuk kaṭalkōḷkaḷāl aḻiyāmal taṉittu niṉṟatu. itaṉāl ēṟattāḻa 300 kōṭi mutal 450 kōṭi variyilāṉa āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉirunta uyiriṉamaṟṟa pērūḻik kālattil ārkkēyaṉ tōṉṟiya tamiḻakap pāṟikaḷ iṉṟum aḻintuviṭāmal iruk kiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟu cēlam māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa kañcamalip pāṟi ip pērūḻik kālattil tōṉṟiyatākum. ceṉṉikkarukiluḷḷa pallāvaram pakutiyil kiṭit tuḷḷa pāṟip pakutiyoṉṟiṉi āynta tiru, āvaṇṭu(Holland) eṉpār ulakil vēṟu eṅkum kiṭikkāta mikat toṉmivāynta pāṟiyeṉṟu itaṉik kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. irumpum, macaṉīciyak caṉipporuḷkaḷum kalanta ippāṟi vakiyi cārṉaṉakaṭṭup pāṟi (Charmickitc) eṉpar. māmallapurattuk kuṭivarik kōyilkaḷum it toṉmi vāynta 'cārṉaṉakaṭṭuppāṟiyil kuṭintaviyē ākum. tamiḻakattil nīlamali, āṉimali, paḻanimali mutaliyaṉavum ittakiya pāṟikaḷāl āṉaviyē. itaṉāltāṉ uyiriṉaṅkaḷ tōṉṟu vataṟku muṉṉiya kālantoṭṭē tamiḻakam pāṟiyākaviruntu piṉ nilappakuti yāki mutal uyiriṉam taṉṉakattē tōṉṟuvataṟkut takutiyuṭiyatā yiruntatu eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. 
mēṟkūṟiya uṇmikkuc cāṉṟāka vēṟu pala eṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷiyum kūṟalām. tamiḻnālkaḷilum, vaṭamoḻi ilakkiyaṅ kaḷilum kaṭal kōḷkaḷ nikaḻntatu paṟṟiya cāṉṟukaḷ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. 
itaṉāl tamiḻakattōṭu nilattāl toṭarpu koṇṭirunta ataṉ nīṇṭateṉpakuti kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiyati aṟikiṟōm, moḻi nālāriṉ muṭivuppaṭiyum teṉṉakattiliruntu iṅkilāntu nāṭu vari oruvaki moḻik kōrviyiruntatu eṉṟum, teṉṉakattil tōṉṟiya mutal māntaṉ teṉṉakam kaṭal koṇṭatāl āciyā, airōppā mutaliya nāṭukaḷukkuc ceṉṟu piṉṉar kipar, pōlaṉ kaṇavāykaḷ vaḻiyākat. teṉṉakam pōntāṉ eṉṟum nampap paṭukiṟatu. itaṉāl iṅkirunta tolpaḻantamiḻarētiraviṭarē! piṟa nāṭṭu nākarikattiṟku mūlakkūṟāy viḷaṅkiṉar eṉpatum, avarkaḷ tōṉṟiya kālam mutal iṉṟuvari avarkaḷiṉ paḻanteṉṉāṭu aḻintupaṭiṉum aḻiyāmal eñci niṟkum tamiḻakattil avarkaḷiṉ nākarikam nilittirukkiṟatu eṉṟum ārāycci yāḷar kūṟuvar. ivarkaḷiṉ kūṟṟi valuppaṭuttuvatippōl vāṉanūl, nilanūral, maṇṇiyal nūl, āvimaṇṭala nūl ākiya aṟiviyal nūlkaḷiṉ muṭivukaḷum terivikkiṉṟaṉa. ivi aṉittumē ulakil mikap paḻiya pakutiyum, nākarikat toṭakkam ēṟpaṭṭa iṭamum, teṉṉakam allatu ataṟkum teṟkilirunta oru nilappakutiyē eṉkiṉṟaṉa. ap pakutiyit tāṉ 'ilemūriyāk kaṇṭam' eṉpar. ituvē, 'kumarikkaṇṭam" eṉṟap paḻantamiḻ nāṭāka, paḻampāṇṭi nāṭāka iruntatu eṉṟum, kaṭal kōḷāl iḵtu aḻintu paṭṭapiṉ eñci niṟpatē iṉṟiyat tamiḻnāṭu eṉṟum kūṟuvar. 
māntaviṉam iṟṟikku ēṟattāḻa 4 kōṭiyāṇṭukaḷukku muṉ tōṉṟiyatu eṉṟōm. appoḻutu vaṭintiyap pakuti kaṭalāka iruntatu eṉṟum, piṉṉartāṉ paṉiimayamali tōṉṟiyateṉṟum, ataṟkup piṉṉarē kaṅki, yamuṉi mutaliya āṟukaḷ tōṉṟiṉa veṉṟum ārāycciyāḷarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl, tamiḻakatti luḷḷa malikaḷum, āṟukaḷum, ulakiluḷḷa piṟa āṟukaḷum, malikaḷum tōṉṟuvataṟku muṉpākavē tōṉṟiyaviyākum. ākavē kaṅkikkup piṉ kāviriuṇṭāṉatu eṉṟu kūṟuvatu aṟiviyal muṟippaṭi porutta maṟṟatākum. imayamaliyil paṉippaṭalaṅkaḷil kaṭalil vāḻnta uyiriṉaṅ kaḷiṉ eccaṅkaḷum, piṟavum kāṇappaṭuvatiliruntu itu muṉpu kaṭalāka iruntatu eṉpataṉit teḷivāka aṟikiṟōm. vaṭa intiyāvilum, imaya malip pakutiyilumuḷḷa pāṟi, maṇ ākiyavi tamiḻakattiluḷḷa pāṟi, maṇ ākiyavaṟṟik kāṭṭilum akaviyil vayatil) mikak kuṟintaviyāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. imayamalip pakutikaḷil vaḷarum ceṭi koṭikaḷum, tamiḻakattiluḷḷa ceṭi koṭi vakikaḷum muṟiyē aṇmikkum toṉmikkum eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷāy uḷḷaṉa. 
- māntaviyal ārāycci muṭiviṉpaṭi tamiḻakattil nelli māvaṭṭam āticcanallūr mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa paḻamporuḷkaḷ ulakiṉ maṟṟa pakutikaḷiliruntu eṭukkappaṭṭa paḻam poruḷkaḷiviṭap paḻami vāyntaviyeṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ākavētāṉ tamiḻakamē perumpālum māntaviṉam tōṉṟiya mutaliṭa māka irukkalāmeṉa nampappaṭukiṟatu. itaṟku nām mēlē kūṟiya cāṉṟukaḷēyaṉṟi itaṉ iyaṟkic cūḻalittakka kaḷamākak koṇṭu aṟiyalām. tamiḻakam nila naṭukkōṭṭiṟku veku arukil iruppatālum, ulakam tōṉṟiya kālam mutal iṉṟu vari (taṉ nilapparappil kuṟintum nilap peyarcciyaṭiyāmal nilittu niṟpatālum, paṉip paṭalaṅkaḷ cūḻnta vaṭa, teṉ muṉikaḷilum, cuṭalkōḷkaḷāl aṭikkaṭi mūḻki eḻuntu iṭamāṟṟam peṟṟa piṟa iṭaṅkaḷilum illāta uyiriṉam tōṉṟuvataṟkāṉa cūḻalkaḷ iṅkē atikam iruntiruppa tālum mutaṉ mutalil iṅkutāṉ māntaṉ tōṉṟiṉāṉ eṉṟum, avaṉ paṭitta nākarikamē ulakil paḻamiyāṉatu eṉṟum kūṟuvar. ākavētāṉ tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ toṉmi ataṉ nilaviyalālum paṇpāṭṭālum muttatu eṉṟu yāvarum kūṟuvar.paṭimuṟi vaḷarcciyiṉpaṭi [evolutionary gOwth) nōkkumpōtum, ulakam tōṉṟiya kālam mutal nilappoti yiyal, maṇṇiyal, uyiriyal, kumukaviyal(camukaviyal pōṉṟavaṟṟiṉ ellāp paṭimuṟi vaḷarccik kūṟupāṭukaḷiyum tamiḻnāṭṭil kāṇkiṟōm, 
ēṟattāḻa patiṉōru kōṭiyāṇṭukaḷukku muṉ tamiḻakam 'kōṇṭuvāṉā" eṉṉum perunila amippuṭaṉ iṇintiruntatāl āppirikkā, āstirēliyā mutaliyavaṟṟuṭaṉ nilattāl toṭarpu koṇṭiruntatu eṉṟum, āppirikkā, ācutirēliyāk kaṇṭaṅkaḷi lirunta uyiriṉaṅkaḷum, nam nāṭṭilirunta uyiriṉaṅkaḷum parimāṟṟam aṭintaṉa veṉṟum aṟiviyalār kaṇittuḷḷatōṭu, avaṟṟiṉ paṭimuṟi vaḷarcciyiṉiyum virittuḷḷaṉar. ivvaṇṇamē tamiḻakam kiḻakkilum mēṟkilumuḷḷa toli nāṭu kaḷōṭu koṇṭirunta uṟavālpaṇpāṭṭup parimāṟṟaṅkaḷ ceytu vantatu eṉpataṉiyum aṟiya muṭikiṟatu. 
ilemūriyāk kaṇṭam, aṭlāṇṭik kaṇṭam, 'mū' eṉṉum pacipik kaṇṭam ākiyavi iruntaṉaveṉpati aṟiviyalār iṉṟu muḻumiyāka oppukkoḷḷavilli. eṉiṉum, ilaṅki, teṉṉakat tuṭaṉ iṇintiruntatu eṉpatiyum, kumarimuṉikkut teṟkēyum nilapparappu iruntatu eṉpatiyum ēṟṟukkoḷkiṉṟaṉar. itip pōlavē, teṉṉakamum, āppirikkāvi yaṭuttuḷḷa maṭakācukarum oruṅkiṇintiruntaṉa eṉpatiyum iṉṟiya ārāycciyāḷarkaḷ ēṟṟukkoḷkiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻnāṭṭil iṉṟikku mūṉṟilakkam āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉ ettakiya nilaviyakka acivukaḷō nila naṭukkaṅkaḷō allatu erimalik kumuṟalkaḷō ēṟpaṭavilli yeṉpatum ataṟkup piṉṉarē aintu pēruḻikaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉaveṉṟum, avvūḻikaḷāl kaṭalōrap pakutikaḷ maṭṭum kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiṉa veṉṟum aṟiviyalār kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻnāṭṭiluḷḷa pāṟikaḷi āynta ārāycci yāḷarkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭil ek kālattilum perum paṉiGlacials) paṭarntu mūṭikkiṭakkavilli eṉpati uṟutipaṭuttu kiṉṟaṉar. ivvāṟu paḻikaḷāl āṭkoḷḷappaṭāmalum, paṉippaṭalat tāl mūṭappaṭāma lum, māntaviṉam vāḻvataṟkēṟṟa tōtuvāṉa cūḻniliyiyum, taṭpaveppattiyum tamiḻakam ulakam tōṉṟiya kālam mutal iṉṟuvari peṟṟirukkiṟatu. itaṉāltāṉ paṉipaṭar vaḻik kālattilum (glacial age) tamiḻakam uyiriṉam vāḻvataṟkēṟṟa taṭpaveppa niliyip peṟṟiruntatu eṉpatāl iṅkutāṉ māntaviṉamē mutaṉ mutalil tōṉṟiyirukka vēṇṭum eṉ kiṉṟaṉar. iṉṟikku 15000 ōrilakkatti yimpati ṉāyiram āṇṭu kaḷukku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻariṉ mūtātiyar ittakiyap paṉipaṭar vaḻikaḷiyum kaṇkūṭākak kaṇṭirukkalām. itaṉāltāṉ namatu nāṭṭiṉappāvalar cuppiramaṇiya pāratiyār tamiḻakattiṉ toṉmi yik kūṟumpōtu, 
'toṉṟu nikaḻntatāṉattum uṇarntiṭum cūḻkayiāḷarkaḷum ivaḷ 
 eṉṟu piṟantavaṉ eṉṟuṇarāta iyalpiṉaḷāmeṅkaḷ tāy" 
eṉa viyanturikkiṟār. 


2. tol paḻaṅkālam 
varalāṟṟāciriyar, varalāṟṟukkum muṟpaṭṭa kālam (Pre-Historic Period) eṉṟum, varalāṟṟuk kālam (Historic Period) eṉṟum pakuttuk koṇṭu varalāṟṟi eḻututal vaḻakkamāka iruntu varukiṟatu. varalāṟ ṟukku muṟpaṭṭa kālattit tol paḻaṅkālam eṉavum aḻikkalām. iḵtu eḻuttuc cāṉṟukaḷ tōṉṟuvataṟku muṉṉuḷḷa kālamākum. 
eḻuttuc cāṉṟukaḷ tōṉṟiya kālam nāṭutōṟum vēṟupaṭu kiṟatu. eṉavē tolpaḻaṅkālam eṉpataṉ kālavelliyum nāṭu tōṟum vēṟupaṭum. varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muntiya it tol paḻaṅkālam mikutiyāṉa kālak kaṇakkiyuṭiyatu. toṉmāntaṉ vāḻniliyil vaḷarccip peṟṟa niliyē, varalāṟṟuk kālattil avaṉ aṭinta muṉṉēṟṟattiṉ paṭinili. itaṉāl, varalāṟṟil ik kālattiṟkuriya mukāmi viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum. 
tolpaḻaṅkālattik kaṟkālam' eṉṟum, 'māḻik kālam' (ulōkak kālam eṉṟum iraṇṭākap pakuttu ārāyntu vantaṉar, māḻik kālattil cempuk kālam (Copper age), irumpuk kālam (iron age) eṉṉum pākupāṭukaḷum iruntaṉa ip pākupāṭṭil varalāṟṟuk kālattiṉ toṭakkap pakutiyum aṭaṅkuvatu iyalpē. piṉṉar aṇmikkāla āyviṉ viḷivāl kaṟkālamum mēlum pāku paṭuttap peṟṟatu. paḻiya kaṟkālam (Paleolithic period), putiya kaṟkālam (neolithic period) eṉṟum, paḻiya kaṟkālatti mutaṟ kaṟkālam (lower paleolithic period), naṭuk kaṟkālam (middle paleolithic period), kaṭik kaṟkālam (upper paleolithic period) eṉṟum pākupaṭuttik kāṇ kiṉṟaṉar.paḻiya kaṟkālattiṟ kum putiya kaṟkālattiṟkum iṭip paṭṭa kālatti iṭik kaṟkālam [mesolithic period) eṉṟu aḻippar. māḻikkālam eṉṟu pākupāṭu ceyvataṟkuc cāṉṟākak kiṭitta poruḷkaḷ perumpālum putikuḻi kaḷiliruntē (burials) kiṭit tuḷḷaṉa. ip putikuḻikaḷip peruṅkaṟ paṭi' eṉpatu tamiḻ vaḻakku. eṉavē, ikkālattip peruṅkaṟpaṭik kālam eṉap poruḷpaṭum vakiyil peruṅkaṟkālam(mEgalithic period) eṉṟē kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉar. ik kālappākupāṭu ellā nāṭṭiṟkum poruntiyavāṟu iṉṟu ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ivvakiyil tamiḻakattiṉ tol paḻaṅkāla varalāṟu paḻiya kaṟkālam, iṭik kaṟkālam, putiya kaṟkālam, peruṅkaṟkālam eṉṉum pākupāṭṭiṉ aṭippaṭiyil iṅkuk kūṟappeṟukiṉṟatu. 
tolpaḻaṅkāla varalāṟṟiyaṟintukoḷvataṟku namakkuk kiṭikkum mūlac cāṉṟukaḷ akkāla makkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya karuvikaḷē. ak karuvikaḷuṭaṉ elumpu, māṉkompu, maram, caṅkup poruḷkaḷ mutalāṉavum kuṟinta aḷavil āyviṉpōtu kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ip poruḷkaḷikkoṇṭu ak kāla makkaḷiṉ vāḻkki muṟiyiṉi uyttuṇara vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. ivvuyt tuṇarvuc ceytikaḷē tol paḻaṅkāla varalāṟṟi nirappukiṉṟaṉa, 
kālak kaṇippu 
tolpaḻaṅkālap poruḷkaḷiṉ kālattik kaṇippatil mūṉṟu vakiyāṉa muṟikaḷ piṉpaṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. 
1. poruḷ kiṭikkappeṟṟaviṭattiṉ kālattik kaṇittu, atip poruḷiṉ kālamākak koḷḷutal. 
2. elumpu mutalāṉa poruḷkaḷic cāmparamaṭiyavaṇuttiṟaṉ kālak kaṇippu (Potassium argonisotobic dating method) muṟiyil kālattik kaṇṭaṟital. 
3. ceykaruvikaḷiṉ taṉmiyi oppiṭṭuk kālam kāṇal. 
nilattiṉ mēṟparappil kiṭikkum poruḷkaḷiṉ kālattik kaṇikka mēṟkūṟiya mutaṉmuṟi payaṉpaṭātu. akaḻvāyviṉpōtu āṟṟuppaṭuki nilattiṉ aṭippakutiyil kiṭikkum poruḷkaḷukkē im mutaṉmuṟiyāl kālattik kaṇittaṟiya muṭiyum. āṟṟup paḷḷat tākkil paṭiyaṭukku paṭivaṭukku eṉṉum iraṇṭu amitikaḷ uṇṭu, 'paṭiyaṭukku" eṉpatu āṟṟiṉ arippāl tōṉṟuvatu. itil mēṟpaṭivil kiṭikkum poruḷkaḷik kāṭṭilum kīḻppaṭivil kiṭippavi kāpattāl pintiyavi. *paṭivaṭukku" eṉpatu maṇ paṭintu āṟu mēliṭuva tāl amivatu. itil mēṟpaṭikaḷil kiṭikkum poruḷkaḷik kāṭṭilum kīḻppaṭivil kiṭippavi kālattāl pintiyavi. āṟṟup paṭivukaḷ, paṭivu nērnta kālattiṉ taṭpaveṭpa niliyik kaṇṭaṟiyavum utavukiṉṟaṉa. kūḻāṅkaṟkaḷ mikutiyākap paṭintiruppatāl perumaḻi peyta kālameṉalām. kaḷimaṇ paṭintiruntāl ak kālattil kāṟṟu mikutiyāka vīci maṇpaṭivu nērntateṉalām. ivaṟṟāl eccūḻalil, evvāṟu tol paḻaṅkāla makkaḷ vāḻntaṉareṉa ōraḷavu eṇṇip pārkkamuṭiyum, 
iṉṟiya nila amippil kaṭici nili 'piḷi cutaṉ' (Pleist Arie) tāḻiyil nikaḻnta tu. itaṟku muṟpaṭṭa 'piḷiyōciṉ' (Fleiscent) kāḻiyil māntar iruntaṉar eṉpataṟkāṉa aṟikuṟikaḷ kāṇappaṭa villi. ituvari kiṭittuḷḷa tolpaḻaṅkālac ciṉṉaṅkaḷil mikap paḻimi yāṉavikiḻakku āppirikkāviluḷḷa tāṅkeṉiyākā nāṭṭu ōlṭuvāy (Oldvi) eṉṉumiṭattil kiṭittaṉavākum. avi cāmparamaṭiya aṇut tiṟaṉ kaṇippu muṟiyil āyvu ceyyappaṭṭu ēṟattāḻa iṉṟikku irupatilakkam āṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭavi yeṉṟu kaṇikkappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa, 
ulakiṉ vaṭakōṇap pakutikaḷil paṉipaṭarvuc ciṟṟūḻikaḷ (glaciation) nikaḻntaṉa. airōppāvilum, intiyāvil imayamalic cāralilum attakiya kālavūḻikaḷ iruntamikkāṉa taṭayaṅkaḷ kaṇṭaṟiyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ulakiṉ nilanaṭukkōṭṭup(quator) pakutikaḷil ivaṟṟukkuc camamāṉa kālattil perumaḻic ciṟṟūḻikaḷ (Pluvial AgES) iruntaṉaveṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar karutukiṉṟaṉar. āppirikkāvil ic ciṟṟūḻikaḷ iruntamikkāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ teḷivākak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻnāṭṭiluḷḷa āṟṟuppaṭukikaḷi nōkkumpōtu iraṇṭu perumaḻic ciṟṟūḻikaḷ iruntami teḷivākiṟatu. ipperu maḻic ciṟṟuāḻikaḷi yaṭuttu iṭiveḷi yūḻikaḷum(Inter plurals) iruntaṉa, 
iv vakiyāṉa pākupāṭukaḷikkoṇṭu tolpaḻaṅkālac ciṉṉaṅkaḷiṉ kālam kaṇikkappaṭukiṟatu. 
kaṟkāla varalāṟṟil nākarikam, paṇpāṭu, toḻilnuṭpam, toḻil muṟi ākiya pākupāṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ōriṭattallātu oru kuṟukiya vaṭṭāra makkaḷ kaṟkaruvikaḷi uruvākkuvatil oru muṟiyiṉip piṉpaṟṟi yiruppar. itu toḻilmuṟi Hindustry) eṉṟu vaḻaṅkappaṭum. it toḻilmuṟi ulakiṉ palvēṟiṭaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭum niliyil toḻil nuṭpam (technique) eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. ittoḻilmuṟi paranta aḷavil kāṇappaṭiṉ atu paṇpāṭu[culture) eṉappaṭum. orē vakiyāṉa vāḻkki muṟiyil palvēṟu paṇpāṭukaḷik koṇṭu vāḻum makkaḷiṉ vāḻkki neṟi, nākarikam (civilization) eṉṟu peyar peṟum. ceṉṉit toḻil muṟi' (Madras Industry), 'acūliyaṉ toḻil nuṭpam (Achulean Techniques). apaviliyō acūliyaṉ paṇpāṭu (Abbevillip-Ahulean Culture) paḻaṅkaṟkāla nākarikam ākiya col vaḻakkukaḷi ivaṟṟiṟku ovvōr eṭuttukkāṭṭākak koḷḷalām. 
kaṟkaruvikaḷ iruvakiyiṉa. mūlakkal oṉṟil peyartteṭutta cillukaḷiliruntu uruvākkap peṟṟavi oruvaki, aḷavāṉavoru kallil ciṟuciṟu cillukaḷip peyartteṟintuviṭṭu uruvākkap peṟṟavi maṟṟoru vaki. ekkallil ceyyappeṟṟālum ataṉi uruvākkuvataṟkut taṉit toḻil nuṭpam vēṇṭum. kaṟkāla varalāṟṟil toḻil nuṭpaṅkaḷ palavuṇṭu. avaṟṟuḷ kiḷattōṉiyaṉ' (Elactonian), * acūliyaṉ" (Achalean), "ilevalāyciyaṉ' (LEvalleisian) mutalāṉavi kuṟippiṭattakkaviyākum. 
iyaṟkiyākak kiṭikkakkūṭiya kal allatu karpāṉaṅkaḷil (piḷanta cullil cila periya cillukaḷip peyartteṟintuviṭṭuk karuvikaḷākap payaṉpaṭuttappeṟṟavi 'kiḷattōṇiyaṉ toḻil nuṭpam uṭiyaviyākum. nīḷuruḷic cuttiyalip payaṉpaṭuttip paṭṭiyāṉa cillukaḷip peyartteṭuttuk karaṭumuraṭāṉa tōṟṟa miṉṟi, nērttiyākac ceyyappeṟṟavi 'acūliyaṉ' toḻilnuṭpa vakiyiṉavākum. aḷavāṉa oru kallil vēṇṭiya vaṭivam kiṭikkum vari mika nuṭpamākac cillukaḷip peyartteṟintu viṭṭuk karuviyiṉ kippiṭippakkamum vēṇṭiyavāṟu cila cillu kaḷip peyarttu viṭṭuc ceyyappeṟṟavi ilevalāyciyaṉ' toḻilnuṭpa vakiyiṉa. 
karuvikaḷiṉ vaṭivaṅkaḷi vittu avaṟṟiṉ payaṉpāṭṭi uyttuṇarntu pala peyarkaḷiṭṭu vaḻaṅkutaluṇṭu veṭṭikaḷ (Choppers), curaṇṭikaḷ, kiḻippāṉkaḷ(Cleavers), kikkōṭarikaḷ (Hand Axes) mutalāṉavi kuṟippiṭattakkavi, maṟṟum amippukaḷi vittu vaṭṭakkaruvi, kōṭak karuviyāpīciya vaṭivam, mukkōṇak karuvi, nīḷ vaṭṭak karuvi, piṟik karuvi muṉikaḷ eṉṟum aḻippar. mēṟkaṇṭa pākupāṭukaḷum peyarkaḷum nammāl uruvākkap peṟṟavi eṉpati maṟattalipākātu. 
intiyāvil tamiḻakattiltāṉ mutal mutalākap paḻiya kaṟkālak karuvikaḷ (Paleoliths) kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭaṉa. 1853 ām āṇṭu irāparṭ purucu puṭ(Robert Bruce Futc) eṉpavar ceṉṉiyi yaṭutta pallavapurap (pallāvaram pakutiyil avaṟṟik kaṇṭu piṭittār. piṉ avaruṭiya taṉipperu muyaṟciyāl intiyā muḻuvatum palaviṭaṅkaḷil taṟkālat karuvikaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkap peṟṟaṉa. intiyat tolliyal tuṟi 1930 ām āṇṭu tiruvaḷḷūr māvaṭṭam, attirampākkam eṉṉumūril koṟṟaliyāṟṟup paṭukiyil āyvu naṭattiyatu. 1963 ām āṇṭu iv vaṭṭattil kuṭiyam eṉṉumūriluḷḷa kaṟkālak kukiyoṉṟu kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭu, tolliyal tuṟiyiṉarāl āyvu ceyyappeṟṟatu. maṟṟum, mayilcu parkiṭ (Miles Burkict), pāṭṭarcaṉ(T.T. Paterson) viṭi. kiruṭṭiṇacāmi, cāyṉar(F.E. Zeuner), ālciṉ(Bridget Allchin) mutalā ṉōrum tamiḻakap pakutikaḷil āyvukaḷ naṭattit tam muṭivukaḷi veḷi yiṭṭuḷḷaṉar. 
tamiḻnāṭṭil mutaṟ kaṟkālak karuvikaḷum kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. potuvākap pāvāṟṟukku vaṭakkē mutaṟ kaṟkālak karuvikaḷ mikutiyākak kiṭittuḷḷami nōkkattakkatu. teṉ māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil maturi māvaṭṭattic cērnta tirumaṅkalam, tēṉi māvaṭṭam periya kuḷam pakutikaḷil iṭikkaṟkālak karuvikaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. kiḻakkuk kaṭaṟkariyōramākat tarattukkuṭikku arukil kiṭikkaṟkālatkaruvikaḷ mikutiyāṉa aḷavil kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. 
koṟṟaliyāṟu. tiruvaḷḷūr, poṉṉēri vaṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻiyāka ōṭi, eṇṇūrukku arukil kaṭalil kalakkiṟatu. pālāṟu paṇṭik kālattil inta āṟṟil ōṭikkoṇṭiruntatāka maṇṇiya vār (Geologists) karutukiṉṟaṉar. inta āṟṟup paṭukiyil paṭiyaṭukku kaḷum, paṭivaṭukkukaḷum amintuḷḷaṉa, ivvaṭukkukaḷiliruntu kaṟkaruvikaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. avi īrilakkam āṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭaṉavācuvum, ōrilakkam āṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭaṉavākavum aimpatiṉāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭaṉavākavum mūvēṟu kāla niliyil uruvākkappaṭṭaṉavāka uḷḷaṉa. iṅkuk kiṭitta kik kōṭarikaḷiṉ mutaṟkaṭṭamāṉa 'apaviliyaṉ' paṇpāṭṭiṉatākavum, aṭutta kaṭṭam' acūliyaṉ' paṇpāṭṭiṉatākavum uḷḷatu. 
caruvikaḷiṉ amippik koṇṭu avaṟṟiṉ payaṉpāṭṭi uyt tuṇarabām avi maṇṇit tōṇṭuvataṟkum, marattik kaṭitaṟkum, cīvutaṟkum, marakkiḷi, iṟicci mutalāṉavaṟṟi veṭṭutaṟkum, tōlik kīṟutaṟkum, vilaṅkukaḷi eṟintu tākkutaṟkum payaṉpaṭuttap peṟṟirukka vēṇṭum eṉpatu pulaṉā kiṟatu. ik karuvikaḷip payaṉ paṭuttiya makkaḷ vēṭṭiyāṭutali mēṟkoṇṭiruntaṉar eṉalām. ivarkaḷ elumpukaḷiyum karuvikaḷākap payaṉpaṭuttiyirukkavēṇṭum, malik kukikaḷil ik karuvikaḷ kiṭikkap peṟṟamiyiṉ ivarkaḷ kukikaḷil vāḻntārkaḷ allatu taṅkiṉārkaḷ eṉṉum muṭivukku varavēṇṭi yuḷḷatu. āṟṟuppaṭukikaḷil ivarkaḷ vēṭṭiyāṭit tirintaṉar. ivartam vēṭṭi vilaṅkukaḷ eviyeviyeṉat teriya villi, itē kālattil makārāṭṭirattil nīṇṭa kompuḷḷa yāṉikaḷ, mukkuḷampuk kutirikaḷ, kāṭṭu āviṉaṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa vāḻn tamikkāṉa taṭayaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mutaṟkālak karuvikaḷ kūḻāṅkal tiraḷikaḷiṉūṭē kiṭippatiliruntu ikkālattil peru maḻipeytavaṇṇamiruntatu eṉpatu teḷivākiṟatu. kaṭaṟkariyōraṅ kaḷil ivarkaḷ vāḻntatākat teriyavilli. vēṭṭiyāṭiya vilaṅku kaḷiṉ iṟicci, iyaṟkiyākak kiṭitta kāykaṉikaḷ, kiḻaṅkukaḷ ākiyaṉavaṟṟi ivarkaḷ uṇṭu vāḻntirukkavēṇṭum. neruppip paṟṟi ivarkaḷ aṟintirukkalām. 
ik kāla māntavelumpukaḷ kiṭikkappeṟavilli. eṉavē, māntar evvāṟu tōṟṟamaḷittār eṉa uyttuṇara muṭiyavilli. im mātiriyāṉa kaṟkaruvikaḷic ceyta makkaḷ āppirikkāvil 'nimir māntaṉ' 'ōmō erekṭacu' (Horo Erectus) iṉattic cērntavarāvar. - tamiḻakattil ik kālattil vāḻnta makkaḷum ōmō erakṭacu' iṉattava' rākavē irukkavēṇṭum. iv viṉatti liruntē nām vaḷarcci peṟṟuḷ ḷōm. karuvikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiya im makkaḷ cintaṉittiṟaṉ vāyntavarākavē irukkavēṇṭum. ēṉeṉil, ivvaṟivuttiṟaṉ oṉṟē vilaṅkukaḷiliruntu māntaṉi vēṟupaṭuttik kāṭṭuvatu. ivarkaḷ tam karuttip piṟarukku veḷip paṭuttum vakiyil vevvēṟovikaḷi eḻuppip pēciyirukka vēṇṭum. ivar tam moḻiyip paṟṟi vēṟoṉṟum kūṟutaṟkiya lātu. 
tamiḻakattil im mutaṟkaṟkāla nākarikam īrilakkam āṇṭukaḷukku muntiyateṉpatu teḷivu. in nākarikattil kāṇappaṭum kikkōṭarip paṇpāṭu (Hand AXE Culture) vaṭintiyāvil nālilakkam āṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭatākavum, āppirikkāvil ēḻariyilakcum āṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭatākavum koḷḷattakka vakiyil taṭayaṅkaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻakattil pōtiya aḷavu akaḻvāyvukaḷ ceyyappeṟavilli. etir kālattil nikaḻttap peṟum akaḻvāyvukaḷāl kālattāl muntiya taṭayaṅkaḷ kiṭikkappeṟalām. 
āppirikkak kikkōṭarip paṇpāṭṭukkum, teṉṉintiyak kikkōṭarip paṇpāṭṭukkumiṭiyē kuṟippiṭattakka aḷavil oṟṟumikaḷ uḷḷaṉa, makkaḷiṉ iṭappeyarcciyālō, eṇṇap peyarcciyālō ivvoṟṟumikaḷ kāṇappeṟalām.
iṭik kaṟkālam 
intiya varalāṟṟil paḻiya kaṟkālattil mutal, kaṭikkaṟkālaṅ . kaḷukkiṭiyē oru kālakkaṭṭam iruppatic caṅkāliyā eṉpār kaṇṭaṟintār, ak kālattic cupparāv eṉpār 'iṭik kaṟkālam' eṉak kuṟippiṭalāmeṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭataṉit toṭarntu ik kālam taṉittu ārāyappaṭukiṟatu. tamiḻakattil ik kālak karuvikaḷ perumpā lum mutaṟ kaṟkālkkaruvikaḷ kiṭittaviṭaṅkaḷilēyē kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. 
tiruvaḷḷūr māvaṭṭam 	-	attirampākkam 
		kuṭiyam 
maturi māvaṭṭam 	-	putuppaṭṭi 
		cīvarakkōṭṭi 
tūttukkuṭi māvaṭṭam 	-	meyññāṉam 
		(tiruccentūrukkarukil) 
mutaṟkaṟkālak karuvikaḷippōlavē iṭik kaṟkālak karuvi kaḷum tolluyirit taṭaṅkaḷōṭu (fossils) kiṭikkavilli . eṉavē, ivi kiṭitta āṟṟuppaṭuki maṇṇiṉ niliyik karutiyē kālattik kaṇikka vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. ivvāṟu kaṇittatil ivi iṟṟikku ēṟattāḻa nāṟpatāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭavi
yeṉat teriyavarukiṟatu. ikkālattilum perum maḻi pey0
tavaṇṇamiruntatu. 
ik kālattuk karuvikaḷ perumpālum cilluk karuvikaḷākavē yuḷḷaṉa. "cācuppar' (Jasper), cerṭ' (Cher) kal vakiyil iviceyyap peṟṟavikaḷākavuḷḷaṉa (mutaṟkaṟkālakkaruvikaḷ kuvārṭṭar ciṭ' iṟiaitak kaṇa āṟṟuppu culvakiyāl āṉavi. iṭikkaṟkālak karuvikaḷ ciṟiyaṉa; nuṭpamāṉa vēlippāṭu koṇṭavi. aḻakik kāṭṭilum payaṉpaṭuttamē ik karuvikaḷ uruvākkappaṭumpōtu mutaliṭam peṟṟatu. mutaṟkaṟkālak karuvikaḷ kiyil piṭittup payaṉpaṭuttum taṉmiyaṉa. iṭikkaṟkālak karuvikaḷō marakkiḷi mutalāṉa kippiṭikaḷil poruttip payaṉ paṭuttum taṉmiyuṭiyaṉa. 
ivarkaḷ ik karuvikaḷi marakkompu nuṉikaḷil kaṭṭi vēlkaḷākap payaṉpaṭuttiyirukkavēṇṭum. ik karuvikaḷ ciṟiyaṉavā yiruntamiyiṉ neṭun tolivu avaṟṟic cumantu cella eḷitā yiruntirukkum. eṉavē, ivarkaḷ vēṭṭiyāṭiya nilapparappu, mutaṟ kaṟkāla makkaḷiṉ vēṭṭi nilattiviṭa virintirukkavēṇṭum. katti pōtum kūrmiyāṉa karuvikaḷāl vilaṅkukaḷiṉ tōlik kiḻittuc cīviyirukka vēṇṭum. eṉavē, ik kāla makkaḷ vilaṅkukaḷiṉ tōli āṭiyākap payaṉpaṭuttiyirukka vēṇṭum, ivarkaḷ mutaṟ kaṟkāla makkaḷip pōlavē maliyaṭivāraṅkaḷilum āṟṟup paṭukikaḷilum vāḻntaṉar. nellik kaṭaṟkarip pakutikaḷil ik kālak karuvikaḷ kiṭippatāl ivarkaḷ kaṭaṟkari yōraṅkaḷilum vāḻntirukka vēṇṭum, mīṉpiṭikkum toḻiliyum mēṟkoṇṭirukkalām. ivarkaḷ niliyākat taṅki vāḻntārkaḷā eṉpatu teḷivākat teriyavilli ). 
nam nāṭṭil kiṭikkum ik karuvikaḷ nāṟpatiṉāyiram āṇṭu kaḷukku muṟpaṭṭavi, ulaka aḷavil in nākarikam nūṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭatākavum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ilevalāyciyaṉ toḻil nuṭpattiyuṭiya ik karuvikaḷ āppirikkā, airōppiyap pakutikaḷil kiṭikkum curuḷikaḷōṭu oppumiyuṭiyaṉavāyuḷḷa miyi ālciṉ eṉpār eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār. itaṉāl ak kālattil ivviru pakuti makkaḷukkumiṭiyē uṟaviruntirukka vēṇṭu meṉa maṉacikkalām. 
kaṭik kaṟkālam 
kaṭik kaṟkālak karuvikaḷil perumpālāṉavi oru viral ( aṅkulattiṟkum kuṟivāṉa nīḷamuṭiyavi. ivaṟṟil pala kuyiṉṟu ceyyappeṟṟaviyāka uḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟip piṭikaḷil poruttittāṉ payaṉpaṭutti irukka vēṇṭum. ivaṟṟil pal iru pakkaṅkaḷilum muṉikaḷuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ciṟu kampiṉ nuṉiyil poruttiṉāl avi ampākap payaṉpaṭum. itaṉāl ik karuvikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiya makkaḷ villampukaḷi aṟintavar kaḷeṉakkoḷḷalām. pala karuvikaḷi iṇikkattakka marattuṇṭil pūṭṭiṉāl kaṟukkuṭiya arivāḷ aḻiyum, itaṉāl ivarkaḷ vēḷāṇmiyil īṭupaṭṭu aṟuvaṭi ceytaṉar eṉak koḷvatu poruntum, maṟṟum piṟi vaṭivuḷḷa kaṟkaruvikaḷum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ivimikavuyarattiluḷḷa paḻaṅkaḷiyum tēṉaṭikaḷiyum eṭukkap payaṉpaṭum toraṭṭukaḷāka irukka vēṇṭum. kaṭaṟkariyōraṅkaḷil vāḻnta im makkaḷ oruvaki mitaviyip payaṉpaṭuttik kaṭalil ceṉṟirukka vēṇṭumeṉpatu ālciṉ pōṉṟōr karuttākum. ik karuvikaḷ kiṭikkap peṟṟaviṭaṅ kaḷil ivaṟṟic ceyvataṟkāṉa mūlakkaṟkaḷ illi. avi neṭun toliviliruntu koṇṭu varappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum. ivarkaḷiṉ karuvikaḷ mika nuṭpam uṭiyaṉavāka uḷḷamiyiṉ ak karuvikaḷic ceyvatil tērccipeṟṟa kaliñarkaḷ iruntārkaḷ eṉavum koḷḷalām. 
poruti tāmparaparaṇi yāṟṟiṉ irumaruṅkilum, nellik kaṭaṟkariyōraṅkaḷilumuḷḷa 'tēri' eṉṉum maṇal tiṭṭup pakutikaḷillum kaṭikkaṟkālak karuvikaḷ mikutiyākak kiṭit tuḷḷaṉa. ak kālattil ip pakutiyil kaṭalmaṭṭam ippoḻutuḷḷa niliyiviṭa iṉṉum irupatu aṭi uyarattil iruntatu. kaṭal vaṭintapōtu kāṟṟiṉāl maṇal mēṭukaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. am maṇalmēṭukaḷē uḷnāṭ ṭil 10, 12 kilō mīṭṭar tolivu vari 5 mutal 50 mīṭṭar uyara muḷḷaṉavākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ip paṭivukaḷi ārāynta cāyṉar eṉpār avi kimu. 4000 āṇṭukaḷic cērntaviyeṉṟu kuṟippiṭ ṭuḷḷār. eṉavē, ik kaṭik kaṟkālam ikkālattic cērnta teṉalām. 
maṟṟum tiruvaṇṇāmali, vēlūr, kāñcipuram, tiruvaḷḷūr māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil iṭik kaṟkālak karuvikaḷ kiṭikkumiṭaṅkaḷilum, maturi māvaṭṭattil tirumaṅkalam, tēṉi māvaṭṭam periyakuḷam vaṭṭaṅkaḷilum taṭik kaṟkālak karuvikaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. tirumaṅ kalam vaṭṭattil ciṟappākat tiṭiyaṉ, tē.kalluppaṭṭi mutaliya uṇarkaḷi yaṭutta maliyaṭivāraṅkaḷilum, pōṭināyakkaṉūrukku arukil kōṭāṅ kippaṭṭi, tātaṉōṭi ākiya ūrkaḷiyaṭutta maliyaṭivāraṅkaḷilum aṭikkaṟkālak karuvikaḷ ērāḷamākak kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. 
nellit tēri'kaḷil kiṭikkum irupakka ampumuṉi īḻatti lum. intōṉēciyāvilum kiṭikkum akkā ampumuṉikaḷōṭu oppumi yuṭiyaṉavāyuḷḷaṉa. ivvoppumi annāṭukaḷuk kiṭiyē makkaḷukkirunta toṭarpi uṟutippaṭuttat tuṇi ceykiṉṟatu. 
kaṭik kaṟkāla makkaḷ iṉṟu āntirap pakutiyil vāḻum ceñcukaḷChenichu), īḻattil vāḻum vēṭṭarkaḷ (Validah) ākiyōrip pōlavē vāḻkki naṭattiyirukkavēṇṭum eṉṟu ālciṉ karutukiṟār. im makkaḷ ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil veḷiyulakat toṭarpil vātapōtu vil, ampu, tōṇṭutaṭi mutalāṉa karuvikaḷik koṇṭu vāḻntu vantaṉar. pirampuk kūṭikaḷ, pāykaḷ mutalāṉavaṟṟip piṉṉik koṇṭaṉar. nīr nilikaḷi nōkkiyē avartam vāḻviṭaṅkaḷ amintiruntaṉa. vēṭṭarkaḷ iṟantavarip putittuc caṭaṅkukaḷ ceytaṉar. vēṭṭikku utavum ciṟu teyvaṅkaḷi vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. at teyvaṅkaḷukkut tam taliyi veṭṭik kāvu koṭuttaṉar(kaṭik "kaṟkāla makkaḷiṉ ciṉṉaṅkaḷākak kiṭittuḷḷa elumpukaḷil tali yelumpu kiṭikkāmi ivarkaḷum avvaḻakkamuṭiyavarō eṉṉum aiyappāṭṭit tūṇṭukiṟatu. ivvāḻkki muṟi kaṭik kaṟkāla makkaḷiṉ vāḻkki muṟiyeṉa eṇṇuvatu ōraḷavu cariyē. 
paḻiya kaṟkāla nākarikattiṉ iṟuti niliyākiya kaṭik kaṟkāl nākarikamum paṇpāṭukaḷum muṟiyē mutaṟkaṟkāla, iṭikkaṟkāla nākarikap paṇpāṭukaḷiṉ vaḷarcciyeṉalām. 


putiya kaṟkālam 
paḻiya kaṟkālattilirunta karuvikaḷip pōlallāmal vaḻa vaḻappāka merukēṟṟappaṭṭa kaṟkaruvikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiya makkaḷ vāḻnta kālamē putiya kaṟkālameṉṟu muṉṉāḷil karutappaṭṭatu. attuṭaṉ vēḷāṇmit toḻil ceytu makkaḷ nilittu vāḻat talippaṭṭa kālam putiya kaṟkālam eṉa ikkālattil aṟiyappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. toṭakkattil putiya kaṟkālam eṉṉum kālakkaṭṭam iruntatā eṉṉum aiyappāṭum iruntuvantatu. kaṭanta āṇṭukaḷil mēṟkoḷḷap paṭṭa āyvukaḷiṉ viḷivākap putiya kaṟkālam eṉṉum kālakkaṭṭam iruppatu uṟuti ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. 
cēlam, tacuṭur (tarumapuri māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil cērvarāyaṉmalit toṭarkaḷilum, tiruvaṇṇāmali māvaṭṭattil cavvātumali, ēlamalip pakutikaḷilum putiya kaṟkālak kaṟkōṭarikaḷ kaṇṭu piṭikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. vēlūr māvaṭṭam, tiruppattūr vaṭṭampiyam paḷḷiyi aṭutta maliyaṭivārattil naṭuvaṇaracuttolliyal tuṟi akaḻvāyvu naṭatti, iru kālakaṭṭanākarikaṅkaḷi veḷik koṇarntatu. mutaṟkālak cuṭṭam putiya kaṟkālattiṉ toṭakkap pakuti, iraṇṭāvatu kālakaṭṭam peruṅkaṟkālattic cērnta kāla kaṭṭam. 
mutaṟkāla kaṭṭattil elumpiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa karuvikaḷum, 'cācuppar', 'akēṭ', 'cerṭ' ākiya kalvakikaḷik koṇṭu ceyyap peṟṟa ciṟu tūvaṟkatti (Pen knife) pōṉṟa karuvikaḷum, celṭ' eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭum vaḻavaḻappāṉa kallālākiya kikkōṭarikaḷum kiṭittaṉa. iraṇṭām kālakaṭṭattil elumpuk karuvikaḷ kiṭikkap peṟavilli. kiṭitta piṟa karuvikaḷ toḻilnuṭpam mikuntaṉavāy iruntaṉa. iraṇṭām kāla kaṭṭattil kuṭiyiruppukaḷilum muṉṉēṟṟam kāṇappaṭṭatu. maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷil paḷapaḷappāṉa cāmpal niṟap pāṉikaḷ(grey"twares) mutaṟ kālakaṭṭattavi. cenniṟap pāṉikaḷ (redwarts) iraṇṭām kāla kaṭṭattavi. 
paḻiya kaṟkālak kōṭarikaḷ kiyil piṭittup payaṉpaṭuttum taṉmiyaṉa. putiya kaṟkālak kōṭarikaḷō piṭiyil poruttip payaṉ paṭuttum taṉmiyaṉa.putiya kaṟkālattil kalluḷi,kalvāycci, kaṟkottu mutalāṉa karuvikaḷum kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.kaṭik kaṟkāla makkaḷ marappiṭikaḷil poruttip payaṉpaṭuttiya kaṟukkari vāḷ pōṉṟa karuvikaḷum kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cuṟkottum, cuṟukkarivāḷum vēḷāṇmikkup payaṉpaṭuvaṉa. vāycciyum, uḷiyum marattic cīvit tuḷiyiṭa utavum. maṟṟum vitiyūṉṟap payaṉpaṭum tōṇṭu taṭiyiṉ taliyil kaṉattiṟkākap poruttap paṭṭavi yeṉṟu karutappaṭum tuḷiyiṭappaṭṭa vaṭṭaṅkaḷum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. maṟṟum tummi, kuḻavi, tirikimutalāṉaviyum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. 
elumpuk karuvikaḷil tuḷiyiṭum karuvikaḷ muṉipōṉṟa karuvikaḷ, curaṇṭukaruvikaḷ mutalāṉavi kuṟippiṭattakkavi. ivi tōl tikkap payaṉpaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum. 
cāmpalniṟa maṇpāṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ vāyviḷimpil ceṅkāḷi yiṉāl {red ochre) vaṇṇam tīṭṭappaṭṭiruntatu. pāṉikaḷōṭu, maṇṇāl ceyyappaṭṭa oru muyal pommiyum kiṭittuḷḷatu. karunāṭakap pakutikaḷil akkāla makkaḷ taṅkaḷ kuṭiyiruppukaḷukku arukiluḷḷa pāṟikaḷil timilum, nīṇṭakompukaḷumuṭiya māṭṭu uruvaṅkaḷit tīṭṭiyiruntaṉar. appakutikaḷil māṭṭuruvam koṇṭa maṇ pommi cuḷum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ivi akkāla makkaḷiṉ kaliyuṇarvi eṭuttukkāṭṭuvaṉ. 
maṟṟum kēḻvaraku, koḷḷu, paccippayaṟu ākiyaṉa tīynta niliyil kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟi ak kāla makkaḷ payiriṭṭaṉar eṉalām. ivaṟṟi ivarkaḷ cēmittu vittaṉareṉak koḷḷattakka vakiyil vīṭṭil tavacattōṭu kūṭiya periya maṭkalam akaḻvāyvil kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. 
māṭu, āṭu, kōḻi, puḷḷimāṉ, paṉṟi, kāṭṭuppūṉi, kāṇṭāmā kāṇṭāmirukam mutalāṉa vilaṅkukaḷiṉ elumpukaḷum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ivvilaṅkukaḷiṉ iṟicciyi akkāla makkaḷ uṇavākap payaṉpaṭutti ṉar. āṭu, māṭu, kōḻi mutalāṉavi vīṭṭilum vaḷarkkappaṭṭirukka lām. 
karunāṭakap pakutikaḷil cāṇac cāmpal paṭivukaḷum kāṇappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. ivi ak kāla makkaḷ āṭu māṭukaḷik kiṭiyamittu vāḻnta pāṅki yuṇarttuvaṉa. tamiḻnāṭṭilum iv vāḻkki muṟi iruntirukka vēṇṭumeṉpatit tamiḻaka, karunāṭakap putiya kaṟkālap paṇpāṭṭuk kūṟukaḷ palavakiyil ottiruppati eṇṇukiyil uyttuṇaramuṭikiṟatu 
ivarkaḷatu kuṭiyiruppukaḷ kuṟiñci nilattil amintiruntaṉa. piyampaḷḷi akaḻvāyviliruntu ivarkaḷatu vīṭukaḷippaṟṟi oruvāṟu uyttuṇaramuṭikiṟatu. makkaḷ kuṭicikaḷil vāḻntaṉar. kuṭicikaḷ perumpālum vaṭṭam allatu nīḷvaṭṭa vaṭiviṉa: ōriru aṟikaḷik koṇṭavi; cuvarkaḷ mūṅkil cuḻikaḷi niṟutti amikkappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu koḷḷattakka vakiyil tuḷikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa! vāyiṟpaṭi amip piruntatu. taḷam perumpāṭṭik kaṟkaḷāl pāvappaṭṭiruntatu; ataṉmēl kūḻāṅkaṟkaḷiyum maṇṇiyum cērttup picintu parappik keṭṭippaṭuttappaṭṭiruntatu; ak keṭṭittari cāṇattālō cuṇṇam kalanta cāṇattālō meḻukappaṭṭiruntatu; vīṭṭil oru pakkam kallaṭuppu maṟṟoru pakkam tavacam vittirunta periya maṭkalaṅkaḷ kuṭicikku veḷiyil kuppikaḷik koṭṭi vit tiruntaṉar eṉṟu koḷḷattakka kuḻikaḷ mutaliyaṉavum kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. 
cērvarāyaṉ, cavvātu ākiya malippakutikaḷil vāḻnta akkāl makkaḷiṉ kuṭiyiruppukaḷil cila iyaṟkiyākavē pāṟi kaḷālāṉa araṇ koṇṭaviyāy viḷaṅkiṉa. cila kuṭiyiruppukaḷil perumpāṟikaḷi uruṭṭi ūric cuṟṟilum araṇ amittuk koṇṭa pāṅkum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. karunāṭakap pakutikaḷil ōr araṇ ellikkuḷ 10, 15 kuṭicikaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. oru kuṭiciyil 5,5pēr vāḻntaṉar eṉak koṇṭāl ōr naṉaril ēṟattāḻa 100 pēr vāḻntirukkalām. orē maliyil 23 ūrkaḷ itupōl kāṇappaṭuvatāl oru malippakutiyil ēṟattāḻa 200, 300pēr vāḻntaṉar eṉalām. 
piyampaḷḷiyil kāṇappaṭum ip putiya kaṟkālac ciṉṉaṅkaḷ ki. mu. 1300 - 1200-aic cārntaṉavākavuḷḷaṉa. vaṭakkil ik kālac ciṉṉaṅkaḷ kimu. 2300 - 1050 -kac cārntaṉavākavum, īrāṉ nāṭṭil ki.mu. 4000 -aic cārntaṉavākavum pāvakattiṉattil ki. mu. 8000 -aic cārntaṉa vākavum uḷḷaṉa. maṟṟum ak kāla īrāṉ nāṭṭu maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭuc camakāla maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷip peritum ottuḷḷamiyi ālciṉ, caṅkāliyā mutalāṉa aṟiñarkaḷ kaṇṭaṟintu, īrāṉ makkaḷ teṉṉāṭṭil kuṭipukuntirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷiṉ karutti uṟutippaṭutta vēṟu cāṉṟukaḷ illi. 
tamiḻnāṭṭil vāḻnta putiya kaṟkāla makkaḷ taṟkālattil tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ pēcum makkaḷiṉ mūtātikaḷāka irukkalām eṉṟu ālciṉ karutukiṟār. 
peruṅ kaṟkālam (Megalithic Period) 
peruṅkaṟ kālam eṉṉum toṭaril peruṅkal eṉpatu kaṟkaruviyi uṇarttātu. paḻiya kaṟkālam, putiya kaṟkālam eṉpavaṟṟil kal kaṟkaruvikaḷi uṇarttum, peruṅ kaṟkālam eṉpatiluḷḷa kal, kallaṟikkuḻikaḷ mītu pōṭappaṭṭirunta peruṅkaṟkaḷi uṇarttum peruṅkaṟkālac ciṉṉaṅkaḷil perumpālāṉavi iṟantōriṉ elumpukaḷip putitta putikuḻikaḷil kiṭittamiyāṉ ik kaṟkālatti perum kaṟkālam' eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. ik kālac ciṉṉaṅkaḷil irumpālāṉa poruḷkaḷ mikutiyākak kiṭittamiyāl itaṉi "irumpuk kālam' eṉṟum kūṟi vantaṉar, 
peruṅkal ciṉṉaṅkaḷ 
tiru. vi.ṭi. kiruṭṭiṇacāmi eṉpār peruṅkaṟ ciṉṉaṅkaḷi vakippaṭuttik kūṟiyuḷḷār. avaṟṟuḷ cilavaṟṟik kāṇalām. 
kaltiṭṭi 
itu nilamaṭṭattiṟku mēl amikkappaṭṭirukkum. iyaṟki yāṉa kaṟkaḷ pakkattiṟku oṉṟākac cāyāvaṇṇam nāṉku pakkaṅkaḷilum niṟuttappaṭṭirukkum. iṭiyiluḷḷa aṟiyil īmap poruḷkaḷ iṭappaṭṭirukkum. talippakkam oru kal vittu mūṭappeṟṟirukkum. iraṇṭu mūṉṟu. caṟkaḷāl mūṭappaṭṭiruppatum uṇṭu, pakkaccuvar kaṟkaḷukkut tuṇiyākac ciṟu kaṟcuvarkaḷ amikkap peṟuvatuṇṭu. ivvāṟāṉa cuvarkaḷ iraṇṭu mūṉṟu amintaṉavum uṇṭu. iṟutiyil kūḻāṅkaṟkaḷ, caraḷi, maṇ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ tuṇi koṇṭu mūṭappaṭ ṭirukkum. iḵtoru tiṭṭuppōl kāṇappaṭum, cila camayam it tiṭṭukaḷ citivuṟā tirukkap periya uruṇṭik kaṟkaḷi vaṭṭamāka vippatum uṇṭu. 
kalpatukki 
itu nilamaṭṭattiṟkuk kīḻē amikkappaṭṭirukkum. cetukkap paṭṭa kaṟkaḷ pakkac cuvarkaḷākap payaṉpaṭuttap peṟṟirukkum, pakkac cuvarkaḷ 'svastikā' vaṭivil turuttiya niliyil cāyāmal niṟuttap peṟṟirukkum aṟiyil īmap poruḷkaḷ iṭṭu mūṭap peṟṟirukkum, piṟa pātukāppukaḷ kaltiṭṭikku uḷḷatu pōl amikkap peṟ tirukkum. 
ik kaltiṭṭiyilum, kalpatukkikaḷilum iṟantavarkaḷiṉ cati pirinta elumpukaḷē iṭappeṟṟaṉa. piṇam iṭampeṟṟataṟkāṉa cāṉṟu kiṭikkavilli. cila kaltiṭṭikaḷil maṇṇālāṉatum, cila camayam kālkaḷ cittira vēlippāṭu koṇṭatumāṉa oṉṟiraṇṭu īmat toṭṭikaḷum vikkappaṭuvatuṇṭu. āṭu pōlum puruvaṅkoṇṭa īmat toṭṭi oṉṟum kiṭittuḷḷatu. elumpukaḷōṭu pala aṇimaṇikaḷ, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ irumpiṉāl ceyyappeṟṟa kalaṅkaḷ mutalāṉavaṟṟi yum iṭṭuvittaṉar. kaltiṭṭiyilum kalpatukkiyilumuḷḷa pakkakkal oṉṟil iṭutuḷi(port hole) amikkappaṭṭirukkum. iṭutuḷi perumpālum 15 ce.mī. viṭṭam koṇṭatāyirukkum, rāḷ nuḻiyum aḷavukkup periyatāṉa iṭutuḷi uṭiyaṉavum kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṭutuḷi perumpālum kiḻakku nōkkiyirukkum vēṟu ticikaḷilum kāṇappaṭuvatu uṇṭu. 
maṇkuvi, kalkuvi, kalkiṭi 
ivi cultiṭṭi allatu kalpatukkiyiṉṟi mūṭappeṟṟavi, tāḻiyō īmattoṭṭiyō īmacciṉṉaṅkaḷuṭaṉ ivaṟṟil putikkap paṭṭirukkum. putikkappaṭṭaviṭattil maṇ kuvikkappaṭṭiruntāl ataṉik "maṇkuviyeṉṟum, kaṟkaḷ kuvikkappaṭṭiruppiṉ ataṉik "kalkuvi' eṉṟum, peruṅkaṟkaḷ kiṭattappaṭṭiruppiṉ ataṉik kal kiṭi' yeṉṟum vaḻaṅkuvar.


kuttukkal, kalvarici 
cilaviṭaṅkaḷil īmap poruḷkaḷ putikkappaṭṭa iṭattiṉ mītu orukal niṟuttap peṟṟirukkum. itaṉik kuttukkal' eṉṟu kuṟippiṭuvar. pala kaṟkaḷ oru variciyākavum, pala variciyākavum naṭappaṭutalum uṇṭu. itaṉik 'kalvarici' yeṉpar. iv vakiyil niṟuttappaṭṭa kaṟkaḷ perumpālum iru mīṭṭar uyaram koṇṭavi, 44 mīṭṭar uyaram koṇṭa kaṟkaḷ niṟuttappaṭṭa ciṉṉaṅkaḷum uṇṭu. kuttukkaṟkaḷ cetukkappaṭṭum, cetukkappeṟāmalum kāṇappaṭum. 
toppikkal, kuṭikkal, kuṭivarit tāḻvaṟi 
ivi kēraḷanāṭṭuppakutiyil kāṇappaṭuvaṉa.ceppam ceyyap paṭṭa cuṟkaḷic cāyvākak kūmpu pōl niṟutti ataṉ ucciyil vaṭṭavaṭivamāṉakal vittu mūṭappaṭṭirukkum ciṉṉam toppiccal' eṉappaṭum. itu kāḷāṉip pōṉṟu tōṟṟamaḷikkum. itil kuttuk kaṟkaḷukkiṭiyē tarimaṭṭattiṟkuk kīḻē tōṇṭappaṭṭa kuḻiyil īmacciṉṉaṅkaḷ vikkappaṭṭirukkum. 'kuṭikkal" kērakarāp pakutiyil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum vaṭṭamāṉa kuṭiyip pōṉṟatu. itaṟkuk kuttukkal illi. camap poruḷkaḷ putikkappaṭṭa iṭattiṉmītu appaṭiyē kaviḻttu vikkappaṭṭirukkum. cilaviṭaṅ kaḷil pāṟi kaḷit tāḻvākak kuṭintu īmac ciṉṉaṅkaḷ vikkappaṭṭirukkum. avaṟṟik "kuṭivarittāḻvaṟiyeṉṟu kuṟippiṭukiṟōm. itaṉi amippataṟkup pāṟiyil ōr āḷ iṟaṅkumaḷavukkuc caturamāṉa paḷḷam mutalil tōṇṭappaṭum paḷḷattiṉ aṭimaṭṭattiyoṭṭip pakkac cuvaroṉṟil āḷ nuḻiyum aḷavukku oru tuḷi ceyyappaṭum. it tuḷiyi vāyilākak koṇṭu uḷḷē aṟi tōṇṭappaṭum. avvaṟiyil vicip palaki, mukkāli pōṉṟa amippukaḷuṭaṉ kallilēyē mēṭikaḷ cetukkap peṟum. im mēṭikaḷiṉmītu īmapporuḷkaḷ vikkappaṭum. aṟiyiṉ naṭuvil tūṇ niṟkumāṟu viṭappaṭṭa aṟikaḷum uṇṭu. oṉṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa aṟikaḷik koṇṭa kuṭivarittāḻvaṟikaḷum uṇṭu. īmac ciṉṉaṅkaḷ uḷḷē vikkappaṭṭapiṉ, vaḻiyāka amikkappaṭṭa tuḷiyum kavvittu mūṭappeṟum. ivi tolpaḻaṅkālak kuṭivarik kalikku eṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷākavum tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. 
tāḻi 
itaṉi 'mutumakkaḷ tāḻi' eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. it tāḻikaḷ mikavum periyavi. tāḻikaḷiṉ aḷaviviṭac caṟṟup peritāṉa paḷḷam tōṇṭi atil tāḻi iṟakkappaṭum, kuḻiyiṉ aṭiyil tiṇṇi yamikkappaṭṭu īmapporuḷkaḷ vikkappaṭṭa piṉ tāḻi iṟakkap paṭum.tāḻiveṟum maṇṇāl nirappappaṭum. cilaviṭaṅkaḷil tāḻiyilum elumpukaḷ iṭappaṭṭaṉa. iṟutiyil kuḻi paḻiya maṇṇik koṇṭō putu maṇṇik koṇṭō mūṭappaṭum. 
peruṅkal ciṉṉaṅkaḷil cila potuvākat tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ ellāp pakutikaḷilum uḷḷaṉa. vaṭṭāra vāriyāka avaṟṟiṉ ciṟapput taṉmikaḷi maṭṭum nām kāṇpōm. 
kēraḷa nāṭṭil kāṇappaṭuvatākak kūṟappaṭum toppikkal, kuṭikkal ākiyavi kōvi māvaṭṭattil pavāṉiyāṟu, moyyāṟu. noyyal ākiya āṟṟuppakutikaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nīlamalip pakutiyil āyirakkaṇakkāṉa kalkuvikaḷ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiluḷḷa īmacciṉṉaṅkaḷuṭaṉ urōmāṉiyak kācukaḷum iruntami kuṟippiṭattakkatu. kalkuviyil kiṭitta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷil pala vilaṅkuruvattōṭu kūṭiya mūṭikaḷ uṭiyaṉavāka iruntami akkāl ' makkaḷiṉ kaliyuṇarvi veḷippaṭuttum cāṉṟākum. veṇkal ēṉaṅkaḷ palavum ivaṟṟil kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. 
teṉ māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil tāḻikaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. nelli māvaṭṭattil tāḻikaḷ putikkappaṭṭa iṭattiṉ mītu enta aṟikuṟikaḷum kāṇappaṭavilli. kuṟṟālam pakutiyil kaṟkuvikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. teṉkācip pakutiyil maṇ kuvikaḷ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. alekcāntar rē kāyalpaṭṭiṉattiṟku aṇmiyiluḷḷa tāḻikkāṭṭik kaṇṭupiṭittār. āticcanallūrt tāḻikkāṭu akaḻ vāyvu ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. iṅku oṉṟiṉ mēl oṉṟākap putikkappaṭṭa ṭirunta tāḻikaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. in nili, iti tāḻikkāṭu pallāṇṭukaḷākap payaṉpaṭuttappeṟṟamiyik kāṭṭukiṟatu. maṇ kalaṅkaḷ, irumpu, veṇkalam, taṅkam ākiya māḻip poruḷkaḷuṭaṉ tāḻikaḷil muḻu elumpukkūṭum kiṭittuḷḷami kuṟippiṭattakkatu. 
maturi māvaṭṭattil aṉuppāṉaṭi, viḷāṅkuṭi, paravi mutalāṉa ūrppakutikaḷil tāḻikkāṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. kōṭikkāṉal, paḻaṉimalip pakutikaḷil kaltiṭṭikaḷ pala kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṅku makkaḷ eppōtumē vāḻntirāta kal matilkaḷ koṇṭa pakutikaḷ pala uḷḷaṉa. ivikaḷil tāḻikaḷ uḷḷaṉavākat terikiṟatu. 
putukkōṭṭip pakutiyil kuraṅkuppaṭi, matamatakkat tāḻi, pāṇṭavar kuḻi eṉṉum peyarkaḷāl makkaḷ vaḻaṅkum culpatukki kaḷum kaltiṭṭikaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kuṟuṅkaṟpa.ai- mutu makkaḷ tāḻi paṇṭiyārkuḻi allatu paṇṭikkuḻi eṉpavi ivvāṟu tirintu 'pāṇṭavarkuḻiyeṉṉum vaḻakkiṉip peṟṟirukkalām. 
cittaṉṉavācal, āraṇippaṭṭi, kurantappaṭṭi, takkaṉpaṭṭi, toṭiyūr, muttukkāṭu, kāṇiyappaṭṭi, kaḷakkuṭippaṭṭi, tiruppūr mutalāṉa pārkaḷil kaltiṭṭikaḷ akaḻvāyvu ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. naṭuvaṟi veḷiyaṟi, veḷiyaṟiyil mēl kīḻaṟikaḷ, aṟikaḷuk kiṭiyē iṭutuḷikaḷ mutalāṉa amippukaḷuṭaṉ culkuviyic cuṟṟi oru kalvaṭṭa amippum cila iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷaṉa. *pulvayal' eṉṉum kāṭṭilirunta tāḻikaḷ iraṇṭuḷ iru muḻumāntavelumpuk kūṭukaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. ivvelumpuk kūṭukaḷ uṭkārnta niliyil valakkiyil kuṟuvāḷip piṭittuk koṇṭum iṭakkiyit tam toṭimītu vittukkoṇṭum kāṭciyaḷittaṉa. 
tirucci māvaṭṭam perumāḷpāḷiyattil oru periya culkuvi kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu. karuvūr-tiṇṭukkal cāliyiluḷḷa malik kōvilūril kalkuvikaḷiyum, kaltiṭṭikaḷiyum koṇṭa oru periya īmakkāṭu uḷḷatu. maṟṟum tirucci, tañci māvaṭṭaṅkaḷilum pala kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cēlam, tarmapuri, viḻuppuram, kaṭalūr, tiruvaṇṇāmali, vēlūr ākiya māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷavi iṉṉum teḷivāka āyvu ceyyappaṭavilli. tiruvaḷḷūr māvaṭṭattil 'lēṭṭariṭ' pāṟikaḷālāṉa ciṉṉaṅkaḷ naṉku āyvu ceyyappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa.
elumpuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷ 
māntaṉiṉ elumpukkūṭukaḷ muḻumiyāṉa aḷavil oṉṟiraṇṭu kiṭittiruppiṉum perumpālāṉavi ciṟcila elumpu kaḷik koṇṭaviyākavē uḷḷaṉa. cilaviṭaṅkaḷil oṉṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa māntavelumpukaḷ orē ciṉṉattil putikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. oruvēḷi avarkaḷ orē camayattil iṟantavarkaḷāka iruttal vēṇṭum. iv velumpukaḷ erikkappaṭṭaviyākat teriyavilli. piṇam vīci yeṟiyappaṭṭu nīṇṭa nāṭkaḷukkuppiṉ eñciya elumpukaḷ eṭuttup putikkappaṭṭaṉa pōlum putiya kaṟkāla makkaḷ piṇaṅ kaḷit taṅkaḷ vīṭukaḷilō vīṭukaḷukku arukilō putittārkaḷ, peruṅkaṟkāl makkaḷō taṉittaviṭattil vēḷāṇmikkup payaṉpaṭāta nilattil putittaṉar. peruṅkal ciṉṉaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭum iṭaṅkaḷukku arukil nīrvaḷam kuṟinta pakutikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, ak kāla makkaḷiṉ ūrkaḷ nīr vaḷam kuṟinta pakutikaḷil amintiruntaṉa eṉalām. 
īmap poruḷkaḷ 
kiṭittuḷḷa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ niṟattāl nāṉku vakippaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. mutalvaki civappu maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ (red wares). ivi uruvil periyavi.tavacac cēmippup pāṉikaḷākavum, taṇṇīrp pāṉikaḷāka vum, tāḻikaḷākavum akkāla makkaḷāl ivi payaṉpaṭuttap peṟṟaṉa veṉalām. kucciyāl pativukaḷ ceytu amitta kali vēlippāṭu kaḷum ivaṟṟil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. 
iraṇṭāvatu vaki, karuppuniṟa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ (black Wares) kāṟṟu pukātapaṭi vittuc cuṭṭāl maṭpāṇṭam karuniṟampeṟum. im muṟiyil ivi ceyyappeṟṟaviyeṉalām. aḷavil ciṟiyaṉavāka vuḷḷa ivi camiyal pāṇṭaṅkaḷākap payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭaṉa.
