karikāṟ peruvaḷattāṉukkuppiṉ āṇṭa kiḷḷivaḷavaṉi mutalām kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ eṉṟatāl ivaṉi iraṇṭāmkiḷḷi vaḷavaṉ eṉkiṟōm. ivaṉ uṟiyūriyāṇṭavaṉ. ivaṉukku neṭumāvaḷavaṉ, pacumpūṇ kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ, poṉṉiyal perumpūṇvaḷavaṉ, polam pūṇvaḷavaṉ eṉṟa virutup peyar kaḷum uṇṭu. itaṉāl ivaṉ poṉ aṇikaḷ aṇivatil viruppa muḷḷavaṉ eṉpatu terikiṟatu. ivaṉ kālattilum uḷnāṭṭup pōrkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa, avaṟṟiṉiṉṟu taṉṉāṭciyi nili niṟuttik koṇṭu, cēra nāṭṭiṉ kōnakarāṉa karuvūri muṟṟukiyiṭṭu veṉṟāṉ. muḷḷūr maṉṉaṉ maliyamāṉiṭam pakimikoṇṭu avaṉuṭiya iru kuḻantikaḷip piṭittuvantu yāṉikkālil iṭṭukkollamuyaṉṟāṉ. itaṉiyaṟinta kōvūrk kiḻār takka aṟivuri kūṟi ac ciṟārkaḷi mīṭṭār eṉa aṟikiṟōm. 
pāṇṭiyaṉ kōti mārpaṉ mēl paṭiyeṭutta ivaṉip pāṇṭiyaṉuṭiya paṭittalivaṉ paḻiyaṉ māṟaṉ kūṭalukku arukil tōṟkaṭittāṉ. appoḻutu pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiyāṇṭavaṉ tali yālaṅkāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa neṭuñceḻiyaṉ āvāṉ. ivarkaḷuṭiya kālam ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyākavum, mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkamākavum irukkalām. ivaṉ kurāppaḷḷiyil (kuḷamuṟṟam iṟantupaṭṭāṉ. itaṉāl ivaṉic 'cōḻaṉ kurāp paḷḷit tuñciya kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ' allatu "kuḷamuṟṟattut tuñciya kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ' eṉṟu aḻikkiṉṟaṉar. 
ivaṉukkup piṉ irācacūyam vēṭṭa perunaṟkiḷḷi eṉpāṉ āṇṭāṉ. ivaṉ koṅku nāṭṭiyāṇṭayāṉik kaṇcēy māntarañ cēral irumpoṟiyuṭaṉ pōriṭṭu veṟṟiyaṭintāṉ. itit tavira ivaṉip paṟṟiya vēṟu mukāmic ceyti etuvum namakkuk kiṭikka villi . 
cōḻaṉceṅkaṇāṉ 
iraṇṭām kiḷḷivaḷavaṉukkuppiṉ aracāṇṭavaril kuṟippiṭat takkavaṉ cōḻaṉ ceṅkaṇāṉ' āvāṉ. kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷil aṟiyap paṭum kaṭiciyaracaṉ ivaṉēyāvāṉ. ivaṉ koṅku nāṭṭiyāṇṭa cēraṉ kaṇikkāl irumpoṟiyuṭaṉ cuḻumalam eṉṉumiṭattil pōriṭṭu veṟṟi kaṇṭāṉ. mīṇṭum pōr' eṉṉum ūril iruvarum mōtikkoṇṭaṉar. atilum poṟiyaṉ tōṟṟuc ciṟip piṭikkap paṭṭāṉ. cōḻa nāṭṭuc ciṟiyil vāṭiya poṟiyaṉ orunāḷ nīrvēṭki mēliṭṭu nīr kēṭka, avaṉik kāvalarkaḷ iḻivāka naṭattiṉar. māṉam tāṅkātu poṟiyaṉ ciṟiyilēyē uyir nīttāṉ eṉṟu avaṉē pāṭiyatākak karutappeṟum puṟappāṭaliliruntu aṟikiṟōm. ciṟimīṇṭa ivaṉ taṉ nāṭṭiliruntu cōḻaṉukkaṭaṅki āṇṭāṉeṉpatu kaliṅkattupparaṇiyil kāṇum ceytiyākum, 
ceṅkaṇāṉ ēṟattāḻa irupattintu āṇṭukaḷ āṇṭāṉ. tōrāyamāka ki.pi. 225-l ivaṉ iṟantirukkalām. ivaṉukkuppiṉ kaḷappirar cōḻanāṭṭik kippaṟṟik koṇṭaṉar. ivaṉōṭu kaḻakakkālac cōḻar āṭciyum muṭivaṭintatu. 
(i) kaḻakakkālap pāṇṭiya vēntar
tamiḻkkaḻakam eṉṟavuṭaṉ pāṇṭiyariṉ tamiḻccaṅkamē niṉi vukku varum. caṅkam vittut tamiḻ vaḷartta ap pāṇṭiya vēntariṉ varalāṟṟic caṟṟē aṟivōm. 
kaḻakakkālap pāṇṭiyanāṭu 
ellikaḷ: paḻampāṇṭiya nāṭu eṉpatu tamiḻakattiṉ teṉ pakutiyil iruntatu. itaṉmēṟkilum kiḻakkilum kaṭalkaḷēelli kaḷāyiruntaṉa.kaḻakakkālattil ilaṅkikkum pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṟku miṭiyil kuṟukalāṉa ciṟukaṭal oṉṟu iruntatu. itaṉi 'ārkalik kaṭal' eṉṟaṉar. ik kaṭal paṟṟit tālamiyiṉ kuṟippukaḷilum, perippuḷucu' eṉṟa nūlilum aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟatu. avaṟṟil iḵtu 'ōrkalik kaṭal' (Orgalic Sea) eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṟatu. kiḻakku, mēṟkuk kaṭalkaḷiyum, inta ārculik kaṭaliyum yavaṉarkaḷ potuvāka 'eritirikkaṭal' ceṅ kaṭal) eṉṟu aḻittaṉar. itaṉit tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷ koṟkik kaṭal' allatu 'koṟkik kuṭākkaṭal" eṉṟu aḻittaṉa. ak kālattil tāmparaparaṇiyāṟu intak koṟkikkuṭākkaṭalil kalantatu. inta āṟu kaṭaluṭaṉ kalakkumiṭattiltāṉ koṟkit tuṟimukap paṭṭiṉam iruntatu. ittuṟimukappaṭṭiṉattil kiṭitta muttukaḷip paṟṟi ak kāla ulaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷum, varalāṟukaḷum palappaṭap pēcukiṉṟaṉa, cāṇakkiyaṉ taṉatu aruttacāttirattil koṟkiyiṉ muttuc celvattip pōṟṟik kūṟiyuḷḷāṉ. ittakiya ciṟappuvāynta ik koṟkit tuṟi tōrāyamāka ki.pi. 10 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tārntu pōyiṟṟu. iṉṟu iṅku māṟamaṅkalam eṉṉum ciṟṟūrē kāṇappaṭum kiṟatu. kaḻakakkālattil pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ teṉpakuti iṟṟi irāmēcu varattiyum tāṇṭiyiruntatu. tōrāyamāka ki.mu. 500 āṇṭukaṭku muṉputāṉ perumpuyalāl kaṭal poṅki arippu ēṟpaṭṭatāl irāmēcuvaram taṉittīvu ākiyatu. pōrttukkīciya vaṇikarkaḷ kaṭalaritta pakutiyi āḻappaṭuttik kappal pōkkuvarattukkup payaṉpaṭuttik koṇṭaṉar. 
itaṟkum palavāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉ irāmēcuvarattip pōl ilaṅkiyum pāṇṭiya nāṭṭup pakutiyiliruntu pirintu viṭṭatu. itaṉi īḻanāṭṭu nūlāṉa makāvamcattil virikkak kāṇalām. avvāṟu ilaṅkikkum, teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ teṉkarikkumiṭiyil tuṇṭippu ēṟpaṭṭa piṉ nīril maṇ karintuviṭṭatu. āṉāl, pāṟi kaḷum, kuṉṟukaḷum iṉṟuḷḷaṉa. iḵtu iyaṟkiyāl nērnta ceyal. āṉāl, 'pāmarar' ivi irāmar ilaṅkikkuc cellumpōtu vāṉaraṅkaḷāl pōṭappaṭṭa kaṟkaḷ eṉkiṉṟaṉar. 
ilaṅki, tamiḻakattiṉiṉṟu pirintatu pōl, paḻantamiḻakattiṉ teṉpakuti kaṭal koṇṭa ceytiyi iṟiyaṉārakapporuḷ urip pāyiram, cilappatikāram, kalittoki mutaliya nūlkaḷum kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāṟṟāṉ mutal iṭik kaḻakaṅkaḷik kaṭal koṇṭateṉṟum, iṉṟiya akaḻvārāycciyum, maṇṇiyalum, kaṭal nūlum itaṉi uṟutippaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. 
kaḻakakkālap pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ mēṟ kelli kaṭal eṉṟālum nuṇukippārkkumpōtu iṉṟiya teṉtiruvitāṅkūr, potikimali, nāñcilnāṭu mutaliyavaṟṟi itu taṉṉakattē koṇṭiruntatu. potiki maliyil āyeṉṉum vēḷirkaḷ pāṇṭiyarukku uṭpaṭṭu āṇṭaṉar. viḻiñam vēḷir tuṟimuka mākum. iṉṟiya taṉucukōṭi mutukōṭi) aṉṟiya pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ vaṭavelliyāka iruntatu. vaṭappuṟattil koṭumpāḷūr koṭumpi) iruntatu. ataṉiyaṭuttuc cōḻa nāṭu iruntatu. koṭumpāḷūriyāṇṭa kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷ iruṅkō vēḷ' eṉappaṭṭaṉar. ivarkaḷ camayattiṟkēṟṟapaṭi pāṇṭiyarōṭum, cōḻarōṭum kūṭṭuc cērntu koḷvar. 
malikaḷum kuṉṟukaḷum 
kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷi āyumpōtu pāṇṭiya nāṭṭilirunta malikaḷ, kuṉṟukaḷ, āṟukaḷ, tuṟimukaṅkaḷ, nakaraṅkaḷ mutaliyavi paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. 
iṉṟiya maturikku vaṭakkiluḷḷa iruṅkuṉṟam kaḻakak kālattil tirumālkuṉṟu, tirumāliruñcōli, cōlimali mutaliya peyarkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu. itaṉ ucciyilirunta iyaṟkik kukiyil camaṇa, cākkiya muṉivarkaḷ vāḻntārkaḷ. itaṉ kal paṭukkikaḷiluḷḷa pirāmik kalveṭṭu iv vuṇmiyit teḷivākkukiṟatu, kaḻakakkāl nakkīrar tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭiyil pāṭiya tirupparaṅkuṉṟam' eṉṉum tiruppatiyilum, camaṇa cākkiya muṉivarkaḷ vāḻnta iyaṟkik kukiyum, kal paṭukkikaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itiluḷḷa pirāmik kalveṭṭil kīḻakappulavar nallantuvaṉārpeyarum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. itippōlavē āṉimaliyiṉ ucciyil iyaṟkik kukiyum, kalpaṭukkikaḷum uḷḷaṉa. itiluḷḷa pirāmik kalveṭṭu kaḻakak kālattuc cākkiya muṉivarkaḷ iṅkut tavam ceytaṉar eṉpatik kūṟukiṟatu. 
caṅka nūlkaḷil aṟiyappaṭum potikimali pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil iruntatu. malayattuvaca pāṇṭiyaṉ itil tavam ceytatākak kūṟappaṭu kiṟatu. akattiyarukkum im malikkum toṭarpu illi. avarip piṇittup paṭitta kati kipi. 8 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiyatu. caṅka nūlkaḷil perumiyuṭaṉ pēcappaṭum paṟampu maliyum, paṟampunāṭum pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiltām iruntaṉa. itil vāḻnta pāriyiṉ kālam tōrāyamāka. kipi. 100-125 āka irukkalām. iṉṟu kaḻukumali eṉa . vaḻaṅkappaṭum kuṉṟukaḷil kukikaḷum, kalpaṭukkikaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉ ṟaṉa. avaṟṟiluḷḷa pirāmik kalveṭṭukaḷ pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉ, nanti āciriyar eṉṉum muṉivarukku oru kukiyit tāṉam ceytatik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. ikkalveṭṭukimu. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭic cērntatu. 
āṟukaḷ 
viki, tāmparaparaṇi, cilampāṟu, paḵṟuḷiyāṟu, kumariyāṟu mutaliya āṟukaḷ pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil pāyntōṭiyatik kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅ cukaḷiliruntu aṟikiṟōm. ivaṟṟil paḵṟuḷiyāṟum, kumariyāṟum kaṭal koṇṭa pāṇṭiya nāṭṭuṭaṉ kaṭalil maṟintu viṭṭaṉa. 
tuṟimukappaṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ 
toṇṭimaruṅkūrppaṭṭiṉam, koṟki, kumari, viḻiñam mutaliya tuṉmukaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷil aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟil ēṟṟumatiyāṉa poruḷkaḷ paṟṟiyum, ayalakac culuṅkaḷ ivaṟṟil vantu pōṉamipaṟṟiyum kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷilēyaṉṟi ayalakac cāṉṟukaḷilum kuṟippukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. 
nakaraṅkaḷ 
pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ mukāmi nakaraṅkaḷāṉa teṉmaturiyum katava puramum kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiṉa. kūṭal (maturi), mōkūr, taṅkāl, alivāy centil, picir ākiyavi caṅkakālap pāṇṭiyar nakaraṅkaḷākum. pāṇṭiyariṉ koṭivaḻit tāṉit talivarkaḷ (paḻiyar, paḻiyaṉ māṟaṉ mōkūril iruntaṉar, 
pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ toṉmi 
pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ toṉmiyip paṟṟi mēṟkaṇṭa ceytikaḷi viruntē oruvāṟu ūkittaṟiyalām. tamiḻaka varalāṟu' pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil tāṉ toṭaṅkukiṟatu. ulaka varalāṟē ilemūriyā allatu kaṭal koṇṭa teṉṉāṭṭilētāṉ toṭaṅkukiṟatu. mutal māntaṉ iṅku tāṉ tōṉṟiṉāṉ eṉṟellām kūṟumpōtu pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ toṉmiyik kaṇittuk kūṟaviyalātu. kimu. nāṉkām nūṟṟāṇṭic cērnta ilaṅki varalāṟu tīpavamcam an nāṭṭu mutal ciṅkaḷa maṉṉaṉāṉa vicayakumāraṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ makaḷi maṇantatōṭu avaṉuṭiya rappa tōḻarkaḷum pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ peṇkaḷi maṇantu koṇṭu pāṇṭiyarukku āṇṭutōṟum viliyuyarnta muttukkaḷiyum, piṟa poruḷkaḷiyum paricāka aṉuppiṉar eṉak kūṟukiṟatu. pāṇṭiyaṉ taṉ makaḷik koṭuttatōṭu parivāraṅkaḷiyum, patiṉeṇ vakittoḻi lāḷarkaḷiyum ilaṅkikku aṉuppi, an nāṭṭi nākarikamuṭiya nāṭākkiṉāṉ eṉavum aṟikiṟōm. ki. mu. nāṉkām nāṟṟāṇṭil aracāṇṭa cantirakupta muriyaruṭiya avikku vanta mekcuttaṉicu eṉṟa yavaṉat tūtuvaṉ pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil oru peṇ aracāṇṭatāka kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷāṉ.kimu. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil muriyap pēraracarāy viḷaṅkiya acōkar tamatu pāṟik kalveṭṭukaḷil mūvēntarkaḷip paṟṟik kuṟippiṭuvatiyaṟivōm. kimu 185 ām āṇṭil poṟikkappaṭṭa kaliṅka nāṭṭukkāravēlaṉiṉ attikumpāk kukik kalveṭṭil avaṉuṭiya pāṇṭiya nāṭṭup paṭiyeṭuppu kuṟippiṭap paṭukiṟatu. 
ivaṟṟit tavira nām mēlē kaṇṭa kuṉṟukaḷiluḷḷa pirāmik kalveṭṭukaḷ kaḻakakkālattil pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil camaṇamum, cākkiyamumpeṟṟirunta ciṟappiṉi aṟivikkiṉṟaṉa. 
pāṇṭiya vēntar 
iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷurippaṭi katavapurattiliruntu āṇṭa pāṇṭiya vēntaril muṭattirumāṟaṉ kaṭiciyaracaṉ āvāṉ. ivarukkup piṉ āṇṭa vēntarkaḷiṉ vivaram kōviyākat teriyavilli. ki.mu. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil veṭṭappaṭṭa mēṟkūṟiya kaḻukumaliyi luḷḷa kalveṭṭiṉpaṭi mutalām pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉi muṭattirumāṟaṉi yaṭuttu aṟikiṟōm. ik kalveṭṭu maturikku vaṭakiḻakkē patiṉāṟu kal toliviluḷḷa ariṭṭāpaṭṭi eṉṉum ūrukku arukiluḷḷa māṅkuḷam eṉṉum ūriluḷḷa kuṉṟukaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. tamiḻaka maṉṉarkaḷiṉ pirāmik kalveṭṭukaḷil mikavum paḻamiyāṉatu ituvēyākum. ip pāṇṭiyaṉ taṉ kulakkuru nanti āciriyaṉukkum, avaric cērntavarukkum tavam ceyya ik kuṭivari yiyum, kaṟpaṭukkikaḷiyum camittuk koṭuttāṉ eṉṟa ceytiyē ik kalveṭṭil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. itaṉic camitta peruntaccaṉ paḻiyaṉ kaṭalaṉvaḻuti eṉṟum, maṟṟum caṭikaṉ, iḷañcaṭicaṉ ākiya araca ūḻiyarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum itil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟit tavira ik kalveṭṭil ivaṉip paṟṟi vēṟoṉṟum aṟiya  muṭiyavilli , 
mutalām pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉukku muṉ āṇṭavar yāreṉṟu teriyātatu pōlavē piṉ āṇṭavar-yāreṉṟum teriya villi. āyiṉum puṟanāṉūṟu oṉpatām pāṭalil 'neṭiyōṉ” eṉpavaṉippaṟṟik kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ivaṉtāṉ paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟi veṭṭi nīrppācaṉa ēntukaḷ ceytāṉ eṉpatiyum, munnīr viḻā (vāraṇaṉ viḻā eṭuttāṉ eṉṟum ip pāṭaliliruntu aṟikiṟōm. inta āṟu kaṭalkōḷāl aḻintupaṭṭatu eṉpatiliruntu ivaṉ ki.mu. mutalām nūṟṟāṇṭukkum muṟpaṭṭavaṉ eṉat teḷiyalām. 
neṭṭimiyār eṉṉum pulavar (puṟam 15itaṉip paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟu maṇaliṉum atikamāṉa āṇṭukaḷ vāḻumpaṭi vāḻttukiṉṟamiyāl palyākacāli mutukuṭumip peruvaḻuti paḵṟuḷiyāṟu kaṭal koḷḷappaṭu vataṟku muṉ āṇṭatākak karutappaṭu kiṟatu. āṉāl, atu poruttamā yilli, cilappatikārattiṉ kālamāṉa ki.pi. 180kku muṉpē āṇṭirukka vēṇṭumeṉa aṟikiṟōm. 
puṟanāṉūṟu tarum cāṉṟukaḷiliruntu ivaṉ pala nāṭukaḷi veṉṟā ṉeṉṟum, veṉṟa nāṭukaḷilirunta nakaraṅkaḷik kaḻutikaḷik kaṭṭi ēr uḻutavaṉeṉṟum, veṉṟa nāṭukaḷil kiṭitta koṇṭip poruḷkaḷip pulavarukkum, vaṟiyarukkum vāri vaḻaṅkiyavaṉeṉṟum aṟikiṟōm. ivvāṟu pala nāṭukaḷiyum veṟṟi koṇṭa perumitat tāl ivaṉ vēḷvikaḷ pala ceytu 'palyākacāli' eṉṉum virutum peṟṟāṉ. piṉ eḻunta maturik kāñciyilum "māṅkuṭi - marutaṉār' taliyālaṅkāṉattuc ceru veṉṟa neṭuñceḻiyaṉi vāḻttumpōtu palyākacāli mutukuṭumiyippōl pala ciṟappukaḷ peṟṟu vāḻumāṟu vāḻttu kiṟār (matu. 759.763). ki.pi. 8 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻutappaṭṭa vēḷvik kuṭic ceppēṭṭilum ivaṉip paṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷ kāṇappaṭum kiṉṟaṉa. ivaṉ āṇṭa āṇṭukaḷil eṇṇikkiyum, ivaṉukkuppiṉ āṇṭavarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum teriyavilli. 
aṭuttu āṇṭa pāṇṭiyaṉ peruvaḻuti eṉpavaṉippaṟṟip puṟa nāṉūṟu vāyilāka aṟikiṟōm. ivaṉ pōril vallavaṉ (karuṅki oḷvāḷ peruvaḻuti eṉṟum, kavuriyar marukaṉ eṉṟum irumpiṭart taliyār eṉṉum pulavar pāṭukiṟār. itit tavira ivaṉip paṟṟiyaṟiya vēṟu ceytikaḷ kiṭikkavilli. ivaṉuṭiya tampi iḷamperuvaḻutieṉpavaṉ oru pulavaṉ āvāṉ. ivaṉ puṟanāṉūṟṟil 12 ām pāṭali yum, paripāṭalil 15 ām pāṭaliyum pāṭiyavaṉ. ivaṉ kalattil cellum pōtu kaṭalil mūḻki iṟantāṉ. 
aṭuttu aṟiyappaṭum pāṇṭiyaṉ olliyūri veṉṟa olliyūr tanta pūtappāṇṭiyaṉ eṉpavaṉ āvāṉ. olliyūrc ciṟṟaracaṉ cōḻarukku uṭpaṭṭavaṉ. ātalāl cōḻaṉ pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ mītu paṭiyeṭuttāṉ. ap pōril pūtap pāṇṭiyaṉ māṇṭāṉ. avaṉuṭalit tīyiliṭṭapōtu pūtappāṇṭiyaṉ kōpperuntēviyum uṭaṉkaṭṭiyēṟiṉāḷ. 
aṭuttu aṟiyappaṭum pāṇṭiyaṉ pacumpūṇpāṇṭiyaṉ āvāṉ. ivaṉ potiki maliyil aracāṇṭa āy aṇṭiraṉ tuṇiyuṭaṉ koṅku nāṭṭiṉ kiḻakkup pakutiyik kippaṟṟiṉāṉ. ak kālattil koṅku nāṭṭiṉ naṭuvil takaṭūr iruntatu. ataṉiyāṇṭa neṭumiṭal añciyip pacumpūṇ pāṇṭiyaṉ tuḷuvanāṭṭiṉ mītu pōr toṭukkac ceytāṉ. appōril neṭumiṭal añci iṟantupaṭṭāṉ. ākavē, ippōril pāṇṭiyaṉ tōlvi kaṇṭāṉ. ēṉeṉil, takaṭūr neṭumiṭal añci pāṇṭiyaṉiṉ tāṉittalivaṉ āvāṉ. ip pāṇṭiyaṉukku irunta maṟṟoru tāṉittalivaṉ kaḷvar perumakaṉ teṉṉavaṉāvāṉ. 
āriyappaṭi kaṭanta neṭuñceḻiyaṉ 
kimu, iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭilirunta neṭuñceḻiyaṉi mutalām neṭuñceḻiyaṉ eṉṟōm. ivaṉ ki.pi. 2 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉiṭip pakutiyil pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiyāṇṭavaṉ .vaṭapulatti liruntu teṉṉakat tiṉ mītu paṭiyeṭuttu vanta yavu tēyar eṉṉum kūṭṭattāriṉ āriyap paṭiyit tōṟkaṭittut turattiyatāl ivaṉ 'āriyappaṭi kaṭanta neṭuñceḻiyaṉ" eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭukiṟāṉ. ivaṉ ciṟanta aṟiñaṉ. kalvi kaṟṟaliṉ payaṉippuṟam.183ām pāṭalil ciṟappuṟap pāṭiyuḷḷāṉ. 
ivaṉ araciyal poṟuppukaḷ aṉittiyum tam ūḻiyar kaṉiyē nampi viṭṭuviṭṭu nēraṭik kaṇkāṇippuc ceyyāmal poṟuppiṉṟik kiṭantāṉ. oru camayam vārttikaṉ eṉṉum pārppaṉa ṉuṭiya makaṉ tiruṭiyatāka ūḻiyarkaḷ mūlam kēṭṭut tīra ucāvāmal avaṉic ciṟiyil aṭittāṉ; vārttikaṉ maṉivi kārttiki eṉpavaḷ koṟṟavikkōyil muṉ muṟiyiṭṭāḷ. ak kōyil katavu tāṉākavē mūṭikkoṇṭatu. tavaṟṟiyuṇarnta pāṇṭiyaṉ pārp paṉaṉi viṭuvittu maṉṉippukkēṭṭatōṭu taṉ kuṟṟattiṉ kaḻuvāyāka nilaṅkaḷiyum avaṉukku aḷittāṉ. 
itip pōlattāṉ taṉ nakarukkup piḻipput tēṭic cilampu viṟka vanta kōvalaṉik kaḷvaṉeṉṟu kūṟiya araṇmaṉip poṟkollaṉ pēcci nampit tīra ucāvāmal kōvalaṉik koliceyyumpaṭi āṇiyiṭṭāṉ. piṉṉar kaṇṇaki muṟi yiṭṭuc cilampiṉ katiyi viḷakkumpōtu maṉa atircciyāl aracuk kaṭṭivilēyē māṇṭāṉ. itaṉāl ivaṉ 'aracuk kaṭṭilil tuñciya neṭuñceḻiyaṉ' eṉap paṭṭāṉ. ivaṉuṭiya muṟiyaṟṟa ceyalikkaṇṭu kotitteḻunta nakara makkaḷ araṇmaṉiyit tīyiṭṭukkoḷuttiṉar. itiyē kaṇṇaki maturiyi yerittatākak kūṟuvar. 
tōrāyamākakipi.180-l ivaṉ iṟantirukkalām. ivaṉ kālattil cēranāṭṭiyāṇṭavaṉ cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ. ic ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ pāṇṭiyar tāṉit talivaṉ mōkūrppaḻiyaṉ māṟaṉ taṉ naṇpaṉ aṟukiyeṉṉum aracaṉippōr ceytu koṉṟāṉ eṉpataṟkāka mōkūrmītu paṭiyeṭuttu, paḻiyaṉ māṟaṉuṭiya kāval maramāṉa vēppa maratti veṭṭi, avaṉiyum koṉṟu, anta vēppamaratti avaṉuṭiya uvaḷakap peṇkaḷi moṭṭiyaṭittuk kūntalāl tiritta cuyirik koṇṭu piṇittu, yāṉiyāl iḻuttu varacceytāṉ. ip pōr cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ iḷavaracaṉāka irukkum pōtu naṭantatu. ic cēraṉē piṉṉar kaṟputtēvati kaṇṇakikkuc cili yeṭuttavaṉ āvāṉ cāttaṉārum ivviru maṉṉarkaḷuṭiya kālattavarē āvār.
pāṇṭiyaṉ naṉmāṟaṉ 
ivaṉuṭiya iyaṟpeyar veṟṟivēṟ ceḻiyaṉ eṉpatu. avaṉ koṟkippaṭṭiṉattil iḷavaracaṉāka neṭuñceḻiyaṉukkut tuṇi yāka āṇṭu vantāṉ. neṭuñceḻiyaṉ iṟantapiṉ aracukkaṭṭil ēṟiyatum 'pāṇṭiyaṉ naṉmāṟaṉ' eṉṟu peyar pūṇṭāṉ. kaṇṇaki vaḻakkurikka vantu iv vaḻakkil neṭuñceḻiyaṉ tīrppukkūṟumuṉ tāṉē iṟantāṉ. kaṇṇakiyum maturiyi viṭṭu veḷiyēṟiṉāḷ. ākavē, aracu muṟippaṭi av vaḻakki ucāvit tīrppuk kūṟum poṟuppi aṭuttu vanta ip pāṇṭiyaṉ ēṟṟāṉ. kōvalaṉik kuṟṟavāḷiyākki avaṉ kolikkuk karaṇiyamāyirunta araṇmaṉip poṟkollaṉiyum, avaṉukku uṟutuṇiyāka niṉṟa poṟkollar palariyum kolicceyalukkut tūṇṭiya taṟkākavum, aracamātēviyiṉ cilampit tiruṭiyataṟkākavum, ak kāl muṟippaṭi avarkaḷiṉ talikaḷi veṭṭumpaṭi tīrppaḷittāṉ. neṭuñceḻiyaṉ tāṉē it tīrppukku āḷāki ēṟkeṉavē māṇṭatāl tappiṉāṉ. 
cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ vaṭapulam ceṉṟu, cuṉāvicayari veṉṟu cuṇṇakikkuc ciliyeṭuttu mīṇṭāṉ. piṉ, ak kaṉaka vicayarip pāṇṭiyaṉ naṉmāṟaṉukkuk kāṭṭumpaṭi aṉuppiṉāṉ. avarkaḷit tuṟavi vēṭattil kaṇṭa naṉmāṟaṉ pōrkkaḷattil tōṟṟut tuṟavi vēṭam pūṇṭavaric ciṟippiṭittu vantatu tamiḻar paṇpukkup putumi yāṉatueṉṟāṉām! 
ivaṉ aracukkaṭṭilēṟa tāyamuṟiyiṉar ceyta cūḻcci kaḷiyum, kalakaṅkaḷiyum veṉṟu kālam tāḻttiyē muṭi cūṭṭik koṇṭāṉ, ivaṉ taliciṟanta vīraṉ eṉpatik kārikkaṇṇaṉār, marutaṉ iḷa nākaṉār mutaliya pulavarkaḷ pāṭiya puṟanāṉūṟṟup pāṭalkaḷāl aṟiyalām. ivaṉ ilavantikip paḷḷi allatu kaṭakāram eṉṉum māḷikic cōliyil tuñciṉāṉ.cāttaṉār pāṭiya pāṭaliṉ kīḻuḷḷa kuṟippil ivaṉ cittira māṭattil tuñciyavaṉ' eṉavum aṟiyappaṭu kiṟāṉ. 
taliyālaṅkāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa neṭuñceḻiyaṉ 
ivaṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ naṉmāṟaṉukkuppiṉ aracukkaṭṭil ēṟiyavaṉ. 'neṭuñceḻiyaṉ' eṉṉum variciyil ivaṉ mūṉṟām neṭuñceḻiyaṉ āvāṉ. ivaṉ ciṟanta pulavaṉākavum iruntāṉ, ivaṉ māṅkuṭi marutaṉāriṭam muṟippaṭi tamiḻ payiṉṟāṉ. taṉṉi etirttup pōriṭṭacōḻaṉiyum, cēraṉiyum, avarkaḷukkut tuṇiyāka vanta titiyaṉ, eḻiṉi, erumiyūraṉ, iruṅkōvēḷ, porunaṉ ākiya aintu aracarkaḷiyum taliyālaṅkāṉam eṉṉum ūril naṭanta pōril veṉṟu, 'taliyālaṅkāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa neṭuñceḻiyaṉ' āṉāṉ. iv veṟṟiyip pukaḻntu māṅkuṭi marutaṉār maturikkāñci' pāṭiṉār. nakkīrar, iṭikkuṉṟūrkkiḻār, kallāṭaṉār, potumpil kiḻār makaṉār veṇkaṇṇiyār ākiyōrum ivaṉ veṟṟiyip pukaḻntu pāṭiyuḷḷaṉar. 
piṟa veṟṟikaḷ 
taliyālaṅkāṉattu veṟṟikkuppiṉ vēḷeṉvi eṉpavaṉuṭiya miḻali nāṭṭiyum, muttāṟu eṉṉum ūriyum, aḻumpil vēḷ eṉpavaṉuṭiya aḻumpil eṉṉum palariyum veṉṟāṉ. koṅku nāṭṭuc cēra maṉṉaṉ yāṉikkaṇcēy māntarañcēral irumpoṟi eṉpavaṉip pōril veṉṟu ciṟippiṭittāṉ. piṉṉar ic cēraṉ payampuk kuḻiyil akappaṭṭa yāṉi taṉ muyaṟciyiṉāl kuḻiyiliruntu tappiōṭiyatu pōl ciṟiyiṉiṉṟu tappi ōṭiviṭṭāṉ. 
cōḻavēntaṉ kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ, cēraṉ yāṉikkaṇcēy māntarañ cēral irumpoṟiyi veṉṟāṉ. piṉṉar pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉmītu paṭiyeṭuttāṉ. kūṭalukkarukil avaṉ paṭikaḷ neruṅkiyatum neṭuñceḻiyaṉiṉ tāṉit talivaṉ mōkūrppaḻiyaṉ māṟaṉ avikaḷic citaṟaṭittāṉ. cōḻaṉ paṭikaḷip pōrkkaḷattilēyē viṭṭuviṭṭu ōṭiṉāṉ. avaṟṟit tāṉit talivaṉ kippaṟṟiṉāṉ. 
neṭuñceḻiyaṉ cēra nāṭṭiṉ mītu paṭiyeṭuttuk kuṭṭuvaṉ cēraliṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ makaṉ tuṟimukamākiya muciṟiyi muṟṟuki yiṭṭu, aṅkirunta teyvac ciliyik kavarntu vantāṉ. ivaṉ āṇṭa kālatti nammāl kaṇikka muṭiyavilli. 
ukkirap peruvaḻuti 
taliyālaṅkāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa neṭuñceḻiyaṉukkup piṟaku pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiyāṇṭavaṉ ukkirap peruvaḻuti āvāṉ. ivaṉip paṟṟi aṟivataṟku virivāṉa cāṉṟukaḷilli, ivaṉ cōḻanāṭṭuk kōṭṭikaḷi veṉṟāṉ. attakiya oru kōṭṭikāṉappēreyil eṉpatākum. eṉavē, ivaḷ "cāṉap pēreyil kaṭanta ukkirap peru kivaḻuti' eṉa aḻikkappaṭukiṟāṉ. ivaṉ ciṟanta pulavaṉum āvāṉ. akanāṉūṟṟiyum, naṟṟiṇiyiyum tokuppittāṉ. ivaṉ kālattil nallantuvaṉār, marutaṉ iḷanākaṉār mutaliya pulavarkaḷ iruntaṉar. kaṭic caṅkattil vīṟṟirunta pulavar nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmaril ivaṉum oruvaṉ. eṉavē, ivaṉ kaṭikkaḻakattiṉ puravalaṉākavum, pulavaṉākavum tikaḻntāṉ. kipi. 3ām nūṟṟāṇṭil iv vukkirap peruvaḻutiyiṉ kālattiltāṉ pāṇṭiya aracum, kaṭikkaḻakamum kulintaṉaveṉṟu karutap peṟukiṉṟatu. pāṇṭiya vēntaril ivaṉē kaṭiciyaracaṉ āvāṉ. ivaṉukkup piṉ pāṇṭiya nāṭṭik kaḷappirar kippaṟṟikkoṇṭa ṉar. kaḷappirar āṭciyi. āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭuvari nilittiruntatu. kipi. ēḻām nāṟṟāṇṭil mīṇṭum pāṇṭiyariṉ āṭci ēṟpaṭṭatu. 
(ī) kaḻakakkālak kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷ
kaḻakakkālattil cēranāṭu, cōḻanāṭu, pāṇṭiyanāṭu koṅku nāṭu, tuḷunāṭu, toṇṭināṭu aruvānāṭu eṉṟu tamiḻakam pala pakutikaḷāka aṟiyappaṭṭālum cēranāṭṭilum, koṅku nāṭṭilum cēra vēntar māṟimāṟiyum, orē camayattilum āṇṭatippōlavē piṟa muṭiyuṭi vēntarkaḷum tuḷu nāṭṭilum, toṇṭi nāṭṭilum āṇṭuḷḷaṉar. ākavē, yāryār ententa nāṭukaḷi evvep poḻutu āṇṭaṉar eṉpati variyaṟuttuk kūṟamuṭiyātu. āyiṉum, cērarip paṟṟiyaṟiyum pōtu cēranāṭṭiyāṇṭavar, koṅku nāṭṭiyāṇṭavar eṉappaṭittaṟiya ēntāka avvāṟu pirittuk koṇṭōm. uṇmiyil aḵtoru cariyāṉa muṟiyēyaṉṟu. kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷiṉ nāṭukaḷum, nāṭṭiṉ ellikaḷumkūṭa variyaṟuttuk kūṟamuṭiyātaviyē. āyiṉum ilakkiyaṅkaḷil kaṇṭavāṟu avarkaḷippaṟṟik kūṟalām. 
kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷ vaḷḷalkaḷākavum, peruvēntarkaḷip pōl ciṟappeytiyavarkaḷākavum, makkaḷiṉ matippip peṟṟavarkaḷāka vum tikaḻntārkaḷ. par. vi. ē. cimittiṉ kaṇakkuppaṭi caṅkakkālattil tōrāyamāka nūṟṟuyirupatukkum mēṟpaṭṭa kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷ irunta tākkūrappaṭukiṟatu. avar cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiyar koṭivaḻiyiṉariyum cērttuk kaṇakkiṭṭirukkiṟār. namatu kaṇakkiṉpaṭi ēṟattāḻa eḻupat tāṟu kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷē kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷil orucilarip paṟṟi maṭṭum kūṟuvōm. 
kaṭiyēḻu vaḷḷalkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyalit tokuttuk kūṟum peruñcittiraṉār, nattattaṉār ākiya pulavarkaḷ avvēḻu vaḷḷal kaḷiyum kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷākavē kāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. ākavē, kaṭiyēḻu vaḷḷalkaḷ eḻuvarum kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷē. paṟampu malip pakutiyi yāṇṭavaṉum, mullikkut tērntavaṉumāṉa pāri, maliyamāṉ nāṭu muḷḷūrp pakutiyiyāṇṭakāri, ceṉṉi malip pakutiyiyāṇṭari, kaṇmaramalip pakutiyiyāṇṭanaḷḷi, paḻaṉimalip pakutiyiyāṇṭa pēcuḷ, potiya malippakutiyi yāṇṭa āy, takaṭūrppakutiyiyāṇṭa atiyamāṉ ākiya kaṭiyēḻu vaḷḷalkaḷum caṅka kālakkuṟunila maṉṉar kaḷē. ivarkaḷ ovvoruvarum koṭic ceyalil pukaḻpeṟṟavarkaḷ. 
mutiramalippakutiyiyāṇṭa kumaṇaṉ, ciṟukuṭiyil vāḻnta paṇṇaṉ, valvār eṉṉum nakaril vāḻnta paṇṇaṉ, cantūr pakutiyil vāḻantatōyaṉ māṟaṉ, ōymānāṭṭu nalliyakkōṭaṉ, kōṭikkāṉal pakutiyiyāṇṭapaṇṇi, nāñcilnāṭṭu vaḷḷuvaṉ toṇṭi nāṭṭu iḷantiriyaṉ, potiyamalit titiyaṉ, karuvūriyaṭutta tāṉtōṉṟi malippakutiyiyāṇṭa tāmāṉ tōṉṟikkōṉ, cōḻanāṭṭup pūccāṟṟūril vāḻnta pārppaṉaṉ viṇṇattāyaṉ, vēṅkaṭamalip pakutiyiyāṇṭa ātaṉuṅkaṉ, karumpaṉūrp pakutiyi yāṇṭa curumpa tūrk kiḻāṉ, ōymāṉ nāṭṭu villiyātaṉ, maṉōkkattic cūḻnta pakutiyi yāṇṭa aviyaṉ, kutirimalip pakutiyiyāṇṭa piṭṭaṉ, teṉṉāṭṭuc ciṟukuṭiyeṉṉum ūril vāḻnta vāṇaṉ, irumpi eṉṉum ūril vāḻnta virāṉ, ampareṉṉumūril vāḻnta aruvatti, tarumaputtiraṉ mutaliya piṟa vaḷḷalkaḷum kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷē, 
potiyamalip pakutiyiyāṇṭatitiyaṉ, vākiyik kōnakara mākak koṇṭu vēṇāṭṭiyāṇṭa āyeyiṉaṉ, mōkūrppakutiyi yāṇṭa paḻiyaṉ māṟaṉ, kāviriyāṟu pāyum pōr eṉṉum karp pakutiyiyāṇṭa paḻiyaṉ, micūrppakutiyi yāṇṭa miyūrkkiḻāṉ, cellūrp pakutiyi yāṇṭa ātaṉ eḻiṉi, viḷaṅkil pakutiyiyāṇṭa cuṭavaṉ ākiyōr tamiḻakattiṉ mēlippakutiyil teṟkiliruntu vaṭakku variyilirunta kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷ āvar. vēṅkaṭattiyāṇṭa pulvi, cōṇāṭṭup pakutiyilirunta ārkkāṭṭiyāṇṭa aḻici , avaṉ makaṉ cēntaṉ, malliyiyāṇṭa kāriyāti, pacci maliyiyāṇṭa viccikkō , nīṭūrp pakutiyiyāṇṭevvi. koṭumpāḷūrppakutiyi yāṇṭa pulikaṭimāl ākiyōr vaṭakkiliruntu teṟkē nōkkit tamiḻakattiṉ kiḻakkup pakutiyilirunta kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷ āvar. 
naṉṉaṉ eṉpavaṉum kuṟunila maṉṉaṉē. ip peyaril nālvar iruntatāka aṟikiṟōm. avarkaḷ kōcar kuṭiyic cērnta pāḻiyit talinakarākak koṇṭu āṇṭa naṉṉaṉ, vēḷir kuṭiyic cērnta viyārit talinakarākak koṇṭu āṇṭa naṉṉaṉ vēṇmāṉ, āykuṭiyic cērnta paṟampumalip pakutiyiyāṇṭa naṉṉaṉ āy, cēykuṭiyic cērnta nākarkuṭi ceṅkaṇmā pakutiyiceṅkam yāṇṭa cēy naṉṉaṉ ākiyōr āvar. ivarkaḷil pāḻiyit talinakarākak koṇṭu āṇṭataṉṉōṭu kūṭṭuc cērntu, muṭiyuṭi vēntarōṭu pakimi koṇṭu etirttavarkaḷ aṟuvar. avarkaḷ ēṟṟi, atti, cuṅkaṉ, cuṭṭi , pūntuṟi puṉṟuṟi), cuṇiyaṉ eṉpōr āvar. ivarkaḷ tamiḻakattiṉ vaṭakkelli yōraṅkaḷil āṭci celutti vantaṉar. 
mūvēntarkaḷiṉ tuṇivarkaḷāy viḷaṅkiya palarum kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷē. ivarkaḷ ivvēntarkaḷukkut tāṉittalivar kaḷāyp payaṉpaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷil kuṟippiṭattakkavarkaḷ cōḻaṉukku utaviya paḻiyaṉum, pāṇṭiyaṉukkutaviya paḻiyaṉ māṟaṉ, nākaṉ, atikaṉ ākiyōrum, teṉṉavaṉukku utaviya kantaṉ, paṇṇi eṉpōrum, vāṉavaṉukku utaviya piṭṭaṉ, aṟuki ākiyōrum āvar. maṟṟum cōḻariṭattil 'ēṉāti' paṭṭam peṟṟa ēṉāti tirukkiḷḷi, cōḻiya ēṉāti tirukkuṭṭuvaṉ, maliyamāṉ cōḻiya ēṉāti, tirukkaṇṇaṉ mutaliyōrum kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷip pōl iruntaṉar. 
"kiḻār' eṉṉum peyar peṟṟirunta palarum makkaḷiṉ cel vākkip peṟṟuc ciṟappuṭaṉ vāḻntaṉar. ivarkaḷi maṉṉariṉ aluvalāḷar eṉak kūṟuvativiṭa makkaḷ talivar eṉakkūṟuvatu mikavum poruttamāṉatu. āvūr kiḻār, karuvūr kiḻār, kūṭalūr kiḻār, cellūr kiḻār, ciṟukuṭikiḻāṉ paṇṇaṉ, vallārkiḻāṉ paṇṇaṉ, curumpalūr kiḻāṉ, miyūr kiḻāṉ, ampar kiḻāṉ, īntarar kiḻāṉ,olliyūr kiḻāṉ, koṇkāṉaṅ kiḻāṉ mallikiḻāṉ, nāli kiḻavaṉ, ilaṅki kiḻavōṉ, nīṭūr kiḻavōṉ, ampar kiḻavōṉ, vēṅkaṭaṅ kiḻavōṉ, vallaṅ kiḻavōṉ, kāvirik kiḻavaṉ, poṟiyāṟṟuk kiḻāṉ, malik kiḻavaṉ, varikkiḻavaṉ mutaliyōr mukāmiyāṉavarkaḷ āvar. 
(u) kaḻakakkāla āṭcimuṟi
kaḻakakkālattil tamiḻakattiyāṇṭa cērar, cōḻar, pāṇṭiyar ākiya muṭiyuṭi vēntarkaḷuṭiyaṉavum, maṟṟum kuṟunila maṉṉarka ḷuṭiyaṉavumāṉa āṭcimuṟiyē īṇṭuk kaḻakak kāla āṭci muṟi yākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. mūvēntarēyaṉṟit tōrāyamāka 120 kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷum kaḻakak kālattil āṇṭaṉar. āyiṉum, tamiḻkūṟu nal lulakattil ivarkaḷ naṭattiya āṭcimuṟi orē mātiriyākavē iruntatu. 
piṟaṅkaṭiyurimi 
paṇṭikkālattil piṟanāṭukaḷil tōṉṟiyatippōlavē tamiḻakattilum aracappatavi tōṉṟiyatu. mūttavaṉ aracaṉāka vēṇṭumeṉṟa marapuvaḻimuṟi nīṭittatu. tantikkup piṉ mūtta makaṉ, avaṉukkup piṉ aṭutta mūttamakaṉ eṉṉum muṟiyilē kaḻakakkāl maṉṉarkaḷ aracuk kaṭṭil ēṟiṉar. ōr aracaṉukku iruvēru maṉiviyarukkup piṟanta mūṉṟu piḷḷikaḷ iruntālum avarkaḷiṉ akavippaṭi mūtta vaṉukkup piṉ iḷiyavaṉ eṉṟa muṟiyil muṭicūṭik koṇṭaṉar. piṟaṅkaṭiyurimippaṭi mūtta makaṉukkuppiṉ avaṉuṭiya mūtta makaṉ! aracurimi illi . karikālaṉit tavira vēṟu enta maṉṉaṉum makkaḷāl tērn teṭukkappaṭṭatākat teriyavilli. mūttavaṉ āḷumpōtu iḷiyavarkaḷ piṟapakutikaḷukku iḷavaracarkaḷāyiruntu mūttōṉukku uṟutuṇiyāyiruppar. itaṉāl avarkaḷukku. āḷum nāṭṭup pakuti kaḷik koṇṭē virutuppeyarkaḷum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. tāṉit tali varkaḷ taṉinakaraṅkaḷil vāḻntaṉar. mōkūr eṉṟa ūril vāḻnta cōḻar tāṉittalivaṉ “paḻiyaṉ" eṉṟum, cēramaṉṉariṉ tāṉittalivaṉ 'paḻiyaṉ māṟaṉ' eṉṟum aḻikkappeṟṟaṉar. potuvāka ivarkaḷi *vayavar perumakaṉ' vīrarkaḷiṉ talivaṉ) eṉṟē aḻippar. ittakiya vayavar perumāṉākavum iḷavaracarkaḷ iruppatuṇṭu. nāmaṟintavari kaḻakak kālattil makaḷir aracāṇṭatākac cāṉṟilli. pāṇṭiya nāṭ ṭil taṭātakip pirāṭṭiyār āṇṭatākak kūṟappaṭuvatu toltic ceytiyē. 
aracavi 
pāṇṭiya, cōḻavēntar taṅkaḷi matikkulam cantirakulam), katirk kulam cūriyakulam ākiyavaṟṟic cārntavareṉṟum, itikācavīrarkaḷiṉ vaḻivantavareṉṟum kūṟikkoḷvatu akkāla vaḻakku muṭicūṭṭu viḻā naṭakkumpōtu mūttōr aviyiṉarum, piṟarum kūṭi am maṉṉaṉi ēṟpatāy oppuvar. maṉṉaṉum aṟavaḻi naṭantu kuṭimakkaḷit taṉ makkaḷ pōl kāppāṉ, āyiṉum avaṉukku aṟivuri kūṟap pulavarkaḷum, amiccarkaḷum, pūcārikaḷum iruppar. potuvāka aracaviyil iruppavar kuṭi, kalvi, vāymi, tūymi, naṭunilimi, taṉṉalamiṉmi ākiya paṇpukaḷ niṟinta cāṉṟōrākattikaḻvar. ivarkaḷ aracaṉukku aṭikkaṭi aṟatti valiyuṟutti nayaṉmiyiṉ kāvalaṉāka irukkumpaṭi ceyvar. aracaṉ aṉṟāṭam aracaviyik kūṭṭi, kāṭcik keḷiya ṉākik kuṭimakkaḷiyum aracaviyil amarttik kuṟikēṭṭu muṟi vaḻaṅkuvāṉ. ayalakat tūtuvarkaḷum, ayalaka viruntiṉarum aracaviyil varavēṟkappaṭṭaṉar. tāymoḻiyām tamiḻik kaṟṟaṟinta aṟiñar perumakkaḷi vāḻttiyum, pōṟṟiyum paricil vaḻaṅkiyum, aracaviyil camamāka irukkiyaḷittum, perumippaṭuttum marapuvaḻakkam tamiḻaracar yāvariṭattilum iruntatu. aimperuṅkuḻuvum eṇpērāyamum aracamippiṉ mukāmiyuṟuppukaḷākum. 
ki.pi. 7 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻunta tivākara nikaṇṭil amiccar, purōkitar, tāṉittalivar, tūtuvar, cāraṇar ākiyōr aimperuṅ kuḻuvil iṭampeṟṟiruntaṉar eṉṟum, karaṇattiyalavar, karuma vitikaḷ, cuṉacuc cuṟṟam, kaṭikkāppāḷar, nakaramāntar, paṭit talivar, yāṉi vīrar, kutiri maṟavar ākiyōr eṇpērāyatti liruntaṉar eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. aimperuṅ kuḻuviṉ mantira moḻikaḷi aracaṉ ēṟṟāṉ. itu makkaḷālum ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭa amippākumeṉṟu oru cārār karutukiṉṟaṉar. itu maṉṉaṉ taṉṉicci pōl māṟāmal taṭuttu niṟuttum kuḻuveṉpatu cimittiṉ karuttākum, eṇpērāyam araca ṉuṭiya āṭci muṟikku uṟutuṇiyāka niṉṟa ceyaṟkuḻuvāka irun tirukkalām. 
ivarkaḷit tavira tiṟampaṭitta, nuṇṇaṟivuḷḷa amiccar kaḷikkoṇṭa kuḻuvoṉṟum iruntatu. aracaṉi neṟippaṭuttavum, ceyalpaṭāmal taṭuttu niṟuttavum inta amiccaravikku atikāram iruntatu. ciṟanta amiccarkaḷukku virutup peyarkaḷum vaḻaṅkap paṭṭaṉa. 
amiccarkaḷukku aṭuttapaṭiyāka araciyalil uṟuppāṇmi peṟṟōr tūtuvarkaḷē. ivarkaḷ aracaṉiṉ uḷḷamāka iruntu ataṉi etirolittaṉar. ayalakam ceṉṟu aracariṉ karuttukaḷik kūṟavum, ayalacu aracariṉ patilkaḷip peṟṟu vantu kūṟavumāṉa mukāmic ceyal cuḷil īṭupaṭṭaṉar. ivarkaḷilum mūvakip piriviṉar iruntaṉar. pulavarkaḷ tāmē perumpālāṉa camayaṅkaḷil tūtu ceṉṟa nikaḻcci cuḷiyum pārkkiṟōm. 
oṟṟar eṉpōr tūtuvariṉum vēṟupaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ vēvu allatu oṟṟup paṇiyic ceytaṉar. aracaṉiṉ kaṇṇākavum, kātākavum iruntu ceyalpaṭṭa. oṟṟarkaḷ pakivarip paṟṟiya ceytikaḷi muṉkūṭṭiyē terintu aṟivippar. ivarkaḷ kamukkak kuṟikaḷik kiyāṇṭaṉar. oṟṟarkaḷ mūvakippaṭuvar eṉṟum, oruvaṉ kūṟiyati maṟṟa iruvarik koṇṭu uṟuti ceyta piṉṉarē aracaṉ ceyalpaṭavēṇṭumeṉṟum vaḷḷuvar kūṟukiṟār. tūtuvaṉi araca ṉukku īṭāka matittu naṭatta vēṇṭum. āṉāl, oṟṟaṉik koṉṟu viṭalām eṉpatu kaḻākkāla marapu. 
paṭi 
paṭi, koṭi, ariyaṇi, vāṉ, veṇcāmaram (kavari), muracu mutaliyavi araciṉ iṉṟiyamiyāta poruḷkaḷāy matikkap peṟṟaṉa, paṭiyaṟṟa aracaṉ niliyaṟṟa kumiḻiyākak karutappaṭṭāṉ. 'vallāṉ vakuttatē vaḻi" eṉṉum kōṭpāṭu nilaviya ak kālattil paṭi yillāmal enta nāṭum, araciyalum iyaṅka muṭiyātu. ākavē, tamiḻaka maṉṉarkaḷum paṭikaḷi vittē makkaḷik kāttum, piṟa nāṭukaḷik kavarntum avarkaḷukku ākkamaḷittum vantaṉar. yāṉi, kutiri tēr paṭikaḷum, kālāḷ paṭikaḷum ovvoru maṉṉariṭamum iruntaṉa. peruvēntarkaḷiṭam kappaṟpaṭiyum iruntatu. tamiḻ maṟavar yāvarum vēṟupāṭiṉṟip paṭiyil cērttukkoḷḷappaṭṭaṉar. vaṭapulatti viruntatip pōl kuṟippiṭṭa cattiriyak kulattavar maṭṭumē paṭiyil irukka vēṇṭum eṉṟa neṟiyit tamiḻakap paṭiyil kāṇamuṭiya villi. aracaṉatu meykkāval paṭi avaṉuṭiya uṭaluṟuppukaḷip pōl ceyalpaṭṭatu. 
paṭivīrarkaḷ paṭam kuppāyam, meymmaṟi, vaṭṭuṭi mutaliya āṭikaḷiyum, vīrakkaḻaliyum, kāluṟiyum aṇintaṉar. vīram ivarkaḷiṉ mūccākavum, vīrap paṇpu ivarkaḷiṉ kurutiyākavu miruntatu. pōrkkaḷattil māḷvōr vīrattuṟakkam aṭivareṉṟa kōṭpāṭ ṭil ivarkaḷ nampikkiyuṭiyavarkaḷ.vīrappiḷḷikaḷip peṟṟatāl tāymārkaḷ perumita maṭintaṉar. peṟṟa vayiṟṟip pulikkuki yeṉṟum, vīraṉip puliyeṉṟum kūṟiṉar. pōril māṇṭavarukku vīrakkalnāṭṭi, malarcūṭṭi vaṇaṅkiṉar. avar piṟaṅkaṭiyari vīrar vaḻivantōr eṉap pōṟṟiṉar. vīrarkaḷukku aṉṟāṭam muṟiyāṉa payiṟcikaḷ aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa. ivarkaḷ piṟaviyilēyē vīrarkaḷāyi runtaṉar. muṉvitta kālip piṉvikkātavarum, mārpil ampupaṭṭu māḷpavarum, taṉ talivaṉukkut taṉṉiyē paṭippavarum perum vīrarkaḷākak karutappaṭṭaṉar. nāṭṭuppaṟṟum, ceñcōṟṟuk kaṭṭaṇumē ivarkaḷiṉ kaṭami varalāṟākum. 
kollarkaḷāl vaṭittaḷikkappaṭṭa vēl, vāḷ, kōl mutaliya tākkum paṭikaḷiyum, kēṭayam, kavacam mutaliya kākkum paṭikaḷiyum ikkāla vīrarkaḷ kiyāṇṭatāka aṟikiṟōm. vilvīrarum, vēl vīrarum ik kālattil pukārpaṭap pēcappaṭṭaṉar. tōlālāṉa nakiyuṟikaḷum, kāluṟikaḷum iruntaṉa, vāḷ, vēl, ampu mutaliyaṉa vikkavum tōluṟikaḷ iruntaṉa. yāṉi kaḷukkum, kutirikaḷukkum kūṭattākkum paṭikaḷum, kākkum paṭikaḷum poruttappaṭṭaṉa. palavakippaṭṭa vāḷkaḷum, vēl kaḷum paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷik kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷi liruntu aṟikiṟōm. 
pōr 
pōrmēṟ celvataṟku muṉ pōr vīrarkaḷ puṉita nāḷil paṭikaḷi nīrāṭṭi, pūccūṭik kālakkaṇakkar kuṟitta nērattil pōrukkup puṟappaṭuvar. puṟappaṭumuṉ vīrarkaḷum, aracaṉum pakivari veṉṟu tirumpuvōm - iṉṟēl cettu maṭivōm' eṉac cūḷurippar. itaṉi 'vañciṉam' kūṟutaleṉṟu aḻippar. vāḷ, kuṭi. muracu ākiyavaṟṟuṭaṉ pōr muracam koṭṭa aṇivakuppu ūrvalam naṭakkum, vīrarkaḷ veḷḷāṭiyaṇintu pōrukkup puṟappaṭuvar. taṅkaḷiṉ paṭiyeṭuppi muṉkūṭṭiyē pakiyaracaṉukku aṟivippar. mūppaṭin tōr, kuḻantikaḷ, ūṉamuṟṟōr, iyalātōr, peṇṭir mutaliyōri yum, āpacukkaḷiyum, pārppaṉariyum, naṉmakkaṉiyum veḷiyēṟum paṭi ceyvar. maṟavaṉ maṟavaṉōṭutāṉ mōta vēṇṭum eṉpatu marapup paṇpu. paṭivīrarkaḷtāṉittalivarkaḷip piṉtoṭarntu celvar, talivarkaḷum, aracaṉum yāṉi mītamarntu meykkāppāḷaruṭaṉ celvar, avarkaḷ amarntuḷḷa yāṉikaḷiṉ mītuḷḷa ampārikaḷiṉ mēl koṭikaḷ paṟakkum. ākavē, pakivar paṭit talivariyum aracaṉiyum toliviliruntē aṭiyāḷam kaṇṭukoḷvar. 
pōr, perumpālum kōṭikkālattil camataḷattil naṭakkum. iruvaruṭiya ellikaḷik kuṟikkum kōṭukaḷ allatu iyaṟki ellikaḷ amikkappaṭum. kuṟippiṭṭa nērappaṭiyē pōr toṭaṅkum. tol kāppiyattil pōriṉ aintu nilikaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. avi veṭci, vañci, uḻiñi, tumpi, vāki eṉpaṉavām. pakivaṉ nāṭṭit tākkum muṉ avaṉuṭiya kālnaṭikaḷik kavarvatu vaḻakkam. itaṟku āniri kavartal" eṉṟu peyar, āniri kavarvōr veṭci māliyic cūṭic celvar ivarkaḷ cakuṉam pārttuc celvar. taṟkāppu muyaṟcil īṭupaṭṭavarum, paṭiyeṭuttuc celvōrum vañci māliyic cūṭiṉar. kōṭṭiyi muṟṟukiyiṭuvōr uḻiñi māliyiyum, muṟṟukiyi uṭittu veṟṟi peṟuvōr tumpi māliyiyum cūṭuvar. potuvākap pōrk kaḷattil veṟṟi peṟṟōr 'vāki' māliyic cūṭuvar. potuvāka oru nāṭṭimaṇṇik cuvarum nōkkattuṭaṉ toṭukkum pōrukku veṭcippōr' eṉṟum, paḻivāṅkum nōkkattōṭu allatu aṭakkiyāḷum nōkkattōṭu veṟṟikku maṭṭum toṭukkumpōrukku vākip pōreṉṟum peyar. 
pōril puṟamutukiṭṭu ōṭum pakivariyum, āṭiyiḻantu niṟpōriyum, mēyccal nilattil vāḻvōriyum, nīril pāyntōri yum, paṭikkalaṅkaḷiṉṟit tavippōriyum, puṇpaṭ ṭōriyum, mutiyōr, peṇṭir, kuḻantikaḷ mutaliyōriyum pōrkkaḷattil etirttup pōriṭamāṭṭār. muṟṟukikku uṭpaṭṭavaṉ kōḻittaṉat tāl uḷḷirukkac ceyvatu mikat tāḻvāka matikkappaṭṭatu. pōrti talivaṉ allatu aracaṉ pōrkkaḷattil iṟantāl avaṉuṭiya paṭikaḷ toṭarntu pōriṭā. pōril kiyāḷappaṭṭavalakkāraṅkaḷ, uttikaḷ mutaliyaṉa paṟṟiyum aṟikiṟōm. pōrt toṭarntu naṭakkum pōtu vīrarkaḷum, talivarkaḷum, aracaṉum pācaṟikaḷamittu avaṟṟil taṅkuvar. ap pācaṟikaḷiṉ amippup paṟṟiya virivāṉa viḷakkaṅkaḷ puṟanāṉūṟṟiliruntu aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ip pācaṟikaḷ ellā ēntukkaḷnaṭaṉum amikkappaṭṭu ōr ūrip pōlavē irukkum, itaṉāl pācaṟikaḷ aṭaṅkiya iruppik kaṭṭūr' eṉṟaṉar. aracaṉatu pācaṟiyiṉ kūriyil avaṉuṭiya koṭi paṟakkum. aracaṉiṉ pācaṟi ceṅkaṟkaḷāl kaṭṭappeṟṟup pal aṟikaḷākap pakukkappaṭṭiruntatu.' pakalil maṭṭumē pōr naṭakkum, iravil ōyvu koḷḷumpōtum aracaṉ vīrarkaḷik kaṇṭu ūkkamūṭṭuvāṉ; maṟunāḷ naṭakkappōkum pōriyeṇṇiyavāṟu viḻittiruppāṉ. veṟṟiyaṭintavar viḻāk kōlam pūṇṭu āṭi makiḻvar. vīrarkaḷ takkavāṟu paricaḷikkappaṭuvar. matuvarunti, pēruṇṭimaṇ uṇavu uṇṭu, tuṇaṅkiyāṭik kaḷippar; koḷḷip poruḷkaḷ koṇṭip poruḷ, vīrarkaḷukkup pakirn taḷikkappaṭum; cilapōtu kuṭimakkaḷukkum itu pakirntaḷikkappaṭum; vīrarkaḷukkup poṉmalar paricaḷikkum camayaṅkaḷum uṇṭu. tōlvi kaṇṭavarkaḷ palvēṟu tuṉpaṅkaḷukkum āḷāvar. avarkaḷi iḻivāka naṭattuvatum uṇṭu. kiyil vilaṅku māṭṭutal, taliyil neyyūṟṟutal - mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkum āḷāvar. kaṉakavicayar tuṟavi vēṭamiṭṭu ōṭiyum, viṭātu piṭittuk kaṇṇakiyiṉ ciliyic cumakkac ceytatu īṇṭu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. pakivariṉ kāval maratti veṭṭi, avaṉuṭiya uvaḷakattār antappuṟattup peṇkaḷiṉ kūntali iḻuttu, kayiṟu tirittu, akcayirāl ammarattiṉ aṭippākattik kaṭṭiyāṉiyik koṇṭu iḻuttu vantu pōr muracam ceyvatum, tōṟṟa aracaṉiṉ kōṭṭi kaḷiyum, piṟa kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷiyum iṭittu niravik kaḻuti kaḷik kaṭṭi ēr uḻutalum, ūrkaḷit tīyiṭṭuk koḷuttuvatum ak kāla vaḻakka mākum. 
pōrkkaḷaṅkaḷilum pulavarkaḷ nuḻintu irutarattārukkum aṟivuri kūṟip pōrit taṭuttuḷḷaṉar. itip pōlavē pōrit toṭaṅkāmal kāttatum uṇṭu. pakivaṉ paṇintāl maṉṉittu avaṉ nāṭṭiyum, uṭimikaḷiyum avaṉukkē tiruppit taruvatum uṇṭu. pōrilum aṟavaḻiyē kaṭipiṭikkappaṭṭatu, 
muṟi vaḻaṅkutal 
paṭikkum nayaṉmikkum nītikkum) aracaṉē talivaṉ. araca ṉuṭiya kolumaṇṭapamē aṟanītimaṉṟam, avaṉuṭiya aviyil evarum muṟiyiṭṭut tīrppup peṟalām. perumpālāṉa cuḻacukkāl maṉṉarkaḷ paṉṉūṟ paṇṭitarkaḷāyiruntatāl nīti nūlkaḷiṉpaṭiyē tīrppu vaḻaṅkuvar. maṉaccāṉṟē avarkaḷiṉ tīrppukku aḷavukōl. āyariṉum, aṟivuri kūṟa aṟiñarkaḷum iruntaṉar, avarkaḷik kalakkāmalē aracaṉ tīrppu vaḻaṅkiyatum uṇṭu. āṉāl aracaṉ illāmal aviyiṉar tīrppu aḷippatilli. iṟivaṉic cāṉṟiṭṭu vaḻakkukaḷ ucāvappaṭṭaṉa. aracaṉ nayaṉmiyil piḻiceytāl nāṭṭil paruvamaḻi poykkum. kaṟpuṭiya makaḷir arukuvar; tavam keṭum eṉṟu makkaḷ nampiṉar. nayaṉmiyil piḻi ceyta aracaṉ vaḻittōṉṟalkaḷi naṉmakkaḷ matiyār, pulavarkaḷ pāṭamāṭṭārkaḷ.' aracaṉ tāṉē kuṟṟavāḷiyeṉatterintālum taṉakkuttāṉē taṇṭaṉi yaḷittuk koḷvāṉ. kuṟṟavāḷi taṉ makkaḷāyiṉum, uṟaviṉarāyiṉum allatu naṇparkaḷāyiṉum tappāmal taṇṭippāṉ. koṭukkal, vāṅkal, uṭaṉpaṭikki, oppantaṅkaḷ mutaliya poruḷiyal kuṟṟaṅkaḷum, aracurimikaḷukku aḻivutēṭal, pārppaṉaṉik kollutal, āppaka) maṭiyaṟuttal, karukkalippu, mānta uyirkkoli, mutaliya peruṅ kuṟṟaṅkaḷum ak kālattil naṭantaṉa. piṉkūṟappaṭṭa payaṅkarak kuṟṟaṅ cuḷukkuk kolittaṇṭaṉi taliyi veṭṭutal, yāṉik kāvil iṭṭuk kollutal mutaliyaṉa) vitikkappaṭṭaṉa. piṟaṉil viḻiyum kuṟṟattiṟkum, māṟṟāḷ kaṇavaṉi virumpum kuṟṟattiṟkum uṟuppu kaḷic citikkum taṇṭaṉiyum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. tiruṭṭuk kuṟṟattiṟ kuttaṇṭamum, ciṟittaṇṭaṉiyum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. kappalil tuṭupput taḷḷutal, kōyilukku viḷakkēṟṟutal, potuppaṇikaḷic ceytal mutaliya taṇṭaṉikaḷum vitikkappaṭṭaṉa. poṉ, yāṉi, kutiri, māṭu, āṭu mutaliyavaṟṟi iḻappīṭākap peṟum taṇṭaṉikaḷum vaḻaṅkap paṭṭaṉa. 
- kari cāṭcikaḷ ucāvappaṭuvatum, pāmpukkuṭattil kiviṭumpaṭi ceytu kuṟṟavāḷiyi aṟitalum uṇṭu terintē cāṉṟu kūṟa maṟuppava rum, poyc cāṉṟu kūṟuvōrum taṇṭaṉi peṟṟaṉar. kātalittuk kiviṭṭa oruvaṉip piṭittu aviyārē tirumaṇam ceyvitta nikaḻcciyum uṇṭu. aracukkuc celutta vēṇṭiya vari kaḷic celuttāta varkaḷum taṇṭaṉip peṟṟaṉar. kaḻakakkālattil ciṟikaḷ niliyāṉa kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷākavē iruntaṉa. ciṟiyil kuṟṟavāḷikaḷ toṭari caṅkilira yāl piṇikkap paṭṭiruntaṉar. kuṟṟavāḷikaḷukku nīr koṭukkāmalum, uṇaviṭāmalum, cittiravati ceyyappaṭuvatuṇṭu. āyiṉum, avarkaḷi nōyāḷikaḷippōl aṉpāka naṭatta vēṇṭumeṉpatu akkāla neṟi. 
nakaraṅkaḷum mārkaḷum 
nakaraṅkaḷilum ūrkaḷilum taṉittaṉi maṉṟaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. ivi taṅkaḷukkuṭpaṭṭa niruvākattiyum, muṟivaḻaṅkum tiṭṭaṅkaḷiyum kavaṉittaṉa. perumpālāṉa ūr maṉṟaṅkaḷ marattaṭiyilum, kuḷak kariyilum allatu potukkaṭṭaṭattilum kūṭiṉa. oḻukkam, kalvi, pakti ākiyavaṟṟi kāril vaḷarkkum talimiyirukkiyē inta harmaṉ naṅkaḷ. ivaṟṟil akavi mutirntōrum, kalvi kēḷvikaḷil ciṟantō rum paṅkēṟṟaṉar. nāṭu kōṭṭaṅkaḷākavum, ūṉarkaḷākavum pirikkappaṭ ṭiruntatu. kōṭṭaṅkaḷ iḷavaracarkaḷāl iḷaṅkō) āḷappaṭṭaṉa. tuṟimukappaṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ - vaṇika nakaraṅkaḷ, kōṭṭat talinakaraṅkaḷ, aracaṉiṉ kōnakaram, paṭittalivar vāḻnta nakaraṅkaḷ eṉṟu nakaraṅkaḷ palavakiyāy amintu nakara vāḻkki ciṟantu kāṇappaṭṭatu. ivi akaḻiyālum cūḻappaṭṭiruntaṉa. araṇmaṉi kōyil' eṉappaṭṭatu. itaṉit taṉikkāvalarkaḷ kāttaṉar. ivarkaḷ porunar, pulavar, paraṇar, pārppaṉar ākiyōri maṭṭumē taṭiyiṉṟi uṭcella icivar. āṉāl, araciyāriṉ irukkikku araṇmaṉiyic cērntavar tavira piṟa āṭavarcella iciyār. araṇmaṉikkuḷḷēyē uvaḷakam iruntatu. itilirunta aḻakiya makaḷir urimi makaḷir' eṉappaṭṭaṉar. 
varuvāy 
kaḻakakkālattil āṭciyic cevvaṉē naṭatta varuvāy tēvip paṭṭatu. itaṟkākap pala vitikaḷiṉ varikaḷ taṇṭappaṭṭaṉa. koṭuppō rukkut tuṉpam ēṟpaṭātavāṟum, avarkaḷē maṉamuvantu koṭukku māṟum varittaṇṭal ceytaṉar. viḷiccalil āṟil oru paṅku variyākac celuttappaṭṭatu. cila camayaṅkaḷil varittaḷḷupaṭi ceytalum uṇṭu. aracaṉukku nilavariyit tavira ulku vari, pōril kiṭitta koṇṭip poruḷ, 'ciṟṟaracarkaḷum, tamakku aṭaṅkiyāḷvōrum celuttiya tiṟikaḷ, urimiyaṟṟu niṟkum cottukaḷ mutaliyaṉavum varuvāyāka vantaṉ.vāṇipam ceyvōr taṉippaṭṭa muṟiyil vaṇikavaricelutti ṉar. nayaṉminīti maṉṟaṅkaḷil vitikkappaṭum taṇṭaṅkaḷum, ēṟṟumati, iṟakkumatip poruḷkaḷiṉ mītu vitikkappaṭṭa varikaḷum, aṉpaḷippāka aḷikkappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷum varuvāykaḷē. 
aḷavikaḷ 
kaḻakakkālattil nīṭṭalaḷavi, mukattalaḷavi, eṭuttalaḷavi, eṇṇal aḷavi ākiyaviyum vaḻakkattil iruntaṉa. cuḻiccikkāy (cuḻaṅku eṉpatu eṭi aḷavākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu. toḷḷāyiram, āyiram, nūṟāyiram, veḷḷam, āmpal, tāmari mutaliya eṇṇap peyarkaḷum, kāṇi, muntiri mutaliya piṉṉaṅ kaḷum vaḻakkil iruntaṉa. kaṉṉattik kuṟiccup pāḻ' eṉṟa col payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. mā, vēli mutaliyaṉa nila aḷavik kuṟittaṉa. ampaṇam eṉpatu mukatta vaḷaviyākum. itu oru marakkālukkuc camamākalām. taṅkam tulāk kōliṉāl niṟukkappaṭṭatu. atu 'cā' eṉappaṭṭatu tāṭi' eṉpatu eṭiyil mikac ciṟiya aḷavāka irukkalām, kā, kāṉam, poṉ mutaliyaṉa poṉṉiṉ eṭiyik kuṟittaṉa. ivipoṟkācukaḷākavum irukkalām. ēṉeṉil kaḻakak kālattil tamiḻakattuṭaṉ vāṇipam ceyta urōmar, poṟkācukaḷiyē kiyāṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. ākavē, ivarka ḷum poṟkācukaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiyirukkalām, ākap paṇṭamāṟṟa muṟikkum mēmpaṭṭa kācup paḻakkattiyuṭiya ciṟanta nākarikattik kaḻakakkālat tamiḻarkaḷ peṟṟiruntaṉar. - 
(ū) kaḻakakkālap poruḷiyalum vāṇikamum
vēḷāṇmi 
kaḻakakkālak kumukāyattil ēṟṟat tāḻvukaḷ iruppatik kaṇṭōm. avarkaḷ vāḻnta iṭaṅkaḷum ceyta toḻilkaḷumē iv vaki ēṟṟat tāḻvukaḷukkuk kāraṇiyaṅkaḷāyamintaṉa. vēḷāṇmi ik kālattil ciṟantu viḷaṅkiya toḻil, intu uyirnāṭiyāṉatum ākum. ūrppuṟattu makkaḷēyaṉṟi nakaraṅkaḷil vāḻnta makkaḷil oru cārārum it toḻil ceytaṉar. ittoḻilukku iṉṟiyamiyāta māṭukaḷip peṟṟiruntaṉar. vikkōlāl vēynta vīṭukaḷilē vāḻntārkaḷ eṉpati marutanila vāḻkkiyāl aṟikiṟōm. ivarkaḷiṭam irunta kāḷikaḷ, kalappikaḷ, ivaṟṟiṟkāṉa koṭṭilkaḷ mutaliya vaṟṟik koṇṭu oru kalappi uḻavik koṇṭavarum, pala kalappi uḻavik koṇṭavarkaḷum iruntaṉar eṉa aṟikiṟōm. maruta nilattu uṟavumuṟi ēṟakkuṟiya iṉṟiya tamiḻnāṭṭu uḻavu muṟiyip pōlavē iruntatu. cēṟṟil nāṟṟu naṭuvatu, puḻutiyil vitippatu, aṟuttal, aṭittal, kuvittal yāvum iṉṟupōl aṉṟum naṭantaṉa. 'veṇṇel" cen nel' eṉṟu palavakiyāṉa nel vakikaḷum payiriṭappeṟṟaṉa. karumpup payirpaṟṟiyum, karumpāli paṟṟiyum aṟikiṟōm. nelli yaṟuttavuṭaṉ uḷuntuppayirum piṉṉar curumpum payiriṭappeṟṟaṉa. nañcikku āṟu, kuḷaṅkaḷiliruntu nīrp pāyccappaṭṭatu. maruta nilattil perumpālum naṉceyp payirkaḷum piṟa nilaṅkaḷil puṉceyp payirkaḷum payiriṭap peṟṟaṉa, varaku, tiṉi, eḷ, avari mutaliya puṉceyta tavacaṅ kaḷum, malinellumivaṉamtōri eṉappaṭum oruvaki nellum, iñci, aiyavi kaṭuku, kavalik kiḻaṅku mutaliyaviyum kuṟiñci mulli nilaṅ kaḷil viḷivikkappeṟṟaṉa. karumpum, vāḻiyum mulliyilum viḷin taṉa. mūṅkil nellum, nāvaṟkaṉiyum, uyavik koṭiyum, kūvik kiḻaṅkum, mā, palā, teṉṉi mutaliyaṉavum iyaṟkiyāka viḷintaṉa. kōṭikkālattil oru vakip parutti payiriṭappeṟṟatu. vēḷāṇmi yil īṭupaṭṭōr ōraḷavu taṉṉiṟivōṭu vāḻntaṉar. āyiṉum iṉṟupōl aṉṟum ivarkaḷiṉ allal tīravilli. 
āyar toḻil 
vēḷāṇmiyiyaṭuttuk kaḻakakkālattil āṭu, māṭu vaḷarttal oru mukāmittoḻilāka iruntatu. māṭukaḷ vēḷāḷariṉ kūṭṭāḷikaḷ, āṭukaḷ pālukkum iṟiccikkum piṟavaṟṟiṟkum payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. āpacu), kāḷi, erumi, nāku mutaliya māṭukaḷiṉ ciṟappukaḷum, kuṟumpi , varuṭi mutaliya āṭukaḷiṉ ciṟappukaḷum, ivaṟṟi vaḷarkkum muṟikaḷum piṟavum aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. 
piṟa toḻilāḷarkaḷ 
vēḷāḷarukku uṟutuṇiyāyirunta caṅkaṟuppōr, maṇikaḷi tuḷippōr, poṉṉaki ceyvōr, poṉṉi māṟṟuk kāṇpōr, tuṇi, cempu, curāli(cāmpirāṇi) mutaliyaṉa viṟpōr, ōviyar 
mutalāṉa yāvariyum cummiyar eṉṟē maturikkāñci kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. kollaṉum, taccaṉum vēḷāṇmikkum, vīṭu kaṭṭutalukkum, tēr ceytalukkum, araṇmaṉi yamittalukkumāka makkaḷ nākarikattiṉ mutukelumpāy niṉṟārkaḷ. karuvikaḷum, paṭikaḷum ivarkaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. ākavē, ivarkaḷip paṟṟic caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ palapaṭap pēcukiṉṟaṉa. cempu, veḷḷi ākiyavaṟṟālum ēṉaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. kuyavar maṇpāṇṭam ceytaṉar. poṟkollar nakikaḷ ceytaṉar. parutti, paṭṭu āṭikaḷi neytavarkaḷip paṟṟiyum, kacaṅkāta āṭikaḷi neytavarkaḷip paṟṟiyum aṟikiṟōm, eli mayirik koṇṭu tāvari kampaṉ) āṭikaḷ neyyappaṭṭaṉa. paruttiyip patappaṭutti nāṟṟal peṇkaḷukkum, neytal āṇkaḷukkum uriya parikaḷāyiruntaṉa. kaṭaṟkarippakutiyil vāḻntōr mīṉpiṭikkum toḻil ceytaṉar. ularttiyum uppiṭṭum mīṉkaḷip patappaṭuttum muṟikaḷiyum ivarkaḷ aṟintiruntaṉar. uppu eṭukkum toḻilum kaṭalōraṅkaḷil naṭantatu. muttuk kuḷittal, caṅkaṟuttal, tōl pataṉiṭutal mutaliya toḻilkaḷum naṭipeṟṟaṉa. naṟṟiṇiyil tōl vēli ceypavaṉ iḻintavaṉākakkuṟippiṭappaṭuvatāl toḻil vaḻic cātikaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa eṉpati aṟiya muṭikiṟatu. 
vāṇikam
uḷnāṭṭu vāṇikam 
kaḻakakkālattil uḷnāṭṭu vāṇikamum, ayalnāṭṭu vāṇikamum naṭantaṉa. kaḻaka ilakkiyak kuṟippukaḷ perumpālum uḷnāṭṭu vāṇikattip paṟṟiyum, kirēkkak kuṟippukaḷ ayalnāṭṭu vāṇikattip paṟṟiyum aṟiya utavukiṉṟaṉa. uḷnāṭṭu vāṇikattiṟku iṉṟiyamiyāta pōkkuvarattup pātikaḷum, piṟatuṇikkāraṇikaḷum akkālattil atikamilli . āyiṉum vaṇṭikaḷiṉ mītu tavacaṅkaḷiyum uppu mutaliya vaṟṟiyum ēṟṟicceṉṟu viṟṟaṉar. vaṇikarukkut tuṇiyākak kāvalarkaḷum ceṉṟaṉar.uppuvaṇikar umaṇar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. ivarkaḷ paratavar allar. ivarkaḷ nāṭōṭikaḷākat tirintavarkaḷ. kūṭṭam - kūṭṭamāy vaṇṭikaḷil uppi ēṟṟikkoṇṭu ōr ūrē nakaruvatip pōl kuṭumpaṅkaḷuṭaṉ ūr ūrākac ceṉṟu ivarkaḷ uppu vaṇikam appuṟam ceytaṉar. kuṟippiṭam viṟpōrum, ceytaṉar. āṟalik kaḷvarālum, maḻiyālum ivarkaḷiṉ vaṇikap poruḷukkut tīṅku nēriṭṭatu. kaḻutikaḷiṉ mītum ivarkaḷ uppi ēṟṟicceṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷippōlavē tavaca vāṇikarkaḷum, malip poruḷkaḷiyum kaṭaṟporuḷkaḷiyum viṟpōrum, paṇṭamāṟṟu muṟiyil vāṇikam ceytaṉar. kuṟippiṭṭac cālikaḷ cērumiṭaṅkaḷi lum, ellip puṟaṅkaḷilum cuṅkaccāvaṭikaḷ iruntaṉa. ic cāvaṭikaḷē ittakiya vaṇikarkaḷukku paṇṭamāṟṟum niliyaṅkaḷākavum payaṉ paṭṭaṉa. ciṟṟūrkaḷilum, pērūrkaḷilum ittakiya poruḷkaḷi viṟkum aṅkāṭikaḷ iruntaṉa, terukkaḷil āymakaḷ tayir, ney mutaliyavaṟṟi viṟṟuviṭṭu nelliyum, uppiyum vāṅkic ceṉṟāḷ. pāṉmakaḷ mīṉavap peṇ) itip pōlavē mīṉiviṟṟu uṇavut talacaṅkaḷi vāṅkic ceṉṟāḷ. uḻavappeṇ putumalarkaḷit terukka ḷil viṟṟāḷ. ivvāṟu, mottamāka ūr ūrāka vantu viṟpōrum, kuṟippiṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷil canti māṟṟik koḷvōrum, ūr aṅkāṭi vaṇikarum, teruvil viṟpōrum ak kālap poruḷ naṭamāṭṭattiṟku ūkkamaḷittaṉar. '
nakara aṅkāṭikaḷ 
maturi. pūmpukār mutaliya nakaraṅkaḷilirunta nāḷaṅkāṭi avlaṅ kāṭi mutaliya kaṭivītikaḷip paṟṟiyum, avaṟṟil viṟkappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷip paṟṟiyum ēṟkeṉavē kūṟiviṭṭōm. ākavē, nakaraṅ karil naṭanta vāṇikattippaṟṟi vivarikkat tēviyilli, 
ayalaka vāṇikam 
tamiḻakattil mūṉṟu pakkattilum nīr cūḻntiruppatāl kaṭal kaṭantum vaṇikat toṭarpukaḷ toṉṟutoṭṭē iruntuvantaṉa. uppu eṭuttal, muttuk kuḷittal, kappal kaṭṭutal, kittoḻilkaḷ ākiyavi ayalaka vāṇikattiṟku uṟutuṇiyāyiruntaṉa. muṭiyuṭi vēntariṉ kaṭal āḷumiyum, ayalakaṅkaḷil tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ celvākku ōṅki vaḷarak karaṇiyamākiyatu. tamiḻarkaḷ palatarappaṭṭa kalṅkaḷ kaṭṭik kaṭal vāṇikattiyum, kaṭal āḷumiyum peṟṟaṉar eṉpatik kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷiliruntum, ayalakak kuṟippukaḷiliruntum aṟikiṟōm. vaṅkam, nāvāy eṉṉum kalaṅkaḷ peruṅkaṭalil neṭuntolivu ceṉṟaṉa. iviyāṉikaḷ pōṉṟu periyavi, kaṭalil niṟkum kappal kaḷiliruntu carakkukaḷi iṟakkuvataṟkum allatu ēṟṟuvataṟkum payaṉ paṭṭavi tōkarikaḷeṉṟum, ivi kaḻikaḷ mūlam curikaḷic cēru meṉṟum, kaṭaṟkari ōraṅkaḷiluḷḷa ūrkaḷuṭaṉ vaṇikam ceyyap payaṉpaṭṭavi eṉṟum, paḵṟi eṉpatu oruvakik kālam eṉpatiyum, ittakiya paḵṟikaḷ kutirikaḷippōl variciyākakkarikaḷil niṉṟu, koḷḷut tiṉṉum kutirikaḷ taliyi acippatippōl ivi acintu niṉṟaṉaveṉṟum aṟikiṟōm. ākavē, nāvāy, vaṅkam, tōṇi, paḵṟi mutaliya kalaṅkaḷum ampi, timil eṉappaṭum mīṉpiṭi kalaṅkaḷum ik kālattil iruntatiyaṟikiṟōm. kipi 75-l eḻutappaṭṭa *erittiriyak kaṭaṟcelvu' eṉṉum kirēkka nāvil pala maraṅkaḷik koṇṭu kaṭṭappeṟṟa 'caṅkarā'(caṅkāṭam eṉṟa kalamum, mikap periya urukkoṇṭa kolāṇṭiyā' eṉṟa kalamum, kariyōramākac cel lum nāṭṭuk kalamumāka mūvakik kalaṅkaḷ tamiḻakattil iruntatākak kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṟatu. 
kalaṅkaḷippaṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷip pōlavē tamiḻakat tuṟi mukaṅkaḷip paṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷum ilakkiyaṅkaḷilum, kaḻaka ayalcuc cāṉṟukaḷilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa, mēṟkuk kariyil muciṟi, pantar, koṭumaṇam mutaliya tuṟimukaṅkaḷum, kiḻakkuk kariyil koṟki, maruṅkūrppaṭṭiṉam, kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam, eyiṟpaṭṭiṉam mutaliya tuṟimukaṅkaḷum iruntaṉa. mēṟkūṟiya 'erittiriyak kaṭaṟcelavu" eṉṉum nūlil nakari, tiṇṭicu ākiya tuṟimukaṅkaḷ kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nakara eṉpatu kaṇṇaṉūrākavumnaṟavu), tiṇṭik toṇṭiyākavum irukkalām. mēlum, in nūlil ivi cēranāṭṭut tuṟikaḷ eṉavum cuṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tālami eṉṟa kirēkka āciriyarum it tuṟikaḷiyum, yavaṉarkaḷil kappalkaḷ vantuceṉṟa vakikaḷiyum, vaṇikam ceyta poruḷkaḷiyum paṟṟik kuṟippiṭukiṟāṉ. pakkariyeṉṟa tuṟiyip paṟṟip piḷiṉi kuṟippiṭukiṟāṉ. itu vikkariyākalām. nīrkkuṉṟam, vikkari ākiya ivi kuṭṭa nāṭṭilirunta tuṟimukaṅkaḷ. itaṟkum teṟkē paraliyā' eṉṟa tuṟi iruntatākak kirēkkarkaḷ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷa ṉar. itu 'palita' (varkkali eṉṉum tuṟiyākalām. kīḻkkariyil kolki, kamara potukkē, cōpaṭmam mutaliya tuṟimukaṅ kaḷiruntatik kaṭaṟcelavu nūl kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. ivi muṟiyē koṟki, kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉam, putuccēri, marakkāṇam ākiya tuṟikaḷākalām. koṟki yil muttukkuḷittalip paṟṟi in nūlil vekuvākap pēcappaṭukiṟatu. iṉṟu putuvikkarukil arikkamēṭṭil naṭattap peṟṟa akaḻvā rāycci in nūliṉ kūṟṟi meyppikkiṟatu. ciṟupāṇāṟṟuppaṭi, perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭi ākiyavaṟṟil muṟiyē eyiṟpaṭṭiṉam, nīrp peyaṟṟu eṉṟa tuṟimukaṅkaḷ kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟil nīrp peyaṟṟu iṉṟiya māmallapuram ākalām. 
paṇṭiya tamiḻakattuṭaṉ kirēkkam, urōmap pēraracu, ekiptu, arēpiyā ākiya mēli nāṭukaḷum, malināṭu malēyā), kiḻakkintiyat tīvukaḷ, īḻam, cīṉam mutaliya cīḻināṭukaḷum vāṇikat toṭarpu koṇ ṭiruntaṉa. kirēkkam teṉṉakattōṭu nēraṭit toṭarpu koṇṭiruntatu. alekcāntiriyā intiya vāṇikap poruḷkaḷāl ciṟappuṟṟatu. , urōmap pēraracu vāḻntataṟkum, vīḻntataṟkum, tamiḻakattōṭu atu koṇṭirunta vāṇikat toṭarpē karaṇiyam eṉiṉum mikiyākātu. āṇṭoṉṟukku ēṟattāḻa 120 kappalkaḷ urōmāpurikkum teṉṉakat - tiṟkum iṭiyē naṭamāṭiṉa. paṇṭiya tamiḻakam mēli nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ tāṉ atikamāṉa neruṅkiya vāṇikat toṭarpukoṇṭiruntatu, iḻi nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ avvaḷavu neruṅkiya toṭarpukaḷ illi, iti iṉṟiya akaḻvārāyccikaḷ valiyuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa. poṉṉi yēṟṟi vanta yavaṉariṉ kalaṅkaḷ poṉṉik koṭuttu miḷakiyum, kaṭal viḷi poruḷkaḷiyum, malipaṭuporuḷkaḷiyum ēṟṟic ceṉṟaṉa. 'puṭpa rākam' eṉṉum ariya maṇikkallum, melliya tuṇikaḷum, ōviya vēlippāṭukaḷ niṟinta nārāṭikaḷum liṉaṉ, añcaṉakkal, pavaḷam, kaṇṇāṭi, cempu, takaram, matuvakikaḷ, tāḷakam aritāram, centakāḷam oruvaki maruntu mutaliyaṉavum iṟakkumati ceyyap paṭṭaṉa. palavaki muttukaḷ, yāṉi maruppu, paṭṭut tuṇi, kaṅkip pakutiyil kiṭitta ilāmicci, marapattiram, kaṇṇāṭik kaṟkaḷ, vayiram, nīlam, caṅku mutaliya poruḷkaḷ iṅkiruntu mēli nāṭukaḷukku ēṟṟumatiyāyiṉa, intiyāviṉ uḷnāṭṭup pakutikaḷiliruntu vanta poruḷkaḷum, kiḻakkintiyat tīvukaḷiliruntu vanta poruḷkaḷum tamiḻakat tuṟimukaṅkaḷiliruntu ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. itip pōlavē yavaṉarkaḷ arapu nāṭukaḷilum, naṇṇilak kaṭaṟpakutikaḷilum, piṟa airōppiya nāṭukaḷilum kiṭitta poruḷkaḷi iṟakkumati : ceytaṉar. ivarkaḷukkeṉṟu paṇṭaka cālikaḷum, kuṭiyiruppukaḷum tamiḻakattil ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. ittakiya ēṟṟumati, iṟakkumatikaḷāl aracukkuc cuṅkavari atikam kiṭittatu. māmallapurattil nīrppeyaṟṟu) uyarnta māṭi vīṭukaḷ iruntaṉa. vāṇikac ceṭṭikaḷ (paratavar) irunta ṉar; veḷḷik kutirikaḷum, kaṅkip pakutiyil viḷinta poruḷ kaḷum it tuṟiyil atikam kāṇappaṭṭaṉa; iṅku uyarnta kalaṅkari viḷakkamiruntateṉap perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭi kūṟukiṟatu. ēṟṟumati yāṉa poruḷkaḷil ēlakkāy, akil, cantaṉam mutaliya naṟumaṇap poruḷkaḷum, yāṉi maruppu, vayiram mutaliyavi kuṟinta aḷavilum, miḷaku muttu, paṭṭu, ariya kalvakikaḷ periya aḷavilum iruntaṉa. iṟakkumatiyāṉa poruḷkaḷil poṟkācukaḷum, kutirikaḷum, matuvaki kaḷum atikamāka iruntaṉa. ittakiya vāṇikap poruḷkaḷāl ciṟappaṭinta tamiḻaka nakaraṅkaḷum, ayal nāṭṭu nakaraṅkaḷum piṟkālattil varalāṟṟilum, ilakkiyaṅkaḷilum iṭampeṟṟaṉa. 
(e) kaḻakak kālak kumukāyam
meypporuḷ vāḻvu 
cuḻacukkālat tamiḻkkumukāyam vāḻkki neṟiyic ciṟanta paṭṭāṅkukaḷiṉ tattuvaṅkaḷiṉ mēl kaṭṭiyiruntatu. taṉakkeṉa vāḻāt takimiyāḷarē kaḻakakkālat tamiḻar. taṉṉiyum, taṉṉuṭiya āṟṟaviyum vaḷarttu ataṉvaḻi taṉṉic cārnta kumukāyattiṟkum, nāṭṭiṟkum, moḻikkum vaḷarcci tantu, aṟaneṟi niṉṟu, vāḻvāṅku vāḻum neṟiyē aṉṟiya tamiḻkkumukāya neṟiyākum. ākavē, paṇṭiya kirēkkarip pōl – araciyaluk kākavē vāḻum kōṭ pāṭō, ivvulaka iṉpatti maṭṭum nukarum kōṭpāṭō, tuṟakkat tiyē yeṇṇi ulaka iṉpaṅkaḷit tuṟakkum camayak kōṭpāṭō taṉittu ivarkaḷiṭam kāṇappaṭa villi. tām vāḻum iṭam, kālam ākiyavaṟṟi mutaṟ poruḷākavum, uyiriṉaṅkaḷiyum ulakap poruḷ kaḷiyum karup poruḷākavum koṇṭatāl tamiḻar ulakiyal vāḻkkiyi maṉamāra nukarntu. tāmum vaḷarntu, ulakiyum vaḷarttuc. ciṟanta toru nākarikattit tam aṭiccuvaṭāka viṭṭucceṉṟaṉar. tām vāḻnta iṭattik kuṟiñci, mulli, marutam, neytal, pāli eṉṉum aintiṇikaḷākavum, kālattik kāli, naṇpakal, ēṟpāṭu, māli, yāmam, vikaṟi eṉṉum ciṟupoḻutukaḷākavum, noṭi, nāḻiki, nāṉ, tiṅkaḷ, āṇṭu eṉavum, taṭpaveppattiṟkēṟpak kār, kūtir, muṉpaṉi, piṉpaṉi, iḷavēṉil, mutuvēṉil ākiyavi yākavum pirittu vāḻkki yik kālattiṟkēṟpak kaṇakkiṭṭu naṭattiṉar. kālam poṉ pōṉṟatu; kaṭami kaṇ pōṉṟatu eṉak karutiya ivarkaḷiṉ vāḻkki neṟiyāl kumukāyam eṇṇip pārkka muṭiyāta aḷavukku ēṟṟam peṟṟatil viyappilli . 
nāṉilattilum vāḻnta makkaḷ kātalāl oṉṟukūṭi vāḻum illaṟa vāḻkkiyi 'akam' eṉṟum, ataṟkāṉa taḷaṉṟu kōlāyuḷḷa araciyalum, pōrum, poruḷīṭṭalum 'puṟam' eṉṟum koṇṭaṉar. aṟamum, poruḷumuṭiya vāḻkkiyē iṉpamāyiṟṟu. eṉavētāṉ, caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷil perumpālāṉavi akapporuḷ paṟṟiyē pēcukiṉṟaṉa. āṇum peṇṇum kātalittu illaṟam mēṟkoḷḷum niliyil maṭṭum amiyātu maṇṇum poṉṉum akavāḻvukku aṭippaṭiyāka amintatāl iv vāḻvukkup puṟattēyum avarkaḷ ceyalpaṭṭaṉar. kātal kaḷavākippiṉ kaṭimaṇa māka muṭiyum. piṉṉar kaṟpu neṟiyik kaṭippiṭittu oḻukutal ivarkaḷiṉ kaṭimi yāyiṟṟu. pōr mēṟceṉṟu veṟṟi peṟutalum, tirikaṭal ōṭip poruḷ tēṭalum āṇiṉ puṟa oḻukkamāyiṟṟu. avvāṟu pōr mēṟcollum pōtu vīramaraṇameytuvatum, veṟṟi peṟuvatum ciṟappeṉṟē karutap paṭṭaṉa.vīrac cāvu eytuvatum, ivarkaḷiṉ maṟuvulakak kōṭpāṭākum. tuka vāḻvu ciṟakka ayalakam ceṉṟu poruḷ tēṭaliṉ poruṭṭum, ōtaliṉ poruṭṭum, pōrkkaḷam pōtaliṉ poruṭṭum kātalaṉ kātaliyip pirintu celvāṉ. pōr mēṟceṉṟavaṉ kār kālattil tirumpi varuvatāyk kūṟic celvāṉ. avaṉ varum kār kālatti iyaṟkiyiṉ aṟikuṟikaḷ kātalikku niṉivūṭṭum, kātali avaṉ varukiyiyeṇṇi vāṭi, melintu, vaḷikaḻaṉṟu vaḻimēl viḻi vittu nōkkuvāḷ. kātalaṉ veṟṟic celvattuṭaṉ tirumpuvāṉ. kuḷirkālam muḻuvatum kūṭik kaḷippuṟṟu vāḻvar. ivvāṟu kālam, iṭam (nilam) ākiyavaṟṟiṟkēṟpa avarkaḷ naṭattiya nāṉila vāḻkki neṟi yiṉic curuṅkak kūṟuvōm. 
potut taṉmikaḷ 
paṇṭiya tamiḻkkumukāyattil āṇum peṇṇum kūṭi vāḻum amippākiya 'kuṭumpamē" mukāmi uṟuppākum. kuṭumpattalivaṉ kiḻavaṉ eṉṟum, talivi maṉiyōḷ eṉṟum aṟiyappaṭṭaṉar. pala kuṭumpaṅkaḷ cērntu vāḻnta iṭam *cēri' eṉappaṭṭatu. pala cērikaḷik koṇṭatu ōr uḷar. ovvōr paḷarilum toḻil vaḻi makkaḷ. aṟiyappaṭṭaṉar. itaṉ vaḻi avarkaḷ vāḻnta cēriyum aḻikkap paṭṭatu. ovvoru toḻiṟkuḻuvukkumcērikkum talivaṉ ēṟpaṭ ṭāṉ. it talivarkaḷiṉ kīḻ kuḻu maṉappāṉmi vaḷarntu kumukāyak kaṭṭukkōppukkut tuṇipurintatu. 
paṇṭit tamiḻar vāḻnta iṭaṅkaḷukkēṟpa mēṟkaṇṭavāṟu cērntu vāḻnta cērikaḷaṭaṅkiya ivarkaḷiṉ vāḻkki neṟikaḷum vēṟupaṭṭaṉa. tamiḻakam kuṟiñci, mulli , marutam, neytal eṉṟu nāṉilamāka iyaṟkiyiṉaṭippaṭiyil pirittaṟiyappaṭṭatu. ivaṟṟil vāḻnta makkaḷiṉ vāḻkki neṟikaḷ vāḻum nilattiṟkēṟpa amiyap peṟṟatāl tamiḻakattil nila vaḻi, toḻil vaḻi palatiṟappaṭṭa kumukāya amippukaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. kālappōkkil ivarkaḷ taṅkaḷiṉ muṉṉōr kaḷiṉ kālvaḻiyē taṅkaḷip pirittuk kūṟik koṇṭaṉar. ākavē, kuṭikaḷ(kulam) pala tamiḻakattil tōṉṟiṉa, avvāṟu tōṉṟiya kuṭikaḷuḷ kuṟavarkuṭi vēṭṭuvarkuṭi āyarkuṭi, kāṉavarkuṭi, uḻavarkuṭi, paratavarkuṭi, nuḷiyarkuṭi eyiṉarkuṭi, maṟavarkuṭi mutaliyaṉa kuṟippiṭat takkaṉa. ivvāṟu, ivarkaḷ vāḻnta nilattiṟkum, ceyta toḻilukku mēṟpappala kuṭikaḷāy aṟiyappaṭṭaṉarēṉum ivarkaḷ yāvarum tamiḻk kuṭikaḷē; tuṭiyaṉ, pāṇaṉ, paṟiyaṉ, kaṭampaṉ mutaliyōr vīrak kuṭiyiṉar. naṭukalliyē vaṇaṅkiyavar. 
kaḻakakkālattil maṉṉarum, tāṉit talivarkaḷum, atikāri cuḷum kōṭṭi kottaḷaṅkaḷuṭaṉ amikkappaṭṭa nakaraṅkaḷil vāḻntaṉa reṉpatāl nakara vāḻkkiyum, nāṭṭuppuṟa vāḻkkiyum ōrē pōtu iruntaṉa veṉpatiyaṟikiṟōm. ākavē, nāṉilattil vāḻnta makkaḷip paṟṟi yaṟivatōṭu, nakara vāḻkki muṟiyiṉiyum aṟital vēṇṭum. 
nāṉil vāḻkki 
kuṟiñci : maliyum malicārnta iṭamum kuṟiñci eṉappaṭum. kuṟiñcic ceṭikaḷ mikuntu kāṇappaṭṭatāl in nilam kuṟiñci eṉap paṭṭatu. kuṟiñci nila vaḷar kuṟicci eṉṟum, makkaḷ kuṟavar eṉṟum, kāṉavar eṉṟum aḻikkappeṟṟaṉar. āṭavar kuṟavaṉ, kāṉavaṉ, veṟpaṉ, puṉavaṉ eṉṟum, makaḷir kuṟatti, koṭicci, eṉṟum aḻikkap peṟṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ marakkiḷikaḷil ciṟu kuṭilkaḷi yamittu vāḻntaṉar. malinel (aivaṉam), tiṉi, vaḷḷik kiḻaṅku, vāḻimutaliya vaṟṟip payiriṭṭum, vilaṅkukaḷi vēṭṭiyāṭiyum, paḻaṅkaḷiyum, kiḻaṅkukaḷiyum, tēṉiyum, cuṉi nīriyum uṇṭum, parukiyum vāḻntaṉar. paraṇ amittu ataṉ mītamarntu payirkaḷip pātukāttaṉar. peṇkaḷ paraṇkaḷ mītu amarntu pala ōcikaḷiyeḻuppi vilaṅku kaḷiyum, paṟavi kaḷiyum ōṭṭuvatum, iravil tīmūṭṭi vilaṅkukaḷi viraṭṭuvatum iṉiya kāṭcikaḷākum. ivarkaḷiṉ kaṭavuḷ cēyōṉ eṉṉum murukaṉākum. kuravikkūttāṭi paṇṭaṅkaḷip paṭittu, kāvu koṭuttuc cēyōṉākiya murukaṉi vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. 
mulli : kāṭum, kāṭucārnta iṭamum mulli eṉappaṭum. iṅku mullik koṭikaḷ atikamiruntatāl innilam mulli yeṉap paṭṭatu. iṅku vāḻnta makkaḷ kōvalar,iṭiyar, āyar, āycciyar eṉappaṭṭa ṉar. ivarkaḷ kēḻvaraku, varaku avari mutaliya puṉceyp payirkaḷip payiriṭṭaṉar. āṭu, māṭukaḷic cottākak koṇṭiruntaṉar. eṉavē, puṉceyt tavaca uṇavu kaḷiyum, pāṟporuḷkaḷiyum peritum uṇṭaṉar. maṇcuvar eḻuppiya ciṟu kuṭicikaḷil vāḻntaṉar. āṭu, māṭukaḷi mēykkum ivarkaḷiṉ kōvalar vāḻkki iṉimiyāṉatu. kuḻalūti mantiyi naṭattic cellum kāṭci iṉiyatu. pāṟporuḷ kaḷiyum, tōṟporuḷkaḷiyum viṟṟuttaṅkaḷukku vēṇṭiya vaṟṟip peṟuvar. ivarkaḷ tiṉiyarici uṇṭālum pālic cērttē uṇpar. anta aḷavu pāl ivarkaḷukkup perumitamāyk kiṭittatu. peṇkaḷ pāl, tayir, ney viṟkum kāṭciyum, kiḷikaḷuṭaṉ koñcum kāṭciyum piṟavum iṉiyaṉa. ivarkaḷ kaṭavuḷ māyōṉ āvāṉ. 
marutam kāṭṭukkum kaṭalōrattiṟkumiṭippaṭṭa ceḻippunilam marutam eṉappaṭum. maruta maraṅkaḷ atikam vaḷarntatāl innilam marutam eṉappaṭṭatu. itu vayalum vayal cārnta iṭamumākum. iṅku vāḻnta makkaḷ uḻavar, kaḷamar, kaṭiyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. peṇkaḷ uḻattiyar. kaṭiciyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. in nilattut talivaṉ makiḻnaṉ, ūṉaraṉ eṉṟum, talivi maṉivi eṉṟum aḻikkap peṟṟaṉar, uḻutoḻil ceytu vāḻnta immakkaḷiṉ vāḻkki ciṟappuṭiyatum makiḻvuṭiyatu mākum. palatarappaṭṭa neṟpayir kaḷiyum, veṟṟili, karumpu, vāḻi mutaliyavaṟṟiyum payiriṭṭaṉar, āṭavar ciṟappum, kāḷi nōkkum peṇkaḷiṉ ciṟappum īṇṭu kaṇṭu kaḷikkattakkaṉavākum. ivarkaḷiṉ kaṭavuḷ vēntaṉ intiraṉ āvāṉ. 
neytal : kaṭal cārnta nilaṅkaḷum, kaṭalōrappakutikaḷum neytal nilappakutiyākum. maṇal niṟinta ip pakuti peru maṇal ulakam' eṉappaṭṭatu. in nilattil vāḻntavar paratavar (paravar eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭṭaṉar. ivarkaḷ vāḻnta pakuti pākkam, kuppam eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭṭatu. ivarkaḷ kaṭalil mīṉ piṭittaṉar. im makkaḷ nuḷiyar eṉavum aḻikkappeṟṟaṉar. ivarkaḷiṉ talivaṉ cērppaṉ, pulampaṉ koṇkaṉ, tuṟivaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭṭāṉ. kaṭaṟkarikaḷiyoṭṭi uppaṅkaḻikaḷum iruntaṉa. eṉavē, im makkaḷ mīṉiyum, uppiyum viṟṟē vāḻntaṉar. kaṭaṟkariyōraṅkaḷil paṉi, teṉṉi, puṉṉi, ñāḻal, cuṇṭal, tāḻi kiti), aṭappaṅ koṭikaḷ mutaliyaṉa ceḻittu vaḷarntaṉa. puṉṉi marattaṭiyil ivarkaḷiṉ ūr maṉṟaṅkaḷ kūṭum. kaṭṭumarattil ivarkaḷ kaṭalmēṟ ceṉṟu mīṉ piṭikkum toḻil neṟiyum, kuppattil vāḻum vāḻkki neṟiyum cuṟṟuppuṟac cūḻalkaḷukkēṟpa amintaṉa. ivarkaḷiṉ vaḻipaṭu teyvam vāraṇaṉ (varuṇaṉ ākum, 
pāli: pāliyeṉṟa nilappakuti tamiḻakattil niliyāka amin tirukkavilli. paruvamaḻi poyppatāl mēṟkūṟiya nilaṅkaḷ vaṟaṇṭu pāli pōl kāṭciyaḷippatiyē pāli eṉṟaṉar. āyiṉum, pāli eṉṟa aintāvatu nilattiyum, ataṉ iyalpukaḷiyum atil vāḻnta makkaḷiyum nūlil kaṇṭavāṟu curuṅkak kūṟuvōm. 
pāliyil vāḻnta makkaḷ vēṭṭuvar, eyiṉar, maṟavar eṉap paṭṭaṉar. cilar vaḻippaṟi ceytē vāḻntatāl 'āṟalik kaḷvar' eṉappaṭṭaṉar. peṇkaḷ eyiṟṟiyar, maṟattiyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. ivarkaḷ vāḻnta kārkaḷ kuṟumpu, paṟantali eṉappaṭṭaṉa. ivarkaḷiṉ vaḻipāṭu teyvam koṟṟavi (yi)yākum ivarkaḷ vāḻnta pakutiyil iruppi, ōmi, nemi, pāli, yā eṉṉum maraṅkaḷ vaḷarntaṉa. ivarkaḷiṭam 'añcāmi' eṉṉum paṇpē atikam kāṇappaṭṭatāl "maṟakkuṭi' māṇpukku ivarkaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭāyiṉar. inta añcāmi peṇkaḷiṭamum kāṇappaṭṭatu. 
nakara vāḻkki 
cuḻacukkālattu muṭivuṭi vēntarkaḷum avarkaḷic cērnta araca kuṭumpattārum vāḻnta nakaraṅkaḷilum, piṟa kuṟunila maṉṉar kaḷum, tāṉit talivarkaḷum vāḻnta nakaraṅkaḷilum pēratikāri kaḷum ayal nāṭṭavarum, celvarkaḷum, vaṇikarkaḷum vāḻntaṉar. ākavē, muṭiyuṭi vēntar mūvaruṭiya kōnakarkaḷēyaṉṟip pala nakaraṅkaḷ caṅka kālattil iruntaṉa. āyiṉum, kaḻakakkāla nakara vāḻkkiyip potup paṭa viḷakku muṉ pāṇṭiyariṉ kōnakarāṉa kūṭalampatiyip paṟṟiyum, cōḻariṉ kōnakarum, tuṟimukappaṭṭiṉa mumāṉa pūmpukārippaṟṟiyum kuṟippākak kūṟalām.
 kūṭal (maturi) nakara vāḻkki 
nāḷaṅkāṭi : maturiyil nāḷaṅkāṭi (pakaṟkaṭitteru, alluṅ kāṭi (iravuk kaṭitteru eṉṉum iruvakik kaṭitterukkaḷ muṟiyē pakalilum, iravilum ceyalpaṭṭaṉa. viki āṟṟut tuṟi kaḷil uḷḷa cōlippakutikaḷil perumpāṇ" makkaḷ vāḻntaṉar. nāṭṭi iḻanta maṉṉarkaḷum, kuṭiyiḻanta piṟanāṭṭu makkaḷum, pāṇṭiya nāṭṭi tākkit tōṟṟa maṉṉarkaḷum maturi nakariṉ kōṭṭikkuḷ amintirunta uyarnta māṭi vīṭukaḷil vāḻntaṉar. pāṇar nakariṉ akaṉṟa teruṅkaḷil āṅkāṅkē iciyiyeḻuppik koṇṭiruntaṉar. iciyik kēṭṭuc cilar makiḻcciyil āṭiṉar. viḻāk koṭi, maṉṉa ṉiṉ palvēṟu koṭikaḷ, kaḷḷukkaṭiyil paṟakkum koṭi mutaliya pala koṭikaḷ akaṉṟa terukkaḷil iru maruṅkilum paṟantaṉa. kaḷḷuṇṭa maṟavar cerukkōṭu ceṉṟaṉar. yāṉikaḷ, tērkaḷ, kutirikaḷ vantu pōyk koṇṭiruntaṉa, palvaki malarkaḷ, malar mālikaḷ, mēṉi maṇappoṭikaḷ, veṟṟili, aṭikkāy (pākku, cuṇṇāmpu mutaliya poruḷkaḷi viṟpōr paṭinaṭamāṭṭattiṟku añci māṭi vīṭukaḷiṉ niḻalil niṉṟu viṟṟaṉar. palvēṟu akaviyiṉarāṉa peṇkaḷum kaṭi vītikku vantu vēṇṭiya poruḷkaḷik kalivēlippāṭuṭiya ceppukaḷil vāṅkic ceṉṟaṉar, paṇpaṟṟa cila āṇkaḷ ivarkaḷik kaṇṭu nakiyāṭi niṉṟaṉar. ittakiya pōkkuvarattāl eḻunta āravāra olimaṉṉaṉiṉ veṟṟikkup piṉ ēḻu nāḷkaḷ naṭakkum peruñcōṟṟu viḻāvil eḻum āravārattiyottiruntatu'. 
allaṅkāṭi 
muṉṉiravu : muṉṉiravil celvarkaḷ kutirikaḷ pūṭṭiya tēril kāvalar piṉtoṭara ulā vantaṉar. makaḷir māṭaṅkaḷiṉ mītuḷḷa nilā muṟṟaṅkaḷil naṭamāṭiṉar. tāymārkaḷ kuḻanti kaḷiyum, pūcip poruḷkaḷiyum ēntikkoṇṭu civaṉ kōyilukkuc ceṉṟaṉar.kuṉṟu kuṭintaṉṉa antaṇar vīṭukaḷil maṟiyoli kēṭṭatu. camaṇap paḷḷikaḷil pūvum, pukiyum maṇantaṉa. pala poruḷkaḷiyum viṟṟu muttum maṇiyum vili yākap peṟṟut tirumpuvōrum, viliyuyarnta poruḷkaḷi viṟṟu vīṭu tirumpuvōrum, vēntaṉukku utaviya kaliñar kaḷum piṟarum vītikaḷil kāṇappaṭṭaṉar. mā, palā, mutalāṉa kaṉikaḷum, kiḻaṅku kaḷum, taṉaṉ cōṟum piṟavum viṟkum kaṭikaḷil avaṟṟi uṇṭa makkaḷ āravāram ceytaṉar. muḻu nilāvoḷiyil taṅkaḷip palavāṟu oppaṉi ceytukoṇṭu potumakaḷir mēṉi miṉukki aṭittiḷitta ṉar. poṅkaliṭṭup pūci ceyta iḷam piḷḷit tācciyarum, cāmi yāṭum cāliṉiyum, veṟiyāṭumvēlarum kuravik kūttāṭikkoṇṭu ceṉṟaṉar. cērikaḷil coṟpoḻivukaḷum, iciyum, kūttum nikaḻnta ṉa. 
naḷḷiravu : naḷḷiravil viṟpaṉik kaṭikaḷ mūṭappaṭṭaṉa. aṭi, pāyacam (vicayam), koḻukkaṭṭi mōtakam) mutaliyavaṟṟik kūvi viṟṟaṉar.viḻāvil mūṅkili niṟuttik kaṭṭiya kayiṟṟiṉ mēl āṭiya vayiriyar tāṅkiṉar. tarkkāval paṭiyiṉar naṭamāṭiya vaṇṇa miruntaṉar 
piṉṉiravu : vikaṟiyil antaṇar vīṭukaḷil maṟiyoli kēṭṭatu yāḻ vallōr marutappaṇ pāṭiṉar; kaḷiṟukaḷukku uṇavu uruṇṭikaḷ iṭappaṭṭaṉa; kaṭikaḷ meḻukappaṭṭaṉa; aḷviṟpōr vilikūṟi viṟṟaṉar; kaḷḷuṇṭōriṉ karakarappāṉa oli kēṭṭatu; maṉṉaṉ tuyileḻac cūtarkaḷ pāṭiṉar; viṇṇiyalar (mākatar) nēratti yaṟivittaṉar; vētakāḷi eṉṉum icikkaruvi muḻaṅkiṟṟu: makaḷir katavukaḷit tiṟakkum olikēṭṭatu. 
tuyileḻunta maṉṉaṉ kaṭamikaḷi muṭittukkoṇṭu kolu maṇṭapattil amarntu maṟavarukkum, pulavarukkum, kaliñarukkum paricukaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉāṉ. poṟkalattil makaḷir tanta tēṟalip parukiṉāṉ. 
yāvarum vāḻttum vāḻttolikaḷ kēṭṭaṉ. ivvāṟu aracaṉ aṟam purital, araciyal naṭattutal, iṉpamāyk kālaṅkaḻittaleṉa oru nāḷi mūṉṟākap pakirntu paṇiyāṟṟuvāṉ. 
mēṟkūṟiya muṟiyilēyē pukār nakara vāḻkkiyum amin tiruntatip paṭṭiṉappāliyāl aṟikiṟōm. pukārcelvam koḻitta nakaram. atil vāḻnta celvarum vaṇikarum, poṉṉum maṇiyum viravap peṟṟiruntaṉar. paratavar pāṇar, yavaṉar cērikaḷilum makkaḷ makiḻntu vāḻntaṉar. palvakittoḻilāḷar kaḷum, uḻavarkaḷum, kalivallār kaḷum in nakaril vāḻntaṉar. caṅku aṟuttal, yāṉi maruppukaḷik kaṭital, poṉvēli, maṉari vēli, cempu vēli, mārpukkacci ceytal(vampu muṭital), ōviyam tīṭṭutal, māli kaṭṭutal, cuṇṇam iṭittal, tiriyal maṭittal pīṭā) mutaliya ciṟutoḻilkaḷum," veṇkala vēli, necavuvēli, civiki, vaṇṭi tēr meyppuk kavacam, yāṉiyi aṭakkum tōṭṭikaḷ, kiyil māṭṭik koḷḷum tōluṟikaḷ, maruntukaḷ, cūḻiyam , veṇcāmaram, palavakiyāṉa kēṭayaṅkaḷ ceyyum toḻilkaḷum maturiyil naṭantaṉa. 
maturiyilum, pukārilum palvēṟu tavacaṅkaḷi viṟkum vāṇikat terukkaḷum iruntaṉa. tavacam viṟpōr kūlavāṇikar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. kappal vāṇikattil īṭupaṭṭōr mācāttu vaṇikar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. uppu vaṇikarum, eṇṇey vaṇikarumkaḷ, tēṟal, tōppi, maṭṭu makiḻ, naṟavu, naṉi, ariyal mutaliya palatarappaṭṭa matuvakikaḷi viṟpōrum, appam, piṭṭu paṇṇiyam, ūṉuṇavu mutaliya uṇavukaḷi viṟpō nam pū pūmāli, virimāmēṉi maṇappoṭi), poṉ, muttukaḷ, pavaḻam, viram mutaliyaṉa viṟpōrum, pūvēlippāṭuṭiya āṭikaḷ viṟpōrum, palvaki āṭikaḷ viṟpōrum, ōviyaṅkaḷ, pom mikaḷ, valippiṉṉal, viḷiyāṭṭupporuḷkaḷ viṟpōrum, iṉṉapiṟa eṇṇilā vaṇikarkaḷum maturi, pukār kaṭi vītikaḷil kāṇappaṭṭaṉar. 
kaṭal vaḻivanta ayalnāṭṭup poruḷkaḷum in nakaraṅkaḷil viṟkappaṭṭaṉa. kutirikaḷ, miḷaku, maṇikkaṟkaḷ, akil, cantaṉam mutaliyaṉavum viṟkappaṭṭaṉa. ayalnāṭṭu vaṇikarum tamiḻaka vaṇikarum palvēṟu moḻikaḷil pēcikkoṇṭaṉar. maruvūrp pākkattilum ittaku vaṇikar iruntaṉar. 
araṇmaṉi vāḻvu | 
mēṟkūṟiya makiḻcci āravāraṅkaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya nakarattiṉ naṭuvil aracaṉ vāḻum kōyil araṇmaṉi irukkum. maṉṉaṉ vikaṟiyil tuyileḻuntu veḷutta āṭiyaṇintu aṟam ceytu, ceṅkōlōc cuvatākak kūṟiṉōm. ivaṉ iravup poḻuti makaḷiroṭu iṉpamāyk kaḻittāṉ. poṉ maṇikaḷālāṉa aṇikaḷiyum, palatarappaṭṭa āṭi kaḷiyum aṇintu oppaṉi ceyyappaṭṭa araṇmaṉiyil vāḻnta maṉṉaṉiṉ vāḻkkiyum, uṟiviṭamum cepputaṟkariya ciṟappuṭiyaṉa. kaḻakakkāla maṉṉar peṇṭir palari maṇakkum vaḻakkamuṭiyavar. araciyārum aracaṉuṭaṉ koluvīṟṟiruntār. aracaṉ iṟantāl araci yum, paṭit talivarum piṟarum uṭaṉ kaṭṭiyēṟum vaḻakkam iruntatu. yāṉiyēṟṟam, kutiriyēṟṟam, vēṭṭiyāṭutal, piṟa peṇ kaḷuṭaṉ peṇṭāṭṭikaḷ kūṭikkaḷittal mutaliya paḻakkavaḻakkaṅ kaḷum, maṉṉariṭamuṇṭu. 
makkaḷ 
ituvari nām kaṇṭa kaḻakakkālattu nāṉila vāḻkkimuṟi kaḷiyum, nakara vāḻkkimuṟiyiyum tuytta makkaḷ yāvarum tamiḻarkaḷē. ivarkaḷ iṭattāl, toḻilāl vēṟupaṭṭaṉarēyoḻiya, uḷḷattāl tamiḻarkaḷē. uḻavar, toḻilāḷar, kaliñar, pulavar, vaṇikar mutaliya palatiṟappaṭṭavarkaḷik kaṇṭōm. iṉi im makkaḷiṉ uṇavu, uṭi aṇikalaṉkaḷ, poḻutu pōkkukaḷ, camayak kōṭpāṭu, vāḻviyal vēṟupāṭukaḷ mutaliyaṉa paṟṟic curuṅkak kūṟuvōm. 
uṇavu 
ik kāla makkaḷ orēvakiyāṉa uṇavi uṇṭaṉar eṉak kūṟa muṭiyātu. avaravar vāḻnta iṭaṅkaḷukkēṟpa uṇavu, uṭi, uṟi viṭaṅkaḷ amintati mēlē kaṇṭōm. āyiṉum perumpālāṉa makkaḷiṉ uṇavu aricic cōṟēyākum. varaku, cāmi mutaliya puṉceyta tavaca uṇaviyum, mīṉ iṟiccikaḷiyum, kaṉi, kiḻaṅkukaḷiyum uṇṭa makkaḷiyum kaṇṭōm. miḷaku, puḷi, uppu mutaliyaṉavum uṇavil cērttuk koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. aracaṉ aḷikkum viruntukaḷilum, piṟa viruntukaḷilum uṇṭa uṇavukkum, taṉi māntaṉ uṇṭa uṇavukkum, ainnila makkaḷum, araṇmaṉiyil vāḻntōrum uṇṭa uṇavukkum vēṟupāṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. kimpeṇṭir vitavikaḷ, tuṟavikaḷ, vīrarkaḷ mutaliyōriṉ uṇavu vakikaḷ vēṟu vēṟāka iruntaṉa. aṟaccālikaḷ pala iruntaṉa. palvēṟu ciṟṟuṇṭikaḷum ciṟṟuṇṭi viṟpōrum aṟiyappaṭu kiṉṟaṉar. palatarappaṭṭa paḻaccāṟukaḷiyum, palatarappaṭṭa kaḷḷiyum, cuvi nīriyum, kuṭivakikaḷiyum ik kālattil kāṇkiṟōm, ūṉ uṇṇum vaḻakkam ellōriṭamum iruntatu. veḷḷāṭu, cemmaṟiyāṭu, māṉ, muyal, āmāṉ, mīṉ, naṇṭu, īyal, kōḻi, kāṭṭukkōḻi, kāṭi uṭumpu mutaliyavaṟṟi uṇṭaṉar. pālum, pāl poruḷum, paruppu vakikaḷum uṇṇappaṭṭaṉa. nāṉ uṇavip pōlavē kaḷḷuṇṇum paḻakkamum, tēṟalip parukum paḻakkamum palariṭamum kāṇappaṭṭatu. piṟkālattil kaḷḷuṇṇu taliṉ kēṭukaḷiyuṇarntōr kaḷḷuṇṇāmiyi valiyuṟut tiṉar. 
uṭi 
kaḻakakkālac cāṉṟukaḷiliruntu akkāla makkaḷ taḻikaḷālum, pūkkaḷālum, koṭikaḷālum āṉa āṭikaḷiyum aṇintaṉar eṉpatu pulaṉākiṟatu. nārālum, marappaṭṭiyālumāṉa maravuri āṭiyum iruntatip pattuppāṭṭiliruntu aṟikiṟōm. pulittōl, māṉtōl mutaliyavaṟṟip paṭukkikkum, irukkikkum payaṉpaṭuttiṉārkaḷ. , paruttiyālāṉa meṉtukil, kaliṅkam, vaṇṇa āṭikaḷ mutaliyaṉa aṇiyappaṭṭaṉa. veṇmiyāṉa āṭikaḷ tuṇṭutuṇṭāka iruppiṉ aṟuviyeṉṟum, mūṅkiliṉ mēl ōṭu pōlavum pālāṭi pōlavum iruppiṉ meṉtukil eṉṟum aṟiyappaṭṭaṉa. kaliṅka nāṭṭiliruntu koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa parutti yālāṉa āṭiyē kaliṅkam eṉṟum, paṭṭāṭikku vaḻaṅkiya peyarē kaliṅkam eṉṟum palavāṟu kūṟappaṭu kiṟatu. nālākkaliṅkam, oṇpūṅkaliṅkam eṉṟu kaliṅkattip palavaki yākak kūṟiṉar. nuri pōṉṟa veṇmiyāṉa tuṇi. mūṅkili liruntu urikkappaṭṭa veḷḷiyatōl pōṉṟa kaṇṇukkut teriyāta iḻika ḷāl neyyappaṭṭa tuṇi ākiyaviyē muṟiyē nūlākkaliṅkam, oṇpūṅkaliṅkam eṉṟu aṟiyappaṭṭaṉa. maṇappeṇ aṇinta āṭi. kōṭikkaliṅkam eṉappaṭṭatu. curuṅkāta kaliṅkatti maṇamakaḷ aṇintāṉ eṉavum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. itiliruntu cucaṅkāta allatu curuṅkāta veḷḷiya āṭi kaliṅkam eṉappaṭṭirukkalāmeṉat teri kiṟatu. nīlam, civappu mutaliyavaṇṇaṅkaḷālāṉa āṭikaḷum iruntaṉa. 
paṭṭālum, elimayirālum āṭikaḷ neyyappaṭṭaṉa. āṭi kaḷil piṉṉal vēlippāṭukaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. piṉṉal vēli ceytavar cāliyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. āṇ, peṇ irupālarum palvaki yāṉa āṭikaḷiyum aṇintaṉar. nīṇṭa koycakam vittu āṭi aṇiyum vaḻakkam kaḻakakkāla makaḷiriṭiyē kāṇappaṭṭatu. ivarkaḷ "vampu'. 'vār' eṉṉum mārpuk kaccikaḷi aṇintaṉar. maṟavarkaḷ veṭṭuṭiyaṇintaṉar. āṇkaḷ ariyum caṭṭi 'paṭam' meyppi mutaliya peyarkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭṭaṉa. āṇum, peṇṇum oṉṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa āṭikaḷi aṇintaṉar. talippāki aṇivatum, mēl --- tuṇṭu pōṭuvatum uṇṭu. 
aṇikaḷ 
cuḻacukkālat tamiḻarkaḷ, aṇinta aṇikalaṉkaḷ aṇi, pūṇ, iḻi, kalam, toṭi, kuḻi, vaḷi mutaliya coṟkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. kāl mutal tali vari palvēṟu aṇimaṇikaḷi ivarkaḷ aṇintārkaḷ. āṇkaḷ maṟavar) kālil vīrakkaḻal aṇivar. peṇkaḷ taṇṭu, cilampu mutaliyavaṟṟik kālil aṇintaṉar. kiṇkiṇi eṉṉum ciṟu cataṅkiyum ciṟuvarkaḷukkukkālil aṇivikkappaṭṭatu. kaḻuttil āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum, muttu, poṉ mālikaḷiyum, kaṟkaḷ patitta mālikaḷiyum aṇintaṉar. āṇkaḷ tōḷil toṭi, kaḻaltoṭi, koṭi ākiyavaṟṟiyum, kiyil kaṭakattiyum aṇintiruntaṉar. peṇkaḷ muṉkiyil toṭiyum, ataṉiyaṭuttu vaḷiyum aṇintaṉar. kuṟuntoṭi, avirtoṭi, āytoṭi pūntoṭi, ceṟitoṭi eṉap palavakiyāṉa toṭikaḷ iruntaṉa. celvamakaḷir tōḷukkuk kīḻ aṇinta oruvaki aṇi vanta" eṉappaṭṭatu. itaṉi vaṅki eṉalām. poṉṉālum, muttālumaṉṟic caṅkālum vaḷiyalum, piṟavum ceytaṇiyappaṭṭaṉa. valampuric caṅkālāṉa vaḷi "valampurivaḷi' eṉappaṭṭatu. aṟuttuc ceyyappaṭṭa vaḷi elvaḷiyeṉṟum iluṅkuvaḷiyeṉṟum aḻikkappaṭṭatu. makaḷir pūṅkuḻi polaṅ kuḻi, ciṟukuḻi, oṇkuḻi, vārkkuḻi, kaṉaṅkuḻi, pollāṅkuḻi, makarakkuḻi, varikkuḻi mutaliya palatarappaṭṭa kātaṇikaḷi aṇin tiruntaṉar. ivaṟṟiliruntu poṉṉāl ceyta pūppōṉṟa kuḻiyum, cumpi pōṉṟa kuḻiyum, vārppuk kuḻiyum, paccikkal patitta kuḻiyum allatu taḻi, koṭi pōṉṟa kuḻiyum piṟavum ivarkaḷāl aṇiyap paṭṭaṉaveṉpati aṟikiṟōm, 
peṇkaḷ kūntal oppaṉikkuppiṉ taliyil poṉṉālāṉa taliyaṇi, curitakam mutaliya aṇikaḷiyum aṇintaṉar. aracarkaḷiṉ muṭicuḷ nīlamaṇikaḷ patittu aṇiceyyappeṟṟiruntaṉa. avarkaḷ mārpilum, kaḻuttilum, iṭuppilum, tōḷilum kiyilum palvēṟu aṇikaḷiyaṇintaṉar. ivvāṟu nōkkumpōtu caṅkakālattil āṇ, peṇ, ciṟuvar, ciṟumiyar yāvarumē ēḻi, celvantar eṉṟa vēṟupā ṭiṉṟi aṇimaṇikaḷi aṇintaṉar eṉa aṟikiṟōm. muttu, maṇi, poṉ, koṭi, vāri mūṅkil, caṅku mutaliyavaṟṟālāṉa aṇikaḷ iruntaṉaveṉpatiyum aṟikiṟōm. kuṟum poṟi, cilkāḻ, parumam, palkāḻ, vikkāḻ mutaliyaṉa iṭiyaṇikaḷākum. mārpil aṇinta aṇi "matāṇi' eṉappaṭṭatu. yāṉikku mukapaṭām kuḻi) eṉṉum poṉṉaṇi pōṭuvatuṇṭu. ciṟuvarkaḷukkup pulippal kōtta tālikaḷiyum, aimpaṭit tālikaḷiyum aṇivippar. kūntalic caṭiyākap piṉṉi atil tiruciyaṇiyappaṭum aṇi curitakam eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭṭatu. talimayiri vārivakiṭu eṭuttu oṉpatu maṇikaḷip patitta oruvaki aṇiyi aṇivārkaḷ. ataṟkut 'toyyakam' eṉṟu peyar. itaṉit talippāḷi eṉṟum aḻippar, makarappaṭuvāy eṉpatu cuṟāmīṉ vaṭivilāṉa oruvaki aṇiyākum. cuṟā vaṭivilāṉa maṇipatitta poṉvaḷiyam 'makaravaḷiyam' eṉappaṭṭatu. nīlamaṇi patitta poṉṉaṇi 'kuṇimpi' eṉappaṭṭatu. vāku vaḷiyam, nittilamatāṇi mutaliya aṇikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum toṉmaṇikaḷāl (navamaṇikaḷ) āṉa nakikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. 
naṟumaṇap poruḷkaḷ 
naṟumaṇap poruḷkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttum vaḻakkam ak kāl makkaḷiṭam kāṇappaṭṭatu. malarkaḷ, kāykaḷ, marakkaṭṭikaḷ, vilaṅkup poruḷkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi iṭittu naṟumaṇap poṭikaḷum naṟumaṇap poruḷkaḷum ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. pūkkaḷic cūṭikkoḷvatum, paṭukki yil tūvuvatum naṟumaṇam karutiyē yākum. palvēṟu malarkaḷiyum, koṭikaḷiyum, kāykaḷiyum, cērttuk kūṭṭappeṟṟa katampa māli kaḷippaṟṟiyum, avaṟṟi liruntu eḻunta naṟumaṇam paṟṟiyum aṟikiṟōm. veṟiyāṭum, vēlaṉ aṭṭikam cātikkāy, takkōlak kāy, kāṭṭumalli, veṇṭa kāḷi malar ākiyavaṟṟik kalantu katampa māli yaṇintāṉ. kañcaṅkullip pūvi makaḷirum, kuḻantikaḷum taliyil aṇiva tuṇṭu. ivvāṟu nāṉila makkaḷum palvēṟu mālikaḷic cūṭiyati yaṟikiṟōm, ceṅkaḻunīr māli, kāntaḷkaṇṇi, kaḷaṅkāykkaṇṇi, veṇṭāḷimāli, pittimāli mutaliya mālikaḷip paṟṟiyum aṟikiṟōm. 
poṉtakaṭu, toṇmaṇikaḷ, cantaṉam, karuppūram mutaliyavaṟṟip paṉinīril naṉittu iṭittuc cuṇṇam uruvākki virimāvākamēṉip poṭiyāka-Powderp pūcikkoṇṭaṉar. maṇapporuḷkaḷi ammiyil arittuc cāntākavum pūcikkoṇṭaṉar. akiṟpukipōṭṭut tuṇikaḷiyum, kūntaliyum maṇamaṭiyac ceytaṉar. itaṉāl tuṇikaḷ pūcciyarikkāmalum kākkappaṭṭaṉa. 
viḻākkaḷum viḷiyāṭṭukaḷum 
avvannilattiṟkēṟṟapaṭi viḻākkaḷum, viḷiyāṭṭukaḷum iruntaṉa. potuvāka viḷakkukaḷi ēṟṟik koṇṭāṭum viḷakkīṭṭu viḻā' ellōriṭattilum kāṇappaṭṭatu. aṟumīṉ cērum muḻunilā nāḷil māli vēḷiyil iv viḻā naṭipeṟum, terukkaḷil viḷakkukaḷi ēṟṟi, malar mālikaḷit toṅkaviṭṭup pāl poṅka viṭṭu viḻāk koṇṭāṭuvar. malikaḷiṉ uccikaḷilum viḷakkukaḷi yēṟṟuvar, tirumāl ulakattil tōṉṟiya nāḷi 'ōṇam' eṉṉum viḻāvākak koṇṭāṭuvar. aṉṟu vīrarkaḷ yāṉikaḷip pōriṭac ceytu kaṇṭukaḷippar. tinnīrāṭum viḻā, pērāṟukaḷil puṉal nīrāṭum viḻā, maṇiyi iṭuppilē kaṭṭikkoṇṭu teruvilē āṭum uḷḷi viḻā, mutaṉmutalil vīrar ampu toṭukkumpōtu koṇṭāṭum vīrar viḻā', kūttarkaḷ koṇṭāṭum kōṭiyar viḻā, mēḻicittiri) ōriyil muḻunilāk kālattil koṇṭāṭum vēntar intira viḻā, iḷavēṉiṟ kālattil koṇṭāṭappaṭum kāmavēḷ viḻā mutaliya viḻākkaḷik kaḻakakkāla makkaḷiṭiyē kāṇkiṟōm. 
viḻākkaḷippōlavē palvēṟu viḷiyāṭṭukaḷilum ivarkaḷ īṭupaṭṭaṉar. palvēṟu vakiyākat taṉittum, palar kūṭiyum nīril viḷiyāṭiṉārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ maṇalvīṭu kaṭṭutal, kaḻaṟcikkāy, ammāṉikkāy mutaliyaṉa āṭutal, paṟavikaḷivittāṭutal, ūñcalāṭṭam, puṉalāṭṭam mutaliya āṭṭaṅkaḷil īṭupaṭutalum uṇṭu. poruniyāṟṟaṅkariyil peṇkaḷ kikōttup piṉṉiyāṭiya viḷiyāṭṭu teṟṟik kiḻaṅku viḷiyāṭṭu' allatu kuravi viṉi yāṭṭu' eṉappaṭukiṟatu. pāvikaḷ, pūkkaḷ, kiḻaṅkukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟik koṇṭu, ciṟṟil kaṭṭiyum, cōṟākkik kuṭumpam naṭattiyum ciṟumikaḷ viḷiyāṭiṉar. kaṉṟukaḷuṭaṉ cērntum, nīril mūḻkiyum, marattivēṟiyum ciṟuvarkaḷ viḷiyāṭiṉar. ic ciṟuvar, ciṟumiyar viḷiyāṭṭukaḷum, periyōrkaḷ viḷiyāṭṭukaḷum nāṉilattiṟkēṟṟavāṟu palavakiyāka iruntaṉ. vīrarkaḷ viḷiyāṭṭuk kaḷaṅkaḷil pala viḷiyāṭṭukaḷiyum, uṭaṟpayiṟci kaḷiyum ceytaṉar. yāṉiyōṭu uṭal valimiyic cōtikkum viḷiyāṭṭu, kāḷikaḷit taḻuvum viḷiyāṭṭu mutaliya añcattakka viḷiyāṭṭukaḷiyum ivarkaḷ viḷiyāṭiṉārkaḷ. 
kartikaḷ 
kaḻakakkālattil nīr, nilam, vāṉvaḻikaḷik kaṭakka ūrtikaḷ iruntaṉa. yāṉi, kutiri, erumi, kōvēṟu kaḻuti mutaliya vaṟṟiṉ mītum, kutiri, māṭṭuvaṇṭikaḷiṉ mītum ūrntu ceṉṟaṉar. vēntarum, vīrarum, āṇkaḷum tēril ceṉṟaṉar. tērkaḷiṉ vakikaḷ virivāka aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. vaṇṭikaḷ tavacaṅkaḷi ēṟṟikkoṇṭum, uppu muṭṭikaḷi ēṟṟikkoṇṭum variciyākac ceṉṟa kāṭcikaḷ cittarikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nīrik kaṭappataṟkuk kaṭṭumaraṅkaḷum, marakkalaṅka kaḷum iruntaṉa, periya kappalkaḷ 'kalam' eṉṟum, ciṟu ōṭaṅkaḷ 'tōṇi" eṉṟum aḻikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tōṇikaḷ kalampuka muṭiyāta kaḻikaḷilum cellum. vīṭu pōṉṟa periya paṭakukaḷil mīṉ viṟṟa nel mūṭṭikaḷi aṭukki vantatāka aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu. timili eṉṟa tōṇi nīrippiḷantu virintu cellakkūṭiyatu. ayalakam ceṉṟa kalam vaṅkam' eṉappaṭṭatu: nāvāy eṉṟum itaṉiyaḻippar. vāṉvaḻiyākac cellum vāṉavūrtikaḷ kaḻakakkālattil iruntaṉavā eṉpatu paṟṟit tiṭṭamākak kūṟamuṭiyavilli. āyiṉum vāṉavūrti' eṉṉum toṭar puṟanāṉūṟṟiliruntu aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu. "valavaṉ ēvā vāṉavūrti' eṉṟu aḵtu cuṭṭappaṭuvatāl ōṭṭupavaṉ illāmalēyē atu ceṉṟateṉa aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu, 
nampikkikaḷ 
kaḻakakkāla makkaḷiṭam palatarappaṭṭa nampikkikaḷ iruntaṉa. cakuṉam pārttal, naṟcol kēṭṭal mutaliyavaṟṟil avarkaḷ peritum nampikki yuṭiyavarkaḷ. katiravaṉ, nilavu, viṇmīṉkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟil iṭap peyarccikaḷiṉāl viḷiyum kēṭukaḷiyum naṉmikaḷiyum paṟṟip pala nampikkikaḷik koṇṭiruntaṉar. kirakaṇam ēṟpaṭuvatit tiṅkaḷip pāmpu viḻuṅkukiṟateṉṟu nampiṉar, viṇmīṉ kuṟitta kālattil viḻumāṉāl aracar pōlum talivarkaḷ iṟappar eṉṟu nampiṉar. pōrkkaḷattilum, iṭukāṭṭilum pēykaḷiruppatāyum, avi piṇaṅkaḷit tiṉṟu vāḻumeṉṟum nampiṉar. neyyiyum, veṇciṟu kaṭukiyum kalantu tūvik kaṇṇēṟu kaḻippārkaḷ. pēykaḷ neruṅkā vaṇṇamum ivaṟṟit taliyil appikkoḷvārkaḷ. pāmpukaḷ mārikkak kaṟkaḷi umiḻum eṉṟu ivarkaḷ nampiṉārkaḷ. 
paṟavikaḷ, ōṇāṉ, niṟikuṭam mutaliyavi etirppaṭutalik koṇṭum, nērattik koṇṭum nimittam pārttu naṭakkum nampikki cuḻacukkālat tamiḻarkaḷiṭam uṇṭu, yārō iruvar pēcum collit tām eṇṇiya eṇṇam niṟivēṟumā eṉpataṟkup pārkkum ceyal viricci kēṭṭal eṉappaṭum. "varuntaṟka', 'varuvāṉ' eṉṟu pēcik koṇṭāl niṉittavaṉ varuvāṉ eṉṟum, māṟākap pēcik koṇṭāl vārāṉeṉṟum nampiṉar. vaḷiciṟuttal, uṟuppukaḷ tuṭittal, kaṉavukaḷ, palli col, kākki karital pūtaliyavaṟṟilum ivarkaḷukku nampikkiyuṇṭu. maram, cuṉi, ōṭi āṟu, kaṭal mutaliya vaṟṟilum teyvikam urivatāy nampiṉar. 
aṟattāl naṟpiṟavi kiṭṭum eṉṟum, aṉṟēl kuṟipaṭṭa piṟaviyē kiṭṭum eṉṟum, immi, maṟumiyil avarkaḷ nampikki vittaṉar. kuṟatti kuṟi collum vaḻakkamum, muṟṟattil nellip parappik kuṟi kēṭṭal, oṟṟi iraṭṭiyeṉayeṇṇi muṭiveṭuttal, teyvam vantu āṭutal vēlaṉ veṟiyāṭal, cāliṉi pēyāṭal) mutaliyavaṟṟilum nampikki vittiruntaṉar. 
muṭivuri 
kaḻakakkālak kumukāyatti āyntu, nōkkumpōtu maṉṉarum, celvarum, maṉṉar kuṭumpattiṉarum, pēratikārikaḷum, tāṉittali varkaḷum, ivarkaḷic cērnta toḻilāḷarkaḷum, kali vallunarkaḷum nakaraṅkaḷil iṉpa vāḻkkiyi nukarntaṉar eṉṟum, ivarkaḷil uṇavu, uṭiaṇikaḷ mutaliyaṉavum poḻutupōkkukaḷum ētō oru kaṉavu ulakil kāṇum kāṭcikaḷāka uḷḷaṉaveṉṟum aṟikiṟōm. āṉāl, nāḷil makkaḷiyum, pālinila makkaḷiyum paṟṟi aṟiyumpōtu nākarika maṟiyāta toṭakkakkāla makkaḷip pōlavē vēṭṭiyāṭiyum, āṭu, māṭu mēyttum. kiḻaṅku kāykaḷiyuṇṭum, maravuriyum, taḻi, tōlāṭikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟi uṭuttiyum vāḻntatik kāṇkiṟōm. kaṭiyēḻu vaḷḷalkaḷiyum, maṉṉarkaḷiṉ koṭittaṉmi kaḷiyum ivarkaḷi nāṭic cellum pulavarkaḷiyum, pāṇar kaḷiyum kāṇum pōtu kañcikkaḻum makkaḷ orupuṟamum, celvatti maliyeṉakkuvit tuḷḷa celvarkaḷ oru puṟamum irukkak kāṇkiṟōm, kaṟpiṉ mēṉmi paṟṟiyum pēcappaṭum atē nērattil parattiyar paṟṟip palavāṟu aṟikiṟōm. uvakip peṇkaḷōṭum pal aḻakiya maṉiviyaroṭum aracarkaḷ matuvuṇṭukaḷikkum kāṭciyum, maṟavar kāṭṭuk kiḻaṅkik kiḷaṟiyuṇṭu, nāṭṭukkākap pōriṭum kāṭciyum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. pārppaṉar, kumukāyattil uḻikkāmal ēṟṟam peṟṟuḷḷaṉar. vaṇi karkaḷ, peruñcelvattik kuvittuḷḷaṉar. vaṟiyarkaḷ, vaḷḷalkaḷi nāṭip paḻuttamaram nāṭic cellum paṟavikaḷ pōla alikiṉṟaṉar, murukaṉ, tirumāl, vēntaṉ intiraṉ), vāraṇaṉ (varuṇaṉ), koṟṟavi mutaliya teyvaṅkaḷum, camaṇa, cākkiya camayaṅkaḷum nilaviniṉṟaṉa. catukkap pūtaṅkaḷ, pēykaḷ mutaliyaṉa paṟṟiyum tuṟakkam, maṟupiṟappu paṟṟiyum ik kāla makkaḷ aṟintiruntaṉar. 
potuvāka oḻukkamum, vīramum, paṇpum ivarkaḷ yāvariṭattum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. kaṟṟavarum, pulavarum, tuṟavikaḷum, oḻukkamuṭiyārum, kaṟpuṭip peṇṭirum yāvarālum pōṟṟap paṭṭaṉar. kalviyum, poru, ḷum makkaḷiṭiyē viravi, parantu vaḷaravēṇṭumeṉṟa aṭippaṭik koḷki annāḷil illi. 'ciṟu mīṉkaḷip perumīṉkaḷ viḻuṅku vatippōl vallavarkaḷē piṟari yaṭakkiyāṇṭaṉar. aṭikkaṭi māṟum āṭciyālum, iyaṟkik kōṭukaḷālum avatiyuṟṟa makkaḷ taṅkaḷ vāḻkkiyip paṟṟi mikutiyāka ōrāmal cintikkāmal taṉṉoḻukkat tiyē vaḷarttu vāḻvāṅku vāḻntaṉar. 
(ē) kaḻakak kālac camayam
kaḻakakkālat tamiḻar iyaṟkiyit teyvamāka vaḻipaṭṭavarkaḷ, iv vaḻipāṭē paḻamiyāṉatu. itaṉ vaḷarccippāṅkil civaṉ, māl, koṟṟavi, murukaṉ mutalāṉa teyva vaḻipāṭṭu muṟikaḷ tamiḻar vāḻvil iṭampeṟṟaṉa. maṟṟum maram, naṭukal, pēyt teyvaṅkaḷ peṇ teyvaṅkaḷ, ciṟu teyvaṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉavaṟṟiyum kaḻakakkāla makkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. vantēṟikaḷum, vantēṟikaḷiṉ māyavalippaṭṭavarkaḷum āriyat teyvaṅkaḷiyum, camaṇa, puttat teyvaṅkaḷiyum vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar. iṉṟupōl kaḻakak kālattilum tamiḻakam palvēṟu camayaṅ kaṉiṉ pukaliṭamāka viḷaṅkiyatu. 
iyaṟki vaḻipāṭu 
tiṅkaḷ, ñāyiṟu, maḻi ākiyavaṟṟi vāḻtti iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ tam cilappatikāra nūlit toṭaṅkukiṟār. itu tamiḻar neṟiyil aminta vaḻipāṭṭumuṟi, ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ, tī ākiya muccuṭar vaḻipāṭṭumuṟi koṭi nili, vaḷḷi, kantaḻi eṉṉum peyarkaḷāl tolkāppiyattil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu tol. 1034). piṟa kaḻakap pāṭalkaḷilum ic cuṭar vaḻipāṭu paṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. viṇmīṉ vaṇakkam (naṟ. 231), kāṟṟut teyva vaṇakkam(kali. 15, kaṭal teyva vaṇakkam akam. 240) mutalāṉa vaḻipāṭukaḷum kaḻakak kālattil nilavi vantaṉa. ālamar celvaṉ (puṟam. 198), vōmpaṭik kaṭavuḷ (akam. 309), paṉiyaṭik kaṭavuḷ(naṟ. 3030), kaḷḷi marattaṭik kaṭavuḷpuṟam. 260), uṉṉamarak kaṭavuḷ tol. 1006) mutalāṉa marattaṭik kaṭavuḷarum kaḻakak kālattil vaḻipaṭu cuṭavuḷarāy viḷaṅkiṉar. kaḻakakkāla makkaḷ vīṭṭut teyva vaḻipāṭu ceytaṉar. akam. 282, naṟ. 2, 35, kali. 133, patiṟ. 84). teyvaṅkaḷ palvēṟu uruvaṅkaḷikkoṇṭaviyāka viḷaṅkiṉakuṟi. A). potiyilcāvaṭi, maṉṟam, tiṇ, vīṭṭut tiṇṇi , marattaṭi mutalāṉa pala iṭaṅkaḷil kaṭavuḷiṉ tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ vittu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. cēyōṉ murukaṉ allatu civaṉ, māyōṉ tirumāl vēntaṉ intiraṉ), vāraṇaṉ(kaṭal teyvam ākiya teyvaṅ kaḷit tolkāppiyam kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. itteyvaṅ kaḷuṭaṉ palarāmaṉ, kāmaṉ cāmaṉ(kali. 94), ēṇōṉa(kali. 26 piramaṉ(kali.2,129, naṟ. 337, muruku 165), mānitik kiḻavaṉ(akam. 5ki), kūṟṟuvaṉ(patiṟ. 71, *pācam' puṟam. 195), intiraṉmuruku 159) mutalāṉa teyvaṅkaḷum kaḻakakkālat tamiḻarkaḷāl vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. 
peṇ teyvaṅkaḷil koṟṟavitol. 10051, tirumakaḷ (puṟam, 658), māyavaḷ (kali.35) kalimakaḷkali.36), cūli(kuṟun. 21) kaṭalteyvam (akam. 370), nīrara makaḷirpuṟam. 42), variyar makaḷir(aiṅ. 191.204), cūrara makaḷir akam. 162, muruku 12-37 mutalāṉa teyvaṅkaḷum vaḻipaṭap paṭṭaṉa. 
' maṟṟum kollip pāvi akam. 62, naṟ. 201), tātu ceypāvi (akam. 392, vaṇṭaṟpāvi(akam. 258), uppiyaṟpāvikali. 138) maṉṟaṅkaḷil aminta maṇ cili akam 157mutalāṉa pāvi uruvaṅ kaḷum vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. 
accantarattakka pēy, kamutu, aṇaṅku, cūr, mutalāṉapeyarkaḷāl kuṟippiṭṭuk kaḻakakkāla makkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭuvantaṉar. 
-- makkaḷik kākkap pōriṭṭu māynta vīrarkaḷukkuk kalnāṭṭut teyvamākki vaḻipaṭṭa naṭukal vaḻipāṭu kaḻakakkāla varalāṟṟil ciṟappiṭam peṟṟa oṉṟu. itu kōyil kaṭṭi vaḻipaṭum nilikku vaḷarntatic cilappatikāram kāṭṭukiṟatu. 
vaḻipāṭṭu muṟi 
paccilipuṟam. 105, muruku, 37, malar mutalāṉavaṟṟit tūviyum, naṟi viri pukittum kuṟi. 5-7), poṅkaliṭṭup paṭittum, arukampul, pūḷāppū, nel, tiṉi mutalāṉavaṟṟit tūviyum kaḻakakkāla makkaḷ kaṭavuḷi vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. nīrāṭiya piṉṉarē avarkaḷ vaḻipāṭu ceytaṉar.
