(patiṟ. 88). cammaṇam pōṭṭu uṭkārntu koṇṭum muruku]4), niṉṟu koṇṭum avarkaḷ vaḻipāṭu ceytamikkāṉa kuṟippukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. kaṭavuḷiṉ muṉṉar yāḻ mīṭṭiyum vaḻipāṭu ceytaṉarnaṟ 189). makaḷir kūṭṭam kūṭṭamākak kōyilkaḷukkuc ceṉṟu vaḻipāṭu ceytaṉar. naṭukallukkup pīlicūṭṭiyum, tuṭi muḻakkiyum, tōppi, kaḷ mutalāṉa vaṟṟip paṭiyal ceytum vaḻipāṭu ceytaṉar. akam.385), veḷḷāṭṭuk kuṭṭi mutalāṉa vilaṅkukaḷik kāvu koṭuttu vaḻipaṭṭatum uṇṭu (akam. 156. cantaṉa māliyi vaḻipāṭṭiṉ pōtu oru māliyākap cepamāliyākap payaṉpaṭuttiyatākavum terikiṟatu acum. 13). maḻi peyvataṟkākavum, peyyum maḻiyi niṟuttuvataṟkākavum avarkaḷ vaḻipāṭu ceytatuṇṭu puṟam.143). ivi tamiḻar vaḻipāṭṭu muṟikaḷ. 
āriyap pārppaṉar camaṟkiruta vētam ōti, kāliyilum, māliyilum vaḻipāṭu ceytaṉar. avarkaḷ tī vaḷarttu vēḷvi ceytu vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. uruttiraṉ, cuppiramaṇiyaṉ, kiruṭṭiṇaṉ mutalāṉa teyvaṅkaḷ toṭarpāṉa kaṭṭukkatikaḷit tamiḻar teyvaṅkaḷukku iṭṭukkaṭṭi iṇittuviṭṭaṉar. itaṉāl, caṅkakālattiṟkup piṉṉar civaṇiyamcivam, māliyam/viṇavam) eṉṉum camayappirivukaḷ putitāka muḷittaṉa. 
camaṇa, putta neṟikaḷ 
kaḻakakkālac camaṇar kukikaḷ, tamiḻnāṭṭil paravalākak kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. maturikkāñci, cilappatikāram, maṇimēkali mutalāṉa nūlkaḷilum, camaṇa, putta neṟic ceytikaḷ iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa, ivaṟṟik koṇṭu ic camayaneṟikaḷ kaḻakakkālattil toṭakka niliyilum, caṅkam maruviya kālattil ōraḷavu celvākkup peṟṟa niliyilum viḷaṅkiṉaveṉṟu kūṟalām. 
ōka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ
36 nūlkaḷik kaḻaka nūlkaḷ eṉṟu piṟkālattavar kuṟippiṭṭaṉar. avaṟṟuḷ 18 nūlkaḷi mēṟkaṇakku nūlkaḷ eṉṟum, 18 nūlkaḷik kīḻk kaṇakku nūlkaḷ eṉṟum pakuttu vaḻaṅkiṉārkaḷ, mēṟkaṇakku nūlkaḷuḷ pattuppāṭṭu 10 nūl), eṭṭuttoki (8 nūl) ākiyavaṟṟi aṭakkiṉārkaḷ. 
cilappatikāram, maṇimēkali ākiya kāppiyaṅkaḷik kaḻaka nūlkaḷ eṉṟum, kaḻakakkāla nūlkaḷ allaveṉṟum, iru vēṟu 
karuttukaḷik kūṟivarukiṉṟaṉar. in nūlkaḷiṉ kālam toṭarpāka nām oru teḷivāṉa muṭiviṟku varamuṭiyavilli eṉṟālum avi pallavar kālattiṟku muntiyavi yeṉṟu teḷivākak kūṟalām. itaṉāl, ivaṟṟik kaḻakam maruviya kāla nūlkaḷ eṉalām. atāvatu ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkum mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkum iṭippaṭṭa kālattaviyeṉalām.
pattuppāṭṭu 
nīṇṭa pāṭalkaḷik koṇṭa pattu nāṅkaḷ it tokik kuṟippāl cuṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭi, poruna rāṟṟuppaṭi, ciṟupāṇāṟṟuppaṭi, perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭi, kūttarāṟṟuppaṭi eṉṉum malipaṭukaṭām ākiya aintu nūlkaḷ iravalarip puravalarpāl āṟṟuppaṭuttum āṟṟuppaṭi nūlkaḷāka amintuḷḷaṉa, mullippāṭṭu kuṟiñcippāṭṭu, paṭṭiṉappāli ākiya mūṉṟum akattiṇi nūlkaḷ, maturikkāñci taliyālaṅ kāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa neṭuñceḻiyaṉic ciṟappittup pāṭiya puṟattiṇi nūl, neṭunalvāṭiyum avaṉatu ciṟappiṉik kūṟukiṟatu. itu puṟattiṇi nūl eṉpatu tollōr muṭivu. ik kāla ārāycci yāḷarkaḷil cilar akattiṇi nūl eṉavum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. inta nūlkaḷip pāṭiyōriyum pāṭalkaḷil ciṟappikkappaṭṭōriyum kāṭṭum aṭṭavaṇiyi aṭiyil kāṇalām.

eṭṭuttoki 
eṭṭuttoki nūlkaḷ intat tokik kuṟippāl cuṭṭap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ puṟanāṉūṟu, patiṟṟuppattu, paripāṭal ākiya mūṉṟu nūlkaḷum puṟattiṇip pāṭalkaḷik koṇṭavi. ivaṟṟil palvēṟu maṉṉarkaḷip paṟṟiyum kuṟunilat talivar kaḷip paṟṟiyum, maṟakkuṭi makkaḷippaṟṟiyum, palvēṟu pulavarkaḷ pāṭiya pāṭalkaḷ iṭampeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivi caṅkakāla makkaḷiṉ vāḻkkip pāṅkiṉi yum, paṇpāṭṭuc ciṟappiṉiyum veḷip paṭuttum karuvūlaṅkaḷākat tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. 
aiṅkuṟunāṟum, kalittokiyum akattiṇi nūlkaḷ. ivaṟṟil ovvoru tiṇiyic cērnta pāṭalkaḷi ovvoru pulavar pāṭi uḷḷār. itaṉik kīḻē uḷḷa aṭṭavaṇiyil kāṇalām. 
	kuṟiñci 	kapilar 	yilar 
	mulli 	cōḻaṉ nalluruttiraṉ 	pēyaṉār 
	marutam 	marutaṉiḷanākaṉār 	ōrampōkiyār
	neytal 	nallantuvaṉār 	ammūvaṉār 
	pārppu 	peruṅkaṭuṅkō 	ōtalāntiyār 
kuṟuntoki, neṭuntoki eṉṉum akanāṉūṟu, naṟṟiṇi ākiya mūṉṟu nūlkaḷil palvēṟu pulavarkaḷ pāṭiya akattiṇip pāṭalkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ kuṟuntoki 4-8 aṭikaḷi uṭiya pāṭalkaḷik koṇṭatu. naṟṟiṇip pāṭalkaḷ -12 aṭikaḷik koṇṭavi. akanāṉūṟṟup pāṭalkaḷ 1337 aṭiyaḷavuṭiyaṉa. mēlē kūṟappaṭṭa akattiṇip pāṭalkaḷ yāvum tamiḻar vāḻvil amin tirunta āṇ, peṇ uṟavup pāṅkiṉ mēṉmiyi uṇarttu kiṉṟaṉa, pāṭalkaḷ puṉinturiyiṉṟi iyalpāka iyaṟki yōṭum, vāḻkki yōṭum iyintu kāṇappaṭuvatu ciṟappukkuriya tākum. paripāṭalil ovvoru pāṭalukkum ici kuṟippiṭap paṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṉāl, itaṉi icittamiḻ ilakkiyam eṉṟu kūṟalām. 
tirukkuṟaḷ mutalāṉa patiṉeṇkīḻkkaṇakku nūlkaḷil kumukāya nalamkaruti makkaḷ evvāṟu vāḻavēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟaneṟi pukaṭṭum pāṭalkaḷ amintuḷḷaṉa. 
cilappatikāramum, maṇimēkaliyum iraṭṭik kāppiyaṅkaḷ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cilappatikāram, iyal, ici, nāṭakam eṉṉum muttamiḻkkūṟukaḷiyum taṉṉakattē koṇṭuḷḷa muttamiḻk kāppiyam cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiyum avaṟṟiṉ maṉṉarkaḷiyum 
kōvalaṉ, kaṇṇaki varalāṟṟiṉ vāyilāka itu ciṟappittukkāṭṭukiṟatu. maṇimēkali putta matattiṉ mēṉmiyi viḷakkum nāḷ. 
tolkāppiyam 
kiṭittuḷḷa tamiḻ nūlkaḷil paḻamiyāṉatu tolkāppiyam. itu ilakkaṇa nūl; eṉṟālum itiluḷḷa poruḷatikārattil tamiḻariṉ vāḻkkip pāṅkukaḷ etirolikkiṉṟaṉa. 
(ō kaḻakak kālak kalikaḷ
kaḻakakkālat tamiḻariṉ vāḻkki makiḻcciyākak kaḻintatu eṉpatip pārttōm, attakiya makiḻcciyiṉūṭē avarkaḷiṉ palatarappaṭṭa kalikaḷip paṭittiruppatip pārkkiṟōm. avvāṟu paṭikkappeṟṟa kalikaḷik kaṭṭaṭakkali, ōviyakkali, ciṟpakkali mutaliyaṉavāka aṟiyalām. 
kaṭṭaṭakkali 
kaḻakakkālattil vīṭukaḷ, māḷikikaḷ, kōṭṭi kottaḷaṅkaḷ kaṭṭappeṟṟaṉa. kaṭṭaṭakkaliyeṉpatu mutalil iyaṟkiyāka aminta cantu, kuṭi, kuki mutaliyavaṟṟiliruntē tōṉṟiyirukka vēṇṭum. ākavē, pāṟikaḷilum, kuṉṟukaḷilum iyaṟkiyāka aminta kukikaḷ paṇṭik kālat tamiḻakattil kōyilkaḷākavum, muṉivarkaḷ vatiyum iṭaṅkaḷākavum payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. pāṇṭiya vēntar ittakiya kukikaḷic cākkiya muṉivarkaḷukku otukkiyatāka ilakkiyam, kalveṭṭuc cāṉṟukaḷiṉ liruntu aṟikiṟōm. maturi mānakaril vāḻnta antaṇariṉ vīṭukaḷ kuṉṟukuṭintāṟ pōlirun taṉaveṉṟu maturik kāñciyil kūṟap peṟuvatāl, kuṉṟukaḷik kuṭintu kōyilkaḷākap paṭikkum paḻakkam iruntati aṟiyalām. karuṅkaṟkaḷi oṉṟaṉ mēl oṉṟāka aṭukki amikkappeṟṟa karuṅkaṟ kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ, kuṭivarikaḷukkup piṉ ēṟpaṭṭirukkalām ittakiya karuṅkaṟkaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷik kaṟṟaḷikaḷ' eṉkiṟōm. ivaṟṟiṟkellām mūlamāṉa kuṭicip paṭivattiliruntu tōṉṟiya marattālāṉa kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ paṇṭit tamiḻakattil iruntirukka vēṇṭum. iṉṟum tilliyil cavi maṇṭapamum, kēraḷattiluḷḷa kōyilkaḷ palavum marakkaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷāyiruppati aṟiyalām. ceṅkaṟ kaḷiyum, cuṇṇak kārikaḷiyum payaṉpaṭuttik kaṭṭappeṟṟa kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷum, kaḻakakkālattil iruntaṉa. iṉṟiya akaḻvāyvil kaḻakakkālattilirunta ceṅkaṟ kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. itaṟkoru ciṟanta eṭuttukkāṭṭup putuccērikku arukiluḷḷa arikka mēṭu akaḻvāyvil kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatākum. ittakiya ceṅkaṟ kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ pala iruntaṉaveṉpataṟku ilakkiyac cāṉṟukaḷ ērāḷamā yuḷḷaṉa. kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅ kaṇṇaṉār eṉṉum pulavar ceppaṉiṭap peṟāta ceṅkaṟcuṭṭaṭam iṭintu pōṉati uvamiyākak kāṭṭi aḻakupaṭak kūṟiyuḷḷār'. kaḻakakkālac cōḻaṉ ceṅkaṇāṉ tirumāluk kum civaṉukkum eḻupatukkum mēṟpaṭṭa kōyilkaḷik kaṭṭiṉāṉ eṉṟu piṟkālattut tirumaṅkiyāḻvārum, tirunāvukkaracarum aḻaku paṭak kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. 
cilar kaḻakakkālattil karuṅkaṟkaṭṭaṭamē illiyeṉṟu kūṟu kiṉṟaṉar. ki.mu. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil vaṭintiyāvi āṇṭa acōkap pēraracar kālattil kavvi merukiṭum kali mikacciṟantu kāṇappaṭṭa teṉpati iṉṟum nām kaṇṭu viyakkiṟōm. mēlum, pāṟiyik kuṭintu kukiyic camikkum kaliyippaṟṟik kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷil kūṟappeṟukiṉṟatu. āyiṉ iyaṟkiyākak kiṭikkum karuṅkaṟkaḷikkoṇṭu kaṭṭaṭam amippatik kaḻakak kālat tamiḻar uṟutiyāka aṟintirukka vēṇṭum. mēlum ceṅkaṟ kaḷic camippa teṉpatu ōr ariya muyaṟciyālāṉa kaliyākum. attakiya muyaṟcikkum muṟpaṭṭa cuviyaṟivē curuṅkaṟkaṭṭaṭa mākum. itaṉ tāy marakkaṭṭaṭa meṉṟum, marakkaṭṭaṭattiṉ cēy karuṅkaṟkaṭṭaṭameṉṟum, ataṟkup piṉṉarē piṟa poruḷkaḷik koṇṭu kaṭṭum kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉ veṉṟum kaliñar ipi. ēval(E. B. Havell) kūṟuvatu īṇṭu. āyntu nōkkattakkatām. valivāṉa kōṭṭi kottaḷaṅkaḷil karuṅkallōṭu piṟa poruḷ kaḷum payaṉpaṭuttap peṟṟirukkalām. 
kaḻakakkālattil maṉṉarkaḷ vāḻnta kōnakarkaḷ tāmari malar vaṭivil amikkappeṟṟiruntaṉa. cōḻariṉ kōnakarāṉa kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattiṉ amippup paṟṟic cilappatikāram, maṇimēkali mutaliya ilakkiyaṅkaḷil kāṇalām. itu nīḷ catura vaṭivil amin tiruntatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ittakiya kōnakarkaḷ miḷikkāṭu kāvaṟkāṭukaḷ, akaḻikaḷ, puṟamatilkaḷ, akamatilkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟāl pātukākkap paṭṭaṉaveṉṟum, ivaṟṟiṟkuḷ aracaṉum, amiccarum, piṟa atikāri kaḷum vativataṟkāṉa palatarappaṭṭa kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉaveṉṟum aṟikiṟōm. akaḻiyi nirapputaṟkuc curuṅkit tūmpiṉ vaḻiyāka nīr ōṭivantateṉṟum, aḵtu yāṉiyiṉ tutikki pōṉṟu nīṇṭirunta teṉṟum kūṟappeṟukiṉṟatu. kōṭṭi valivākavum, pātukāppākavum iruntateṉṟum, matil uyarvākavum akalamākavum, tiṇmiyākavum, arumiyākavum iruntateṉṟum kūṟappeṟukiṉṟatu. kōṭṭikaḷ maṇṇālum, ceṅkallālum, karuṅkallālum kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. karuṅ kaṟkaḷi iṇippataṟkuc cempu, īyam ākiya māḻikaḷ urukki vārkkap peṟṟaṉa, matilkaḷ viṇṇuṟa vōṅki vaḷarntu niṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ mēl pantum, pāviyum, cilampum, taḻiyum aḻakuṟap poṟittu aṇi ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ mēṟpuṟattilum, uṭpuṟat tuḷikaḷilum palatarappaṭṭa poṟikaḷ poruttappaṭṭiruntaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, viṟpoṟi, kuraṅkuppoṟi, kavaṇ poṟi, yāṉippoṟi, tūṇṭiṟpoṟi, toṭakkuppoṟi, aṭuppup poṟi, kavippoṟi, kaḻuppoṟi, puti katavu, aiyavittulām, ciraṟpoṟi, paṉṟippoṟi, muracuppoṟi mutaliyaṉa kuṟippiṭattakkaṉa. 
kōṭṭiyiṉ vāyilkaḷ pōrvīrarkaḷ yāṉiyiṉ mītamarntu koṭikaḷiyēnti uṭcellum aḷavukku akaṉṟum, uyarntum iruntaṉa. ivi malikaḷik kuṭintāṟpōṉṟu amintiruntaṉa veṉṟu maturik kāñciyil kūṟappeṟukiṉṟatu. katavukaḷ kitērnta kammiyarāl ceyyap peṟṟavi: avi keṭāmalirukka ney pūcap paṭṭatu. iruppup paṭṭikaḷ avaṟṟiṉ mītu cuṟṟappaṭṭiruntaṉa. vil, puli, kayal ākiya ciṉṉaṅkaḷ muṟiyē cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiyar katavukaḷil poṟikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ivaṟṟit tavirap pala ciṟu katavukaḷum kōṭṭikaḷil iruntaṉa, 
āṟu kiṭantaṉṉa akal neṭun terukkaḷum kaḻakakkālattil iruntatāka neṭunalvāṭi mutaliya kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. mokañcātārō, arappā mutaliyaviṭaṅkaḷil kāṇap paṭum kaṭṭaṭaṅ kaḷum piṟa nākarikac ciṉṉaṅkaḷum ilemūriyāk kaṇṭam kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiyapōtu aṅkiruntu tappivaṭakkil kuṭiyēṟiya paṇṭiya tiraviṭa makkaḷiṉ kaṉavuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷēyeṉṟu iṉṟiya uruciya nāṭṭu maṇṇiyal āyvāḷarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ākavē, kaḻakakkālattu vīṭukaḷiṉ amippiyum, terukkaḷiṉ amippiyum mokañ cātārō, arappā nakaraṅkaḷil kaṇkūṭākak kāṇalām. 
maturi nakariṉ amippi maturikkāñci, neṭunalvāṭi, cilappatikāram mutaliya kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ciṟappuṟac ceppukiṉṟaṉa. in nakarilirunta nāḷaṅkāṭi, allaṅkāṭi ākiyavaṟṟip paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ ēṟkaṉavē kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. taṉittaṉiyāka aminta cērikaḷ paṟṟiyum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itippōlavē pūmpukār amippup paṟṟiyum ataṉ arukirunta maruvūrppākkam paṟṟiyum kūṟappaṭṭatu. iṉṟiya akaḻ vāyvil kaṭṭaṭakkaliyamippu veḷiyulakukku aṟivikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. maruvūrppākkam, paṭṭiṉappākkam ākiya iru pakutikaḷukkiṭiyil peruñcāli yoṉṟu iruntateṉṟum, ac cāliyiltāṉ nāḷaṅ cāṭi eṉṉum pakaṟcanti kūṭumeṉṟum aṟikiṟōm. maruvūrppākkat til toḻi lāḷarkaḷum, piṟa eḷiya makkaḷum vāḻntaṉar. paṭṭiṉap pākkattil maṉṉaṉum piṟa uyarkuṭi makkaḷum vāḻntaṉar. ākavē, vaṇikar vatinta māṭavītikaḷ, kōviyar vīti koṭittēr vīti, pīṭikit teru, antaṇar irukki mutaliya perunterukkaḷ palavum māṭa māḷikikaḷum paṭṭiṉappākkattil iruntaṉa. mēlum, amarar taruk kōṭṭam veṉyāṉikkōṭṭam, nākartaṇkōṭṭam, uccikkiḻāṉ kōṭṭam, ūrkkōṭṭam, vēṟ kōṭṭam, vaccirakkōṭṭam mutaliya kōṭṭaṅkaḷum, civaṉ, murukaṉ, tirumāl, palatēvaṉ, intiraṉ mutalāṉōrukkāṉa kōyilkaḷum iṅkiruntaṉa. "vāṉōṅku cimiyattu vāḷoli cittiyam' pūmpukāril iruntateṉṟu kūṟap paṭuvatāl cākkiyar kōyilkaḷum, ilaṅkoḷic cilātaḷamiṭappaṭṭa vāṉperu maṉṟam' aintu terukkaḷ kūṭumiṭattil iruntateṉṟu kūṟappaṭuvatāl camaṇar kōyilkaḷum iruntaṉaveṉpatu teḷivā kiṟatu. veḷḷiṭi. maṉṟam, ilañcimaṉṟam, neṭuṅkalmaṉṟam, pūtac catukkam, pāvimaṉṟam eṉṟa aivaki maṉṟak kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ iruntatākak kūṟappaṭuvatāl kaḻakak kālattil palatarappaṭṭa kaṭṭaṭaṅ kaḷum iruntaṉaveṉa aṟikiṟōm. pukāril mikac ciṟanta tuṟi mukamum, kalaṅkari viḷakkamum, īmappuṟaṅ kāṭukaḷum amikkap paṭṭiruntaṉa. kaṭaṟkariyiyaṭuttirunta pakuti "neytalaṅkāṉal' eṉap paṭṭatu. iṅku matikkuṇṭam cōmakuṇṭam), katirkkuṇṭam cūriyakuṇṭam eṉṉum īrērikaḷum kuḷaṅkaḷum) kāmavēḷukku oru kōyilum iruntaṉa. iṉṟiya akaḻvāyvāḷarāl kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattil kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷa putta vikāriyiṉ amippiyum aḷaviyum koṇṭum kaḻakak kālak kaṭṭaṭak kaliyik kaṇkūṭākak kaṇṭukaḷikkalām. paṭṭiṉappāliyil uṟikiṇaṟṟup puṟañcēri' eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappeṟum uṟikiṇaṟukaḷ pala kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattiṉ kaṭaṟkariyōraṅkaḷil kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṅkuk kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷa kuḷam cuṭṭa ceṅkallāl cuṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷatu. ataṟku uḷpuṟam nīr varavum, veḷippuṟam nīr cellavum ēntākap poṟiyiyal nuṭpattuṭaṉ amikkappaṭṭuḷḷa nīrppāti kaṇṭukaḷikkattakkatām. 
ivvāṟu kaḻakakkālattil taṉik kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷum, periya kōṭ ṭikaḷum, matilkaḷum, kuḷaṅkaḷum, ērikaḷum, iyantira vāvikaḷum, kaḻinīr vaṭikālkaḷum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. pantal, ciṟṟil, māḷikikaḷ, māṭaṅkaḷ, kōyilkaḷ, kōṭṭaṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa iṭattiṟkum payaṉukku mēṟpa amikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. kuvimāṭam, matakalimāṭam, arāmiyam, nilāmuṟṟam, muṉṟil, muṟṟam, paḷḷiyaṟi, uvaḷakam antappuram) mutaliyaṉa aḻakiya amippukaḷoṭum, katavukaḷ, cāḷaraṅkaḷ, nilikaḷ mutaliya amippukaḷuṭaṉ nalla kāṟṟōṭṭamum veḷiccamum peṟutaṟ kēṟpavum amikkap paṭṭiruntaṉa. ittakiya ēntukaḷ kōyilkaḷil kāṇappaṭavilli. avi maṉatti īrttup pakkuvappaṭuttum vakiyil camikkap paṭṭiruntaṉa. kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷil "mayaṉ matam' eṉṉum kaṭṭaṭak kalikkōṭpāṭu iruntatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. neṭunalvāṭi yil kuṟippiṭap peṟum im mayaṉ vīti' iṉṟu kēraḷattil kiṭit tuḷḷa 'mayaṉ matam' eṉṉum vaṭamoḻik kaṭṭaṭakkali nūlil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. innāl kiranta eḻuttil eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṉ mūvalūral kaḻakakkālattil iruntu, aḻintupaṭṭirukkalām. 
kaḻakakkālattil kaṭṭaṭakkaliyil kiyāḷappeṟṟa palvēṟu aḷavu muṟikaḷip paṟṟiyum kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷiliruntu aṟikiṟōm, eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, mātavi āṭiya mēṭi tariyiliruntu oru kōl uyarattil amintiruntatu. aḵtu ēḻukōl akaliṭattiyum eṇkōl nīḷattiyum koṇṭiruntatu. araṅkattiṉ mēlminta uttarappalaki nālukōl uyaramiruntatu eṉak kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṉṟatu. ivvaraṅkattiṉ irumaruṅkilum paṭik kaṭṭukaḷ amikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa eṉavum kūṟap paṭṭuḷḷatu. iṅku oru kōl eṉpatu ēṟattāḻa 2 aṭi viral ( aṅkulamākum. ivvāṟu vittuk kaṇakkiṭṭāl mātavi āṭiya araṅkattiṉ aḷavi yaṟintu koḷḷalām. 
ōviyakkali 
kaḻakakkālattil ōviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭuvataṟkeṉṟē aracamāḷikiyil taṉimāṭam otukkappaṭṭiruntatu eṉpatik kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷiliruntu aṟikiṟōm. pāṇṭiyaṉ naṉmāṟaṉ taṉ ōviya māṭattil taṅkiyirukkum pōtu iṟantupaṭṭatāl 'pāṇṭiyaṉ cittira māṭattut tuñciya naṉmāṟaṉ' eṉṟu puṟanāṉūṟṟil cuṭṭap paṭukiṟāṉ. maturikkāñciyil māṅkuṭi marutaṉār pāṇṭiyariṉ ōviya māṭattik 'kayaṅkaṇṭaṉ viyaṅkuṭi nakarattuc cempiyaṉ ṟaṉṉa ceñcuvar puṉintu' eṉṟu vivarittuḷḷār. itiliruntu kiḻakkālattil perumpālāṉa ōviyaṅkaḷ cepputtakaṭukaḷip pōl paḷapaḷappākameḻukappaṭṭa cuvarppakutiyil tīṭṭappaṭṭaṉaveṉpati aṟikiṟōm. mēlum, ic cuvar veṇcāntu pūcappeṟṟu vaḻavaḻap pākkap peṟṟa teṉṟum, atil taccar, kammiyar mutali yōriṉ kivaṇṇattiṉālāṉa kaṭṭaṭattiṉ uruvaṅkaḷum, ceṭi, koṭi, pūmutaliya iyaṟkik kāṭcikaḷum tīṭṭappeṟṟaṉaveṉ ṟum aṟikiṟōm. nakkīrar pāṭiya neṭunalvāṭiyil pāṇṭiyaṉuṭiya cittiramāṭattil veṇcāntum, nīlamiyum, cempuniṟamum, pacciniṟa mum koṇṭa vaṇṇaṅkaḷ, ōviyaṅkaḷ variyap payaṉpaṭṭaṉa veṉṉum ceytiyaṟiyappaṭukiṟatu. ittakiya cittiramāṭam maturikku arukiluḷḷa tirupparaṅkuṉṟam maliyiluḷḷa murukaṉ kōyili yaṭuttum iruntateṉpatikkuṉṟampūtaṉār eṉṉum pulavar paripāṭalil kūṟukiṟār. im māṭattil tiṭṭappeṟṟa ōviyaṅkaḷi ikkōyilukku varum makkaḷ ceṉṟu kaṇṭukaḷittaṉar. itil kāmaṉ, irati, akaliki, akavitiyiṭam cellum intiraṉ, akalikiyiṉ kaṇavaṉ kavutam muṉivaṉ, ammuṉivaṉik kaṇṭatum pūṉiyiṉ uruvaṅkoṇṭu ōṭum intiraṉ mutaliyōr ōviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. itēpōṉṟa cittirakkūṭam tiruccikku arukiluḷḷa tiruveṟumpūril iruntatik kalveṭṭuc cāṉṟu mūlam aṟikiṟōm. cittirakkūṭam eṉum peyaril ōviyakkūṭaṅkaḷ tillip peruṅkōyil, tiruvaraṅkattup peruṅ kōyil ākiya iṭaṅkaḷil cittirakkūṭam iruntataṟkāṉa kuṟippukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. 
mēṟkaṇṭa eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷiliruntu kaḻakakkālattil ōviyak kaliyi vaḷarppataṟkeṉṟē aracarkaḷ taṅkaḷ araṇmaṉiyi yaṭuttuc cittirakkūṭamoṉṟittaṉiyē cuṭṭi vittiruntaṉar eṉpati yum, ittakiya cittirakkūṭaṅkaḷik kōyil pōṉṟa puṉitaviṭaṅkaḷi lum kaṭṭi ik kaliyip pōṟṟikkāttaṉar eṉpatiyum aṟikiṟōm. kaṇṇil kāṇumāṟu poruḷkaḷi ōviyaṅkaḷākat tīṭṭum iv vōviyak kaliñarkaḷ kaḻakak kālattil "kaṇṇuḷ viṉiñar', eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭṭaṉar. tolkāppiyattil vīrakkal allatu naṭukal eṉpatu paṟṟiya ceyti kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṉṟatu. pōril viḻuppuṇpaṭṭu iṟakkum vīraṉukku ittakiya naṭukal naṭuvatu vaḻakkam. avaṉip putittaviṭattil allatu avaṉ viḻuppuṇpaṭṭu viḻuntaviṭattil avaṉ niṉivāka naṭappaṭum akkallil av vīraṉiṉ uruvattit tīṭṭuvatu vaḻakkam, 
kaḻakakkāla naṭaṉa makaḷir naṭaṉattōṭu vēṟupala kalikaḷiyum kaṟṟaṉar. avaṟṟuḷ ōviyakkaliyum oṉṟāku meṉṟu cilappatikāram kūṟukiṟatu. mātavi naṭaṉamāṭiya araṅkattiṉ mēṟkūriyil pala ōviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa veṉṟum, im mēṟkūri 'ōviya vitāṉam' eṉappaṭṭateṉṟum, māḻiyālāṉa kēṭayaṅkaḷilum kaṇcuvar ōviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉaveṉṟum cilappatikāram ceppukiṟatu. itip pōlavē taliyālaṅkāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa neṭuñceḻiyaṉiṉ kōpperuntēvi paṭuttirunta paḷḷiyaṟiyiṉ mēṟ kūriyilum palavaki ōviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappeṟṟiruntaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ tiṅkaḷiṉ pakkattil urōkiṇi iruppatupōṉṟa ōviyamum oṉṟākum. taṉ kaṇavaṉākiya tiṅkaḷōṭu urōkiṇi iṇintiruppatupōl tāṉum taṉ kaṇavaṉōṭu iṇintirukka muṭiyavilliyeṉṟu eṇṇik kōpperuntēvi perumūccu viṭṭāḷeṉṟu neṭunalvāṭi kūṟukiṟatu. 
celvar vāḻum vāḻṉic cuvarkaḷiṉ mītum, māṭaṅkaḷiṉ mītum tēvar mutal ellāvuyirkaḷiyum kuṟikkattakka oviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭap peṟṟiruntaṉaveṉṟum, avvōviyaṅkaḷ veṇcuti pūciya paḷapaḷappāṉa parappil tīṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉaveṉṟum maṇimēkali -- kūṟukiṟatu. ittakiya eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷiliruntu kaḻakakkālattil taṉiyār vīṭukaḷilum ōviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭum vaḻakkam iruntateṉṟum araṇmaṉiyil palvēṟiṭaṅkaḷilum, paḷḷiyaṟiyilum ōviyaṅkaḷ iṭampeṟṟiruntaṉaveṉṟum aṟikiṟōm. ' 
cuvarkaḷil maṭṭumaṉṟit tuṇiccīlikaḷilum ōviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭum vaḻakkam kaḻakakkālattil iruntatu. ivvāṟu ōviyam tīṭṭappaṭṭat tuṇic cīliyip paṭā am' allatu 'paṭam' eṉṟē aḻittaṉar.tāṉ virumpiya peṇṇi maṇakka muṭiyāta kātalaṉ oru tuṇiyil avaḷuṭiya uruvatti ōviyamāka varintu koṇṭu maṭalēṟuvāṉ eṉṟu akapporuḷ nūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. ōviyaṅkaḷiyamittu āṭi neytal kaḻakak kālattil kāṇap paṭṭa oru ciṟanta kaliyākum. itaṉi maṇimēkaliyil varum, 
''vittakaṉ iyaṟṟiya viḷaṅkiya kaviḷic 
cittirac ceyaṟkip paṭām pōrttatuvē 
ōppat tōṉṟiya uvavaṉam" 
- eṉṉum varikaḷāl aṟikiṟōm, 
oviyaṅkaḷi mutalil mēlōṭṭamāka uruvattiṉ amippi maṭṭum kīṟṟāka (putlinc) varivar. itaṉic caṅkakālattil “puṉiyā ōviyam' eṉṟu aḻittaṉar. itaṟkup piṉṉarē vēṇṭiya vaṇṇaṅ kaḷip payaṉpaṭutti ivaṟṟi uyirōṭṭamuḷḷa ōviyaṅkaḷākku vārkaḷ. ivvōviyak kaliyē kaṭṭaṭakkali, ciṟpakkali, naṭaṉak kāli mutaliya ellāk kalikaṭkum aṭippaṭiyāka amintatu. oru kaṭṭaṭattik kaṭṭuvataṟku muṉpum, ciṟpattic cetukkuvataṟku muṉpum avaṟṟi ōviyamāka varintu koṇṭu, piṉṉarē kaṭṭaṭam kaṭṭum paṇiyum ciṟpam ceyyum paṇiyum toṭaṅkappeṟṟaṉa. ittakiya ōviyaṅ kaḷip paṟṟiya ōviya nūloṉṟu kaḻakakkālat tiliruntatu eṉpati aṭiyārkku nallār kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār. 
ōviyatti vaṭṭikic ceyti yeṉṟu maṇimēkali kuṟippiṭu kiṉṟatu. vaṭṭikiyeṉṉum peyar, ōviyam variyappaṭum tararikiyāl (Brush) vantatu, ōviyaṅkaḷit tīṭṭuvataṟku muṉ it tūrikiyāl vaṇṇaṅkaḷit toṭṭuk kuḻikkum palakikku vaṭṭikip palaki yeṉpatu peyar. tūriki, pātirip pūvip pōliruntateṉpataṉi naṟṟiṇiyiṉ kūṟṟāl aṟikiṟōm. ak kālattup peṇkaḷi ōviyattip pōluḷḷaṉareṉṟum, ōrmi (cintaṉi) yilāḻntu aciyātiruppavarkaḷi ōviyaṅkaḷippōl uḷḷaṉareṉṟum uvami kāṭṭip pēcuvatu vaḻakkam. itaṉic cīvakacintāmaṇi, maṇimēkali mutaliya nūlkaḷil kāṇalām. 
oḷiyak kaliñaṉ naki, uvaki, avalam, vīram mutaliya eṇvaki meyppāṭukaḷiyum iruttal, kiṭattal, niṟṟal mutaliya oṉpāṉ vakiyāṉa viruttikaḷiyum taṉatu ōviyaṅkaḷil cittarittuk kāṭṭiṉāṉ eṉpatip peruṅkatiyil virikkak kāṇalām. cīvakacintāmaṇiyiṉ āciriyarāṉa tiruttakkattēvar maram, ceṭi, koṭi pōṉṟa nilit tiṇikaḷiyum, māṉ, maṟi pōṉṟa vilaṅkukaḷiyum aḻakiya uṟuppu . kaḷiyuṭiya peṇkaḷiyum, teyvattiṉ uruvattiyum, mayiṟpoṟi pōṉṟa viyattaku vimāṉattiyum tīṭṭiya kaliñarkaḷippaṟṟi vivarittuk kūṟukiṟār. ākavē, kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷil kūṟappaṭum ōviyak kali piṟkālattilum toṭarntu pōṟṟik kākkappaṭṭatu eṉpatic cīvakacintāmaṇi mutaliya nūlkaḷiṉāl aṟiyalām. 
ciṟpakkali 
pōril puṇpaṭṭu māṇṭavīrarkaḷukku naṭukal naṭuvatiliruntum, kaṟpuṭiya makaḷirukkup pattiṉikkal (makācati naṭuvatiliruntum ciṟpaṅkaḷic camikkum vaḻakkam ēṟpaṭṭateṉpatik kaḻakakkāla ilakkiyaṅkaḷiliruntu aṟikiṟōm. itaṉit tolkāppiyam - poruḷatikāram - puṟattiṇiyiyal 5 ām pāṭṭiliruntu aṟiyalām. avvāṟu naṭukal naṭṭu avviṭattil 'vīracāli' eṉṉumoru cuṭṭaṭattiyeḻuppi, iṟantupaṭṭa vīraṉatu pukaḻip pāṇarkaḷik koṇṭu pāṭumpaṭi ceyvatum akkāla vaḻakkamāka iruntatu. ittakiya vīrakkal naṭutalum, pattiṉip peṇṭirukkum "catikkal' eṭuppatum kaḻakakkālattiṟkup piṟakum tamiḻakattil iruntuvanta marapukaḷ eṉpati yum ivi vaṭanāṭṭil illāta tamiḻarkaḷukkēyuriya marapukaḷ eṉpati yum piṟkālap puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāli pōṉṟa nāykaḷiliruntum aṟiyalām. 
ki.pi. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻutappaṭṭa tivākara nikaṇṭil kal, māḻi, ceṅkal, maram, maṇ, cuti, maruppu, carukkari, arakku, meḻuku ākiya pattup poruḷkaḷik koṇṭē ciṟpaṅkaḷ ceyyap paṭṭaṉa veṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. maṇimēkaliyil mācillāta maramum, maṇṇum, kallum ciṟpam ceyvataṟkuc ciṟantavi yeṉṟum, ivaṟṟālāṉa ciṟpaṅkaḷ pala pūmpukāril iruntaṉa veṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. intira viḻāviṉpōtu pūmpukārukku vanta makkaḷ an nakarilirunta māṭa māḷikikaḷil vāṉavar mutalāṉa teyvavuruvaṅkaḷum, piṟavuyirkaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷum ciṟpaṅ kaḷākavum, ōviyaṅkaḷākavum kaṇṇik kavarumāṟu kaḷituṟa viḷaṅkiyatik kaṇṭu viyantu niṉṟaṉareṉpati maṇimēkali yiṉ vāyilāka aṟikiṟōm. kaḻakakkāl nakaraṅkaḷil uyarntu niṉṟa māṭaṅkaḷilum, māṭivīṭukaḷilum, nilāmuṟṟaṅkaḷilum palatarappaṭṭa ciṟpaṅkaḷ iruntatip perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭiyum kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. maṉṉarkaḷiṉ araṇmaṉikaḷiyum, piṟa kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷiyum amippa varcuḷ ciṟanta nūlaṟi pulavarkaḷāka iruntatōṭu, kaṇitam, vāṉanūl, nimittikam, poṟiyiyal mutaliyavaṟṟilum tērcciyaṭintirun tārkaḷ. ittakiya taṅkaḷ aṟivik koṇṭu araṇmaṉikaḷ amittum, ticikkēṟpat teyvap paṭimaṅkaḷiyamittum piṟa uruvaṅkaḷic camittum nākarikattip paṭittārkaḷeṉpati neṭunalvāṭi pōṉṟa kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷiliruntu aṟikiṟōm. 
kaḻakakkālat tamiḻakattil irunta kōyilkaḷ, avaṟṟilirunta teyvap paṭimikaḷ mutaliyavi paṟṟic cilappatikārattil kuṟippukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. piṟavā yākkip periyōṉ kōyil civaṉ kōyil), aṟumukac cevvēḷ aṇitikaḻ kōyil, vālvaḷi mēṉi vāliyōṉ palarāmaṉ) kōyil, nīlamēṉi neṭiyōṉ tirumāl) kōyil mutaliyaṉa iruntaṉaveṉpatikkūṟum cilappatikārac ceytikaḷi viruntu civaṉ, murukaṉ, palarāmaṉ, tirumāl ākiya kaṭavuḷariṉ uruvaṅ cuḷum, vaṇṇaṅkaḷum paṭima vaṭivaṅkaḷil teḷivupaṭuttappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatiyaṟiyalām. 
maṇimēkaliyil cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭattil ivvulakattiṉ pōliki yuruvamoṉṟu maṇṇīṭṭil ceytu vikkappaṭṭirunta ceyti kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṟatu. atil ceytu vikkappaṭṭirunta ulaka vuruṇṭiyip(globe) paṟṟiya vaṇṇaṉiyum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. "kaṭal cūḻntuḷḷatu, ataṉ naṭuvil mērumali, mērumaliyic cuṟṟilum ēḻuvakik kuṉṟukaḷ, nāṉilam kāṭṭum peruntīvu, cuṟṟilum ōrāyiram ciṟu tīvukaḷ, aṅkuḷḷa nila amippukaḷ, aṅku vāḻnta uyiriṉaṅkaḷ, cūḻalkaḷ aṉittum amikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. itu kāṇpōr aṟivu peṟum vakiyil amikkappaṭṭiruntatu. itu “mayaṉ' eṉṉum poṟiyāḷarāl amikkappaṭṭāṟ pōṉṟu iruntatu." 
cuḻacukkālattil puttari vaṇaṅkuvataṟku, avaruṭiya pātapīṭiki paṭimamākac ceyyappeṟṟut toḻappaṭṭatu eṉpati maṇimēkali yum. cilappatikāramum kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. piṟkālattiltāṉ puttarukku uruvam kaṟpikkappaṭṭu avaruṭiya irunta kōlattiyum, niṉṟa kōlatti yum, kiṭanta kōlattiyum amittārkaḷ. itē muṟiyip piṉpaṟṟit tāṉ viṇavar kaḷum tirumāliṉ uruvattiyamittārkaḷ. itaṉāṟ nāṉ cila aṟiñarkaḷ putta vikārikaḷi aḻittut tirumāl kōyilka ḷākak kaṭṭiṉareṉṟu muḻakkamiṭukiṉṟaṉar. kaḻakakkālattil teyvat tiruvuruvaṅkaḷip paṭimaṅkaḷāka vittu vaṇaṅkuvataṟku īṭākat teyvaṅkaḷiṉ ciṉṉaṅkaḷic ciṟpa vaṭivilamittu vaṇaṅkiṉārkaḷ eṉpatiyum aṟikiṟōm. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka murukaṉi vaṇaṅkuvataṟku avaṉuṭiya vēliyum, intiraṉi vaṇaṅkuvataṟku avaṉuṭiya vaccirappaṭi allatu avaṉuṭiya veḷḷiyāṉi, kaṟpakattaru ākiya vaṟṟiyum vittu vaṇaṅkiṉārkaḷ. itaṉāltāṉ vēṟkōṭṭam, vaccirakkōṭṭam, veḷyāṉikkōṭṭam, amararitarukkōṭṭam mutaliya palatarappaṭṭa kōṭṭaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya ceytikaḷic cilappatikārat til kāṇkiṟōm. tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭiyil, murukaṉ kiyilēntum vēliyum, cēvaṟ koṭiyiyum makkaḷ vaṇaṅkiyatāka aṟikiṟōm, ivvāṟu tamiḻakattil vaḷarnta marapu muṟiyip piṉpaṟṟittāṉ puttaruṭiya pātapīṭiki pōtimaram mutaliya ciṉṉaṅkaḷi vaṇaṅkuvatu kaḻakakkālattilēyē vaḻakkāṟṟil vantateṉpatu cilappatikāram, maṇimēkali ākiyavaṟṟāl aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatu. piṉṉartāṉ kaṭavuḷarum, kaṭavuḷariṉ kuṭumpaṅkaḷum utirit tēvatikaḷum ciṟpaṅkaḷ ṉākavum cittiraṅkaḷākavum muḻuvuruvil paṭikkap peṟṟaṉa, 
teyvavuruvaṅkaḷēyaṉṟi āṇ, peṇ, ceṭikoṭi, vilaṅkukaḷ, tiṅkaḷ katiravaṉ, viṇmīṉkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ uruvaṅkaḷiyum kaḻakak kāla makkaḷ ciṟpaṅkaḷākac camittārkaḷ, 
''nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulam tillāṉ eḻilnalam 
maṇmāṇ puṉipāvi yaṟṟu " 
eṉak kūṟum tirukkuṟaḷiliruntu maṇṇālum makkaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉaveṉpati aṟiyalām. aḻakāṉa āṭavaṉip paṟṟi varuṇikkum pōtu avaṉ vallāṉ iyaṟṟiya pāvi pōl iruntā ṉeṉṟu maturik kāñciyil kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. tēr, cakaṭam, vaṇṭil, kaṭṭil, irukki, maṇimuṭi mutaliyaṉa ceyyum kaliñarkaḷum, vāḷ, vēl mutaliyaṉa ceyyum kammiyarum, kaṭṭaṭap pulavarum, maṇṇīṭ ṭāḷarum, cuṟṟaccarum, poṟkollarum ittakiya paṭimaṅkaḷ ceyvatil kūṭṭuṟavākac ceyalpaṭṭaṉar eṉpati maṇimēkaliyil kāṇkiṟōm. ivarkaḷukkellām talimiyēṟṟa ciṟpiyip perun taccaṉ' eṉṟaṉar. ittakiya toḻilkaḷ yāvum marapu koṭi vaḻiyāka vaḷarntaṉa veṉpatip perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭi, akanāṉūṟu, cilappatikāram mutaliya nūlkaḷiliruntu aṟikiṟōm. 
tamiḻakattilirunta palatarappaṭṭa marapuvaḻik kaliñarōṭu piṟanāṭṭuk kaliñarum vantu iṇintu paṇiyāṟṟiṉareṉpati maṇimēkali kūṟukiṟatu. vēntaṉiṉ pūṅkā maṇṭapatti amippatil makata viṉiñar, marāṭṭak kammiyar, avantikkollar, yavaṉat taccar ākiyōr tamiḻakattuk kaliñarōṭu iṇintu ceyalpaṭṭaṉar. ic ceytiyiliruntu ovvoru kiviṉiñarum ovvoru toḻilil nuṭpat tiṟamiyip peṟṟiruntaṉar eṉpatiyum, ittakiyat toḻil tiṟami koṭivaḻiyāka vaḷarntiruntatōṭu, nāṭu vaḻiyākavum vaḷarntu, kalikku ākkamum ūkkamum tantatu eṉpatiyum aṟikiṟōm. itaṉāl tāṉ ittakiya nuṇṇiya tiṟami paṭitta kaliñarkaḷi nuṇkalik kammiyar, kiviṉi muṟṟiya nuṇkalikkollar, curuntoḻiṟ kollar eṉak kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. pāvi viḷakku amippatil yavaṉarē taliciṟantavar eṉṟu neṭunalvāṭikūṟukiṟatu. 
(ō) kaḷappirar
kaḻakacaṅkak kālam tōrāyamākak ki.pi. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil muṭintatu. ataṟkuppiṉ ki.pi. nāṉkām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal āṟām nāṟṟāṇṭu vari tamiḻakattil kaḷappirar" eṉṉum putiya iṉattavar tōṉṟi, tamiḻaka varalāṟṟik 'kaḷaṅkam aṭiyac ceytaṉar. ik kaḷappirariṉ tāyakam etu? avarkaḷiṉ paṇṭiya varalāṟu yātu? eṉpaṉa pōṉṟavi iṉṉamum āyntu muṭivu kāṇap peṟāmal uḷḷaṉa. āṉāl, ivarkaḷip paṟṟik ki.pi. eṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil māṟaṉ caṭiyaṉ eṉappaṭum jaṭi laparāntaka pāṇṭiyaṉāl veḷiyiṭappeṟṟa vēḷvikkuṭic ceppēṭukaḷil mutaṉmutalāka aṟikiṟōm.pāṇṭiyar nāṭṭik kaḷappirar veṉṟaṉar eṉṟum ataṉi jaṭilaparāntakaṉ mīṇṭum kippaṟṟiṉāṉ eṉṟum iv vēḷvikkuṭic ceppēṭṭi liruntu aṟikiṟōm. mēlum, kaḷappirar kaliyaracaṉ eṉpāṉ maṉṉarkaḷ palari veṉṟu nāṭukaḷik kippaṟṟikkoṇṭāṉ eṉpatiyum avaṉip pāṇṭiya maṉṉaṉ kaṭuṅkōṉ veṉṟu avaṉiṭamirunta nāṭukaḷittāṉ kippaṟṟik koṇṭatiyum icceppēṭṭiliruntu aṟikiṟōm 
ik kaḷappirar yāvar, eṅkiruntu vantaṉar eṉṟa viṉākkaḷukku iṉṉamum uriya viṭikaḷ illi. 'kaḷappāḷar' eṉṟa iṉattavarē kaḷappirar eṉṟu aṟiyappaṭṭaṉar eṉavum, ivarkaḷ tamiḻakattic cērntavarkaḷ eṉṟum piṭicīṉivāciyaṅkār mutalāṉōr karutukiṉṟa ṉar. tiruvēṅkaṭamalikku arukil vāḻnta 'kaḷavar' eṉpārē kaḷappirar eṉṟu ecu. kiruṭṭiṇacāmi aiyaṅkār, ār.cattiyanātiyar mutalāṉōr karutukiṉṟaṉar. "kaḷvar' eṉṟa collē piṉṉar 'kaḷappirar' eṉa maruviya tākaṭiē.kōpinātarāyar kūṟukiṟār. mēlum, muttariyar ik kaḷappirar iṉattavarē eṉṟum avar karutukiṟār. teṉṉakattil vāḻnta oḷiyar eṉṟa vakuppic cārntavartām kaḷappirar eṉpatu ṭivi. makāliṅkattiṉ karuttu. kuṟumparkaḷ oṉṟucērntu kalakam viḷivittu mūvēntariyum veṉṟu nāṭṭik kippaṟṟiyāṇṭatāl avarkaḷ 'kaḷappirar' eṉap paṭṭaṉar eṉpatum oru kōṭpāṭākum. 
mēṟkūṟiya yāvum ūkaṅkaḷē. āyiṉ kaḷappirar yāvar? avarkaḷ tamiḻarkaḷ allar. eṅkiruntu vantaṉar? kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭiliruntu vantaṉar. cāṉṟukaḷ yāvi? (periyapurāṇam, kallāṭam mutaliyavaṟṟil kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. karunāṭaka maṉṉaṉoruvaṉ pāṇṭiya nāṭṭik kippaṟṟi yāṇṭāṉ eṉṟum, avaṉ kālattil tamiḻakattil civam cāyntu camaṇam taḻittatu eṉṟum ivaṟṟiliruntu aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mēlum kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭil pēḷūrukku arukil kiṭittuḷḷa kalveṭṭilum, ōcupeṭṭukku arukil kiṭittuḷḷa oru vīrakkallilum ivarkaḷip paṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ik kuṟippukaḷiliruntu iṅku vatintavar 'kaḷpōrar' eṉṟum, ivarkaḷ nanti maliyaṭiyil ki. pi. eṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari āṇṭaṉar eṉṟum, piṉṉar avarkaḷuṭiya nāṭu cāḷukkiyar, mēlik kaṅkar nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ iṇikkappaṭṭu viṭṭatu eṉṟum aṟikiṟōm. itaṉi yāpparuṅkalamum valiyuṟuttu kiṟatu. 
ivarkaḷ ēṉ tamiḻakam vantaṉar? cātavākaṉap pēraracāl nerukkap paṭṭum, camaya veṟikoṇṭum ki.pi. nāṉkām nūṟṟāṇṭil ivarkaḷ tamiḻakam pōntaṉar. ivarkaḷ camaṇa, cākkiya camayaṅkaḷic cārnta varkaḷ eṉpatum, tamiḻakattil ēṟkeṉavē kuṭikoṇṭirunta ic camayaṅkaḷukku ūkkamum, ākkamum tēṭavē ivarkaḷ tamiḻakam pōntaṉ reṉpatum ūkittaṟiya vēṇṭiyavi yākum. ivarkaḷ tamiḻakam pōnta nērattil camaṇam, cākkiyam puttam cōḻa, pallava nāṭukaḷil cāykā luṭaṉ viḷaṅkiṉa. eṉavētāṉ camaṇattip parappum nōkkuṭaṉ ivarkaḷ kipi 47-l tamiḻakattil tiramiḷa caṅkam' eṉṟa kaḻakatti niṟuvic camaṇacamayak kōṭpāṭukaḷip parappiṉar. caṅkakālantoṭṭē kaṉṉaṭar tamiḻakattiṟku vantu kuṭiyēṟiya vaṇṇam iruntatāl kaḷappirar tiṭṭavaṭṭamākcipi nāṉkām nūṟṟāṇṭiltāṉ tamiḻakam pōntaṉar eṉṟum kūṟamuṭiyavilli. paḻanimaliyil ēṟkeṉavē kuṭiyēṟiya teṉkaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭavar ataṉip 'paṉṟināṭu' eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ āḷumi ceytatiyum aṟikiṟōm. ākak kaṉṉaṭar kipi. nāṉkām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari kūṭṭam kūṭṭamākat tamiḻakattil kuṭiyēṟip peruvāriyāṉa nilapparappil āḷumi peṟṟaṉar eṉpatip palvēṟu cāṉṟukaḷāl aṟikiṟōm. avvāṟu vantavarkaḷiṉ talivarkaḷip paṟṟiyum avarkaḷ naṭattiya pōrkaḷip paṟṟiyum vivaramilli. āyiṉum " accutavikkantaṉ' eṉpāṉ māperum paṭi tiraṭṭi vantu tamiḻnāṭṭu mūvēntar kaḷiyum veṉṟāṉ eṉṟa ceyti kipi, aintām nāṟṟāṇṭil vāḻnta putta tattar eṉpār eḻutiya 'viṉiya viṉiccayā' eṉṟa nūlil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. inta accuḷikkantaṉ cōḻa nāṭṭil āṭci purintāṉ eṉpatiyum, ivaṉ putta camayattavaṉ eṉpatum in nūliliruntu aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṉip pōlavē toṇṭi nāṭṭilum kaḷappirar āṇṭaṉar eṉpatum aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu. civaṉaṭiyāril oruvarāṉa "kūṟṟuva nāyaṉar' 'kaḷantimaṉṉar kaḷappirar' eṉṟu cēkkiḻār kūṟuvatiliruntu itiyaṟikiṟōm. ivvāṟu oṉṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa kaḷappirar kuṭumpaṅkaḷ tamiḻakattil kuṭiyēṟi āṅkāṅkē āṇṭami aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu. 
kaḷappirar āṭciyāl tamiḻakattil viḷintavi yāvi? amiti keṭṭatu; kalakaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa; makkaḷ vāḻkki cīrkulintatu; tamiḻ marapu nūlkaḷ aḻintaṉapōṉṟa kuṟṟac cāṭṭukaḷē kḷappirar āṭciyip paṟṟik kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. civattil nāṭṭam koṇṭavarē ittakiya kuṟṟaccāṭṭukaḷik kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. kaḷappirarāl tamiḻk kaḻakam aḻiyavilli . ēṟkeṉavē kaḻakakkālam muṭintu, kaḻakamaruviya kālam toṭaṅkiyiruntatu. kaḷappirar vēṟṟu moḻiyiṉar camaṇa, cākkiya camayaṅkaḷip piṉpaṟṟiyavarkaḷ; avarkaḷ tamiḻakattil āḷumi peṟṟa pōtu ic camayaṅkaḷ cāykāluṭaṉ iruntaṉa. ākavē, avarkaḷiṉ toṭakkakāla ākkam ac camayaṅkaḷiṉ koḷḷiṭa moḻikaḷāy viḷaṅkiya pāli, pirākirutam, camaṟkirutam ākiya moḻikaḷukkē payaṉ paṭṭatu, itu varalāṟṟup pōkkil vaḷarnta putiya iyalē yaṉṟik kaḷappirarāl viḷinta kēṭu eṉil muṟṟum poruntātu. āyiṉ, piṉṉar vanta pallavar kālattil camaṟkirutam ākkam peṟṟatēṉ? 
kaḷappirar āṭciyil camaṇarkaḷ tamiḻip pēṇik kāttaṉar. tamiḻil pala ariya ilakkaṇa ilakkiya nūlkaḷ ivarkaḷāl eḻutap peṟṟaṉa. ēṟkeṉavē ki.pi, 470 -l vaccirananti eṉpāṉ amitta "tiramiḷa caṅkam' camaṇa camaya vaḷarccikku uṟutuṇiyāka niṉṟu ittakiya naralkaḷ veḷivara utaviyatu. nālaṭiyār pōṉṟa patiṉeṇ kīḻkkaṇakku nūlkaḷum, cilappatikāram, maṇimēkali ākiya nūlkaḷum camaṇa cākkiya camayac cārpuṭiya nūlkaḷē. ivi kaḻakam maruviya kālat til tōṉṟiyaviyē. ākavē, kaḷappirar āṭciyāl tamiḻakattil putiya polivu ēṟpaṭṭatu, moḻi vaḷarcci yēṟpaṭṭatu eṉpatiyē uṇaramuṭikiṟatu. āṉāl, kaḷappirar tamiḻakattil iruṇṭa kālatti ēṟpaṭuttiviṭṭaṉar eṉap palarum pulampukiṉṟaṉar. avvāṟē eḻutiyum nampavittu viṭṭaṉar. ivarkaḷiṉ pulampalukkum kāraṇam illāma villi, kaḷappirar varukiyāl, tamiḻakattil kālkoṇṭirunta 'varuṇācirama tarumam' āṭṭaṅkaṇṭatu: pārppaṉiyam nacintu pōyiṟṟu. camaṇamum, puttamum ivaṟṟi oḻikkavē vīṟiṭṭeḻunta camayaṅka ḷeṉpatiyum, kaḷappirar ic camaya vīrarkaḷeṉpatiyum maṉattiṟ koṇṭu nōkkiṉāl uṇmi oḷi teriyum. intiya varalāṟṟil eppoḻutellām eṅkellām pārppaṉiyam ceḻittuk kāṇappaṭum kiṟatō appoḻutellām aṅkellām poṟkālam' eṉap pōṟṟappaṭuvatiyum, pārppaṉiyattiṟku māṟākat tōṉṟum āṭci, allatu māṟākat tiḷikkum karuttuccuṭar nilavum pōtellām "iruṇṭa kālam' eṉa iḻitturikkappaṭuvatiyum kāṇkiṟōm. ulaka varalāṟṟilēyē ciṟanta iṭattip peṟuvatu acōkar kālam' intiya varalāṟṟil puttam taliciṟantu viḷaṅkiyatum acōkar kālamē. āṉāl, itaṉip poṟkālam eṉap pōṟṟiṉarā? illi, āriyam ceḻittu, miḷirnta kuptar kālattiyē poṟkālam eṉkiṉṟaṉar. meḷariyar kālattil puttakak kalikaḷum, aṟiviyal nūlkaḷum ērāḷamāyt tōṉṟiṉa; ulaka nāṭukaḷilellām paraviṉa; ulaka nāṭukaḷiliruntu palvēṟu paṇpāṭukaḷ intiyāviṟkuḷ nuḻintaṉa. āyiṉum, itaṉip 'poṟkālam' eṉṟaṉarilli. itip pōlavē puttam pukaḻpeṟṟa kucāṉar kālamum, varttaṉar kālamum poṟkālamākavilli, ivi yāvum āriyariṉ 'varuṇācirama tarumattiṟkum vēḷvi muṟikkum māṟākac ceyalpaṭṭa āṭciyamippukaḷ. tamiḻakattilum āriyattiṟku ēṟṟamaḷitta pallavar āṭcik kālattiyum, cōḻar āṭciyiṉ oru pakutiyiyum poṟkālam eṉṟaṉar. ākavē, camaṇamum puttamum taḻikkavum, "varuṇācira tarmam' oḻiyavum pāṭupaṭṭaḷappirar āṭcik kālatti iruṇṭa kālam' eṉṟu aḻippatik kaṇṭu viyappaṭiyat tēviyilli. āriyam ciṟantōṅki niṉṟa kālaṅkaḷiyē intiya varalāṟṟil poṟkālam' eṉpar. itu kaṇmūṭittaṉamākak kiyāḷap paṭum potu neṟi,
kaḷappirar vīḻcci 
- kipi. āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil pāṇṭiya, pallava aracukaḷ taḻit tōṅkat toṭaṅkiyatāl kaḷappirar vīḻcciyaṭintaṉar. pāṇṭiya maṉṉaṉ kaṭuṅkōṉum, pallava maṉṉaṉ cimmavicuṇuvum kaḷappirari veṉṟu, tamiḻakattiṉiṉṟu veḷiyēṟṟiṉar. āyiṉum, kipi. eṭṭām nāṟṟāṇṭuvara iṅkumaṅkumākat tamiḻakattil ciṟṟaracarkaḷāy ivarkaḷ āṇṭu irukkalām. ivarkaḷ piṉvanta "muttariyarkaḷāka' āṇṭaṉar eṉpatu poruntātu. muttariyar vēṟu, kaḷappirar vēṟu eṉpatē teḷinta muṭipu. kaḷappirar kaṅkarēyeṉṟum, ivarkaḷ orucila āṇṭukaḷ tamiḻakatti āṇṭaṉareṉṟum orucilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. itu āyvukku uriyatē. 


5. tamiḻakam 
1. (ki.pi. 6 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiliruntu 9 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari) 
4 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal 9 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu variyil tamiḻakattil pallavarkaḷiṉ āṭci nili peṟṟiruntatu. takkaṇattil kipi. āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiliruntu paṉṉiraṇṭām nāṟṟāṇṭu variyilum cāḷukkiyar āṇṭaṉar. pallavarkaḷiṉ cama kālattil āṇṭa vātāpic cāḷuk kiyarkaḷ muṟkāla mēlic cāḷukkiyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. ivarkaḷil mutalām pulikēciki.pi. 535-586) im marapil 'makārācā' eṉṉum virutuṭaṉ āṇṭa mutalaracaṉ āvāṉ. ivaṉukkuppiṉ mutalām kīrtti varmaṉ(kipi 57 - 598), maṅkaḷēcaṉkipi. 598-50, iraṇṭām pulikēci (kipi. 10-542) ākiyōr āṇṭaṉar. iraṇṭām pulikēci teṉṉakak kaṭaṟkariyōramākap paṭiyeṭuttup pala kōṭṭikaḷik kippaṟṟi ṉāṉ eṉṟu avaṉuṭiya aykōlik kalveṭṭuk kūṟukiṟatu. pallava maṉṉaṉ makēntiravarmaṉit taṉṉuṭiya kāñcik kōṭṭikkuḷ patuṅkumpaṭi ceytāṉ eṉṟum, piṉṉark kāviri āṟṟik kaṭantu cōḻar, kēraḷar, pāṇṭiyaruṭaṉ naṭpuṟavu pūṇṭu pātāmi tirumpiṉāṉ eṉṟum ak kalveṭṭuk kūṟukiṟatu. ivaṉ kālattil pallavarukkum, cāḷukkiyarukkum iṭiyē pariyalam, maṇi maṅkalam, cūramāram mutaliya 
iṭaṅkaḷil pōr naṭantatākap pallavar ceppēṭukaḷum kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. kaṭiciyāka ivaṉ pallavariṭam tōṟṟut taṉ kōnakariyē iḻanta tākavum aṟikiṟōm. iraṇṭām pulikēcikkup piṉ mutalām vikkira mātittaṉ kipi 855-57), vinayātittaṉcipi. 5-6ātu, vicayātittaṉ kipi. 69-13), iraṇṭām vikkiramātittaṉ ki.pi. 734-745), iraṇṭām kīrttivarmaṉ pi.745-757 ākiyōr āṇṭaṉar. ivarkaḷ kālattilum pallavarukkum, cāḷukkiyarukkum iṭiyē pōrkaḷum, caccaravukaḷum muṇṭaṉa. kaṭiciyāka, inta iraṇṭām kīrttivarmaṉuṭaṉ vātāpi (allatu) pātāmic cāḷukkiyar āṭci maṟintatu. piṉṉar irunūṟu āṇṭukaḷ vari ivvāṭci irāṭṭirakūṭar āṭciyiṉ kīḻiruntatu. inta irāṭṭirakūṭarik kipi. 973-l iraṇṭām tilaṉ eṉpavaṉ tōṟkaṭittu iraṇṭām mēlic cāḷukkiyar āḷkuṭi marapi ēṟ paṭuttiṉāṉ. ivarkaḷ kalyāṇiyikkōnakarākakkoṇṭu āṇṭaṉar. tamiḻakam (kipi 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭiliruntu 9ām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari) 137 
pātāmic cāḷukkiyar āṭciyik kippaṟṟiya irāṭṭirakūṭar kaḷiṉ āṭci kipi. eṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiliruntu takkaṇattil ciṟantu viḷaṅkiyatu. ivarkaḷil mutalām kiruṭṭiṇaṉ ki.pi. 158-773), iraṇṭām kōvintaṉkipi.773-780), turuvaṉ kipi 780-793) mūṉṟām kōvintaṉ(kipi. 793 - 516, amōkavarcaṉkipi. 814-878), iraṇṭām kiruṭṭiṇaṉkipi 876-14) mutaliyōr kuṟippiṭattakkavar. ivarkaḷil turuvaṉ, mūṉṟām kōvintaṉ ākiyōr kālaṅkaḷil irāṭṭirakūṭa rukkum pallavarukkum mōtalkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kaṭiciyākak ki.pi. 973-l irāṭṭirakūṭar āṭciyi mēṟkūṟiya iraṇṭām tilaṉ kippaṟṟi iraṇṭām mēlic cāḷukkiyar āṭciyi ēṟpaṭuttiṉāṉ. 
mēlic cāḷukkiya maṉṉarkaḷāṉa iraṇṭām tilaṉ kipi 973 997), mutalām cōmēcuvaraṉ ki.pi. 104257), iraṇṭām cōmēcuvaraṉ ki.pi. 108-75), āṟām vikkiramātittiyaṉ(ki.pi. 1076-1126), mūṉṟām cōmēcuvaraṉkipi. 1757-36) ākiyōr toṭarntu āṇṭapiṉ mēlic cāḷukkiyar āṭciyum oru muṭivukku vantatu. kaṭiciyākak ki.pi. 1190-l itu maṟintatu. 
ki.pi. ēḻām nāṟṟāṇṭiliruntē vēṅkiyit talinakarākak koṇṭu āṇṭa kīḻic cāḷukkiyar āṭciyum iruntatu. iraṇṭām pulikēci marāttiya nāṭṭiṉ vaṭapakutiyiyum, teṉ pakutiyiyum ki.pi. 15-l veṉṟu taṉ tampiyāṉa vicuṇuvarttaṉaṉikipi. 815-33) āḷac ceytāṉ. inta vicuṇuvarttaṉaṉtāṉ piṉṉar ip pakutiyit taṉittāṇṭu kīḻic cāḷukkiya marapit tōṟṟuvittāṉ. ivaṉukkup piṉ mutalām ceyacimmaṉ ki.pi. 633-63 mutalāṉōr toṭarntu āṇṭaṉar. im marapil tōṉṟiya vimalātittiyaṉ eṉpavaṉ cōḻa aracaṉ mutalām irācarācaṉ makaḷāṉa kuntavi eṉpavaḷi maṇantāṉ, ivaṉ makaṉ irācarāca vicuṇuvarttaṉaṉ tāymāmaṉ mutalām irāca rācaṉ makaḷ ammaṅka tēviyi maṇantāṉ. ivaṉ makaṉ iraṇṭām irācēntiraṉ. ivaṉ ki.pi. 1067-l cāḷukkiya nāṭṭiṉ aracu kaṭṭil ēṟiṉāṉ. ivaṉ kālattil cōḻa nāṭṭiṉ aracurimikkup piṟaṅkaṭi yillātatāl ivaṉē cōḻa nāṭṭiṉ maṉṉaṉ āṉāṉ. avvāṟu aracaṉāṉa vuṭaṉ ivaṉ 'mutalām kulōttuṅkaṉ' eṉap peyar pūṇṭāṉ. atāvatu iraṇṭām irācēntiraṉ eṉṉum iyaṟpeya ruṭaṉ cāḷukkiya nāṭṭiyāṇṭa ivaṉ cōḻa nāṭṭiṉ aracaṉāka muṭicūṭṭik koṇṭavuṭaṉ muṭicūṭṭup peyarāka apicēka nāmam) *mutalām kulōttuṅkaṉ' eṉṉum peyaric cūṭikkoṇṭāṉ. itaṉāl cōḻanāṭum cāḷukkiyanāṭum oru kuṭikkīḻ iṇintaṉa. 
ivvāṟu, takkāṇattil irunta cāḷukkiya irāṭṭirakūṭa aracar cuḷukkum pallava, cōḻa aracarkaḷukkum toṭarpukaḷ iruntati yaṟikiṟōm. ciṟappāka, īṇṭu mutalil pallavarkaḷippaṟṟiyum, piṉṉarc cōḻarkaḷip paṟṟiyum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. 
(a) pallavar varalāṟu
iṉṟuvari pallavariṉ tāyakam paṟṟi āynta aṟiñarkaḷ muṭinta muṭivāka etiyum kāṇavilli. āyiṉum, avarkaḷiṉ āyvukaḷiṉ taṉmikaḷippaṟṟi maṭṭum īṇṭuk kūṟutal iṉṟiyamiyātatu. ivarkaḷip pahlavar eṉṟum, pāracīka nāṭṭiliruntu vantavarkaḷ eṉṟum, kuṟumpar kōpālar, pāvar pōṉṟa in nāṭṭu iṉattavari liruntu vantavar eṉṟum iruvaki āyvukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tāṟkālikamāka ivarkaḷ āntirap pēraracu(cātavākaṉap pēraracu) aḻintapiṉ avarkaḷukkukkīḻc ciṟṟaracarāyirunta ivarkaḷiṉ mūtātiyar ciṟitu ciṟitāka valuppeṟṟut tamiḻakam pōntu, pallavap pērāṭciyi . ēṟpaṭuttik koṇṭaṉar eṉṟu kūṟalām, 
ip pallavar āṭciyi niṟuviyavaṉ 'papparatēvaṉ' eṉṟum, ivaṉukkup piṉ toṭarntu maṉṉarkaḷ palar āṇṭaṉar eṉṟum pal cāṉṟukaḷiliruntu aṟikiṟōm. ivvāṟu āṇṭa pallavarkaḷi muṟkālap pallavarkaḷ, iṭikkālap pallavarkaḷ, piṟkālap pallavarkaḷ eṉa mūvakiyākap pirittum kūṟuvar. 
kipi. 250 mutal 340 variyil āṇṭa pallavar muṟkālap pallavar - eṉṟum, kipi. 340 mutal 55 variyil āṇṭavar iṭikkālap pallavar eṉṟum, kipi.75 mutal 900 vari āṇṭavar piṟkālap pallavar eṉṟum aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉar, īṇṭu nām piṟkālap pallavarkaḷip paṟṟic curukkamāka aṟiyalām. ivarkaḷilum mutal ēḻu pallava maṉṉarkaḷ “mūtta kālvaḻiyiṉar' eṉṟum, piṉvarum maṉṉarkaḷ 'iḷiya kālvaḻiyiṉar' eṉṟum aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. cimmavikaṇumutal toṭaṅkum ivarkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyal aparācitavarmaṉ variyil ceṉṟu muṭikiṟatu. cimmavicuṇu kipi.570 mutal 60 variyil āṇṭāṉ. aparācitavarmaṉ kipi. 895 mutal 13 vari āṇṭāṉ. āka, piṟkālap pallavarkaḷiṉ āṭci tōrāyamāka mūṉṟari nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ iruntatu eṉalām.
cimmavicuṇu (ki.pi. 570-60 
cimmavicuṇu iṭikkālap pallavariṉ kaṭiciyaracaṉāṉa mūṉṟām cimmavarmaṉ ki.pi. 550-570) eṉpāṉiṉ makaṉ āvāṉ. aḵtāvatu mutalām nantivarmaṉ eṉpāṉiṉ oṉṟuviṭṭa uṭaṉpiṟantāṉiṉ makaṉ āvāṉ. ivaṉuṭiya tampiyē pīmavarmaṉ eṉpavaṉ. ituvari ivaṉ kālattup paṭṭayam etuvum kiṭikkavilli, "avanti cuntari kati yi viruntu ivaṉ kaṅka aracaṉāṉa turuvinītaṉ eṉpāṉukkum, cāḷukkiya aracaṉāṉa vicuṇuvarttaṉaṉ eṉpāṉukkum camakālattavaṉ eṉpati aṟikiṟōm. kaṅka, cāḷukkiya aracarkaḷiṉ kālavariyaṟikaḷi mūlamākak koṇṭum vēlūrppāḷiyam, kācakkuṭi, kūram paṭṭayaṅ kaḷikkoṇṭum ivaṉuṭiya kālattik kaṇikkalām. ivaṉ makaṉāṉa mutalām makēntiravarmaṉ, tāṉ iyaṟṟiya 'mattavilācap pirakacaṉam" eṉṉum nūlil 'pallavakkulattit tāṅkum mali pōṉṟavaṉ' eṉṟum, 'nukarccip poruḷkaḷ aṉittiyu muṭiyavaṉ' eṉṟum, 'pal nāṭukaḷiyum veṉṟavaṉ' eṉṟum, 'vīrattil intiraṉiyum, celvat til kupēraṉiyum ottavaṉ' eṉṟum, aracarkaḷuḷ arimāveṉṟum cimmavicuṇuvip pukaḻnturikkiṟāṉ. cimmavicuṇu, cōḻa pāṇṭiya cērari veṉṟatōṭu, īḻattilum pallavarāṭciyi nilināṭṭiṉāṉ eṉpatip piṟkālap paṭṭayaṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. 
vēlūrppāḷiyam ceppēṭukaḷil ivaṉ cōḻanāṭṭi veṉṟa ceyti ciṟappākac cittarikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. “oḷirum kamuka maraṅkaḷiyum, cennel vayalkaḷiyum taṉakku aṇikalaṉkaḷāka aṇin tuḷḷa kavēraṉ makaḷāṉa kāviriyiṉāl ceḻumiyaṭiyum cōḻa nāṭṭic cimmavicuṇu kippaṟṟiṉāṉ.' 
'ataṉ piṟaku ip puviyulakil arimāp pōṉṟu ciṟantirunta cimmavicuṇu cimmaṉ eṉpāṉi veṟṟi koṇṭāṉ. māḷavattiyum piṟaku kaḷappirar, cōḻar, pāṇṭiyar ākiya maṉṉar kaḷiyum, taṉ tōḷvaliyāl iṟumāp puṟṟirunta ciṅkaḷa aracaṉiyum, kēraḷa aracaṉiyum veṟṟi koṇṭāṉ' eṉṟu kācakkuṭic ceppēṭukaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu'. 
ittakiya cāṉṟukaḷiliruntu ivaṉ teṉṉāṭṭu aracarkaḷi veṉṟu, kāviriyāṟu vara taṉatu āṭciyi virivupaṭuttiṉāṉ eṉpati yum, ivaṉē mutaṉmutalākak kāñciyik konakarākak koṇṭu āṇṭāṉ eṉpatiyum ūkittaṟiyalām. ivaṉ kālattil tāṉ kāñci kaḷappirariṭamiruntu kippaṟṟappaṭṭatu eṉak karutuvārum uṇṭu. ivaṉ kāñciyik kippaṟṟiyatāl kaṟṟavarip pakivariṭamiruntu kāppāṟṟiṉāṉ eṉṟu avanti cuntari kati'yil pōṟṟappeṟukiṟāṉ. iṅkuk "kaṭikā' eṉpatik kāñcipuram eṉṟē yāvarum koḷvar, 'kaṭikā' eṉpatu maṟiyiyal kallūri yeṉṟum, aḵtu amikkap peṟṟirunta kāñcipurattiṟkē ataṉ vaḻi ac ciṟappup peyar ēṟpaṭṭa teṉṟum ār. kōpālaṉ, carācup pātiriyār mutaliyōr karutu kiṉṟaṉar'. cimmavicuṇu putukkōṭṭi variyiluḷḷa cōḻa nāṭṭi veṉṟāṉ eṉpatik kañcaṉūrarp paṭṭayattiliruntu aṟikiṟōm, ivaṟṟi viḻuppuram māvaṭṭatti luḷḷa tirukkōvilūr mutaliyaviṭaṅ kaḷik koṇṭa, ak kāla nāṭu maḻavar nāṭu -malināṭu allatu malāṭu eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭṭatu. ataṉiyum cimmavicuṇu veṉṟāṉ. 
ivvāṟu oru perum nilapparappiṟku aracaṉāyt tikaḻnta cimmavicuṇu kāñciyik kōnakarāykkoṇṭu ciṟanta āṭci muṟiyi nilināṭṭiṉāṉ. pala pōrkaḷukkup piṉ amitiyi nilināṭṭiya ivaṉ oru ciṟanta viṇavaṉ āvāṉ. vicuṇuvit 'taṉ pattiyāl vaṇaṅkiyavaṉ' eṉṟu ivaṉi utayēntiram ceppēṭu pukaḻnturik kiṟatu. māmallapurattiluḷḷa ātivarākar kōyililuḷḷa cimmavicuṇu viṉ cili avaṉuṭiya camayappaṟṟi uṟutippaṭuttuvatāyuḷḷatu. āṉāl ikkōyil avaṉuṭiya makaṉāṉa mutalām makēntiravarmaṉāl kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. . 
ivvuruvac ciliyiṉ etirēyuḷḷa pāṟiyil 'srīmakēntiraṉ pōtāti rājaṉ' eṉṟu pallava- kiranta eḻuttukaḷil poṟikkappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. iṅkuk kāṇappaṭum maṟṟōr uruvac cili makēntiravarma ṉuṭiyatu. ivaṉ iḷavaracaṉāyiruntapōtu taṉ tanti cimmavicuṇu vuṭaṉ ikkōyilukku vantirukkak kūṭumeṉṟum, eṉavē, ikkōyil cimma vicuṇuviṉ kālattiltāṉ kaṭṭappeṟṟirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟum kūṟuvār. āṉāl, itu tavaṟāṉa karuttākum. ikkōyil makēntiravarmaṉ kālattiltāṉ kaṭṭappeṟṟateṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar muṭivākum. 
cimmavicuṇu ciṟanta viṇavappittaṉāka iruntatōṭu ciṟanta kaliñaṉākavum, kaliñarkaḷip pōṟṟip purappavaṉākavum viḷaṅkiṉāṉ. taṇṭi eṉṉum vaṭamoḻip pulavarum, pāravi eṉṉum maṟṟoru vaṭamoḻip pulavarum ivaṉuṭiya aviyiliruntōril kuṟippiṭattakkavar āvar. avanti cuntari kati, ciṟanta ilakkaṇa nūlākiya taṇṭiyalaṅkāram mutaliya nūlkaḷit taṇṭiyum, kiratārccuṉiyam eṉṉum nālip pāraviyum eḻutiṉar. avanticuntari katiyiliruntu kīḻic cāḷukkiya maṉṉaṉāṉa vicuṇuvarttaṉiṉum, kaṅka aracaṉāṉa turuvinītaṉum cimmavicuṇuviṉ kālattavar eṉpati aṟikiṟōm. ivaṉ" kālattil māṟavarmaṉ avaṉi cūḷāmaṇi eṉpāṉ pāṇṭiya nāṭṭu aracaṉāka iruntāṉ. ivvāṟu cimmavicuṇu teṉṉakattil pallavaruk kāṉa oru putiyaviyalit toṭaṅkit taṉ vāḻnāḷilēyē pallavamarapi varalāṟṟil iṭampeṟac ceytuviṭṭu maṟintāṉ. ivaṉukkup piṉ ivaṉ makaṉāṉa mutalām makēntiravarmaṉ ariyaṇiyēṟiṉāṉ, 
mutalām makēntiravarmaṉ (ki.pi. 600-630) 
cimmavikaṇuvukkup piṉ ariyaṇiyēṟiya avaṉuṭiya makaṉāṉa mutalām makēntiravarmaṉ, taṉ tantiyāl āḷappeṟṟa vaṭakkē kiruṭṭiṇiyāṟu mutal teṟkē putukkōṭṭi variyi luḷḷa perunilap parappiṟkut talivaṉ āṉāṉ. ivaṉ iḷavaraca ṉāka irunta pōtu teluṅku nāṭṭil taṅkiyiruntāṉ eṉṟum, itaṉik kuṇṭūr māvaṭṭattil cēcarlā eṉṉumiṭattiluḷḷa kapōtēcuvaraṉ kōyil kalveṭṭuk kuṟippiṭukiṟatu eṉṟum kūṟuvar. ivaṉ kālattil pallava - nāṭṭiṉ vaṭavelliyilirunta cāḷukkiya nāṭṭiyāṇṭa iraṇṭām pulikēci katampar, kaṅkar, āḷupar, mayūrar mutaliya ciṟṟaracarkaḷiyaṭakkip pēraraci nili nāṭṭiṉāṉ. ivaṉuṭiya aykōlik kalveṭṭil tāṉ vēṅkiyi veṉṟapiṉ pallava nāṭṭiyum tākkiyatākak kuṟippiṭu kiṉṟāṉ. itiliruntu cimmavicuṇu kālattil toṭaṅkiya pallavar cāḷukkiyar pakimi toṭarntu makēntiravarmaṉ kālattilum nilaviyati aṟikiṟōm. 'pulikēci taṉṉuṭiya cēṉiyi naṭattic cellum pōtu kiḷampiya tūḷiyāṉatu etirkkavanta pallava maṉṉaṉiṉ oḷiyi maṅkac ceytatu, pulikēciyiṉ perum paṭikkaṭalik kaṇṭu kāñcipurattaracaṉ kāñcikkōṭṭiyil oḷintu koṇṭāṉ eṉṟum, pallavarkaḷiṉ paṭiyākiya kuḷirnta paṉikkuk kaṭuṅkatirā yuḷḷa katiravaṉip pōṉṟa avaṉ (pulikēci) cōḻa, pāṇṭiya, kēraḷar kaḷukkup perum makiḻcciyi uṇṭākkiṉāṉ eṉṟum aykōlik kalveṭṭuk kūṟukiṟatu' 
ittakiya cāṉṟukaḷiliruntu mutalām makēntiravarmaṉ cāḷuk kiya nāṭṭi yāāṇṭa iraṇṭām pulikēci (ki.pi. 500-542eṉpāṉiṭam tōṟṟu āntira nāṭṭilirunta taṉ nāṭṭiṉ oru pakutiyic cāḷuk kiyarukkup paṟikoṭuttāṉ eṉpati aṟikiṟōm. appakuti vēṅkiyi uḷḷaṭakkiyatākum. ap pakutiyip pallavariṭamiruntu kippaṟṟiya iraṇṭām pulikēci taṉ tampiyākiya vicuṇuvart taṉaṉit taṉakku aṭaṅkiyāḷumpaṭi amarttiṉāṉ. piṉṉar vicuṇuvarttaṉaṉ itaṉit taṉittāḷa muṟpaṭṭut taṉimarapiyum ēṟpaṭuttik koṇṭāṉ. ivaṉ vaḻittōṉṟiyavēṅkināṭṭuc cāḷukkiyar *kīḻic cāḷukkiya reṉṟu aḻikkap peṟṟaṉar. ataṉāl vātāpiyit talinakarākak koṇṭu āṇṭa mūlac cāḷukkiyar "mēlic cāḷukkiyar' eṉṟu aḻikkap peṟṟaṉar. 
makēntiravarmaṉ iraṇṭām pulikēciyiṭam tōṟṟut taṉatu āṭciyiṉ vaṭavelliyi iḻantāṉāyiṉum, kaṭiciyākap pulikēci yiyum avaṉukkut tuṇiyāka niṉṟa aracarkaḷ palariyum kāñcikku vaṭakkē patiṉintu kal toliviluḷḷa puḷḷalūr eṉṉumiṭattil puṟaṅkāṇac ceytāṉ. puḷḷalūril etirikaḷ aṉivariyum avaṉ aḻittu avarkaḷi vēraṟuttāṉ. piṉṉar avaṉ kaṭṭaḷi aṉiva rālum ēṟṟukkoḷḷappeṟṟatu eṉṟum, intiraṉip pōṉṟu kīrtti 
vāynta makēntiravarmaṉ pūmaṇṭalattip pātukāttu vantāṉ eṉṟum kācakkuṭic ceppēṭukaḷ pukaḻnturikkiṉṟaṉa. 
taṉ nāṭṭiṉ vaṭapakutiyip pulikēciyiṭam tōṟṟapiṉ makēntira ṉiṉ āṭci vaṭakkē vaṭapeṇṇiyāṟṟiliruntu teṟkē putuk kōṭṭi variyil toṇṭi maṇṭalattiyum, cōḻa maṇṭalat tiyum koṇṭatāka iruntatu, aḵtāvatu iṉṟiya nellūr māvaṭṭattiṉ teṉpakuti, cittar, vēlūr, tiruvaṇṇāmali, kāñcipuram, kaṭalūr, viḻuppuram, tirucci, tañcāvūr, putukkōṭṭi , cēlam ākiya māvaṭṭaṅkaḷi uḷḷaṭakki niṉṟatu. 
ivaṉ kālattil malli allatu kaṭalmalli iṉṟiya māmallapuram ciṟanta tuṟimukappaṭṭiṉamākat tikaḻntatu. itu toṇṭimāṉ iḷantiriyaṉ eṉṉum cōḻa maṉṉaṉ kālattilēyē ciṟantu viḷaṅkiya tuṟimukappaṭṭiṉamākum. itaṉik kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉār pāṭiya perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭiyil kāṇ kiṟōm'. 
makēntiraṉ oru ciṟanta kaṭṭaṭakkali vallunaṉ. ivaṉāl amikkap peṟṟa ūrkaḷum, ērikaḷum iṉṟum ivaṉ peyari nilināṭṭi niṟkiṉṟaṉa. māmaṇṭūrppaṟṟil taṉ peyarāl makēntira maṅkalam' eṉṉum ūri ēṟpaṭuttiṉāṉ eṉpataṉit tirupparuttik kuṉṟattu varttamāṉar kōyil kalveṭṭuk kūṟukiṟatu'. vaṭārkkāṭu māvaṭṭam vālājāpēṭṭi vaṭṭattil cōḻaliṅka purattiṟkut teṉkiḻakkē mūṉṟu kal toliviluḷḷa 'makēntiravāṭi' eṉṉum ūrum ivaṉāl ēṟpaṭuttap peṟṟatākum. ivvūrukkuc ciṟanta nīrp pācaṉa ēntiṟkāka 'smakēntirat taṭākam' eṉṉum ēriyiyum ivaṉ veṭṭuvittāṉ. itu pōṉṟa maṟṟoru ciṟanta ēriyi māmaṇṭūrilum amittāṉ. ataṟkuc cittiramēkat taṭākam' eṉṟu peyar, 
makēntiraṉ taṉ peyarāl 'makēntirappaḷḷi" eṉṉum uriyum, civaṉ kōyiliyum cōḻanāṭṭil amittāṉ. iṉṟu ivvūr *kōyilaṭippāḷiyam eṉṟu vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṟatu. ic civaṉ kōyil ceṅkal, kal, kāri, māḻi mutaliyavaṟṟāl amikkap peṟṟirukkalām. itupōṉṟa pala kaṭṭumāṉak kōyilkaḷi ivaṉ amittāṉ eṉpati ārāycciyāḷarkaḷ palarum oppukiṉṟaṉar. 
ivaṉuṭiya virutup peyarkaḷi ivaṉ kālattuk kuṭivarik kōyilkaḷilum, piṟa cāṉṟukaḷilum kāṇalām. vallam kuṭivariyil 'mayēntirap pōttarecaru' eṉṟu tamiḻilum, māmallapurattu varākar kuṭivariyil "srīmahēntra pōtrāti rājaṉ' eṉṟu pirākirutatti lum, pallāvaram, tirucci ākiya kuṭivarikaḷil 'srīmavēhantra vikram' eṉṟu camaṟkirutattilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa, “piṇa piṇakku' eṉṟu tamiḻilum ivaṉ virutupeyar tiruccik kuṭivariyil poṟikkappaṭṭa ṭuḷḷatu. ivaṉ iḷavaracaṉāka iruntapōtu āntira nāṭṭil iruntāṉ. itaṉāl teluṅkilum ivaṉukkup pala ciṟappup peyarkaḷ uḷḷaṉa, avi civilntu, tilvilōṉiyampu, vēntuluvittu, pacarampu, cilampu, malāyu, kaṭuttarampu mutaliyaṉavākum. makēntiravikramavarmaṉ, makēntira pōttaracaṉ, pākappiṭuku, lalitāṅkuraṉ, kuṇaparaṉ, matta vilācaṉ, avaṉiyācaṉ, cattiya cantaṉ, purucōttamaṉ, catturumallaṉ, caṅkīrṇa cāti, citrakārappuli, cētkāri, vicitracittaṉ, aluptakāmaṉ, kalakap piriyaṉ, apimukaṉ, makāmēkaṉ narēntiraṉ mutaliya ciṟappup peyarkaḷum ivaṉukkuṇṭu. ivaṉ kuṭivarikaḷi amittatāl cētakāri (cittiyakkāraṉ eṉṟum, icikkaliyil vallavaṉāṉatāl caṅkīrṇa cāti eṉṟum, ōviyakkaliyil vallavaṉāṉatāl cittirakkārappuli eṉṟum, mattavilācam eṉṉum nakiccuvi nāṭakatti ivaṉ eḻutiya tāl mattavilācaṉ eṉṟum ciṟappāka aḻikkappeṟṟāṉ. 
ittakiya virutuppeyarkaḷik koṇṭē ivaṉ kālattil ivaṉāl vaḷarkkappeṟṟa kalikaḷippaṟṟi aṟiyalām. kuṭivarik kōyilkaḷit tamiḻakattil mutaṉ mutalil ciṟappuṟa amittavaṉ ivaṉē. ivaṉukku muṉṉum kuṭivarikaḷ tamiḻakattil iruntaṉa eṉpataṟkāṉa ilakkiyac cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. āyiṉ iṉṟaḷavum nām kaṇṭukaḷikkum niliyiluḷḷa palvēṟu kuṭivarikaḷic camittavaṉ ivaṉēyātalāl ivaṉ peritum pōṟṟappaṭukiṟāṉ. 'kōyil' eṉpatē ivaṉ kālattiltāṉ mutaṉ mutalākat tamiḻakattil kaṭṭappeṟṟatu eṉṟu niṉippatu tavaṟu. kaḻakakkālattilum, ataṟku muṉṉum tamiḻakatti lirunta maṇṭaḷi, kaṟṟaḷikaḷippaṟṟi virittaṟikiṟōm. kaṭṭaṭak kaliyit tamiḻarkaḷ cevvaṉē aṟintu vaḷarttaṉar. atiloru putumiyi ivaṉ pukuttiṉāṉ eṉpataṟkākavē ivaṉip pōṟṟu kiṟōm. kaṟṟuṇṭu kaḷi aṭukkik cuṟṟaḷikaḷi amitta ivaṉ piṉṉar perumpāṟi kaḷik kuṭintu kuṭivarikaḷi ēṟpaṭuttiṉāṉ eṉpatālēyē ivaṉic cērkkāri' eṉṟaṉar. ivaṉāl amikkap peṟṟa cuṟṟaḷikaḷ aḻintupaṭṭaṉa. kāñci ēkāmparanātar kōyil veḷḷu varā peḷarṇami maṇṭapakkaruṅkaltūṇ oṉṟil kāṇappaṭum - apimuka, citrakāra puvi, kūṟṟampu mutaliya veṭṭeḻuttukkaḷik koṇṭu ivaṉāl kaṭṭappeṟṟa oru kaṟṟaḷiyilirunta it tūṇ eñci niṉṟu im maṇṭapattiṟkup payaṉpaṭuttappeṟṟatu eṉpatiyaṟikiṟōm. itippōlavē mēṟkūṟiya makēntirap paḷḷic civaṉ kōyilum ivaṉāl kaṭṭappeṟṟatē. iṉṟu intu aḻintupaṭṭālum tiruñāṉa campantariṉ tēvārattiliruntu itaṉ ciṟappik kāṇalām. ivaṉāl camikkappeṟṟa kuṭivarikaḷip paṟṟiyum, ivaṉuṭiya piṟa kalip paṇikaḷippaṟṟiyum pallavar kalippaṇi" eṉṉum talippil viḷakkam kāṇalām. 
camayam 
makēntiravarmaṉ kālattil tamiḻakameṅkum puttamum camaṇamum ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉa. makkaḷuḷ perumpālōr ivviru camayaṅkaḷiyē piṉpaṟṟiṉar. makēntiraṉum camaṇaṉākavē iruntāṉ. ivaṉatu piṟkāla āṭciyil civam talitūkkat toṭaṅkiyatu. tiruñāṉacampantariṉ tantiyāṉa civapāta virutiyar ivaṉ kālattil vāḻntavar. avar puttamum, camaṇamum ceḻittu, civam maṅki niṉṟatik kaṇṭu maṉam varuntiṉār eṉpatit tiruñāṉa campantarē tam tēvārattil kūṟi yuḷḷār? 
pāṇṭiya nāṭṭilum camaṇam ciṟappuṟṟuc civam kuṉṟip pōṉatic cēkkiḻār perumāṉum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. 
ik kālattil civamum, viṇavamum kuṉṟiyiruntaṉa. āyiṉum, avviru camayaṅkaḷum makkaḷāl piṉpaṟṟappeṟṟaṉa. orupuṟam ivviru camayaṅkaḷum eḻucciyuṟat tuṭittaṉa. maṟupuṟam camaṇamum puttamum ciṟappuṟṟu niṉṟaṉa. āka ik kālattil iṟineṟi iyakkam' nāṭeṅki lum viṟuviṟuppāka vaḷarntu niṉṟatu, nāḷāvaṭṭattil civanāyaṉmār kaḷum, viṇava aṭiyārkaḷum peritum muyaṉṟu taṅkaḷ camayaṅkaḷi vaḷarkkavum, piṟa camayaṅkaḷi vīḻttavum muṟpaṭṭaṉar. itaṉāl ivaṉ kālattil camayap pūcalkaḷ nāṭeṅkaṇum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. ip pōril camaṇa - putta camayaṅkaḷ vīḻcciyaṭintu maṅkiṉa, civam talitūkki niṉṟatu. itaṟkup peritum uṟutuṇiyāy niṉṟavar tirunāvukkaracar āvār. tam vāḻnāḷil perum pakutiyic camaṇattiṉ camayakuruvāka iruntu kaḻitta ivar tam cūlinōy tīrac civaṉi vaḻipaṭṭuc civar āṉār. pāṭalipurattil camaṇar kuruvāka iruntapōtu ivaric camaṇarkaḷ 'tarumacēṉar' eṉṟu aḻittaṉar. civarāṉapiṉ civa iṟineṟi iyakkattip parappit tirunāvukkaracar' eṉṉum ciṟappup peyar peṟṟār. ivaruṭaṉ cērntu tiruñāṉacampantarum civattip parappiṉār. campantar iḷiñar; mutumiyil civam cērnta nāvuk karacari ivar apparē' (tantiyē eṉṟu aḻittatāl nāvukkaracarukku 'appar' eṉṉum ciṟappup peyarum uṇṭāyiṟṟu. 
camaṇam viṭuttuc civam taḻuviya tirunāvukkaracarē camaṇattil āḻntirunta makēntiravarmaṉiyum civam taḻuvac ceytār. civam taḻuviya makēntiraṉ piṉṉar camaṇatti aḻikka muṟpaṭṭāṉ eṉpataṉip periyapurāṇattiliruntu aṟikiṟōm. periyapurāṇattil varum ap pāṭalil makēntiraṉ pāṭalipurattuc camaṇap paḷḷiyi iṭittu, ak kaṟkaḷik koṇṭu, tiruvatikic civaṉ kōyilik kaṭṭiṉāṉ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu'. itil varum kāṭavaṉ, pallavaṉ, kuṇataraṉ eṉṉum ciṟappup peyarkaḷ makēntiraṉiyē kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. 
avaṉatu tiruccikkuṭivariyil viṅkatti vaḻipaṭum kuṇaparaṉ eṉṟa ciṟappup peyar koṇṭa aracaṉ inta liṅkattiṉāl puṟac camayattiliruntu tirumpiya ñāṉam meyyaṟivu) ivvulakattil nīṇṭu niliniṟpatāka" eṉṟu poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu' 
ivvāṟu, periyapurāṇac cāṉṟukaḷālum, mēṟkaṇṭa kalveṭṭuc cāṉṟālum makēntiraṉ camaṇattiliruntu civa camayattiṟku ! māṟṟap peṟṟāṉ eṉpatit teḷivāka aṟikiṟōm. mēlum, tiruvatikiyilirunta kuṇataravīccaram' eṉṉum civaṉ kōyilik kaṭṭiya "kuṇaparaṉ' ivaṉē eṉpatiyum palvēṟu cāṉṟukaḷiliruntum teḷivuṟukiṟōm. ivaṉ tōrāyamāka ki.pi. 620 - l civaṉāka māṟiyirukkalām ivaṉit toṭarntu kuṭimakkaḷum virintu civar āyiṉar. ivaṉāl camikkappeṟṟa vallam, taḷavāṉūr, cīyamaṅkalam, pallāvaram, tirucci mutaliya kuṭavarikaḷ civaṉ kōyilkaḷākavum, makēntiravāṭik kuṭi vari perumāḷ kōyilākavum iruppatāl viṇavatti ivaṉ veṟukkavilli eṉpati aṟiyalām. āṉāl, camaṇam kuṉṟi aḻiyattoṭaṅkiyatu.
kalippaṇi 
makēntiravarmaṉ pāṟikaḷik kuṭintu kōyilkaḷic camikkum ciṟanta kalippaṇiyic ceytāṉ eṉakkūṟiṉōm. ituvaṉṟi ici, naṭaṉam, ciṟpam, ōviyam, nāṭakam ākiya kalikaḷiyum ivaṉ ciṟappuṟa vaḷarttāṉ. cittaṉṉavācalil uḷḷa kuṭivarik kōyili luḷḷa ōviyaṅkaḷ ivaṉ kālattavi eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭatu. āṉāl iṉṟu ārāycciyāḷarkaḷ ivi pāṇṭiyar kālattavi eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. naṭaṉamātarkaḷ iruvar iṅku kaṇkavar ōviyaṅkaḷākat tiṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. iṉtoru camaṇak kōyilātaliṉ tīrttaṅkarar ' uruvac cilikaḷum itil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟāl akkālac ciṟpak kaliyiṉ perumiyi uṇaramuṭikiṟatu. eṉavē, makēntiravarmaṉiṉ cittaṉṉavācal kuṭivarik kōyil ōviyam, ciṟpam, naṭaṉam ākiya mūṉṟu kalikaḷiyum uṇarttum kūṭamākat tikaḻkiṟatu. ivaṉ oru ciṟanta icip pulavaṉum āvāṉ. ak kaliyiṉ vaḷarccikkākap putukkōṭṭiyiluḷḷa kuṭumiyāṉmalip pāṟi oṉṟil ivaṉ iciyiṉ ōci nayaṅkaḷip paṟṟiya ceytikaḷic cetukkiyuḷḷāṉ. 'intak kalveṭṭu ici māṇavar naṉmikkākap peṇkaḷi vakuttut tanta uruttirāccāriyār eṉpavariṉ māṇavaṉāṉa maṉṉariṉ kaṭṭaḷip paṭi veṭṭappaṭṭatu" eṉṟu ak kalveṭṭil kuṟikkappeṟṟuḷḷatu. cilar ikkalveṭṭu ivaṉ kālattiṟkup piṟaku veṭṭapeṟṟatu eṉṟum kūṟuvar. eṉavē, itupaṟṟiya iṟutiyāṉa muṭivu iṉṉum ēṟpaṭavilli ! māmaṇṭūriluḷḷa kalveṭṭu oṉṟil kāṇappaṭum collāṭcikaḷi yum avaṟṟil kāṇappaṭum curam, varṇam, ivaṟṟi vakutta vāṉmīkiyā rip paṟṟiyatākak kāṇappaṭum kuṟippiyum, mattavilācap pirakacaṉam paṟṟiya kuṟippiyum, aṟinta aṟiñarkaḷ cilar ivi kuṭumiyāṉ maliyiluḷḷa kalveṭṭōṭu ottu iruppatākak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar, mēlum, kuṭumiyāṉmalik kalveṭṭum, tirucciyiluḷḷa kuṭivarik kōyil kalveṭṭum palavaṟṟālum oṉṟē pōl uḷḷaṉa. eṉavē tāṉ kuṭumiyāṉ malik kalveṭṭumakēntiraṉ kaṭṭaḷiyāltāṉ veṭṭap peṟṟatu eṉṟum, itaṉāl avaṉ icik kaliyil vallavaṉāka iruttal vēṇṭumeṉṟum ārāycciyāḷar karutukiṉṟaṉar'. 
makēntiravarmaṉ oru ciṟanta nūlāciriyaṉum āvāṉ. māmaṇṭūrk kalveṭṭil kuṟikkappeṟṟa mattavilācap pirakacaṉam eṉṉum nakic cuvi nāṭakatti ivaṉ vaṭamoḻiyil eḻutiṉāṉ. itil pala camayattavarum, taṉippaṭṭa niliyil naṭattiya vāḻkki muṟikaḷi nakiccuviyōṭu makēntiraṉ viḷakkukiṉṟāṉ. in nūlil catturu mallaṉ, avaṉipākaṉ, kuṇaparaṉ, mattavilācaṉ eṉṉum avaṉuṭiya ciṟappup peyarkaḷum iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. pārppaṉaṉukkup pūnūlum, cupālicuṉukkum cupāvamum iṉṟiyami yātavi eṉpataṉiyum, putta pikkukaḷ taṅkaḷ tuṟavuneṟikaḷi maṟantu iṉpamācukkālam kaḻittārkaḷ eṉpataṉiyum, ic camayattavar yāvarum oruvarukkoruvar taṅkaḷukkuḷ pūcaliṭṭuk koṇṭiruntatiyum vēṭikkiyākavum, nakiccuviyāka vum in nāl vivarikkiṟatu. , . - 
mutalām naracimmavarmaṉ (māmallaṉ) (ki.pi. 630-668) 
mutalām makēntiraṉukkup piṟaku avaṉ makaṉāṉa mutalām naracimmaṉ tōrā) kipi. 630-l aracu kaṭṭil ēṟiṉāṉ. ivaṉ kipi.130 iliruntu nīkki vari āṇṭāṉ'. ivaṉi "vātāpikoṇṭa naracimma varmaṉ' eṉṟē ciṟappuṟa aḻippar. ulakap pukaḻpeṟṟa māmallapurattu aḻiyāta ciṟpaṅkaḷic camitta ivaṉi māmallaṉ' eṉa aḻippatē cālavum ciṟappuṭit teṉpar. ivaṉ iḷavaracaṉāka iruntapōtu kaṭalmalli eṉṉum tuṟimukappaṭṭiṉattil vāḻntu vantāṉ eṉṟum, tāṉ aracuk kaṭṭilēṟiyatum taṉ peyariyē itaṟku iṭṭu 'māmallapuram' eṉṟu aḻittāṉ eṉṟum aṟikiṟōm. ivaṉukkum paṉṉeṭuṅkālattiṟku muṉpiruntē itu kaṭalmalli, nīrppeyaṟṟu, malli (malaṅku eṉṉum peyarkaḷāl aḻikkap peṟṟa ciṟanta tuṟi mukappaṭṭiṉamāka iruntatu. itaṉip paṇṭiya kirēkkarum urōmarum yavaṉarum naṉku aṟintirukkiṉṟaṉar. pukārum koṟkiyum pōlavē it tuṟimukappaṭṭiṉamum paṇṭiya kaṭal vāṇika naṭuva mākat tikaḻntatit taṉṉaka, ayalakac cāṉṟukaḷiliruntu aṟikiṟōm. im māmallaṉ muṭicūṭṭik koṇṭa pōtu naracimmavarmaṉ eṉṟa paṭṭap peyaric cūṭik koṇṭāṉ eṉṟum, ivaṉ iyaṟpeyar māmallaṉē eṉṟum kūṟuvar. 
pallava-cāḷukkiyap pōr 
tantiyip pōlavē vīramum, tīramum koṇṭu tikaḻnta ivaṉuṭiya kālattil teṉṉaka varalāṟṟil ciṟappiṭam peṟṟa pallavar cāḷukkiyap pōr' naṭantatu. māmallaṉiṉ tantiyiṭam kaṭiciyākap paṭutōlviyaṭinta cāḷukkiya maṉṉaṉ iraṇṭām pulikēci ivaṉ aracuk kaṭṭilēṟiya cila āṇṭukaḷukkuḷ pallava nāṭṭiṉ mītu paṭi yeṭuttāṉ. āṉāl, naracimmaṉ cāḷukkiyar paṭikaḷ kāñciyi aṇukumuṉ an nakarukku irupatu kal toliviluḷḷa maṇimaṅkalam eṉṉumiṭattilum, pariyaḷam cūramāram mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum cāḷuk kiyap paṭikaḷit tākki muṟiyaṭittāṉ. itaṉik kūram ceppēṭu mika aḻakākac cittarikkiṟatu. " 'im marapil utayakiriyiliruntu kiḻakku maliyi liruntu) ātavaṉum nilavum tōṉṟuvatu pōl tōṉṟi, tali vaṇaṅkāta im marapu maṉṉarkaḷiṉ maṇimuṭiyil cūṭāmaṇi pōl viḷaṅki, paki maṉṉarka ḷākiya yāṉik kūṭṭaṅkaḷukku arimā pōṉṟu naracimmamūrttiyē maṇṇulakil araca kumāraṉāka avatarittatip pōl naracimmaṉ utittāṉ; ivaṉ cōḻar, kēraḷā, kaḷappirar, pāṇṭiyarkaḷit tirumpat tirumpaveṉṟu āyiraṅki kaḷik koṇṭavaṉ(kārttavīriyaṉ pōl viḷaṅkiṉāṉ; pāriyaḷam, maṇimaṅkalam, cūramāram mutaliya pōrkaḷil puṟaṅkāṭṭiyōṭiya pulikēciyiṉ mutukākiya paṭṭayattil veṟṟi" eṉṉum collip poṟittāṉ. itaṟkuppiṉ, kumpamuṉivar (akattiyar), vātāpiyiā vātāpi eṉṟa acuraṉi aḻittatip pōl ivaṉ vātāpiyi pulikēciyiṉ kōnakari aḻittāṉ. 
pallavariṉ veṟṟi kaṇṭum maṉam niṟivu peṟāta naracimmavarmaṉ pallavariṉ piṟavip pakivaṉāṉa iraṇṭām pulikēciyi aṭiyōṭu aḻittu viṭat tīrmāṉittāṉ. itaṟku āyattamāyirunta pōtu pulikēci mīṇṭum pallava nāṭṭiṉ mītu paṭiyeṭuttu vantāṉ. naracimmavarmaṉ im muṟi avaṉi yetirttup pōr ceytu veṟṟi peṟṟatōṭu avaṉip pōrk kaḷattilēyē koṉṟāṉ. attuṭaṉ nillāmal cāḷukkiyar paṭikaḷit turattic ceṉṟu an nāṭṭiṉ kōnakarākiya vātāpiyiyum kippaṟṟi aḻittāṉ. itaṉāl, ivaṉukku 'vātāpi koṇṭa naracimmaṉ' eṉṟa virutup peyar ēṟpaṭṭatu. ivaṉ vātāpiyi veṟṟi koṇṭa ceytiyi utayēntiram paṭṭayamum, kācak kuṭip paṭṭayamum', vēlūrppāḷiyam paṭṭayamum ciṟappuṟac ceppu kiṉṟaṉa. 
naracimmavarmaṉ vātāpiyiṉ mēṟ paṭikoṇṭu ceṉṟapōtu ap paṭikkut talimi tāṅkic ceṉṟavar parañcōtiyār eṉpavar āvār. ivarē piṉṉar ciṟuttoṇṭa nāyaṉār, eṉṟu tiruttoṇṭat tokiyil iṭampeṟṟār. ivaruṭiya vātāpi veṟṟiyiyum aṅku ivar peṟṟa celvattiyum paṟṟic cēkkiḻār tamatu periyapurāṇattil pukaḻntēṟṟiṉār. 
vātāpiyi veṟṟikoṇṭa naracimmaṉ pulikēciyit tāṉ vātāpiyik kippaṟṟuvataṟku muṉṉamēyē koṉṟu viṭṭāṉā allatu vēṟu aracarkaḷ pulikēciyik koṉṟu viṭṭārkaḷā eṉṟa aiyam aṟiñarkaḷiṭiyē iṉṉamum tīravilli. iraṇṭām pulikēci kipi.642l iṟantāṉ eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷariṉ muṭivu. āyiṉ, naracimma varmaṉum kipi.642-l tāṉ vātāpiyik kippaṟṟiyirukka vēṇṭum. an nakar ēṟakkuṟiya patiṉmūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷ pallavar kiyil iruntatu eṉpataṟkāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. vātāpiyiluḷḷa takkāṇa īrappaṉ eṉṉum kōyilukkarukiluḷḷa kampam oṉṟil naracimmavarmaṉatu patiṉ mūṉṟām āṇṭuk kalveṭṭu oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. atiluḷḷa veṭṭeḻuttu citintu kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. āyiṉum, naracimmavarmaṉ taṉatu vātāpi veṟṟiyi atil poṟittuḷḷāṉ eṉpati aṟiya muṭikiṟatu '. atiluḷḷa veṭṭeḻuttu oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. taṉ tanti muṉṉar cāḷukkiyariṭam iḻanta āntirap pakutiyiyum, naracimma varmaṉ veṉṟatōṭu vātāpiyiyum kippaṟṟiyāṇṭa perumi pallavariṉ vīravaralāṟṟil poṉṉeḻuttukaḷil poṟikkappaṭṭa tākum. ki.pi. 555 - l pallavariṭamiruntu mīṇṭum vātāpi cāḷukkiyar vacamāyiṟṟu. ataṉip pulikēciyiṉ iḷiya makaṉāṉa mutalām vikkira mātittaṉ kipi. 555 - iliruntu 81 variyil āṇṭāṉ. ivaṉ aracuk kaṭṭilēṟiyavuṭaṉ pallavarip paḻivāṅkum poruṭṭu mīṇṭum pallava nāṭṭiṉmītu paṭiyeṭuttāṉ. itaṉāl, pallavar- cāḷukkiyar paki valuppeṟṟatu. vikkiramātittaṉ taṉṉuṭiya karunāl paṭṭayattil taṉatu tantiyākiya iraṇṭām pulikēci mūṉṟu paciyaracarkaḷāl tōlviyuṟṟa tākavum itaṉāl vātāpiyilirunta kōyilkaḷ varuvāyiṉṟit tavitta tākavum kuṟippiṭukiṉṟāṉ. im mūṉṟu aracarkaḷ yāvar eṉpatil aṟiñarkaḷiṭiyē karuttuvēṟupāṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa, pulikēci yi veṉṟa aracarkaḷ mūvar naracimmavarmaṉ māṉavarmaṉ, camarāpirāmaṉ ākiya mūvar āvar.māṉavarmaṉ eṉpavaṉ ilaṅki aracukku uriyavaṉ; taṉatu araciyiḻantu naracimmavarmaṉiṭam vantu aṭikkalam pukuntu kāñciyilē neṭuṅkālam taṅkiyiruntavaṉ. ivaṉ naracimmavarmaṉuṭaṉ cērntu pulikēciyuṭaṉ pōr ceytāṉ eṉpataṉi makāvamcam eṉṉum īḻanāṭṭu varalāṟṟu nūlil kāṇkiṟōm. mūṉṟāvatu aracaṉāṉa camarā pirāmaṉ putukkōṭṭiyic cērnta koṭumpāḷūri āṇṭavaṉ āvāṉ. ivaṉ cāḷukkiya aracaṉi atirāmaṅkalam eṉṉum ūril koṉṟāṉ eṉṟum, ivaṉuṭiya takappaṉāṉa paraturkkamarttaṉaṉ vātāpiyi veṉṟāṉ eṉṟum, koṭumpāḷūril uḷḷa mūvarkōyil kalveṭṭuk kūṟukiṟatu. āṉāl, inta mūṉṟāvatu aracaṉ āntira nāṭṭil pallavariṉ paṭinikarāḷiyākavirunta cimmavicuṇuviṉ tampiyāṉa iraṇiyavarmaṉiṉ vaḻittōṉṟaluḷ oruvaṉāka irukkalām eṉṟu karutuvārum uṇṭu pōrkkaḷattil pulikēci iṟantapiṟakutāṉ 
naracimmaṉ vātāpimēl paṭiyeṭuttāṉ eṉpatē poruttamāṉatu. 
pallavar - pāṇṭiyar pōr 
naracimmavarmaṉ kālattil pāṇṭiya nāṭṭu aracaṉāka iruntavaṉ arikēcariparāṅkucaṉneṭumāṟaṉ) (ki.pi. 641-570) eṉpavaṉ āvāṉ. ivaṉ cōḻariṭam peṇ koṇṭu uṟaviṉaṉākic cērariyum, piṟa kuṟunila maṉṉarkaḷiyum veṉṟu teṉṉāṭṭilēyē ciṟanta taḷapatiyāka viḷaṅkiṉāṉ. ivaṉ cakkaramaṅki eṉṉumiṭattil pallavaṉip puṟaṅkaṇṭāṉ eṉṟu ciṉṉamaṉūrc ceppēṭu kūṟukiṟatu'. āṉāl, naracimmavarmaṉ cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiya, kaḷappiraruṭaṉ pōriṭṭāṉ eṉṟu kūram ceppēṭukaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. pulikēci mutaṉmuṟiyākap pallava nāṭṭiṉ mītu paṭiyeṭuttapōtu, 'cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiyar maṉam makiḻumpaṭi kāvirikkariyi aṭintu pallavaṉip puṟaṅ kaṇṭāṉ' eṉṟu cāḷukkiyar ceppēṭu kūṟuvatāl it tamiḻaracar mūvarukkum potup pacuaivarācup pallavar iruntaṉar eṉpatu teḷivā kiṟatu. aṭuttu, iraṇṭām muṟiyākap pulikēci pallavar nāṭṭiṉmītu paṭiyeṭuttapōtu naracimmavarmaṉ avaṉit tōṟkaṭittuk koṉṟa tōṭu avaṉuṭiya kōnakariyum kippaṟṟiṉāṉ eṉpati aṟivōm. kōnakar vātāpiyik kippaṟṟuvataṟku oru perumpaṭiyuṭaṉ tam tāṉit talivarāṉa parañcōtiyi aṉuppiviṭṭut tāṉ maṭṭum mūvēntarkaḷuṭaṉum, kaḷappiraruṭaṉum naracimmaṉ pōriṭṭāṉ. mutalil cakkaramaṅki eṉṉumiṭattil tōlviyuṟṟa avaṉ taṉatu perumpaṭi utavikku vantavuṭaṉ in nālvariyum tōṟkaṭittirukkalām. āka, naracimmavarmaṉ cāḷukkiyarkaḷiyum cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiya, kaḷappirar kaḷiyum muṭivil veṟṟi koṇṭāṉ eṉpatil ettakiya aiyamum illi
pallavar-kaṅkar pōr 
iraṇṭām pulikēci ki.pi. 642-l iṟantatiyum vātāpi patiṉmūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷ pallavar vacam iruntatiyum, ki.pi. 655-l pulikēciyiṉ makkaḷ mūvaril oruvaṉāṉa mutalām vikkiramātittaṉ aracuk kaṭṭilēṟiyatiyum aṟivōm. āṉāl ivaṉ iyalpāka aracukkaṭṭil ēṟavilli , ivaṉukkuc cantirātittaṉ, ātitta varmaṉ ākiya aṇṇaṉ mār iruntaṉar. ātittavarmaṉukkum mutalām vikkiramātittaṉukkum aracurimip pōr ēṟpaṭṭatu. itil ātittavarmaṉ naracimmavarmaṉiṉ tuṇiyi nāṭiṉāṉ. vikkiramātittaṉ taṉ tāy vaḻippāṭṭaṉāṉa turvinītaṉ tuṇiyi nāṭiṉāṉ. turvinītaṉukku naracimmavarmaṉukkum ēṟkaṉavē pakimi uṇṭu. turvinītaṉ āṇṭa kaṅkanāṭṭiṉ oru pakuti yāṉa koṅku nāṭṭi naracimmavarmaṉ kippaṟṟit turvinītaṉuṭiya tampiyik koṅkunāṭṭiṟku aracaṉākkiyatāl, turvinītaṉukkum, naracimmavarmaṉukkum pakimi ēṟpaṭṭatu. itaṉāl, cāḷukkiya nāṭṭu aracurimip pōril kaṅkaṉum, pallavaṉum etir etirkkaṭcikaḷi ātarittaṉar. piṉṉar, taṅkaḷukkuḷḷum pōriṭṭuk koṇṭaṉar. kaṭiciyil, ip pōrilum naracimmavarmaṉē veṟṟi peṟṟāṉ eṉak kōṭal ciṟappuṭittākum. 
īḻanāṭṭup pōr 
īḻanāṭṭu aracaṉ māṉavarmaṉ (māṉavammā) eṉpavaṉi aṭṭa tattaṉ eṉpavaṉ turattiyaṭittuviṭṭu aracik kippaṟṟik koṇṭāṉ, māṉavarmaṉ pallavanāṭu pōntu naracimmavarmaṉiṭam aṭikkalam pukuntu avaṉ maṉam makiḻumāṟu avaṉukku uṟutuṇiyāy niṉṟu ariya pala paṇikaḷic ceytāṉ. naracimma varmaṉ iraṇṭām pulikēciyuṭaṉ ceyta pōrilum māṉavarmaṉ āṟṟiya paṇi aḷaviṭaṟkariyatu. ittakiya ceyalkaḷāl makiḻcci aṭinta naracimmavarmaṉ, māṉavarmaṉukku māpaṭi, makkaḷpaṭi, marakkalappaṭi mutaliyavaṟṟi aṉuppi, avaṉ iḻanta paṭṭattip peṟa utaviṉāṉ. mutalil veṟṟikaṇṭamāṉavarmaṉ tamiḻar paṭikaḷ kāñcikku mīṇṭatum pakivaṉāl tōṟkaṭikkappaṭ ṭāṉ. mīṇṭum māṉavarmaṉ kāñcikku vantu cila kālattukkup piṉṉar naracimmavarmaṉiṉ perumpaṭiyuṭaṉ māmallapurattut tuṟimukappaṭ ṭiṉattiliruntu kappalēṟi īḻattiṟkuc ceṉṟu, taṉ pakivaṉi veṉṟu āṭciyik kippaṟṟiṉāṉ. ivvāṟu iraṇṭu muṟi pallavap paṭikaḷ kaṭal kaṭantu ceṉṟu ilaṅkiyil veṟṟi peṟṟatōṭu māṉa varmaṉukku avaṉuṭiya aracurimiyiyum mīṭṭut tantaṉaveṉpatik kācakkuṭic ceppēṭṭilum, īḻanāṭṭu varalāṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟa makā vamcam eṉṉum nūlilum teḷivāka aṟikiṟōm, 
camayam 
naracimmavarmaṉ civa camayattip piṉpaṟṟiṉāṉ. ivaṉ tantiyiṉ kālattil civam ceḻittōṅkat toṭaṅkiyatu eṉpatiyum muṉṉarē kaṇṭōm. in nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiya iṟineṟi iyakkam camaṇa, putta camayaṅkaḷi aḻikkavum, civaneṟiyip parappavum muṉintatu. naracimmavarmaṉ kālattil pallava nāṭṭiṟku vanta ki.pi. 640} yuvāṉ cuvāṅku eṉṟa cīṉappayaṇi pallavariṉ kōnakarākiya kāñcipuram āṟu kal cuṟṟaḷavu uḷḷateṉṟum, ak kālattut tamiḻaka nakaraṅkaḷuḷ itu māperum nakaramākak kāṭciyaḷitta teṉṟum, in nakaril nūṟṟukkum mēṟpaṭṭa puttap paḷḷikaḷ iruntaṉa veṉṟum, avaṟṟil patiṉāyiram pikkukaḷ taṅki aṟam vaḷarkka ēntukaḷ amikkappaṭṭiruntaṉaveṉ ṟum kuṟippiṭuvatiliruntu ik kālattil putta camayam peṟṟirunta mēmpāṭṭiyaṟiyalām. ip paḷḷikaḷil payiṉṟu periya aṟiñarākiya varkaḷil tarumapālar eṉpārum oruvarāvar. ip pēraṟiñartāṉ nāḷantāp palkalikkaḻakattil putta camayap pērāciriyarākat tikaḻntavar āvār. yuvāṉ cuvāṅkiṉ kuṟippiliruntu kāñcipurattil eṇpatiṟkum mēṟpaṭṭa kōyilkaḷ iruntaṉaveṉṟum, avi perum pālum tikampararkaḷukku camaṇarkaḷukku uriyaviyeṉṟum terikiṟatu. mēlum, in nakaril acōkap pēraracarāl kaṭṭappaṭṭa nūṟu aṭi uyaramuḷḷa oru paḷḷi iruntateṉṟum ivaṉ kuṟippiṭukiṟāṉ. putta perumāṉ in nakarukku vantu taṅki makkaḷit tam camayattiṟku māṟumpaṭi ceytā reṉṟum avaṉ kūṟukiṟāṉ. tāṉ īḻattiliruntu kāñcikku vantirunta munnūṟu puttappikkukaḷuṭaṉ uriyāṭi yatiyum cīṉappayaṇa kuṟippiṭu kiṟāṉ. ivaṉuṭiya kuṟippukaḷi liruntu kāñcipuram oru taliciṟanta palkalikkaḻaka naṭuvamāka viḷaṅkiyatu eṉpatiyum, in nāṭṭil kaṟṟuttērnta aṟiñarkaḷ kūṭukiṉṟa iṭamāka itu irunta teṉpati yum iṅkiruntu īḻanāṭṭiṟkuk kaṭal vaḻiyākac celvataṟku mūṉṟu nāḷkaḷ ākumeṉpatiyum aṟikiṟōm. pallava nāṭṭil maṭṭumē yaṉṟip pāṇṭiya nāṭṭilum puttam ciṟappuṟṟiruntatu. in nāṭṭiṉ talinakarākiya maturiyil puttam ciṟupāṉmiyākavum, tikampara camayam perumpāṉmiyākavum iruntateṉṟum, innakariṉ kiḻakkup pakutiyil acōcap pēraracariṉ tampiyāṉamakaṉ makēntiraṉ cuṭṭiya putta vikāri iṭintupōṉa kaṭṭaṭamāka iruntateṉṟum yuvāṉ cuvāṅku kuṟippiṭu kiṟāṉ. 
ivvāṟu acikkamuṭiyāta aḷaviṟkup perum cāykāl peṟṟu viṭṭa putta, camaṇacamayaṅkaḷukku etirākat tirunāvukkaracar, appūti yaṭikaḷ, ciṟuttoṇṭar, tiruñāṉacampantar, tirunīlakaṇṭar, neṭumāṟa nāyaṉār, maṅkiyarkkaraciyār, kulacciṟiyār, tirunīlanakkar, muruka nāyaṉār, kuṅkiliyak kaliyar mutalāṉa civacamaya aṭiyārkaḷ civa camayatti makkaḷiṭiyē parappattoṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ. ivarkaḷil tiruñāṉacampantarum, tirunāvukkaracarum tamiḻnāṭeṅkilum cuṟṟup payaṇam ceytu putta, camaṇa camayaṅkaḷi muṭukkamāka etirttup pōrāṭi makkaḷic civar ākkiṉārkaḷ. ivarkaḷāl pāṭappeṟṟa tēvāram, tiruvācakap pāṭalkaḷ piṉṉar civa camayat tirumuṟikaḷākat tokukkappeṟṟaṉa. itaṉāl, tamiḻariṉ camaya neṟikkum, moḻi vaḷarccikkum pērukkamum pērāḷumiyum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. 
civa aṭiyārkaḷip pōlavē viṇava aṭiyārkaḷum iṟi neṟi iyakkatti muṭukkamākap parappuvataṟkup pāṭupaṭṭārkaḷ. mutal āḻvārkaḷ eṉṟu ciṟappittuk kūṟappaṭum poykiyāḻvār, pūtattāḻvār, pēyāḻvār ākiya mūvarum pāṭiya paktippāṭalkaḷ tamiḻariṉ tūya vāḻviyum, tamiḻiṉ mēṉmiyiyum uyarttiṉa. ivarkaḷit tavirat tirumaḻici āḻvār eṉpavarum naracimmavarmaṉ kālattil iruntār. ivarkaḷāl viṇava camayam maṟumalarcci peṟṟatu. ivarkaḷ iyaṟṟiya teyvīkap pāṭalkaḷ yāvum 'nālāyirat tivyap pirapantam' eṉṉum peru nāṭācut tokukkappeṟṟatu. makēntiravarmaṉ kālattil toṭaṅkappeṟṟa iṟineṟi iyakkam naracimmavarmaṉ kālattil peritum valuppeṟṟu viṭṭatāl camaṇamum puttamum kuṉṟic civam taliciṟantu niṉṟatu. viṇavamum maṟumalarccipeṟṟatu. 
mēṟkūṟiya icineṟi iyakkattiṉ ciṉṉaṅkaḷāka vātāpi koṇṭa naracimmaṉ palatiṟappaṭṭa kuṭivarikkōyilkaḷi māmalla purattilum, paravalākat tamiḻnāṭeṅkaṇum camittāṉ. tirucciyiluḷḷa kuṭivarik kōyilum, nāmakkal maliyaṭiyiluḷḷa naraciṅkap perumāḷ kuṭivarik kōyilum ivaṉ kālattaviyeṉak karutappaṭum kiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟit tavira māmallapurattil makiṭācura maṇṭapam, varāka maṇṭapam, tirumūrtti maṇṭapam mutaliyaṉavum iṉṟu pañcapāṇṭavar irataṅkaḷ' eṉap potuvāka aḻikkappaṭum oṟṟik kaṟṟōrkaḷum im maṉṉaṉāl camikkappaṭṭaviyākum. ivvāṟu ivaṉ kalikkum, camayattiṟkum oruṅkē paṇipurintu ulakam pōṟṟum kalic celvatti māmalliyil paṭittu, ataṉōṭu tāṉum aḻiyātu niṟkiṉṟāṉ. māmallapurattil perum pāṟikaḷiṉmītu ivaṉāl cetukkappeṟṟa puṭippōviyaṅkaḷ ulakak kaliñarkaḷiṉ cuvaṉatti īrppaṉavāy uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ kōvarttaṉa maliyik kuṭiyākap piṭikkum kaṇṇaṉiṉ ciṟpamum, kaṅkikkarik kāṭciyum ciṟappākak kuṟippiṭat takkaṉavākum. ivaṉuṭiya kuṭivarik kōyilkaḷil oṟṟik kaṟṟērkaḷilum pāṟic ciṟpaṅkaḷilum kāṇappaṭum kaliyaḻaku kaliñariṉ eṇṇattik kiḷarttuvatāyuḷḷatu. ivaṉāl, tiruccikku arukil tiruttavattuṟi lālkuṭiyiyaṭutta peruvaḷanallūrar eṉṉum ūrukku arukiluḷḷa pallāvaram eṉṉum ciṟṟūril oru kōṭṭi caṭṭappeṟṟateṉṟum, piṉṉar, avaratu aḻintupaṭṭateṉṟum kūṟuvar. 
taṉ tōḷvaliyālum, maṉavaliyālum, aṟivuttiṟattālum mēmpaṭṭa naracimmavarmaṉ, tāṉ camitta kōvilkaḷilellām taṉatu virutuppeyarkaḷ palavaṟṟi veṭṭuvittuḷḷāṉ. avi māmallaṉ, srīparaṉ, srīmēkaṉ, puvaṉamājaṉ, attiyantakkāmaṉ, amēyamāyaṉ, iraṇacayaṉ, nayanāṅkuraṉ mutaliyaṉavākum. māmallapurattut tirumūrttik kuṭivariyil ivaṉ peyar 'malla' eṉṟu pallavak kiranta eḻuttil cetukkappeṟṟuḷḷatu. tirukkaḻukkuṉṟattu oṟṟik kal maṇṭapak. kuṭivariyil "vātāpi koṇṭa naraciṅkap pōttaracar' eṉṟu tamiḻil cetukkap peṟṟuḷḷatu. camaṟkirutattilum ivaṉuṭiya virutup peyarkaḷ poṟikkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ittakiya pērum pukaḻum peṟṟu vāḻnta māmallaṉuṭiya pallavap pēraracu vaṭakkē vaṭapeṇṇiyāṟu mutal teṟkē veḷḷāṟu variyil paraviyiruntatu. atāvatu itu toṇṭi nāṭṭiyum, cōḻanāṭṭiyum taṉṉuḷ koṇṭiruntatu. tarumarācar tēril ivaṉuṭiya uruvam puṭippuc ciṟpamāka amin tuḷḷatu. inta uruvam taṉiyē camikkappaṭṭu iruppatālum, kātu cuḷil kuṇṭalaṅkaḷum, mārpil pūṇūālum, veṟumēṉiyum koṇṭu tikaḻvatālum ivaṉuṭiyacivacamayappaṟṟi itaṉ tōṟṟattiliruntu aṟiyakkūṭum eṉpar. 


iraṇṭām makēntiravarmaṉ (ki.pi. 668-70) 
iraṇṭām makēntiravarmaṉ mutalām naracimmavarmaṉukkup piṟaku ariyaṇiyēṟiṉāṉ. āṉāl, avaṉukku muṭicūṭṭu viḻā eppōtu naṭantatu. avaṉ ettaṉiyāṇṭukaḷ āṇṭāṉeṉpaṉa paṟṟiya tiṭṭa vaṭṭamāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭikkavilli. piṟkālap paṭṭiyaṅkaḷilum ivaṉuṭiya āṭciyil naṭanta nikaḻvukaḷ paṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷ ētum kāṇappaṭavilli, āṉāl, ivaṉ avvakuppār oḻukavēṇṭiya muṟikaḷik kūṟum aṟanūl vaḻi āṇṭāṉeṉṟu maṭṭumē vēlūrp pāḷiyam paṭṭayam kūṟukiṟatu. 'kaṭikikaḷi vaḷarttāṉ" eṉṉum kuṟippiliruntu ivaṉ kalvikkūṭaṅkaḷi yamittuk kaṟṟa pārppaṉar kaḷi yamartti. ak kāla muṟippaṭi kalviyi vaḷarttāṉ eṉa aṟiya lām. cāḷukkiyar paṭṭiyaṅcuṉil makēntiraṉiṉ pukaḻi vikkira mātittaṉ maṟiyac ceytāṉ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭiruppatāl, ivaṉ cāḷukkiya maṉṉaṉ vikkiramātittaṉuṭaṉ porutāṉ eṉṟa ceyti aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu. itit tavira muṉṉāḷ micūr mānilattil cimōkā māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa cattamaṉē eṉṉum ūril kaṉṉaṭa moḻiyil kāṇap paṭum naṭukal kalveṭṭoṉṟilum ivaṉ vaṭavaracaṉ oruvaṉuṭaṉ pōriṭṭa ceyti kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. 
mutalām paramēcuvaravarmaṉ (ki.pi.670-95) 
ivaṉ iraṇṭām makēntiravarmaṉiṉ makaṉ āvāṉ. ivaṉ aḷitta kūram, uṉṉakkuravappāḷiyam ākiya ceppēṭukaḷilum, vēluārppāḷiyam, utayēntiram ākiya ceppēṭukaḷilum, cāḷukkiyar ceppēṭukaḷilum, periya purāṇam mutaliya ilakkiyaṅkaḷilum, māmallapuram kalveṭṭukaḷilum ivaṉip paṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa, uṉṉakkuravappāḷiyam ceppēṭṭiliruntu ivaṉ tōrāya mākak cipi, 568-59-l aracukkaṭṭi pōṉāṉ eṉak karuta iṭamēṟpaṭu 
pallavar - cāḷukkiyar pōr. 
ivaṉ kālattil naṭanta pala mukāmi nikaḻccikaḷil ivaṉ -- cāḷukkiya rōṭu poruti veṟṟikaṇṭatu ciṟappuṭiyatākum. cāḷukkiya maṉṉaṉ iraṇṭām pulikēciyiṉ makaṉāṉa mutalām vikkiramātittaṉ ki.pi. 555-580) taṉ tanti pallavariṭam tōṟṟut talinakariyiḻanta iḻiviyeṇṇip pallavarip paḻivāṅkum poruṭṭut toṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ mītu paṭiyeṭuttāṉ, mutalil pallavariyetirttu veṟṟi peṟṟa ivaṉ kāñciyik kippaṟṟa muṟpaṭṭāṉ. cāḷukkiyariṭam tōṟṟa pallavar paṭikaḷ kōṭṭi matilukkuḷ patuṅkikkoṇṭaṉa. piṉṉar, cāḷukkiyar paṭimatilit tuḷittuk kāñciyik kippaṟṟik koṇṭatu. ivveṟṟikkuppiṉ vikkiramātittaṉ kāvirikkariyiluḷḷa uṟiyūril vantu taṅkiṉāṉ. itaṟkiṭiyil tōlviyaṭinta mutalām paramēcu varavarmaṉ āntiranāṭṭiliruntu perumpaṭi tiraṭṭik koṇarntu tiruccirāppaḷḷikku arukiluḷḷa peruvaḷanallūril vikkiramātit taṉit tōṟkaṭittāṉ. pōril tōṟṟa vikkiramātittaṉ uṭukka uṭiyiṉṟik kantiyic cuṟṟikkoṇṭu ōṭiṉāṉeṉṟu kūram ceppēṭu kūṟukiṟatu. āka, cāḷukkiyar paṭṭayaṅkaḷ vikkiramātitta ṉuṭiya veṟṟikaḷiyum, palvēr paṭṭi yaṅkaḷ, pālpavar veṟṟikaḷi yum kūṟukiṉṟaṉavē yoḻiya, avaravar tōlvikaḷik kūṟavilli. " eṉavētāṉ īrākap pātiriyār, cōvē tuprāyl pōṉṟa varalāṟ nāciriyarkaḷ irutarattārum veṟṟiyum, tōlviyum māṟimāṟip peṟṟuk kaṭiciyākap palpavar vāki cūṭiyirukka vēṇṭum eṉkiṉṟaṉar. kāñcik kayilācanātar kōyiṟ kalveṭṭoṉṟil 'paramēcuvaraṉ vātāpiyi aḻittāṉeṉap poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itiliruntu taṉ pāṭṭaṉip pōlavē naracimmavarmaṉip pōlavē cāḷukkiyar tali nakariṉiyum aḻittu mīṇṭāṉeṉak koḷḷalām. 
kalippaṇi 
paramēcuvaravarmaṉ oru ciṟanta civaṉaṭiyāṉ āvāṉ, nāṭu muḻuvatilum pala civaṉ kōyilkaḷik kaṭṭiyatōṭu pāḻaṭinta kōyilkaḷip putuppittāṉ. ivvāṟu ivaṉ kaṭṭiya kōyilkaḷuḷ kuṟippiṭattakkatu kāñcikku arukiluḷḷa kūram eṉṉum ūril kaṭṭappaṭṭa ciṟu kaṭṭumāṉak kōyilākum. tamiḻakattil kaṭṭaṭappaṭṭa mutaṟkaṟṟaḷi ituvēyeṉak kūṟuvārumuḷar. mutalām makēntiraṉē kaṟkōyilkaḷiyum kaṭṭiṉāṉeṉpati ēṟkaṉavē kūṟiyuḷḷōm. ic civaṉ kōyilukku ivaṉ paramēcuvaramaṅkalam eṉṉum oru ciṟṟūri māṉiyamāka aḷittāṉ eṉṟum, ik kōyil “vityāvinta pallava paramēcuvara kirukam' eṉa peyarpeṟṟatu eṉṟum aṟikiṟōm. - 
ivaṉ kālattil kōyilkaḷil kālamuṟi tavaṟāmal vaḻipāṭukaḷ naṭantaṉaveṉṟum potumakkaḷukkākak kōyilkaḷil pāratam paṭikkap paṭṭateṉṟum kūram ceppēṭukaḷiliruntu aṟikiṟōm. 
māmallapurattiluḷḷa oṟṟikkallālāṉa cuṇēcar tēriyum, irāmānucar maṇṭapattiyum, tarumarācartēril mūṉṟāvatu māṭatti yum ivaṉ camittāṉeṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. kaṇēcar tēril ivaṉ veṭṭuvitta patiṉōru camaṟkirutap pāṭalkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ivi iva ṉuṭiya ciṟanta paṇpukaḷiyum civaṉ mītu ivaṉ koṇṭa pattiyiyum ciṟappuṟac cilēṭiyākac ceppukiṉṟaṉa. tarumarācar tēriṉ mūṉṟām aṭukkil iraṇacayaṉ, attiyantak kāmaṉ eṉṉum paṭṭappeyarkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl it tērukku attiyatantak kāmap pallu vēcuram' eṉṟa peyar uṇṭeṉpatiyum aṟikiṟōm, 
paramēcuvaravarmaṉukkuc cittiramāyaṉ, kuṇapācaṉaṉ, attiyantak kāmaṉ, svastaṉ, srīniti, srīparaṉ, iraṇcayaṉ, taruṇāṅkuraṉ, kāmarākaṉ mutaliya virutup peyarkaḷum uṇṭu. ivaṟṟiliruntu ivaṉuṭiya civapaktiyiyum, vīrattiyum, ivaṉ vaṭamoḻiyil peṟ tirunta pulamiyiyum naṉku aṟiyalām. ivaṉ kaṅkarōṭum pōriṭṭā ṉeṉṟum kūṟuvar. icceytiyikkaṅka maṉṉaṉ cīvikkiramaṉ (kipi. 55() 7) paṭṭayattiliruntu aṟikiṟōm. āṉāl, pallavar paṭṭayaṅkaḷil itaṟkāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ illi. 
irācacimmaṉ (iraṇṭām naracimmavarmaṉ - ki.pi. 695-722) 
pallavar-cāḷukkiyar pōr - mutalām paramēcuvaravarmaṉukkup piṟaku avaṉ makaṉ iraṇṭām naracimmavarmaṉ aracukkaṭṭil ēṟiṉāṉ, avaṉuṭiya rēyūruc ceppēṭukaḷiliruntu avaṉ ki.pi. 700 ām āṇṭil aracukkaṭṭil ēṟiyirukkalām eṉavum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. avaṉuṭiya kalveṭṭukaḷ yāvum camaṟkirutattil uḷḷaṉa. ik kalveṭṭukaḷiliruntu avaṉukkum, cāḷukkiya aracaṉāṉa vinayātittaṉ (kipi 87-59 eṉpavaṉukkum pōr naṭantatiyaṟikiṟōm. eṉavē, avaṉ kālattilum pallava-cāḷukkiyar pōr naṭantatu eṉpatiyaṟikiṟōm. cila āciriyarkaḷ avaṉ kālattil pōrē naṭakkavilli eṉak kūṟuvatu tavaṟāṉa karuttākum. cāḷukkiyar paṭṭayaṅkaḷil “cāḷukkiyar marapiṉ aḻiviṟkum, tāḻviṟkum pallavarē poṟuppāḷikaḷ' eṉṟum, 'vinayā tittaṉ pallavaṉatu muḻuppaṭiyiyum kippaṟṟiṉāṉ; cōḻar, pāṇṭiyar, ciṅkaḷar mutaliyōriṭam tiṟi vāṅkiṉāṉ' eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itiliruntu vinayātittaṉ pallavar, cōḻar, pāṇṭiyar, cērar mutaliyōri oṭukki amiti uṇṭākkap pala paṭiyeṭuppu kaḷi mēṟkoṇṭāṉ eṉpatum pulaṉākiṟatu. 
pallavar kalveṭṭukaḷil iraṇṭām naracimmavarmaṉ pala paṭap pukaḻap peṟṟuḷḷāṉ. avaṉuṭiya aṟivukkūrmi, uṭal valimi, pōrttiṟaṉ, aḻaku, āṇmi , vīram, mēṉmi , pukaḻ mutaliyaṉa mikac ciṟappuṟa vivarikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. itikāca vīrarkaḷāṉa aruccuṉaṉ, irāmaṉ mutaliyōriṉ viṟpōrukkuc camamāka ivaṉuṭiya vilvali pukaḻappaṭṭuḷḷatu. uṭal valiyālum pukaḻālum ivaṉ naracimma avatāratti ottavaṉ eṉṟum, pōril cimmaṉ pōṉṟavaṉ; pōril mikak koṭiyavaṉ; pakivari aḻippavaṉ; pakivarukku iṭiyēṟu pōṉṟavaṉ; vīṇariyaḻippavaṉ; maṉavuṟuti
yuṭiyavaṉ; celvatti yum, pēraracukaḷiyum kippaṟṟupavaṉ; kaḷippaṭiyātavaṉ; iṭikkum iṭi pōṉṟavaṉ eṉṟellām kalveṭṭukaḷil ivaṉuṭiya vīram viḷakkappeṟṟuḷḷatu. ittakiya viḷakkaṅkaḷiliruntu ivaṉ pala pōrkaḷil īṭupaṭṭu veṟṟi kaṇṭavaṉ eṉpatit teḷivāka aṟiyalām. āyiṉ ivaṉōṭu pōriṭṭavarkaḷil ciṟappākak kuṟippiṭattakkavaṉ cāḷukkiya maṉṉaṉāṉa vinaya pātittaṉē āvāṉ. mutalām paramēcuvaravarma ṉiṭam vinayātittaṉuṭiya tantiyāṉa mutalām vikkiramātittaṉ . peruvaḷanallūril muṟiyaṭikkappaṭṭāṉ eṉpati aṟivōm. taṉ tantikku ēṟpaṭṭa iḻivip pōkkavum, pallavarip paḻivāṅkavum vinayātittaṉ pallava nāṭṭiṉ mītu paṭiyeṭuttāṉ. ivvāṟu iraṇṭām pulikēciyum, avaṉukkuppiṉ mutalām vikkiramātit taṉum, avaṉukkup piṉ vinayātittaṉum toṭarntu pallava nāṭṭiṉ mītu paṭiyeṭuttut tōlviyiyē caṇṭaṉar. iṉi vinayātit taṉukkum iraṇṭām naracimmavarmaṉukkum naṭanta pōriṉic caṟṟu viḷakkuvōm. 
vinayātittaṉukkum iraṇṭām naracimmavarmaṉukkum eppoḻutu pōr naṭantatu eṉpatu tiṭṭamākat teriyavilli, āyiṉum iv viruvarukkum kaṭumiyāṉa pōr naṭantatu eṉpatiyum, appōriṉāl nāṭṭiloru koṭiya pañcam ēṟpaṭṭateṉpatiyum kalveṭṭuc cāṉṟukaḷiliruntu ūkittu aṟiya muṭikiṟatu. pallava nāṭum, cāḷukkiya nāṭum ip pañcattāl vāṭiṉeṉṟa ceytiyi maṭṭum nām curukkamāka aṟiyalām. 
pallavar - kaṅkar pōr 
iraṇṭām naracimmavarmaṉ kaṅkar nāṭṭiṉ mītu pōr toṭuttāṉ. tām cāḷukkiyarkaḷuṭaṉ pōr ceytu koṇṭirunta pōtu avarkaḷ taṉakkiḻitta iṉṉalukkāka avarkaḷip paḻi vāṅkiṉāṉ. kaṅkapāṭiyi āṇṭa mutalām cīmāṟaṉ(ki.pi, pi.726) eṉpavaṉ pallavaṉi veṉṟāṉ eṉṟu kaṅkar paṭṭayam kūṟukiṟatu. ittakiya māṟupaṭṭa karuttukaḷāl yār yāri veṉṟatu eṉṟa uṇmiyi uṇaramuṭiyavilli. āyiṉum, pallavarukkum, kaṅkarukkum pōr naṭantatu maṭṭum uṟutiyākiṟatu. 
im maṉṉaṉ tipettiyarkaḷōṭu pōr purivataṟkākap paṭik kalaṉkaḷ kēṭṭuc cīṉap pēraracarukkut taṉ tūtuvaṉi aṉuppiṉā ṉeṉṟum, avaṉ vēṇṭukōḷukkiṇaṅkac cīṉap pēraracaṉum tam paṭikkalaṅkaḷik koṭuttu utaviṉāṉeṉṟum cīṉa varalāṟṟuk kuṟippoṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu. āṉāl, ivaṉ tipettiṉ mītu paṭi yeṭuttāṉā illiyā eṉṟa vivaram atil kuṟippiṭappaṭavilli,
camayam 
irācacimmaṉ eṉṟa ciṟappup peyar peṟṟa im maṉṉaṉ oru ciṟanta civapaktaṉ āvāṉ. toṭarntu naṭanta pallavar - cāḷukkiyar pōrkaḷālum, pallavar - kaṅkar pōrkaḷālum nāṭṭil perumpañcam ēṟpaṭṭatu. ip pañcam mūṉṟāṇṭukaḷ nīṭittatu. appoḻutu vaccirapōti eṉṉum puttap periyār kāñcikku vantār eṉṟum irācacimmaṉ pañcam nīṅka iṟivaṉi vēṇṭumāṟu apperiyāri vēṇṭik koṇṭatākavum, ap periyāriṉ vēṇṭutalukkēṟpa maḻipeytu pañcam nīṅkiyatākavum cīṉa nūl kuṟippoṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu. pañcam nīṅkiyapiṉ irācacimmaṉ kāñciyilirunta kaṭikiyic cemmip paṭuttiṉāṉeṉṟu vēlūrppāḷiyam ceppēṭu kūṟukiṟatu. itaṉi yaṭuttu ivaṉ kāñciyil kayilācanātar kōyiliyum, airāvatēccurar kōyiliyum, māmallapurattiluḷḷa kaṭaṟkarik kōyiliyum paṉi malic civaṉ kōyiliyum kaṭṭiṉāṉ. ik kōyilkaḷilellām ivaṉuṭiya virutuppeyarkaḷ poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iv virutup peyarkaḷiliruntu ivaṉ ciṟanta civa paktaṉ eṉpatiyum, civa cittāntattil pēraṟivu uṭiyavaṉ eṉpatiyum, icikkaruvikaḷi icippatil ciṟanta kaliña ṉeṉpatiyum, ciṟanta icippulavaṉ eṉpatiyum aṟiyalām. pārppaṉarkaḷukkuc ciṟanta kallūriyi amittu atil avarkaḷip pōtikkumpaṭi ceytāṉ eṉpatiyum taṉ kīḻuḷḷa nilaṅkaḷi ap pārppaṉar nukarumāṟu ceytāṉ eṉpatiyum ik kalveṭṭukaḷiliruntu aṟikiṟōm. ivaṉuṭiya pattiyi mecciya civaperumāṉ tiruniṉṟavūr (tiṇṇaṉūr) eṉṟa iṭattil taṉ aruḷi veḷippaṭuttiṉāreṉṟu periyapurāṇattilum, culveṭṭukaḷilum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. im maṉṉaṉukku araṅkapatāki eṉṟu oru tēvi iruntāḷ. ivaḷ ciṟanta kaliccelviyāvāḷ. iva ṉuṭiya civapaktikku mūlakaraṇiyamākavum ivaḷ viḷaṅkiṉāḷ. palvēṟu kōyilkaḷukkum ivaḷ taṉ peyarāl māṉiyaṅkaḷ aḷittāḷ. 
poruḷiyal nilimi
